### [1] [Equivariant Latent Alignment via Flow Matching under Group Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30705)
*Sunghyun Kim, Jaehoon Hahm, Jeongwoo Shin, Joonseok Lee*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (55.34) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Residual Latent Flow，一种基于流匹配的框架，用于纠正等变表示学习中潜在空间的错位问题，从而提升新视角合成在旋转群对称性下的质量和一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的等变表示学习方法在构建潜在空间时，往往存在潜在错位（latent misalignment）问题——即预期的群作用与实际潜在空间中所需的变换之间存在不一致，导致学到的潜在表示无法一致地保持群对称性所要求的等变关系，进而影响新视角合成的质量。

Method: 提出 Residual Latent Flow 框架，利用流匹配（flow matching）来纠正错位的潜在表示，使其更好地满足底层等变关系。该方法在旋转群 SO(n) 下对潜在空间进行校正，增强等变一致性。

Result: 综合实验表明，该方法显著减少了潜在错位问题，并在旋转群 SO(n) 下提升了新视角合成的质量。

Conclusion: Residual Latent Flow 通过流匹配有效纠正等变潜在空间的错位，为几何感知生成模型提供了更一致的等变表示，提升了新视角合成的性能。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于流匹配的等变潜在对齐方法，用于改进新视角合成（novel view synthesis）的质量和几何一致性。新视角合成是3D生成与重建的核心任务之一，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 "3D 生成与重建"。

Importance reason: Focuses on equivariant latent alignment for 3D novel view synthesis, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's key interests in autoregressive/diffusion video generation or unified multimodal models. Signals: Equivariant representation learning, Flow matching for latent correction, Novel view synthesis, SO(n) rotation groups, Not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Geometry-aware generative models and novel view synthesis approaches have shown strong potential in visual fidelity and consistency. In parallel, equivariant representation learning has emerged as a powerful framework for constructing latent spaces where analytically known group transformations could act directly, capturing geometric structure in data and enhancing both interpretability and generalization in novel view synthesis. However, we identify that existing approaches often suffer from latent misalignment, a discrepancy between the intended group action and the actually required transformations in the latent space. Consequently, the learned latents often fail to consistently preserve the equivariant relations imposed by the underlying group symmetry. To address this, we propose Residual Latent Flow, a flow-based framework that corrects the misaligned latents, thereby improving compliance with the underlying equivariance relation. Our comprehensive experiments show that our method significantly reduces latent misalignment and improves novel view synthesis quality, under rotation groups SO(n).

</details>


### [2] [Function2Scene: 3D Indoor Scene Layout from Functional Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30819)
*Ruiqi Wang, Qimin Chen, Daniel Ritchie, Angel X. Chang, Manolis Savva, Kai Wang, Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (52.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Function2Scene 提出从功能规格（自然语言设计简报）生成3D室内布局的框架，通过解析用户角色和活动需求，从17项功能设计约束（空间、人体工学、活动、环境）出发，结合LLM几何测量和VLM视觉评估的迭代检查-修复循环，生成更符合人类使用需求的室内布局，在专业设计案例对比中94.3%被偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本驱动的3D室内场景合成方法大多基于物体中心提示（"放什么家具"），而非从空间使用功能（"谁使用、做什么"）出发。然而真实室内设计的核心是空间如何支持人的活动和生理需求，现有方法缺乏对功能约束的系统建模。

Method: Function2Scene 包含三个模块：(1) 功能解析器，从自然语言设计简报中提取 occupant personas 和 activities；(2) 功能约束引擎，从17项设计准则（空间、人体工学、活动、环境）推导定制化约束；(3) 迭代布局生成器，通过工具增强的 check-and-repair 循环（结合几何测量、LLM上下文推理和VLM视觉评估）逐步优化布局。

Result: 在30个专业室内设计案例上，Function2Scene 生成的布局在满足功能需求方面优于近期基于LLM的场景合成基线，在94.3%的成对比较中被人类评估者偏好。

Conclusion: Function2Scene 重新定义了文本驱动的室内场景合成——从放置合理的物体转向设计支持人类使用的空间，证明了功能约束驱动的迭代优化方法在室内布局生成中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究从功能规格（自然语言设计简报）生成3D室内场景布局，属于3D场景生成范畴，与 canonical 子主题 "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D indoor scene layout from functional specs, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's key boost keywords. Signals: 3D indoor scene synthesis, functional design constraints, LLM+VLM iterative refinement, not autoregressive or diffusion video/image generation, no world model or efficient inference

Abstract: Most text-driven 3D indoor scene synthesis methods generate rooms from object-centric prompts, asking what furniture should be placed rather than how the space is used. Yet in real interior design, a layout is judged by how well it supports its occupants, e.g., their activities and physical needs. We introduce Function2Scene, a framework for generating 3D indoor layouts from functional specifications, i.e., natural-language design briefs describing who will use a room and what they need to do there. Given such a specification, our system parses occupant personas and activities, derives a customized set of functional design constraints from a taxonomy of 17 criteria spanning spatial, ergonomic, activity, and environmental considerations, and uses these constraints to guide layout generation. Rather than relying on an LLM to directly produce a final scene, Function2Scene performs iterative evaluation and refinement through a tool-augmented check-and-repair loop, combining geometric measurements, LLM-based contextual reasoning, and VLM-based visual assessment. Experiments on 30 professionally written interior-design cases show that Function2Scene produces layouts that better satisfy functional requirements than recent LLM-based scene synthesis baselines, with our results preferred in 94.3% of pairwise comparisons. Our work reframes text-driven indoor scene synthesis from placing plausible objects to designing spaces that support human use.

</details>


### [3] [Foundation VAEs for 3D CT Reconstruction, Augmentation, and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30893)
*Qi Chen, Shuhan Ding, Yu Gu, Nan Liu, Jiang Bian, Alan Yuille, Zongwei Zhou, Jingjing Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (41.84) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现，在自然图像和视频上预训练的 Foundation VAE（编码器+解码器冻结）可以作为统一的 CT 重建、增强和生成接口，无需微调即可在异构扫描协议和疾病类型下保持解剖结构完整性，并支持条件潜扩散模型进行多疾病 CT 生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练 CT 专用的 VAE 需要大量计算和工程成本，且在异构扫描仪、协议和疾病条件下性能下降。本文旨在探索是否可以利用在自然图像/视频上大规模预训练的 Foundation VAE，无需微调即可作为 CT 重建、增强和生成的统一接口。

Method: 使用预训练的 Foundation VAE（在自然图像和视频上训练），冻结编码器和解码器，直接对 CT 体积进行压缩和重建。在 VAE 潜空间中训练条件潜扩散模型（conditional latent diffusion model）用于 CT 生成。通过重建后的 CT 数据训练分割模型，评估重建质量对下游任务的影响。

Result: Foundation VAE 重建的 CT 体积能保留解剖结构并抑制采集噪声；基于重建数据训练的分割模型在胰腺肿瘤和肺肿瘤上的表面精度（NSD）平均提升 3.9%；条件潜扩散模型在潜空间中实现 FVD 降低 3.9%、CT CLIP 分数提升 36.2%，跨 18 种疾病类型的生成忠实度 AUC 提升 2.76%。

Conclusion: Foundation VAE 可以作为可扩展的 CT 表示复用和忠实 CT 生成的实用接口，证明了自然图像/视频预训练的 VAE 在医学影像领域具有强大的零样本迁移能力。

Classification reason: 论文研究基于 Foundation VAE 的 3D CT 重建、增强与生成，属于 3D 医学影像生成与重建范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）高度相关，因此复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Medical CT reconstruction is outside your priority subtopics and flagged by negative keywords. Signals: Medical imaging, CT reconstruction, Foundation VAE, negative keyword match, no autoregressive or video generation focus

Abstract: Variational autoencoders (VAEs) compress high resolution CT volumes into compact latents while preserving clinically relevant structure. However, training CT-specific VAEs from scratch or heavily fine-tuning them incurs substantial computational and engineering cost, and often degrades under heterogeneous scanners, protocols, and diseases. This paper makes a progressive stride toward training-free medical VAEs by leveraging a critical observation: a single Foundation VAE, pretrained at scale on natural images and videos, can serve as a unified interface for CT Reconstruction, Augmentation, and Generation. With both encoder and decoder frozen, the Foundation VAE reconstructs CT volumes with preserved anatomy while suppressing acquisition noise; training segmentation models on these reconstructions improves surface accuracy by 3.9% NSD on average for pancreatic tumor and lung tumor. Within the same Foundation VAE latent space, a conditional latent diffusion model achieves 3.9% lower average FVD with 36.2% higher CT CLIP score, and improves multi-disease generation faithfulness across 18 types by 2.76% AUC. These results demonstrate Foundation VAEs as a practical interface for scalable CT representation reuse and faithful CT generation. Our code and demo are available at .

</details>


### [4] [Benchmarking Single-Step Inpainting Methods for Multi-Object 3D Gaussian Splatting Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30987)
*Finn Dröge, Cecilia Curreli, Abhishek Saroha, Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (55.34) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统评测了多种2D修复模型在3D Gaussian Splatting场景中用于多物体移除与修复的效果，发现基于重建的修复模型在3D一致性上优于生成式扩散模型，从头初始化场景比微调现有场景效果更好，并提出了一个带有真实标注的多物体遮挡场景数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS场景中的物体移除与修复面临跨视角3D一致性的挑战，现有工作缺乏对不同2D修复模型在3D场景中适用性的系统比较，且缺少带有真实标注的多物体遮挡场景数据集。

Method: 系统比较了重建型（如LaMa）与生成型（如扩散模型）2D修复模型在3DGS场景中的表现，评估了两种单步方法（从头初始化场景 vs. 微调现有场景），并利用SOTA生成式2D修复模型构建了一个简单基线，强调在3D修复前先进行物体移除的重要性。同时提出了一个包含真实标注的多物体遮挡场景数据集。

Result: 重建型修复模型在3D一致性上优于生成式扩散模型；从头初始化场景比微调现有场景产生更高质量的结果；在3D修复前先进行物体移除能显著提升效果。

Conclusion: 对于3DGS场景的多物体修复，重建型2D修复模型更适合3D场景，从头初始化策略优于微调策略，且物体移除预处理是关键步骤。该工作为3D场景修复提供了系统性的评测基准和方法指导。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦于3D Gaussian Splatting场景中的物体移除与修复方法评测，属于3D场景生成与重建范畴，与[多模态生成]方向下的[3D 生成与重建]（CANONICAL）高度匹配。同时涉及生成式 AI方向下的生成内容评测，但核心贡献在3DGS场景修复，故主方向为多模态生成。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D inpainting evaluation, not directly on user's priority subtopics like autoregressive video generation or world models. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, object removal, 2D inpainter comparison, multi-object occlusion dataset, reconstruction-based vs. generative models

Abstract: The tasks of object removal and inpainting 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) scenes face challenges such as 3D consistency across camera views. In comparing 2D inpainters and their suitability for the 3D domain, we find that reconstruction-based inpainters outperform generative diffusion models in 3D consistency. Integrating these 2D inpainters into different single-step methods for creating and finetuning 3DGS scenes, our results indicate that initializing the scene from scratch produces higher quality results than finetuning the existing scene. Using a state-of-the-art generative 2D inpainter, we create a straightforward baseline to underline the importance of object removal before inpainting in the 3D setting. Since 360° datasets rarely include real-world ground truths, and challenging occlusion scenarios are equally sparse, we introduce a novel multi-object scene with recorded ground truth data and many views with object occlusions.

</details>


### [5] [Topologically Consistent Multi-view 3D Head Reconstruction via Coarse-Guided Layered Surface Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31283)
*Timo Bolkart, Daoye Wang, Prashanth Chandran*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (52.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SHELLS 提出一种高效的层级采样前馈框架，用于从多视图图像进行拓扑一致的3D头部重建。通过DINOv2+LoRA提取多视图特征，先预测稀疏粗网格，再构建分层表面感知采样壳进行精细重建，在保持表面一致性的同时大幅降低显存消耗（88%），提升重建精度（21-29%）和推理速度（3.5倍），且仅用合成数据训练即可泛化到真实场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D头部重建方法通常通过局部特征体独立优化顶点，将内存密集的特征采样与网格分辨率耦合，限制了高密度拓扑（>10k顶点）的可扩展性，并引入表面噪声。作者希望解耦特征提取与网格分辨率，实现高效、高精度且拓扑一致的3D头部重建。

Method: SHELLS 采用层级采样策略：1）使用 DINOv2 骨干网络结合 LoRA 适配提取多视图特征；2）投影采样稀疏全局特征云，预测中间粗网格作为先验；3）基于粗网格先验构建分层的表面感知采样壳（layered surface-aware sampling shells），作为精细重建的离散搜索空间；4）最终输出拓扑一致的密集网格。

Result: 相比体积基线方法，SHELLS 推理 GPU 显存降低 88%（2.4GB vs 20GB），中位配准误差降低 21%-29%，推理速度提升 3.5 倍（0.08s vs 0.29s），支持 18k 顶点网格。模型仅用合成数据训练即可有效泛化到真实场景。

Conclusion: SHELLS 通过粗到细的层级表面采样策略，有效解耦了特征提取与网格分辨率，实现了高效、高精度、拓扑一致的3D头部重建，且消除了对昂贵预配准多视图数据集的依赖，展示了合成数据训练到真实场景泛化的可行性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出多视图3D头部重建方法，属于3D网格生成与重建范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Efficient 3D head reconstruction with strong generalization, but outside core priority subtopics and lacks autoregressive/diffusion generation focus. Signals: 3D head reconstruction, efficient inference, synthetic-to-real generalization, memory reduction, multi-view reconstruction

Abstract: We present SHELLS (Semantic Head Estimation via Layered Local Sampling), an efficient feed-forward framework for 3D head reconstruction in dense semantic correspondence from multi-view images. Existing methods typically refine vertices independently via localized feature volumes. This approach couples memory-intensive feature sampling to mesh resolution, which limits scalability for dense topologies (> 10k vertices) and introduces surface noise. In contrast, SHELLS decouples feature extraction from mesh resolution via a hierarchical sampling strategy. We extract multi-view features using a DINOv2 backbone with LoRA adaptation, projectively sample a sparse global feature cloud, and predict an intermediate coarse mesh. This coarse prior guides the construction of layered, surface-aware sampling shells that serve as a discrete search space for the final reconstruction. SHELLS maintains surface consistency while using 88% less inference GPU memory (2.4GB vs. 20GB) than volumetric baselines. It reduces median registration error by 21% to 29% with a 3.5x inference speedup (0.08s vs. 0.29s) for 18k-vertex meshes. Notably, our model is trained exclusively on synthetic data yet generalizes effectively to real-world captures, eliminating the need for the costly, pre-registered multi-view datasets common in prior work.

</details>


### [6] [VolFill: Single-View Amodal 3D Scene Reconstruction with Volumetric Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31466)
*Tuan Duc Ngo, Chuang Gan, Evangelos Kalogerakis*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 值得关注 (67.32) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VolFill 提出一种基于流匹配的生成式框架，从单张 RGB 图像重建完整 3D 场景几何（包括不可见区域）。该方法使用混合 3D VAE 压缩稀疏截断无符号距离场（T-UDF）网格到隐空间，再通过潜在扩散 Transformer 去噪恢复完整场景，并利用几何基础模型提供空间先验条件。在 SCRREAM 和 NRGB-D 数据集上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张 RGB 图像重建完整 3D 场景几何极具挑战性，尤其是推断被遮挡的隐藏结构。现有方法受限于逐射线约束或非结构化点云查询，难以有效恢复完整场景。

Method: 提出 VolFill 框架：1) 混合 3D VAE 将稀疏截断无符号距离函数（T-UDF）网格压缩到紧凑隐空间；2) 潜在扩散 Transformer（DiT）在隐空间中去噪以恢复完整场景；3) 以几何基础模型（如深度估计、法线估计）提取的空间先验作为条件，引导生成过程。

Result: 在 SCRREAM 和 NRGB-D 数据集上的实验表明，VolFill 在完整场景重建质量上显著优于现有基线方法，能够有效恢复被遮挡区域的几何结构。

Conclusion: VolFill 提供了一种结构化的场景表示，支持直接表面提取和大规模占用查询，为单视图完整 3D 场景理解奠定了坚实基础。

Classification reason: 该论文研究从单张RGB图像进行完整3D场景重建（含隐藏结构），属于3D生成与重建范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with multimodal generation and 3D reconstruction priorities, using flow matching and diffusion transformer for complete scene understanding. Signals: multimodal generation, 3D scene reconstruction, flow matching, diffusion transformer, geometry foundation model

Abstract: Reconstructing the complete geometry of a scene from a single RGB image remains challenging - especially when inferring hidden structures where visual evidence is incomplete. We introduce VolFill, a generative framework that predicts the 3D structure of the complete scene rather than relying on traditional pixel-aligned regression. Our method utilizes a hybrid 3D VAE to compress sparse truncated unsigned distance function grids into a compact latent space, paired with a latent Diffusion Transformer that denoises this representation to recover the complete scene. We condition the generation on geometry foundation models, leveraging rich spatial priors for robust reasoning. Unlike existing methods limited by per-ray constraints or unstructured point-cloud queries, VolFill provides a structured representation that supports direct surface extraction and occupancy queries at scale. Extensive experiments on the SCRREAM and NRGB-D datasets demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms current baselines, providing a robust foundation for holistic spatial understanding.

</details>

*Ulrich Prestel, Stefan Andreas Baumann, Nick Stracke, Björn Ommer*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督新视角合成（NVS）在真实视频数据上训练时面临两大挑战：训练不稳定性和多网络系统设计的不可预测缩放行为。现有方法通常将相机估计、场景重建和渲染分离为多个独立网络，导致系统复杂且难以规模化扩展。作者希望将 NVS 简化为一个统一的、可扩展的单一模型问题。

Method: RayDer 采用统一的 Transformer 架构，将相机估计、场景重建和渲染整合到单个前馈网络中。引入一个最小动态状态（minimal dynamic state）作为 nuisance 因子，吸收视频中的时变内容（如运动物体），使得模型能够在无约束的真实世界视频上稳定训练，同时保持静态场景 NVS 作为目标任务。动态内容仅作为可扩展的监督信号，而非重建目标。

Result: RayDer 在多个模型规模和数量级的数据上展现出清晰的幂律缩放行为，性能优于静态场景数据混合。在大量基准测试上，零样本开放集性能与最先进的有监督方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: RayDer 证明了将自监督 NVS 统一为单一模型缩放问题是可行且有效的，为利用海量真实世界视频数据进行可扩展的 3D 场景理解提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 RayDer，一种自监督新视角合成（NVS）方法，将相机估计、场景重建和渲染统一到单个 Transformer 中，属于 3D 场景生成与重建范畴，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 "3D 生成与重建"。

Importance reason: Strong scaling laws and unified model for novel view synthesis, but not directly in top priority subtopics. Signals: unified transformer for NVS, self-supervised from video, clean power-law scaling, zero-shot competitive with supervised, minimal dynamic state for stable training

Abstract: Self-supervised novel view synthesis (NVS) remains challenging to scale, despite the abundance of video data, largely due to the brittleness of training on realistic videos and the hard-to-predict scaling behavior of multi-network system designs. We introduce RayDer, a unified, feed-forward transformer that consolidates camera estimation, scene reconstruction, and rendering into a single backbone, turning self-supervised NVS into a well-posed single-model scaling problem. A minimal dynamic state, treated as a nuisance factor, absorbs time-varying content and enables stable training on unconstrained real-world video. Importantly, RayDer keeps static-scene NVS as its target task: dynamic content is leveraged purely as scalable supervision, not reconstructed as in dynamic-scene (4D) NVS. Across multiple model sizes and orders of magnitude in data, RayDer exhibits clean power-law scaling with data and compute, and outperforms static-scene data mixtures. On a large number of benchmarks, RayDer achieves strong zero-shot open-set performance competitive with state-of-the-art supervised approaches. Project Page:

*Mungyeom Kim, Minkyeong Jeon, Honggyu An, Jaewoo Jung, Hyuna Ko, Jisang Han, Hyeonseo Yu, Donghwan Shin, Sunghwan Hong, Takuya Narihira, Kazumi Fukuda, Yuki Mitsufuji, Seungryong Kim*

TL;DR: C4G 是一种前馈式 4D 动态场景重建框架，使用紧凑的、时间戳条件化的可学习高斯查询令牌，在全时间上下文中聚合特征并解码 3D 高斯，实现全局一致的运动建模，无需逐场景优化和相机位姿，并引入视频扩散模型增强渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈式动态场景重建方法逐帧逐像素预测 3D 高斯，导致高斯重复和视角依赖偏差，难以有效学习场景运动。需要一种无需逐场景优化、能全局建模运动的前馈式 4D 重建方法。

Method: 提出 C4G 框架：1）使用一组紧凑的、时间戳条件化的可学习高斯查询令牌，每个令牌在全时间上下文中聚合对应特征；2）根据目标时间戳调制解码出的 3D 高斯位置，实现全局一致的运动建模；3）引入视频扩散模型作为渲染增强模块以捕捉细粒度细节；4）将特征提升扩展到 4D 特征场，支持点跟踪和动态场景理解。

Result: C4G 在使用显著更少高斯数且无需相机位姿的条件下，实现了强竞争力的新视角合成性能，展现出更强的运动建模能力和对长时间间隔的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: C4G 通过紧凑高斯查询令牌和全局时间上下文聚合，有效解决了前馈式 4D 重建中的运动建模难题，为动态场景重建提供了一种高效、无需相机位姿的新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 C4G，一种前馈式 4D 重建框架，基于紧凑的可学习高斯查询令牌实现动态场景重建和新视角合成，属于 3D 生成与重建（特别是动态 3D/4D 场景重建）方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"。

Importance reason: Advances feed-forward 4D reconstruction with compact Gaussians and global motion modeling, relevant to multimodal generation and video understanding. Signals: feed-forward 4D reconstruction, compact Gaussian query tokens, global motion modeling, video diffusion enhancement, no camera pose needed

Abstract: Dynamic scene reconstruction from monocular video remains a fundamental challenge in computer vision. Existing feed-forward methods predict 3D Gaussians pixel-wise for each frame, suffering from duplicated Gaussians and view-dependent biases that hinder effective learning of scene motion. We present C4G, a feed-forward 4D reconstruction framework built upon a compact set of timestamp-conditioned learnable Gaussian query tokens. Each token aggregates corresponding features across the full temporal context and decodes a 3D Gaussian whose position is modulated by the target timestamp, enabling globally coherent motion modeling without per-scene optimization. To capture fine-grained details, we further introduce a video diffusion model-based rendering enhancement module. Since our framework effectively aggregates features into Gaussians, we extend this capability to feature lifting, producing a 4D feature field that supports point tracking and dynamic scene understanding. C4G achieves strong novel-view synthesis performance using significantly fewer Gaussians and without requiring camera poses, while exhibiting stronger motion modeling and robustness to large temporal gaps.

</details>


### [9] [Smaller and Faster 3DGS via Post-Training Dictionary Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30396)
*Jiarong Gong, Jonas Unger, Ehsan Miandji*

Main category: cs.GR, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于字典学习的 3DGS 后训练压缩框架，无需重新训练即可显著压缩模型体积（平均 3.95x），同时提升渲染速度（~24%）并保持图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS 模型训练后通常占用大量内存，限制了其在低算力设备上的部署。现有压缩方法往往引入额外可训练参数，虽能实现高压缩比但会带来明显的图像质量下降。

Method: 提出基于字典学习的后训练压缩框架，将 3DGS 的高斯属性（如协方差、颜色等）通过字典学习进行稀疏编码，实现模型压缩。该方法无需修改现有 3DGS 架构或重新训练，可直接应用于任意 3DGS 模型。

Result: 在 13 个基准场景上，对 3DGS、3DGS-MCMC 和 PixelGS 分别实现平均 3.95x、3.10x 和 4.55x 的压缩比，同时渲染速度提升 23.3%、24.3% 和 25.3%，且图像质量保持稳定。

Conclusion: 字典学习是一种有效的 3DGS 后训练压缩手段，能在压缩、速度和质量之间取得良好平衡，且具有即插即用的通用性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) 的压缩方法，属于 3D 场景表示与神经渲染范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel post-training compression for 3DGS boosts efficiency and speed, aligning with model efficiency priority. Signals: 3DGS compression, dictionary learning, post-training, rendering speedup, model efficiency

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is a promising neural scene representation for real-time rendering, but trained models often suffer from large memory footprints, limiting deployment on less powerful devices. Existing compression techniques often lead to architectures with several additional trainable parameters. While achieving outstanding compression ratios, they introduce noticeable drops in image quality. In this work, we introduce the first dictionary-learning-based compression framework for 3DGS. The proposed post-training compression pipeline can be deployed in virtually any 3DGS model without the need for re-training or modifications to existing 3DGS models. Our compression framework is straightforward to implement, yet provides significant compression capabilities, preserves image quality, and improves real-time rendering performance. Across 13 benchmark scenes, our approach achieves an average compression ratio of 3.95x, 3.10x, and 4.55x when applied to 3DGS, 3DGS-MCMC, and PixelGS, respectively. This yields consistent rendering speedups of 23.3%, 24.3%, and 25.3%, while maintaining image quality.

</details>

*Youngtae Han, Sung-hwan Han, Youngmin Yi*

Main category: cs.GR, cs.CV

Subtopic: 4D 与动态场景生成

TL;DR: DSD-GS 提出一种高效的动态-静态分解高斯泼溅框架，利用前馈高斯编码器和光流模型将场景分为静态和动态部分，消除静态区域的冗余计算，在 Neural 3D 数据集上仅需10分钟训练即可达到700+ FPS的渲染速度，无需 COLMAP 预处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态 3DGS 方法将所有高斯体素建模为动态组件，导致计算效率低下；而现有的分解方法仍存在重建质量下降和训练时间过长的问题。本文旨在通过高效的静态-动态分解策略来兼顾重建质量、训练/渲染速度与存储效率。

Method: 提出 DSD-GS 框架，核心是静态-动态分解策略：使用前馈高斯泼溅编码器（Feed-Forward Gaussian Splatting encoder）和光流模型，自动将场景划分为静态区域和动态区域，仅对动态部分进行建模和计算，静态区域复用全局静态高斯体素，从而消除冗余计算。该方法无需 COLMAP 预处理，支持确定性初始化。

Result: 在 Neural 3D 数据集上，训练仅需10分钟，渲染速度超过700 FPS（1352×1014分辨率，单张 RTX 5090 GPU），在渲染质量、训练/渲染速度和存储效率上均超越现有基线方法。

Conclusion: DSD-GS 通过高效的静态-动态分解策略，在动态场景重建中实现了质量、速度和存储效率的全面提升，为动态 3DGS 方法提供了新的高效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于动态-静态分解的高斯泼溅方法用于动态场景重建与新视角合成，属于动态3D场景生成与重建方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "4D 与动态场景生成"（dynamic_4d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong efficiency gains in dynamic scene reconstruction with static-dynamic decomposition, aligning with model efficiency and video generation priorities. Signals: dynamic scene reconstruction, Gaussian splatting, static-dynamic decomposition, 700+ FPS, 10-minute training

Abstract: Dynamic scene reconstruction and novel view synthesis are fundamental to next-generation visual intelligence applications such as virtual reality, robotics, and digital twins. However, high-fidelity reconstruction of complex, time-varying scenes from arbitrary viewpoints remains a significant challenge. Existing dynamic 3DGS methods suffer from computational inefficiency, since they model all Gaussians as dynamic components. While recent decomposition-based approaches address this issue, they still struggle with degraded reconstruction quality and prolonged training time. To mitigate these limitations, we propose a novel dynamic reconstruction framework built upon an efficient static-dynamic decomposition strategy using a Feed-Forward Gaussian Splatting encoder and an optical flow model. By eliminating redundant computations on static regions, our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming existing baselines across rendering quality, training and rendering speed, and storage efficiency. Notably, on the Neural 3D dataset, our framework requires only 10 minutes for training and achieves a rendering speed of over 700 FPS on a single NVIDIA RTX 5090 GPU at resolution of 1352x1014. Furthermore, our decomposition strategy eliminates the need for COLMAP preprocessing and enables deterministic initialization, thereby enhancing both efficiency and reproducibility.

</details>

*Yuyang Zhao, Yicheng Pan, Qiyuan He, Jincheng Yu, Junsong Chen, Tian Ye, Haozhe Liu, Enze Xie, Song Han*

TL;DR: SANA-Streaming 是一个系统-算法协同设计的实时流式视频编辑框架，通过混合扩散Transformer架构、循环反向正则化训练策略和面向NVIDIA Blackwell架构的高效系统协同设计（融合GDN核与混合精度量化），在单张RTX 5090上实现了1280×704分辨率、24 FPS端到端的实时视频编辑，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时流式视频到视频编辑（V2V）在直播、游戏等交互式应用中至关重要，但现有方法受限于时序一致性和推理吞吐量的严格要求，难以在消费级GPU上实现高分辨率实时编辑。

Method: (1) 混合扩散Transformer架构：在部分块中引入softmax注意力以增强局部建模能力，同时保留线性层的效率；(2) 循环反向正则化（Cycle-Reverse Regularization）：通过流匹配从生成内容预测源帧，强制语义一致性，无需成对长编辑视频；(3) 高效系统协同设计：融合GDN核与面向NVIDIA Blackwell（RTX 5090）优化的混合精度量化（MPQ），最大化Tensor Core利用率。

Result: 在单张RTX 5090上实现1280×704分辨率、24 FPS端到端实时编辑，DiT核心达58 FPS。在时序一致性和系统吞吐量上显著优于现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 系统-算法协同设计是实现消费级GPU上实时高分辨率视频编辑的有效途径，混合注意力架构和循环反向正则化为时序一致性提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究实时流式视频到视频编辑（V2V），属于多模态生成方向下的图像与视频编辑（visual_editing）这一[CANONICAL]子主题，同时涉及生成式 AI方向下的视频音频生成应用（video_audio_creation）。

Importance reason: Strong system-algorithm co-design for real-time video editing aligns with user's high-weight video generation and efficient inference priorities. Signals: real-time video editing, hybrid diffusion transformer, efficient system co-design, NVIDIA Blackwell optimization, high-resolution 24 FPS

Abstract: Real-time streaming video-to-video editing (V2V) is critical for interactive applications such as live broadcasting and gaming, yet it remains a formidable challenge due to the stringent requirements for temporal consistency and inference throughput. In this paper, we present SANA-Streaming, a system-algorithm co-designed framework for high-resolution, real-time streaming video editing on consumer GPUs, with the following three core designs: (1) Hybrid Diffusion Transformer architecture introduces softmax attention in part of the blocks to improve local modeling capabilities while preserving the efficiency of linear layers. (2) Cycle-Reverse Regularization is a novel training strategy that enforces semantic consistency by predicting source frames from generated content via flow matching, improving temporal consistency without requiring paired long edited videos. (3) Efficient System Co-design combines fused GDN kernels and Mixed-Precision Quantization (MPQ) optimized for the NVIDIA Blackwell (RTX 5090) architecture. By profiling real-world throughput, our MPQ maximizes Tensor Core utilization while maintaining generation quality. The resulting system achieves real-time 1280 x 704 resolution editing at 24 end-to-end FPS on a single RTX 5090 GPU, with the DiT core running at 58 FPS. Experimental results demonstrate that our co-design approach significantly outperforms existing SOTA methods in both temporal coherence and system throughput.

</details>


### [12] [DTG-Restore: Training-Free Diffusion Refinement for Generative Video Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30431)
*Hidir Yesiltepe, Koutilya PNVR, Gaurav Pathak, Navaneeth Bodla, Bharat Singh, Pinar Yanardag, Jinrong Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 高优先级 (88.71) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 DTG-Restore，一种无需训练的视频超分辨率增强框架，通过解耦分类器自由引导中的条件与无条件分支的时间步，利用更干净时间步的无条件分支提供前瞻先验，逐步从结构校正过渡到细节增强，可即插即用地与任意现成修复模块结合，提升生成视频和真实视频的结构保真度与时间稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型虽能生成高保真内容，但在修复低分辨率/失真视频时，标准分类器自由引导（CFG）中条件分支与无条件分支的强耦合限制了先验的有效利用，导致扭曲内容被复制而非修复。

Method: 提出 Decoupled Time Guidance (DTG)，在采样过程中将无条件分支置于更干净的扩散时间步进行评估，获得前瞻先验以保持几何结构并抑制扭曲内容复制；该时间偏差在采样过程中逐步退火，使模型从结构校正平滑过渡到细节增强。框架即插即用，可与任意现成修复模块结合。同时构建了 GenWarp480 基准数据集，包含 4400 个由多种文生视频模型生成的 480p 失真视频。

Result: 在多个基准上实验表明，该方法在结构保真度和时间稳定性上取得显著提升，无需任何模型训练即可超越现有方法。

Conclusion: DTG-Restore 通过解耦 CFG 时间步提供了一种简单有效的训练-free 视频修复增强方案，适用于 AI 生成和真实视频，具有良好的即插即用性和泛化能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一种无需训练的视频超分辨率增强框架，属于视频后期质量提升与修复任务，与 canonical subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）中的视频编辑/修复方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Training-free video refinement aligns with efficient inference and video generation priorities, but lacks direct focus on autoregressive or world model subtopics. Signals: training-free, video super-resolution, diffusion refinement, classifier-free guidance decoupling, generative video enhancement

Abstract: Recent progress in video diffusion models has enabled remarkable generative fidelity, yet leveraging these priors for restoration remains limited by the strong coupling between conditional and unconditional branches in standard classifier-free guidance. We introduce a training-free framework that enhances distorted and low-resolution videos by decoupling these signals in time. Our proposed Decoupled Time Guidance (DTG) evaluates the unconditional branch at a cleaner diffusion timestep, providing a lookahead prior that preserves geometry while suppressing replication of warped content. This temporal bias is annealed throughout sampling, allowing the model to transition from structure correction to detail refinement without retraining. Combined with any off-the-shelf restoration module in a plug-and-play manner, our approach improves perceptual coherence and restores plausible structure in AIgenerated and real-world videos alike. To facilitate evaluation, we curate GenWarp480, a benchmark of 4,400 distorted 480p videos synthesized from diverse text-to-video models. GenWarp480 focuses on characteristic generative degradations such as warped faces, body misalignments, and spatial artifacts, providing a purpose-built testbed for assessing robustness to generative errors. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves significant improvements in structural fidelity and temporal stability without any model training.

Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) image editors, such as Nano Banana, produce visually compelling results for retouching tasks, enabling non-experts to edit images through text prompts alone. However, the generative nature of these models often introduces spatial misalignment, texture distortion, and content hallucination, all of which are detrimental to downstream workflows that require pixel-level fidelity. We identify a problem setting we call "structure-preserving GenAI fusion" for black-box GenAI image retouching: retain the perceptual enhancements of a GenAI output while enforcing structural faithfulness to the original input image. To address this problem, we propose a post-processing framework that fuses an input image with its GenAI-enhanced counterpart by first establishing coarse spatial and photometric correspondences, then performing a fusion stage that transfers desired enhancements while suppressing hallucinated content. In the absence of direct prior work in this setting, we evaluate our framework against representative methods from photorealistic style transfer and image fusion. Our experiments demonstrate that our method better preserves aesthetic quality while maintaining pixel-level structural consistency and the input resolution.

Abstract: Unconditional diffusion models offer powerful generative priors, yet steering them toward aesthetically enhanced outputs remains largely unexplored. We show that h-space patching, the dominant paradigm for training-free diffusion editing, systematically fails for global, low-level transformations required for aesthetic and perceptual refinement. We introduce a novel, generalized framework for image-editing in unconditional diffusion models without explicit training. This inference-time mechanism operates on low-level features by extracting degradation concept vectors and combining bottleneck patching with classifier-free guidance to guide sampling away from the degraded manifold, producing consistently improved images without any model retraining.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Importance reason: Novel dual-stream diffusion for bimanual multi-object HOI, but outside user's priority subtopics. Signals: dual-stream diffusion, bidirectional cross-attention, motion fusion network, hand-relative object representation, real-time long sequence generation

Abstract: Recent advances in 4D Human-Object Interaction (HOI) generation have enabled increasingly realistic motion synthesis, particularly for single-object manipulation. Yet current research overlooks an inherent property of human behavior: people naturally coordinate both hands and manipulate multiple objects simultaneously. To address this gap, we present Dex2HOI, a unified diffusion model for single- and two-object HOI synthesis from text. At its core, Dex2HOI employs a Dual-Stream Diffusion approach, where each object is processed in a dedicated interaction stream and coordinated through bidirectional cross-attention. To synthesize the final motion, we introduce a Motion Fusion Network integrated with novel hand-relative object representations and contact-aware conditioning applied across the whole sequence. By sampling the diffusion process autoregressively over prefix-conditioned windows, Dex2HOI generates arbitrarily long sequences at real-time speed omitting redundant test-time optimization, achieving up to x540 inference speed-up over prior state-of-the-art methods. Extensive evaluation on both single- and two-object benchmarks demonstrates state-of-the-art quantitative results, marking a step beyond conventional single-object HOI generation and toward expressive multi-object manipulation. Code and models will be released upon acceptance.

</details>

Result: 大量定量和定性实验表明，MultiAct 在复合提示上持续优于现有基线方法，在保持动作真实感的同时实现了更好的语义覆盖。

Conclusion: MultiAct 提供了一种无需配对数据、无需重新训练的有效方案，能够显著提升预训练动作生成模型对复合文本描述的语义覆盖能力，为文本到动作生成的实际应用提供了重要改进。

Classification reason: 该论文研究文本到人体动作生成（text-to-motion），属于多模态生成方向下的数字人与动作生成（avatar_motion_generation）这一[CANONICAL]子主题，该子主题明确包含"人体动作"（human motion）关键词。

Importance reason: Focuses on text-to-motion generation for digital humans, which is a niche subtopic not aligned with user's priority subtopics like video/image generation or world models. Signals: inference-time framework, attention guidance, composite text, motion generation, no retraining

Abstract: Text-to-motion generation has progressed rapidly in recent years, offering an expressive interface for animation and human-computer interaction. However, current models remain brittle when handling prompts that describe multiple actions occurring at the same time. Rather than realizing all components of a composite description, models frequently prioritize a single dominant action and neglect the rest, leading to incomplete or ambiguous motion. We present MultiAct, an unpaired, inference-time framework for compositional text-to-motion synthesis that operates directly on pretrained motion generators without retraining or architectural modification. Our method counteracts semantic collapse by adaptively amplifying cross-attention scores associated with underrepresented prompt components. We note that effective modulation depends on prompt-specific choices, such as which tokens and layers to target, and introduce a lightweight auxiliary decision scheme that determines the most effective attention-strengthening parametrization. Extensive quantitative and qualitative evaluations demonstrate that MultiAct consistently outperforms existing baselines on composite prompts, achieving improved semantic coverage while preserving motion realism. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出 OmniME 框架，包含三个互补组件：(1) 回顾性特征监督（Retrospective Feature Supervision），在 Transformer 层间施加从粗到细的一致性约束；(2) 动作保持机制（Motion Preservation Mechanism），根据源-目标相似度关注细微变化以保持未编辑部分；(3) 三元组语义对齐（Triplet-based Semantic Alignment），增强文本-动作对应关系。三者形成统一的正负学习监督范式。

Result: 在 MotionFix 和 STANCE Adjustment 数据集上的大量实验表明，OmniME 在编辑对齐方面达到了最先进的性能，验证了统一学习框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文通过正负学习范式有效平衡了动作编辑中的变化与不变性，为文本驱动的人体动作编辑提供了新的统一框架，代码和模型已开源。

Importance reason: Focuses on motion editing, not aligned with priority subtopics like video/image generation or world models. Signals: motion editing, diffusion-based, human motion, text-based editing, positive-negative learning

Abstract: Text-based human motion editing aims to modify existing motion sequences according to natural language instructions while maintaining the consistency of the original motion. Existing diffusion-based approaches often rely on heuristic similarity cues or coarse global conditioning, leading to motion distortion and suboptimal semantic alignment. The key challenge lies in balancing change (i.e. precisely editing target regions) and invariance (i.e. preserving unedited parts). To handle such challenge, we propose an Omni-Supervised Positive-Negative Learning framework, named OmniME. Our method integrates three complementary components: (1) retrospective feature supervision that enforces coarse-to-fine consistency across transformer layers,(2) motion preservation mechanism that focuses on subtle variations according to the source-target similarity, and (3) triplet-based semantic alignment that strengthens text-motion correspondence. Together, these components form a unified supervision paradigm that balances change and invariance. Extensive experiments on the MotionFix and STANCE Adjustment datasets demonstrate that OmniME achieves state-of-the-art performance in editing alignment, validating the effectiveness of our unified learning framework. Our source codes and models have been released at:

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双手动作分割对于理解复杂的双手协作活动至关重要，但面临手部间复杂依赖、视觉不对称、表示冲突（主导手垄断梯度）以及细粒度动作语义模糊等独特挑战，现有方法难以有效解决这些问题。

Method: Polyphony 包含三个阶段：(1) 交替双手视觉Transformer（Alternating Dual-Hand Vision Transformer），通过交替训练左右手小批量确保梯度贡献平衡，同时共享时空编码器；(2) 语义特征条件化（Semantic Feature Conditioning），将视觉特征与结构化的组合式动作描述对齐以增强语义相似动作的区分度；(3) 基于扩散的分割（Diffusion-Based Segmentation），结合跨手特征融合实现手部间协调，并采用自适应损失加权平衡性能。

Result: 在 HA-ViD 和 ATTACH 两个双手数据集上取得最优结果，提升高达 16.8 个点；在单流 Breakfast 数据集上达到 82.5%，超越使用 12 倍大骨干网络的先前最佳方法。统一单骨干模型超越了需要独立每手模型的基线方法。

Conclusion: Polyphony 通过交替训练策略、语义条件化和扩散分割有效解决了双手动作分割中的关键挑战，证明了统一单骨干模型在双手动作理解中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究双手动作分割（dual-hand action segmentation），属于人体动作理解与生成范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题"数字人与动作生成"（avatar_motion_generation）高度相关，该子主题涵盖人体动作、手势等。

Abstract: Dual-hand action segmentation, densely predicting actions for both hands from untrimmed videos, is essential for understanding complex bimanual activities. However, it poses several unique challenges: complex inter-hand dependencies, visual asymmetry between hands, representation conflicts where the dominant hand monopolizes gradients, and semantic ambiguity in fine-grained actions. We propose Polyphony, a three-stage method to address these challenges through: (1) an Alternating Dual-Hand Vision Transformer that alternates training between left- and right-hand mini-batches to ensure balanced gradient contributions from both hands while sharing a spatio-temporal encoder; (2) Semantic Feature Conditioning that aligns visual features with structured, compositional action descriptions to enhance discrimination of semantically similar actions; and (3) Diffusion-Based Segmentation with cross-hand feature fusion for inter-hand coordination and adaptive loss weighting for balancing performance. Polyphony achieves state-of-the-art on both dual-hand datasets (HA-ViD, ATTACH) with improvements up to 16.8 points, and on the single-stream Breakfast dataset (82.5%), outperforming the prior best method that uses a 12x larger backbone. Notably, our unified model with a single shared backbone surpasses baselines requiring separate per-hand models. Code is at .

*Beichen Shao, Mengying Xie, Heng Su, Wanyi Zhang, Mingyan Li, Yan Ding, Fausto Giunchiglia, Chao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI

TL;DR: GSAM 是一个面向铰接物体操作的通用且安全的机器人框架，通过视觉感知器生成运动学参数，结合 VLM 的常识推理进行精炼，并利用 LLM 将交互约束函数化以实现安全的轨迹与姿态规划，在 50 个铰链任务上相比基线提升了 36% 的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有铰接物体操作方法（端到端策略学习、视觉-运动规划、LLM/VLM）忽视了铰接物体的多样性和末端执行器与手柄交互的复杂性，导致泛化能力有限且容易发生破坏性碰撞。

Method: 提出 GSAM 框架：(1) 基于视觉的感知器生成运动学参数；(2) 微调 VLM 结合链式思维常识推理来精炼感知结果；(3) 交互约束函数生成器整合铰接物体、交互姿态和避障知识；(4) LLM 将这些约束函数化并应用于轨迹和姿态规划；(5) 运动学感知的操作规划器验证轨迹和姿态的可达性。

Result: 在 5 类物体共 50 个铰链任务和 50 个随机初始化的末端执行器-手柄配置上，GSAM 相比最佳基线将标准差降低 3.1%，操作成功率提升 36.0%，展示了更好的物体泛化能力和交互安全性。

Conclusion: GSAM 通过结合视觉感知、VLM 常识推理和 LLM 约束函数化，有效提升了铰接物体操作的泛化性和安全性，为服务机器人在复杂场景中的应用提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究铰接物体操作中的机器人动作规划与交互，涉及末端执行器与手柄的交互姿态规划、运动学感知的轨迹规划，属于机器人动作生成范畴。虽然论文本身是机器人操作，但其核心是"动作生成与规划"，与现有 canonical subtopic "数字人与动作生成"（avatar_motion_generation）中的人体动作生成在"动作规划与生成"这一主题上有合理关联。考虑到该 subtopic 也涵盖 human motion 和动作生成，且没有更合适的机器人相关 subtopic，故复用此 canonical。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotic manipulation, not aligned with user's priority subtopics in multimodal generation and video/image generation. Signals: Robotics, articulated object manipulation, VLM/LLM for safety, not in priority subtopics, negative keywords absent but direction mismatch

Abstract: Articulated object manipulation is a unique challenge for service robots. Existing methods employ end-to-end policy learning, visionmotion planning, and large-language/visual-language model (LLM/VLM), but often overlook the diversity of articulated objects and the complexity of interactions between end-effector and handle, leading to limited generalization and destructive collisions. To address this, we propose GSAM, a generalizable and safe robotic framework for articulated object manipulation. Specifically, a vision-based perceiver generates the kinematic parameters. Considering that pre-trained markers in perceiver yield raw estimations that may deviate from commonsense, we present a f ine-tuned VLM-based refiner, using chain-of-thought (COT) commonsense reasoning to refine perception. To prevent destructive collisions, we design an interaction constraint function generator, integrating articulated object, interaction pose, and obstacle avoidance knowledge into a base. LLM then functionalize these constraints and apply them to trajectory and posture planning. A kinematic-aware manipulation planner verifies reachability for trajectory and posture. Experiments on 50 hinge tasks across 5 object categories and 50 randomly initialized end-effectorhandle configurations show that GSAM reduces standard deviation by 3.1% and improves manipulation success rate by 36.0% compared to the best baseline, respectively demonstrating the superior object generalization and interaction safety of GSAM in practical scenarios.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话式虚拟人系统通常采用串行流水线（语音识别→文本生成→语音合成→音频驱动面部动画），导致表情僵硬、对话流程不自然。作者发现 Audio-LLM 产生的音频 token 已包含足够信息来重建合理的面部表现，因此希望直接利用音频 token 端到端生成面部动画，避免多阶段级联带来的延迟和表现力损失。

Result: 消融实验验证了分块处理在延迟与质量之间的权衡效果；与现有方法相比，TokTalk 在延迟上相当，在质量、表现力和 3D 面部控制方面通过感知研究显著优于先前方案；展示了在聊天机器人 Avatar、语音驱动用户 Avatar 和动画导演界面等多种音视频应用中的灵活性。

Conclusion: TokTalk 证明了直接从 Audio-LLM 的音频 token 生成富有表现力的实时 3D 面部动画是可行且高效的，为构建更自然、更具表现力的对话式虚拟人提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文从音频LLM的音频token直接实时生成3D面部动画（说话头/虚拟人面部表情），属于多模态生成方向下的[CANONICAL]数字人与动作生成（avatar_motion_generation）子主题，同时与生成式 AI方向下的视频音频生成应用（video_audio_creation）也相关。

Importance reason: Novel use of Audio-LLM tokens for real-time facial animation, but peripheral to core video/image generation and world model priorities. Signals: Audio-LLM tokens, real-time facial animation, conditional flow matching, avatar generation, multimodal generation

Abstract: Recent advances in Audio-LLMs like GPT-4o have ushered in an era of conversational interaction with language models. Conversational avatars however, still seem robotic in facial expression and conversational flow, in part due to sequential stages of speech recognition, text generation, turn-based text response, speech synthesis, and audio driven facial animation. Based on our insight that audio-tokens produced by current Audio-LLMs carry sufficient information to reconstruct a plausible facial performance, we present TokTalk, a system that directly outputs expressive facial animation in real-time from streaming audio-tokens. We construct a novel audio-token to 3D facial motion dataset, on which TokTalk is trained using a Chunk-based Conditional Flow Matching model. A lightweight adaptation strategy allows our trained model to seamlessly connect to any token-based Audio-LLM at minimal computational overhead. Our chunk-based processing further enables parametric trade-off between latency and facial quality, shown through ablation studies. We further show that the real-time performance of TokTalk is comparable in latency to prior art solutions, and significantly favorable (via a perceptual study) in terms of quality, expressivity and control of the 3D facial performance. We showcase TokTalk's flexibility using a chatbot Avatar, a voice-driven user Avatar, and an animation Director's interface, as diverse audio-visual face applications.

Abstract: We present our approach to the FIFA Skeletal Tracking Challenge 2026, which requires estimating 3D world-space poses of soccer players from broadcast video. Our method finetunes SMPLest-X (ViT-H, 687 M parameters) via a stratified clip split, multi-task depth supervision, and broadcast augmentation, paired with a RAFT dense optical flow camera tracker, foot-plane anchoring, and two-pass temporal smoothing. Against the FIFA baseline score of 1.053 on the validation set, SMART achieves 0.647, a 38.6% improvement; on the held-out test set, SMART scores 0.593 (Global MPJPE: 0.324 m, Local MPJPE: 0.054 m).

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有布局到图像生成方法在少样本非典型场景下生成结果碎片化、失真严重，根本原因在于语义身份与视觉细节纠缠导致的表征粒度不匹配（representation fragmentation）。

Method: 提出三组件框架：(1) Semantic Anchoring：将类别语义聚合为锚点以保持稳定身份；(2) Primitive Imbuing：建模可重组基元以实现鲁棒的局部细节建模；(3) Conceptual Steering：利用显著性感知目标函数约束优化，保持前景语义一致性。

Result: 在多个非典型领域的5-shot设置下，该方法在视觉保真度和布局对齐方面一致优于现有最先进的L2I方法。

Conclusion: 通过解耦语义与基元的表征驱动方法，有效解决了少样本布局到图像生成中的表征碎片化问题，为少样本可控图像生成提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究布局到图像生成（Layout-to-Image），属于文本/条件到图像生成的范畴，与 canonical 子主题"文生图与图像扩散"（text_to_image_diffusion）紧密相关。布局条件生成是文生图的一种细粒度控制形式，因此复用该 canonical 子主题。

Signals: Few-shot generation, layout-to-image, representation fragmentation, semantic anchoring, not autoregressive or diffusion video

Abstract: The layout-to-image (L2I) task enables fine-grained control over image generation via object categories and spatial layouts. However, existing L2I methods yield fragmented and distorted generations under few-shot atypical settings. We term this failure as representation fragmentation, arising from a granularity mismatch that entangles semantic identity with visual details. To address this issue, we propose a representation-driven framework that disentangles semantics from primitives for robust few-shot adaptation. Specifically, Semantic Anchoring aggregates categorical semantics into anchors for stable identity, while Primitive Imbuing models recomposable primitives for robust local detail modeling. Conceptual Steering further regulates optimization with a saliency-aware objective to preserve foreground semantic consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent improvements in the 5-shot regime over state-of-the-art L2I methods in both visual fidelity and alignment across diverse atypical domains. The source code is publicly available at .

</details>

*Haoyu Zhao, Jiaxi Gu, Haoran Chen, Qingping Zheng, Yeying Jin, Hongyi Yang, Junqi Cheng, Yuang Zhang, Zenghui Lu, Huan Yu, Jie Jiang, Peng Shu, Zuxuan Wu, Yu-Gang Jiang*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型中的相机控制方法直接将数值相机参数注入扩散骨干网络，难以弥合抽象坐标与视觉内容之间的鸿沟，导致结构失真。需要一种能解耦运动与场景外观、同时忠实保持轨迹动态的相机控制方法。

Method: 提出 CameraNoise 框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 几何引导的重投影流 (Geometry-guided Reprojection Flow)，用于在相机变换下建立帧间像素对应关系；(2) 噪声扭曲算法 (Noise Warping Algorithm)，在保持扩散高斯先验的同时，确保噪声在相机变换下的一致性传播。相机运动被直接编码到噪声空间中，实现运动与场景外观的解耦。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在视觉质量和轨迹忠实度上均显著优于现有方法，生成的视频具有稳定的几何一致性和高保真度。

Conclusion: CameraNoise 通过将相机姿态嵌入噪声空间，有效解决了视频扩散中相机控制的结构失真问题，为精确可控的视频生成提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CameraNoise，通过几何流引导的噪声扭曲方法实现视频扩散模型中的精确相机控制，属于视频扩散模型中的可控生成方法，与 [CANONICAL] 文生视频与视频扩散 子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances controllable video diffusion with a novel geometry-guided noise warping method, highly relevant to your priority on diffusion video generation and world models. Signals: video diffusion, camera control, noise warping, geometric consistency, controllable generation

Abstract: Precise camera pose control is critical for video diffusion, yet maintaining geometric consistency remains a challenge. Existing methods that directly inject numerical camera parameters into the diffusion backbone often fail to bridge the gap between abstract coordinates and visual content, leading to structural distortions. To address this issue, we propose CameraNoise, a flow-to-noise warping method that encodes camera motion into a temporally coherent stochastic representation. Unlike conventional conditioning, CameraNoise embeds camera poses directly into the noise space. This decouples motion from scene appearance while faithfully preserving trajectory dynamics. Specifically, we introduce a novel Geometry-guided Reprojection Flow and a noise warping algorithm, which jointly preserve the Gaussian prior of diffusion and ensure consistent noise propagation under camera transformations. By integrating CameraNoise into the diffusion process, our framework delivers stable, high-fidelity videos. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms prior methods in both visual quality and trajectory faithfulness. The project page and code are available at: .

</details>

Conclusion: 结构化语义表示（而非原始时间容量）是实现持久长视频合成的关键原语。SlotMemory通过物体级记忆有效缓解了长视频生成中的身份漂移和语义不一致问题。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向流式长视频生成的物体级KV记忆机制SlotMemory，基于视频扩散模型（Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B骨干），属于文本到视频生成与视频扩散范畴，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "文生图与视频扩散"（text_to_video_diffusion）。

Importance reason: Directly addresses persistent long-video generation with object-centric memory, a key challenge in video diffusion and world models. Signals: video diffusion, long-video generation, object-centric memory, streaming generation, dynamic consistency

Abstract: Streaming video generation models typically rely on temporal-centric memory, which organizes historical context as raw frames, chunk segments, or unclustered tokens. This organization frequently leads to identity drift and semantic inconsistency when entities exit the frame or during interactive prompt transitions. To address these limitations, we propose SlotMemory, an object-centric Key-Value memory mechanism for streaming video diffusion. Our approach shifts the memory abstraction from "when" an event occurred to "what" is being represented by decomposing the transformer's key-value manifold into discrete, reusable semantic slots. By utilizing these slots as routing addresses to index and store high-fidelity key-value tokens, we enable entity-level persistence and prompt-aware retrieval across long horizons. Evaluated on 60-second interactive narratives using the Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B backbone, SlotMemory achieves a state-of-the-art quality score of 81.61 and a 22.8 percent relative improvement in dynamic consistency over the strongest existing streaming baseline. Our results demonstrate that structured semantic representation, rather than raw temporal capacity, is the essential primitive for persistent long-form video synthesis. Our codes and checkpoints are available at .

</details>


### [25] [TunerDiT: Training-free Progressive Steering of Diffusion Transformer for Multi-Event Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31590)
*Ruotong Liao, Guowen Huang, Qing Cheng, Guangyao Zhai, Lei Zhang, Xun Xiao, Thomas Seidl, Daniel Cremers, Volker Tresp*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (95.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TunerDiT 是一种无需额外训练的视频扩散 Transformer 渐进式引导方法，通过事件分区掩码和跨事件提示融合实现多事件视频生成，在 8 项指标上达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生视频方法在生成长时域、包含多个事件的视频时面临挑战，难以在保持视频整体一致性的同时清晰区分不同事件。

Method: TunerDiT 包含两个引导手柄：(1) 事件分区掩码（Event-Partitioned Masking），强制事件边界同时允许跨事件过渡带；(2) 跨事件提示融合（Cross-Event Prompt Fusion），在去噪后期注入相邻事件语义进行细化。该方法基于对 DiT 去噪轨迹中文本条件从全局布局到细粒度细节影响的内在转折点的发现。

Result: 在自建 Meve 基准上，TunerDiT 在 8 项指标上达到最优，文本对齐度随事件数量增加而提升，展示了随事件数扩展的潜力。

Conclusion: TunerDiT 提供了一种无需训练的多事件视频生成方案，可在视频一致性和事件分离之间进行可调权衡，且随事件数量增加表现更好。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 TunerDiT，一种无需训练的多事件视频生成渐进式引导方法，基于视频扩散 Transformer（DiT）进行文本到视频生成。属于 [CANONICAL] 文生视频与视频扩散 范畴。

Importance reason: Directly advances diffusion video generation with a training-free multi-event method, matching top priority subtopics. Signals: diffusion transformer, video generation, training-free, multi-event, state-of-the-art

Abstract: Text-to-video (T2V) generation faces challenging questions when generating videos with long horizons containing multiple events. Inspired by the intrinsics of the diffusion process, we probe video diffusion transformers (DiTs) and uncover intrinsic turning points in the DiT denoising trajectory where conditioning text affects generation from global layout to fine-grained details. Building on this finding, we present TunerDiT, a simple yet effective progressive steering method that requires no additional training for multi-event generation. TunerDiT comprises two steering handles: (1) Event-Partitioned Masking that enforces event boundaries while allowing cross-event transition bands; (2) Cross-Event Prompt Fusion that injects neighboring event semantics for late-stage refinement. We contribute a self-curated prompt suite for benchmarking multi-event generation, i.e., Meve. TunerDiT achieves state-of-the-art performance across 8 metrics and offers a tunable trade-off between video consistency and event separation, compared with other training-free methods. The improvement in text alignment increases with the event count, indicating a scaling possibility with increasing event count.

</details>

Abstract: AI is being used by people globally, but not everyone is using it in the same ways. Using a large-scale dataset of anonymized, de-identified, and privacy-scrubbed interactions with a widely available and free AI chatbot, we empirically characterize differences in early adopters' usage across countries. Schooling is the most common domain of use in most countries, particularly low-income countries, with a strong inverse association evident between schooling and country-level GDP. Leisure-related use, by contrast, is positively associated with country-level income. Language, we find, also shapes use: English-language interactions are overrepresented in places where the predominant languages were not well-served by existing models during the period of the study. Improving performance across languages may be a key factor, our work suggests, in whether this technology expands digital divides or enables leapfrogging.

Abstract: Building on our previous work, this paper develops practical, low-barrier methods for freelance translators and smaller language service providers to evaluate translation technologies using rigorous yet accessible analytic methods. Here we address a high-stakes, specialized need: offline translation for confidentiality-sensitive domains in which privacy constraints preclude the use of cloud-based engines and commercial LLMs. We expand the Reeve Foundation Trilingual Corpus (RFTC) used in our previous work into a multilingual corpus (RFMC) by adding sentence-aligned German and Simplified Chinese reference translations. We then benchmark several locally runnable language models (via Ollama) across four language directions on 1000+ sentences selected from this corpus. We use consistent single-prompt calls without fine-tuning or domain adaptation, comparing local LLM outputs against commercial NMTs (DeepL, Baidu), a frontier LLM (GPT-5.2), and professional-grade local NMT systems (OPUS-CAT, NeuralDesktop, Promt). Automatic evaluation is conducted with MATEO. Results reveal substantial variation in local LLM performance across language directions and model sizes. The best local LLMs match or surpass local NMT systems and a frontier LLM, though they remain behind top commercial NMTs. These findings underscore the viability of carefully selected local LLM translation for privacy-constrained professionals and inform future research on model scaling and multilingual capability.

TL;DR: PhyDrawGen 是一个神经符号流水线，用于从自然语言生成物理上准确的示意图。它通过 LLM 提取场景图，经确定性求解器转换为精确几何图元，再由微调 Qwen-VL 进行迭代验证修正，在力学、光学和电磁学共 1449 个问题上显著优于 GPT-5-image 等模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型虽然能生成视觉上合理的物理示意图，但系统性地出现力矢量幻觉、忽略守恒定律、违反几何约束等问题，无法保证物理准确性。

Method: 提出 PhyDrawGen 神经符号流水线：(1) LLM 从问题文本中提取带类型的场景图；(2) 确定性求解器将场景图转换为平面直线图（PSLG），编码力平衡、光路和场拓扑为精确几何基元；(3) 微调 Qwen-VL 模型执行视觉化的"提出-验证"循环，迭代修正约束违反。

Result: 在包含 1449 个物理问题的基准上（涵盖力学、光学和电磁学），PhyDrawGen 显著优于 GPT-5-image、Gemini 2.5 Flash 和 Gemini 3 Pro，在非常规物体问题上也展现出鲁棒的物理准确性。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法结合 LLM 语义理解与确定性物理求解器，能够有效解决物理示意图生成中的幻觉和约束违反问题，为科学领域的内容生成提供了可行范式。

Classification reason: 该论文专注于从自然语言生成物理示意图（物理领域垂直应用），属于科学/垂直领域内容生成，因此映射到现有 subtopic "领域内容生成应用"（domain_application），该 subtopic 在 multimodal_generation 和 生成式 AI 方向下均有收录。

Importance reason: Niche application in physics diagram generation with low alignment to user's priority subtopics like autoregressive video generation or world models. Signals: neuro-symbolic pipeline, physics diagram generation, LLM + deterministic solver, fine-tuned Qwen-VL, benchmark on 1449 problems

Abstract: Generating physics diagrams from text requires strict adherence to physical laws. While current generative models produce visually plausible outputs, they systematically hallucinate force vectors, ignore conservation laws, and violate geometric constraints. We present PhyDrawGen, a neuro-symbolic pipeline that decouples semantic scene understanding from physical constraint satisfaction. First, a large language model extracts a typed scene graph from the problem text. A deterministic solver then converts this graph into a Planar Straight-Line Graph (PSLG), encoding force balance, optical paths, and field topologies as exact geometric primitives. Finally, a fine-tuned Qwen-VL model implements a visually grounded propose-verify loop to iteratively correct any constraint violations. Evaluated on a benchmark of 1,449 problems spanning mechanics, optics, and electromagnetism, PhyDrawGen significantly outperforms GPT-5-image, Gemini 2.5 Flash, and Gemini 3 Pro, demonstrating robust physical accuracy even on unusual-object problems.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于云端的视觉监控系统会将原始图像数据暴露给外部服务，这与GDPR的数据最小化原则存在根本冲突。作者希望设计一种隐私内建（privacy-by-design）的流水线，将所有推理限制在边缘设备上，从根本上解决这一矛盾。

Method: 系统架构包括：(1) 在Raspberry Pi 5上使用Hailo-8L AI加速器编译运行YOLOv5n-seg模型进行实时目标检测，推理后立即丢弃原始像素缓冲区；(2) 有状态触发引擎将最小化JSON事件负载转发到本地部署的Phi-3 Mini（3.8B参数，Q4_0量化）模型；(3) LLM生成1-2句自然语言警报供人类操作员查看。整个过程中没有任何图像数据跨越网络边界。

Result: 在目标硬件上报告了推理延迟和资源利用率，并展示了生成的警报示例。结果表明，在单板计算机上结合专用神经网络加速器和本地大语言模型不仅是可行的，而且能产生实际可部署、可读性良好的监控输出。

Conclusion: 该方案通过设计层面符合GDPR第5(1)(c)条的数据最小化原则，证明了边缘端AI+本地LLM的组合在隐私合规的视觉监控场景中具有实际部署价值。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种边缘设备上的隐私保护视觉监控系统，使用本地目标检测和本地LLM生成自然语言警报，属于垂直领域（监控/安全）中的内容生成应用，与现有 canonical subtopic "领域内容生成应用" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on GDPR compliance and edge deployment rather than advancing core generative AI capabilities. Signals: privacy-by-design, edge deployment, YOLOv5n-seg, Phi-3 Mini, low resource utilization

Abstract: Visual monitoring systems that rely on cloud-based AI inference expose raw image data to external services, creating fundamental tensions with the data-minimisation principle of the General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR). This paper presents a proof-of-concept privacy-by-design pipeline that resolves this tension by confining all inference entirely to the edge device. A YOLOv5n-seg model compiled for a Hailo-8L AI accelerator delivers real-time object detection on a Raspberry Pi 5, from which raw pixel buffers are immediately discarded after inference. A stateful trigger engine forwards minimal JSON event payloads to a locally hosted instance of Phi-3 Mini (3.8B parameters, Q4_0 quantisation), which synthesises one-to-two sentence natural-language alerts for a human operator. No image data crosses the network boundary at any point; only the generated text alert is transmitted. We describe the full system architecture and implementation, report measured inference latency and resource utilisation on the target hardware, and present representative generated alerts. The results demonstrate that combining a dedicated neural-network accelerator with an on-device large language model on a single-board computer is not only feasible but produces practically deployable, human-readable monitoring output while aligning with GDPR Art. 5(1)(c) by design.

</details>

<details>

Conclusion: 静态对齐约束不足以维持评估行为的鲁棒性，需要在生成系统中嵌入动态、可存活性驱动的调节机制。将基于人格的评估建模为潜在表征流形上的结构化动力系统，为更自适应、更对齐人类价值观的AI评估提供了基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于人格（persona）的生成式AI对齐评估框架，属于生成式 AI评测范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成式 AI 评测与基准"（evaluation_benchmark）高度匹配。同时涉及生成模型对齐（generation_alignment）和多模态对齐评估（multimodal_rl_alignment），因此也匹配另外两个方向。

Importance reason: Novel evaluation framework but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics in generation efficiency and world models. Signals: Pluralistic alignment, persona-based evaluation, generative AI benchmarking, state-space drift, synthetic cognitive profiles

Abstract: Current alignment paradigms for generative artificial intelligence rely predominantly on monolithic benchmarking frameworks that reduce the plurality of human judgment to aggregated statistical baselines, thereby obscuring cultural, demographic, and contextual variability in evaluation. We introduce a state-space constrained emulation framework for AI evaluation that replaces singular assessment functions with a structured manifold of synthetic cognitive profiles representing diverse human perspectives. We show that modern generative architectures can instantiate and maintain these evaluative personas with high consistency, enabling a form of pluralistic, perspective-dependent benchmarking that more closely reflects real-world consensus variability. However, we further analyze the stability of these simulated evaluators under sequential inference and stochastic prompt perturbations, revealing systematic degradation in persona coherence that manifests as state-space drift and semantic inconsistency. These findings suggest that static alignment constraints are insufficient for sustaining robust evaluative behavior over time. Instead, we argue for the necessity of embedding dynamic, viability-driven regulatory mechanisms within generative systems to preserve coherent cognitive emulation. By framing persona-based evaluation as a structured dynamical system over latent representation manifolds, this study provides a foundation for more adaptive, human-aligned, and context-sensitive approaches to AI evaluation.

</details>

Abstract: Scientific figures are among the most effective means of communicating complex research ideas, yet producing publication-quality illustrations remains one of the most labor-intensive parts of paper preparation. Existing automated systems each target a single figure type under text-only input, leaving the diversity of types and conditions researchers actually use unaddressed; their raster outputs further cannot be locally revised. Because scientific figures are structured compositions of discrete semantic components, the localized errors generators produce on such layouts demand not a stronger backbone but a harness. We instantiate this harness in two complementary systems: Crafter, a multi-agent harness for figure generation that generalizes across figure types and input conditions without architectural changes, and CraftEditor, which applies the same pattern to convert raster outputs into editable SVGs. Moreover, we introduce CraftBench, a benchmark spanning three figure types and four input conditions with human quality annotation. Experiments show that Crafter substantially outperforms both standalone generators and the agentic baseline on PaperBanana-Bench and CraftBench, with ablations confirming each component's independent contribution; CraftEditor faithfully converts outputs into editable SVGs that surpass all baselines. Our code and benchmark are available at .

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可控的潜在扩散模型，通过直方图正则化项约束体素强度分布，在3D CT体积中合成肺结节，改善了不同亚型结节（实性、部分实性、磨玻璃）的纹理真实感和亚型一致性，并验证了其在数据增强和下游分类任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的肺结节合成方法主要优化空间重建损失，虽然能保证体素级相似性，但未能充分约束病灶级别的强度分布，导致生成结节纹理过于平滑，无法准确反映不同亚型结节（实性、部分实性、磨玻璃）的独特衰减特征。这限制了合成数据在临床下游任务中的可用性。

Method: 提出一种可控潜在扩散模型，在潜在空间中进行条件生成。模型以结节亚型、空间掩码和HU直方图为条件，并引入可微分的特征空间直方图正则化项，在生成过程中约束体素强度分布，使其与真实结节的强度分布对齐。该方法在3D CT体积中合成完整肺结节，而非仅生成孤立结节区域。

Result: 在肺部CT数据上的实验表明，该方法在定量指标和视觉图灵测试中均实现了强视觉真实感。用于数据增强时，生成的结节能提升下游临床任务性能，尤其对代表性不足的结节亚型效果显著，并对亚型感知的恶性分类有潜在益处。

Conclusion: 直方图正则化的潜在扩散模型能够有效合成具有亚型特异性强度分布的肺结节，提升生成数据的视觉真实感和临床可用性，为医学影像数据增强提供了一种有前景的方案。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical domain lung nodule synthesis, which is outside user's priority subtopics and has negative keyword "clinical". Signals: medical diagnosis, clinical task, lung nodule, histogram regularization, data augmentation

Abstract: While automated diagnosis systems have achieved remarkable success in computed tomography (CT)-based lung cancer screening, their development remains limited by the scarcity of diverse, annotated pulmonary nodule datasets. Diffusion-based generative models offer a promising strategy for data synthesis; however, many existing conditional approaches primarily optimize spatial reconstruction losses, which encourage voxel-wise similarity but may inadequately constrain lesion-level intensity distributions. As a result, these methods may produce over-smoothed texture profiles and underrepresent the distinct attenuation characteristics of different nodule subtypes, including solid, part-solid, and ground-glass nodules. To address this challenge, we propose a controllable latent diffusion model that synthesizes pulmonary nodules within full 3D CT volumes while accurately modeling nodule-specific intensity distributions. Specifically, rather than relying solely on spatial losses, we introduce a histogram-based regularization term that constrains voxel intensity distributions during the generative process. The model combines subtype, spatial mask, and Hounsfield unit (HU) histogram conditioning with the differentiable feature-space histogram regularization term to better align lesion-level intensity distributions, improving the visual plausibility and subtype consistency of synthesized nodules. Extensive experiments on lung CT data demonstrate that our framework achieves strong visual realism, validated through both quantitative metrics and a visual Turing test. Furthermore, when used for data augmentation, the generated nodules improve performance in downstream clinical tasks, particularly for underrepresented nodule subtypes, and show a potential benefit for subtype-informed malignancy classification.

</details>

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented text-to-music (TTM) systems augment underspecified user prompts using captions retrieved from a music caption dataset. This design introduces an integrity dependency on the music knowledge database. We show that an attacker can poison the database by injecting a small number of crafted music captions, causing the system to retrieve malicious captions that bias prompt augmentation and steer generation away from the user's intended function, without modifying the user prompt, retriever, or generator. To achieve the music caption poisoning attack, we propose a dual-layer caption poisoning strategy that preserves high-level retrieval anchors while injecting low-level acoustic descriptors to steer prompt augmentation and downstream music generation toward an attacker-chosen target intent. In a MusicCaps knowledge database, CLAP retriever, and MusicGen pipeline, poisoned generations move substantially closer to the attacker's target, while remaining comparably aligned with the original user query. These results expose a practical integrity risk for retrieval-augmented creative AI systems. Our demo can be found at:

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出 SteerFace 训练框架，核心思想是在嵌入超球面上将身份嵌入向随机正交方向扰动，作为身份保持的正则化器，惩罚生成器对非身份成分的依赖。同时引入自适应策略，学习每个样本的扰动强度，兼顾样本级偏好和整体统计特性。

Result: 实验表明 SteerFace 有效缓解了视觉倾向问题，在多个下游人脸识别任务上优于先前方法，且在不同训练数据集和生成流水线上均具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SteerFace 通过简单高效的自适应残差扰动框架，有效缩小了合成人脸数据与真实数据之间的分布差距，为合成数据在人脸识别中的实际应用提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究利用扩散模型生成合成人脸数据用于人脸识别训练，属于合成训练数据生成范畴，与现有 canonical subtopic "合成数据与数据集蒸馏"（synthetic_data）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on face recognition debiasing, not aligned with priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: synthetic data, face recognition, diffusion-based generation, identity embedding, visual tendency

Abstract: The shortage of legally compliant data for face recognition training has sparked growing interest in using synthetic data as an alternative. While recent diffusion-based methods enable the generation of photorealistic face images with strong identity adherence and data diversity, their downstream recognition performance still exhibits a significant synthetic-real gap. This paper identifies visual tendency as a previously underexplored limitation, whereby synthetic data exhibit an unrealistic prevalence of visual attributes and thus deviate from the real-data distribution. Visual tendency can be attributed to the generator's conditioning on identity embeddings, through which co-occurring residual visual cues are unintentionally absorbed into learned identity semantics. To discourage the generator from exploiting such visual cues, this paper proposes SteerFace, a simple and efficient training framework that perturbs identity embeddings by steering them toward random orthogonal directions on the embedding hypersphere. The perturbation serves as an identity-preserving regularizer that penalizes the generator's reliance on non-identity components, as supported by theoretical analysis. This paper further introduces an adaptive strategy that learns perturbation strengths with both sample-wise preference and favorable overall statistics. Extensive experiments show that SteerFace effectively mitigates visual tendency, outperforms prior methods in downstream face recognition, and generalizes well across different training datasets and generation pipelines.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的图像往往同时存在多种退化（如模糊、噪声、低光照等），且退化去除的顺序对最终恢复质量有显著影响。现有基于智能体的图像恢复方法需要O((N^D)^2)次专家调用构建训练数据，且无法灵活扩展新的恢复专家，存在效率低和扩展性差两大瓶颈。

Method: 提出DiTTo框架，包含两个核心组件：1) DiTTo模拟器，由∪S-IR（单步恢复动作模拟）和AiO-IQA（每动作质量预测）组成，将ORTD构建复杂度从O((N^D)^2)降至O(N^D)；2) DiTTo智能体，在模拟器生成的ORTD上进行SFT训练，再通过顺序感知恢复对齐（ORA）沿退化识别、恢复动作排序和输出格式三个独立轴进行对齐，支持即插即用的专家扩展。

Result: 在包含最多五种并发退化的MiO-100评测集上，DiTTo智能体在多种退化同时存在的图像恢复质量上超越了所有已有的基于智能体的图像恢复方法，达到SOTA。

Conclusion: DiTTo通过模拟器高效构建训练数据并结合顺序感知对齐，解决了现有智能体图像恢复方法的效率和扩展性瓶颈，为多退化图像恢复提供了一种可扩展的智能体解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于VLM的图像恢复智能体框架DiTTo，使用智能体调度多个恢复专家模型进行多退化图像恢复，属于AI智能体与生成工作流（agent_workflow）范畴。同时涉及图像生成/恢复，也匹配多模态生成方向。

Importance reason: Proposes a scalable, order-aware agent for multi-degradation image restoration, addressing efficiency and extensibility bottlenecks with a novel simulator and alignment method. Signals: agent-based image restoration, scalable extensibility, order-aware restoration alignment, efficient ORTD construction, plug-and-play expert addition

Abstract: Real-world images rarely suffer from a single degradation, and the order in which degradations are removed substantially affects the final restoration quality, motivating agent-based image restoration (IR), where a vision-language model schedules a pool of pre-built restoration-experts. However, existing training-based agents require $\mathcal{O}((N^{\mathbf{D}})^{2})$ restoration-expert calls per image to construct the Optimal Restoration-action Trajectory Dataset (ORTD), where $N^{\mathbf{D}}$ denotes the number of degradation types in the universe $\mathbf{D}$, and couple agent training to a fixed restoration-expert pool, preventing extension to newly introduced restoration-experts without full retraining. To overcome these efficiency and extensibility bottlenecks, we propose \textbf{DiTTo}, a novel order-aware image restoration agent framework consisting of the DiTTo Simulator and the DiTTo Agent. The DiTTo Simulator combines $\cup$S-IR for single-step restoration-action simulation and AiO-IQA for per-action quality prediction, reducing ORTD construction to $\mathcal{O}(N^{\mathbf{D}})$ simulator calls per image; the DiTTo Agent is trained by SFT on the simulator-generated ORTD, followed by \textbf{Order-aware Restoration Alignment (ORA)} that aligns degradation identification, restoration-action-ordering, and output format along independent axes. This enables \textbf{plug-and-play scalable extensibility}: adding a new restoration-expert requires updating only the lightweight ORA stage. On the MiO-100 evaluation set with up to five concurrent degradations, our DiTTo Agent achieves state-of-the-art multi-degradation restoration quality among previous agent-based IR methods.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 自主数据工程可作为 LLM 的一项可测量能力，为智能体驱动的模型专业化开辟了新路径。同时揭示了当前方法的潜力与瓶颈，为未来研究提供了方向。

Classification reason: 论文研究 LLM 作为自主数据工程师，通过智能体驱动端到端数据工程流水线实现模型专业化，核心是智能体自动化工作流，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "AI 智能体与生成工作流" 高度匹配。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong performance on general tasks, while often struggling to adapt to specialized domains without high-quality domain-specific data. Existing LLM-based data curation methods primarily rely on human-designed workflows, leaving it unexamined whether LLMs can autonomously execute an end-to-end data engineering pipeline for model specialization. We formalize \textbf{Autonomous Agentic Data Engineering}, a novel task designed to evaluate LLMs as autonomous data engineers that drive model specialization through end-to-end data curation. We frame data as an optimizable component and study agents that plan, generate, and iteratively optimize training data across multiple domains, guided by post-training performance improvement. Experiments show that autonomous LLM data engineers yield substantial gains, as GPT-5.2 constructs a training curriculum that improves a student model by \textbf{57.29\%}, entirely through iterative, agent-driven data adaptation. By illuminating both potential and bottlenecks, our study establishes autonomous data engineering as a measurable capability and charts a path toward agent-driven model specialization\footnote{Code will be released at .}.

</details>

*Zhihao Wu, Gracia Gong, Qinglin Zhu, Yudong Chen, Runcong Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM文本水印的一个根本性漏洞：当用户访问多个模型时，通过平均多个模型的输出概率分布（线性集成）可以消除水印统计信号。理论证明平均分布能以二阶误差恢复无水印分布，实验表明平均3-5个模型即可将检测z分数从5-300降至2以下，同时提升生成质量27.5%且速度提升6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM文本水印方案假设用户只能访问单个模型，但现实中用户可同时使用多个模型。作者希望揭示这一假设缺陷，证明水印在竞争性多模型市场中的根本脆弱性。

Method: 提出WASH（Watermark Attenuation via Statistical Hybridisation）方法，通过平均多个模型的输出概率分布来消除水印扰动。理论证明平均分布可恢复无水印分布（误差为二阶项），并解决词汇表不对齐和分词差异等工程挑战。

Result: 在6种水印方案和3种LLM上的实验表明：平均3个模型即可将检测z分数从5-300降至2以下（低于检测阈值4），TPR@5%FPR降至50%以下，同时生成质量提升27.5%，长序列生成速度比最优基线快6倍。

Conclusion: LLM文本水印存在根本性脆弱性，线性集成即可有效消除水印。鲁棒的AI文本检测要么接受这一漏洞，要么需要模型提供商之间前所未有的协调合作。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM生成文本的水印脆弱性，属于生成式 AI方向下的水印与版权保护（watermark_copyright）这一规范子主题，直接对应水印的安全性与鲁棒性分析。

Importance reason: Strong theoretical and empirical contribution to watermark vulnerability, but low alignment with user's priority subtopics on generation and efficiency. Signals: LLM watermark vulnerability, linear ensemble attack, theoretical proof, practical WASH method, low personal priority match

Abstract: Watermarking embeds statistical signatures in AI-generated text for detection and attribution. We reveal a fundamental vulnerability: when users access multiple models (today's reality), watermarks trivially fail. Watermarks perturb output distributions away from the original, and in competitive markets, these perturbations are typically independent across providers. We theoretically prove that averaging output probability distributions recovers the unwatermarked distribution with up to a second-order error term. Empirically, simply averaging 3-5 models cancels out these perturbations. We introduce WASH (Watermark Attenuation via Statistical Hybridisation), which solves practical challenges in ensemble generation: vocabulary misalignment and tokenisation differences across heterogeneous models. Experiments across six watermarking schemes and three LLMs show that averaging across 3 models suppresses detection z-scores from 5-300 to below 2 (below the detection threshold of 4) and reduces TPR at 5% FPR to below 50%, while improving quality by 27.5% and running 6 times faster than the best baseline on the long sequence generation. Our results suggest that robust AI-text detection via watermarking requires either accepting this fundamental vulnerability or unprecedented coordination among model providers.

</details>

*Sander Land, Daniel M. Bikel*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 未分类

TL;DR: 本文提出基于项目反应理论（IRT）的指标，用于审计 LLM 基准测试中的标签错误。使用 114 个模型的响应，在 7 个偏好和多选题基准的前 200 个样本中以 95% 的精度识别出可能的错误标签，性能优于有监督分类器。研究发现这些错误源于机械标注启发式、上游数据集继承的标注错误以及本质上模糊的题目。同时发现奖励模型更擅长风格偏好而非事实知识，且某个前沿奖励模型与检测到的错误标签有 78% 的一致性，暗示可能存在基准污染或过度优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 基准测试的标签在发布后就被冻结，错误也随之被无声地传播到下游基准中，影响模型评估的可靠性。现有方法缺乏系统性地检测基准标签错误的手段。

Method: 使用项目反应理论（IRT）构建统计模型，基于 114 个模型在 7 个偏好和多选题基准上的响应，计算每个题目的异常指标，以 95% 精度识别前 200 个最可能错误标注的样本。

Result: 该方法在识别错误标签方面优于有监督分类器。发现错误标签主要来自机械标注启发式、上游数据集继承的错误以及本质上模糊的题目。还发现奖励模型在风格偏好上表现更好而非事实知识，某个前沿奖励模型与检测到的错误标签有 78% 的一致性（其他模型仅 38%）。

Conclusion: IRT 是一种有效的基准标签审计工具，能够高精度识别错误标签并揭示基准构建中的系统性问题。同时发现奖励模型存在基准污染或过度优化的迹象，对 LLM 评估的可信度提出了重要警示。

Abstract: LLM benchmark labels are frozen at release and silently propagated into downstream benchmarks, errors and all. We introduce an Item Response Theory-based indicator that surfaces likely mislabels at 95% precision in the top 200 examples across seven preference and multiple-choice benchmarks using responses from 114 models, outperforming a supervised classifier. We trace these errors to mechanical labeling heuristics, upstream annotation mistakes inherited unchanged from source datasets, and fundamentally ambiguous items without a defensible single label. The same model fit reveals that reward models specialize in stylistic preference rather than factual knowledge, and identifies one frontier reward model that agrees with detected mislabels at 78% accuracy versus 38% for its peers, consistent with benchmark contamination or benchmark-specific over-optimization.
