<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型虽然都可以看作最小化分布间散度，但依赖不同的算法和几何原理，缺乏统一的连续时间理论视角。作者希望建立基于 Wasserstein 梯度流的统一框架，揭示不同方法之间的内在联系。

Method: 基于 Wasserstein 梯度流和 Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto (JKO) 格式，提出生成 Wasserstein 流（GWF）框架。将扩散模型、GAN 等现有方法统一为参数化 JKO 格式对 f-散度目标的实例，并扩展到 IPM（积分概率度量）和平方 MMD（最大均值差异），推导新的 JKO 生成算法。最后分析了参数化 Wasserstein 流，其中动力学被限制在参数化映射诱导的分布上。

Result: 建立了多种生成算法之间的等价关系；将框架扩展到 IPM 和平方 MMD 并推导了新算法；通过实验研究了不同目标函数下 JKO 正则化的影响。

Conclusion: GWF 框架为生成模型提供了统一的理论视角，揭示了扩散模型、GAN 等方法之间的深层联系，并为设计新的生成算法提供了理论指导。

Classification reason: 该论文提出生成 Wasserstein 流（GWF）的统一理论框架，将多种生成模型（如扩散模型、GAN、MMD 生成等）统一为 Wasserstein 梯度流与 JKO 格式的实例，属于生成模型的理论基础与对齐优化范畴。该论文与 multimodal_generation 方向下的 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）高度相关，因其核心关注生成模型的分布优化、散度最小化与正则化对齐。

Importance reason: Provides a unifying theoretical framework for generative models, but lacks direct application to the user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: Unifying theory for generative models, Extends JKO to IPM and MMD, Connects diffusion, GAN, and MMD generation, Theoretical rather than applied, No video or autoregressive focus

Abstract: Many modern generative models can be viewed as minimizing divergences between probability distributions, yet they rely on different algorithmic and geometric principles. Wasserstein gradient flows provide a continuous-time formulation for optimizing over distributions, and can be approximated through their implicit discretization via the Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto (JKO) scheme. In this work, we present a unified theoretical framework for generative modeling based on Wasserstein gradient flows, which we refer to as Generative Wasserstein Flows (GWF). We show that a broad class of existing methods can be derived as instances of parametric JKO schemes for $f$-divergence objectives, and we establish equivalences between several recently proposed algorithms. We extend this framework beyond f-divergence to Integral Probability Metrics and squared Maximum Mean Discrepancy, deriving new JKO-based generative algorithms, and clarifying their connections with GANs. We study empirically the impact of the JKO regularization for a wide set of objectives. Finally, we analyze parametric Wasserstein flows, where the dynamics are restricted to distributions induced by parametrized maps.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图到文本生成主要依赖自回归LLM，但掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）在该任务上的行为尚未被研究。作者希望理解MDLM的生成轨迹特性，并解决SFT对其解码策略的破坏问题。

Method: 1) 系统分析MDLM在图到文本生成中的token去掩码轨迹，按实体词、关系/功能词、结构词分类统计。2) 发现SFT导致模型过早解码句尾结构词，固定输出长度。3) 提出lambda缩放结构解码（lambda-scaled structural decoding），在推理时降低结构词置信度，恢复自然解码顺序。4) 提出Graph-LLaDA，将图Transformer编码器与LLaDA扩散解码器结合，显式编码图结构信息。

Result: lambda缩放结构解码在LAGRANGE数据集上恢复+9.4 BLEU-4。跨数据集评估显示，传统基线过拟合数据集特定模式，而LLM和MDLM方法泛化性显著更好。

Conclusion: MDLM在图到文本生成中展现出与自回归模型不同的生成策略（实体优先），SFT会破坏这一策略，而lambda缩放结构解码能有效恢复。将图结构显式编码到扩散解码中可进一步提升性能。

Classification reason: 论文研究掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）在图到文本生成中的解码轨迹，分析token去掩码顺序并提出lambda缩放结构解码来修正SFT导致的解码策略偏差。这属于生成模型的可控性与解码优化，与multimodal_generation下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）中的guidance与可控性方向高度吻合。同时该工作也涉及生成式 AI领域的文本生成应用。

Importance reason: Focuses on graph-to-text generation, which is not a priority subtopic, and lacks strong alignment with user's key boost keywords. Signals: masked diffusion language models, graph-to-text generation, generation trajectory analysis, SFT failure mode, lambda-scaled structural decoding

Abstract: We present the first systematic study of masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) for graph-to-text generation. We analyze MDLM generation trajectories -- the order in which tokens are unmasked during iterative decoding -- and find that, unlike autoregressive LLMs which generate text linearly, MDLMs naturally prioritize entities first, followed by relational and function words, with structural tokens resolved last. We further identify a previously undocumented failure mode of supervised fine-tuning: SFT disrupts this strategy by prematurely anchoring structural sentence-ending tokens early in the decoding trajectory, effectively fixing the output length which can lead to omitted or hallucinated information. To address this, we propose lambda-scaled structural decoding, a training-free inference-time modification that downweights structural token confidence and recovers +9.4 BLEU-4. Finally, we introduce Graph-LLaDA, which integrates a Graph Transformer encoder into LLaDA's decoding process to explicitly incorporate relational graph structure. Cross-dataset evaluation on LAGRANGE reveals that previous baselines overfit to dataset-specific patterns, while LLM- and MDLM-based approaches generalize significantly better.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抽象推理基准（如ARC）局限于网格或文本格式，缺乏对图结构数据的系统评测。图数据在现实世界中广泛存在（如分子、社交网络、知识图谱），但缺乏一个能够评估模型在图变换任务上泛化能力的标准化基准。

Method: GraphARC将ARC的少样本变换学习范式推广到图数据上。每个任务包含几个输入-输出图对作为示例，模型需要推断变换规则并应用于新的测试图。基准覆盖局部（如节点/边添加删除）、全局（如图同构变换）和层次（如子图合并）三类图变换。支持跨不同图族和大小的可扩展生成。

Result: 在多个SOTA语言模型上的评估显示：模型能回答图属性问题（如节点数、连通性），但在完整图变换任务上表现较差，存在"理解-执行鸿沟"。在更大图实例上性能进一步下降，暴露出规模扩展障碍。

Conclusion: GraphARC为图基础模型提供了一个有前景的测试平台，结合了节点分类、链接预测和图生成等任务于统一框架中。当前模型在图变换推理上存在明显局限，需要进一步发展图感知的推理能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了GraphARC，一个基于图的抽象推理基准，属于生成模型评测与数据集（generation_benchmark）范畴，因为其核心贡献是构建了一个新的评测基准，用于评估模型在图变换任务上的生成与推理能力。同时该基准涉及图生成（graph generation）任务，与多模态生成方向相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on graph-based abstract reasoning benchmark, which is not aligned with user's priority on generative video/image models and efficient inference. Signals: Graph benchmark, Abstract reasoning, ARC generalization, Comprehension-execution gap, Not generative

Abstract: Relational reasoning lies at the heart of intelligence, but existing benchmarks are typically confined to formats such as grids or text. We introduce GraphARC, a benchmark for abstract reasoning on graph-structured data. GraphARC generalizes the few-shot transformation learning paradigm of the Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus (ARC). Each task requires inferring a transformation rule from a few input-output pairs and applying it to a new test graph, covering local, global, and hierarchical graph transformations. Unlike grid-based ARC, GraphARC instances can be generated at scale across diverse graph families and sizes, enabling systematic evaluation of generalization abilities. We evaluate state-of-the-art language models on GraphARC and observe clear limitations. Models can answer questions about graph properties but often fail to solve the full graph transformation task, revealing a comprehension-execution gap. Performance further degrades on larger instances, exposing scaling barriers. More broadly, by combining aspects of node classification, link prediction, and graph generation within a single framework, GraphARC provides a promising testbed for future graph foundation models.

</details>


### [82] [Count Anything](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30846)
*Mengqi Lei, Shuokun Cheng, Wei Bao, Shaoyi Du, Jun-Hai Yong, Siqi Li, Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (45.34) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Count Anything，一个文本引导的跨域通用目标计数模型，并构建了包含约22万张图像、619个类别的跨域大规模目标计数数据集CLOC。模型采用双粒度实例枚举策略（区域级稀疏计数器和像素级密集计数器），通过互补计数融合实现多域泛化，在多个域上优于现有开放世界计数方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标计数模型通常针对特定场景（人群、车辆、细胞、农作物、遥感等）设计，难以跨类别、视觉域、目标尺度和密度分布泛化。缺乏统一的跨域计数基准和通用计数模型。

Method: 提出Count Anything模型，采用离散实例点而非密度图。包含两个核心组件：1) Region-level Sparse Counter：为大尺度稀疏目标提供目标级锚点；2) Pixel-level Dense Counter：通过密集点预测处理小尺度、拥挤和弱边界目标。采用点级监督策略从异构标注中学习，并通过无参数的Complementary Count Fusion融合两个计数器的结果。

Result: 在CLOC数据集上，Count Anything在多个视觉域（通用场景、遥感、组织病理学、细胞显微、农业、微生物）上均取得强准确率，在跨域泛化方面显著优于现有开放世界计数方法。

Conclusion: 本文通过构建统一跨域基准CLOC和提出双粒度实例枚举的通用计数模型Count Anything，有效推动了目标计数从碎片化走向通用化，展示了文本引导跨域计数的可行性和优势。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了跨域大规模目标计数数据集CLOC（涵盖6个视觉域、约22万张图像、619个类别），并提出通用目标计数模型Count Anything。核心贡献在于统一了分散的计数任务并建立评测基准，因此映射到multimodal_generation下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark）。同时，该工作也涉及生成式 AI领域的合成数据与领域应用。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark contribution but focuses on counting, not core generative modeling or world models. Signals: cross-domain counting dataset, text-guided counting, not autoregressive or diffusion generation, no video or world model, negative keyword remote sensing

Abstract: Object counting remains fragmented across domain-specific datasets and task formulations, despite rapid progress in generalist vision models. Existing counting models are often tailored to scenarios such as crowds, vehicles, cells, crops, or remote-sensing objects, and thus struggle to generalize across categories, visual domains, object scales, and density distributions. In this paper, we study text-guided object counting across domains, where a model takes an image and a natural-language query as input and returns an instance-grounded set of target points whose cardinality gives the count. This formulation unifies category-conditioned counting with interpretable spatial localization. To support this setting, we construct CLOC, a Cross-domain Large-scale Object Counting dataset that reorganizes diverse public data sources into a unified benchmark. CLOC covers six visual domains: General Scene, Remote Sensing, Histopathology, Cellular Microscopy, Agriculture, and Microbiology, with about 220K images, 619 categories, and 15M object instances. Based on CLOC, we propose Count Anything, a generalist model for text-guided object counting. Unlike density-map-based methods, which dominate counting models, Count Anything adopts discrete instance points and performs dual-granularity instance enumeration. A Region-level Sparse Counter provides object-level anchors for large and sparse targets, while a Pixel-level Dense Counter handles small, crowded, and weakly bounded targets via dense point prediction. A point-centric supervision strategy enables learning from heterogeneous annotations, and Complementary Count Fusion combines both counters in a parameter-free manner. Extensive experiments show that Count Anything achieves strong accuracy and multi-domain generalization, outperforming existing open-world counting methods. Code is available at: .

</details>

*Junling Wang, Boqi Chen, Heejin Do, Mubashara Akhtar, April Yi Wang, Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL, cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出方程到视觉生成任务，构建E2V-Bench基准评测文生图模型在算术教育中的视觉表示能力，发现现有模型在物体计数和关系结构上存在严重错误，并探索了基准引导的增强策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统越来越多地用于教育内容创作，但现有文生图模型能否生成忠实表达教学概念的视觉内容尚不清楚。作者希望系统性地评估和提升模型在算术教育场景下的视觉生成能力。

Method: 通过教师访谈和教育材料分析，构建了E2V-Bench基准，涵盖四种教学视觉类型（集合、数轴、十格框、算式），并设计自动评估指标（物体计数正确性、关系结构保持等）。基于基准评测结果，探索了提示工程、思维链推理等增强策略。

Result: 现有文生图模型在E2V-Bench上表现不佳，主要错误为物体计数错误和关系结构破坏。基准引导的增强策略能提升部分模型性能，但与人类水平仍有差距。

Conclusion: 当前文生图模型在算术教育视觉生成任务中缺乏数值和关系保真度，未来模型需要更强的数值与关系理解能力。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了面向算术教育视觉生成的E2V-Bench基准，并评测了多种文生图模型，属于生成模型评测与数据集方向，与[multimodal_generation]下的[generation_benchmark]（生成模型评测与数据集）完全匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on educational content generation, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like autoregressive video generation or world models. Signals: education-focused, text-to-image evaluation, arithmetic visual generation, not core generative model advancement, low priority subtopic match

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly used to support educational content creation, yet it remains unclear whether they can generate outputs that faithfully represent the pedagogical concepts they are intended to teach. Thus, we introduce equation-to-visual generation, a task that, in contrast to conventional image generation, requires producing pedagogically meaningful visuals from arithmetic equations while precisely preserving their numerical and relational structure. Informed by interviews with teachers and an analysis of educational materials, we construct E2V-Bench, a benchmark spanning four pedagogically grounded visual types, along with automatic metrics for evaluating visual correctness. Our evaluation reveals that recent text-to-image (T2I) models frequently fail on this task, with errors dominated by incorrect object counts and broken relational structure. Building on this, we explore benchmark-guided enhancement strategies. These strategies improve representative models, while the remaining gap calls for stronger numerical and relational grounding in future T2I models.

</details>


### [84] [Conformal Reliability: A New Evaluation Metric for Conditional Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30807)
*Yachen Gao, Xinwei Sun, Yikai Wang, Ye Shi, Jingya Wang, Jianfeng Feng, Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 值得关注 (72.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于共形预测的条件生成模型可靠性评估指标（reliability score），并设计了 CReL 框架来高效计算该指标。该指标衡量预测集在指定置信水平下的最差情况性能，比现有单输出评估指标更能捕捉生成的不确定性和潜在风险。在合成数据和图像到文本、文本到图像任务上的实验验证了该指标的可解释性和框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件生成模型的评估指标通常只评估单个输出，无法捕捉生成结果的不确定性和潜在风险。缺乏一个能够考虑模型固有不确定性、衡量生成可靠性的合适指标。

Method: 基于共形预测（conformal prediction）提出可靠性分数（reliability score），衡量预测集在预设置信水平下的最差情况性能。提出 CReL 框架，包含两个步骤：(i) 构建具有期望覆盖率的预测集；(ii) 在构建的预测集内精确优化可靠性分数。提供了覆盖率的理论保证。

Result: 在合成数据和图像到文本、文本到图像任务上的实验表明，该方法能生成比现有方法更具信息量的预测集，新的可靠性指标具有良好的可解释性，计算框架有效且高效。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于共形预测的可靠性评估指标为条件生成模型提供了一种新的、考虑不确定性的评测方式，弥补了现有单输出评估指标的不足。CReL 框架在理论和实验上均验证了其有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种基于共形预测的条件生成模型可靠性评估指标（reliability score），属于生成模型评测方法。这与 multimodal_generation 方向下的 [CANONICAL] subtopic "generation_benchmark（生成模型评测与数据集）" 高度匹配，因为该 subtopic 涵盖生成模型评测基准、自动评测指标等。同时该工作也涉及 生成式 AI 评测。

Importance reason: Proposes a new evaluation metric for conditional generation, but focuses on general reliability rather than the user's priority subtopics like autoregressive or diffusion video generation. Signals: new evaluation metric, conformal prediction, conditional generation, image-to-text, text-to-image

Abstract: Conditional generative models have recently achieved remarkable success in various applications. However, a suitable metric for evaluating the reliability of these models, which takes into account their inherent uncertainty, is still lacking. Existing metrics, which typically assess a single output, may fail to capture the variability or potential risks in generation. In this paper, we propose a novel evaluation metric called reliability score based on conformal prediction, which measures the worst-case performance within the prediction set at a pre-specified confidence level. However, computing this score is challenging due to the high-dimensional nature of the output space and the nonconvexity of both the metric function and the prediction set. To efficiently compute this score, we introduce Conformal ReLiability (CReL), a framework that can (i) construct the prediction set with desired coverage; and (ii) accurately optimize the reliability score within the constructed prediction set. We provide theoretical results on coverage and demonstrate empirically that our method produces more informative prediction sets than existing approaches. Experiments on synthetic data and the image-to-text and text-to-image tasks further demonstrate the interpretability of our new metric, and the validity and effectiveness of our computational framework. Source code can be found at .

</details>


### [85] [3DAE: Binaural Quality Assessment for Audio Novel View Synthesis with Spatial Maps and Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30469)
*Jialu Xu, Yifan Zhou*

Main category: cs.SD, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (50.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出3DAE，一个用于音频新视角合成（Audio Novel View Synthesis）模型的双耳质量评估诊断框架。包括3DAE Map（时频音频误差图，覆盖幅度、ILD、IPD、时间对齐、响度、高频失败等维度）和3DAE Bench（模型无关的基准测试集）。实验在ViGAS模型上对Replay-NVAS和SoundSpaces数据集进行评测，揭示了不同数据集上的主导失败模式（时间错位 vs ILD失配）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D音频和新视角声学合成模型通常使用全局指标（如全局误差）进行评估，但这些全局指标往往掩盖了双耳预测在何处以及为何失败。缺乏可解释的、细粒度的诊断工具来指导模型开发与优化。

Method: 提出全参考诊断框架3DAE，构建时频音频误差图（3DAE Map），涵盖幅度误差、ILD（耳间电平差）误差、IPD（耳间相位差）误差、时间对齐误差、响度误差和高频失败等维度。将这些诊断指标整合为模型无关的基准3DAE Bench，可接受任意真实-预测双耳音频对并输出质量报告。

Result: 在ViGAS模型上对Replay-NVAS和SoundSpaces两个数据集进行评测，发现不同数据集上主导失败模式不同：Replay-NVAS上主要是时间错位，SoundSpaces上主要是ILD失配。3DAE Map提供了直观的可视化诊断结果。

Conclusion: 3DAE框架为音频新视角合成模型提供了可解释的失败模式总结和直观的可视化误差图，有助于模型开发与优化。该工作填补了音频生成模型细粒度评测工具的空白。

Classification reason: 该论文提出3DAE Map（音频误差图）和3DAE Bench（空间音频误差基准），用于评估音频新视角合成（Audio Novel View Synthesis）模型的双耳质量。这属于生成模型（音频生成）的评测基准与诊断框架，因此映射到multimodal_generation下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "generation_benchmark"（生成模型评测与数据集）。同时，该工作也涉及生成式 AI领域的生成内容质量评估。

Importance reason: Focuses on audio evaluation, not core visual generation or efficient inference priorities. Signals: Audio novel view synthesis, Binaural quality assessment, Diagnostic framework, Not visual generation, No autoregressive or diffusion focus

Abstract: 3D audio and novel-view acoustic synthesis models are usually evaluated with global , global metrics often hide where and why binaural prediction fails. We propose a full-reference diagnostic framework that uses time-frequency audio error maps for magnitude, ILD, IPD, temporal alignment, loudness, and high-frequency failures, forming a 3D Audio Error Map (3DAE Map) for visual inspection. We frame these diagnostics into a model-agnostic benchmark, Spatial Audio Error Bench (3DAE Bench), which takes arbitrary ground-truth and predicted binaural pairs and reports the prediction quality of audio novel-view synthesis models. Experiments on ViGAS outputs over Replay-NVAS and SoundSpaces show different dominant failure modes: temporal misalignment on Replay-NVAS and ILD mismatch on SoundSpaces. Overall, the framework provides interpretable failure-mode summaries and intuitive visual maps for audio Novel-view-synthesis model development optimization.

</details>

### [86] [MergeTok: Unified Continuous and Discrete Visual Tokenization via Token Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30904)
*Luyuan Zhang, Siyuan Li, Zedong Wang, Qingsong Xie, Cheng Tan, Anna Wang, Yanhao Zhang, Chen Chen, Haonan Lu, Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (95.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MergeTok 提出一种统一的视觉分词器架构，通过 token merging 技术将连续 VAE 和离散 VQ 分词器联合优化，在编码阶段聚类相似 token 建立结构先验，同时为 VAE 分支提供语义对齐正则化、为 VQ 分支提供组内多样性与组间排他性约束，在 ImageNet-256 上取得了优于强基线 VAE 和 VQ 模型的重建与生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉分词器分为连续 VAE 和离散 VQ 两大流派，各有互补性局限：VAE 重建质量高但潜在表征稠密纠缠、不利于语义控制；VQ 模型支持自回归生成但存在梯度稀疏、训练不稳定和码本坍塌问题。作者希望设计一个统一架构，同时发挥两者的优势。

Method: MergeTok 采用编码器-解码器架构，在编码阶段引入 token merging 技术对相似 token 进行聚类合并，建立结构先验。该先验提供双重监督信号：(1) 在 VAE 分支中施加合并 token 的语义对齐约束，正则化潜在空间使其具有解耦的语义感知表征；(2) 导出分组约束，促进组内多样性和组间排他性，稳定 VQ 训练。最终输出同时支持连续和离散两种 token 形式。

Result: 在 ImageNet-256 上，MergeTok 在相同 token 预算下取得了比强 VAE 和 VQ 模型更低的 rFID，重建和生成性能均具有竞争力，且产生的 token 表征具有语义组织结构，兼容自回归和扩散两种生成器。

Conclusion: MergeTok 证明单一架构可以赋予视觉分词器鲁棒的语义组织能力和生成器友好的离散性，为统一视觉表征学习提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MergeTok，统一了连续 VAE 和离散 VQ 两种视觉分词器，属于视觉 token 表征的统一化工作，与 multimodal_generation 方向下的现有 subtopic "统一多模态模型"（理解与生成一体化、统一 token 表征）高度匹配，因此复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Unifies continuous and discrete visual tokenization with token merging, directly advancing unified multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: unified tokenizer, token merging bridge, semantic alignment, autoregressive compatible, multimodal generation

Abstract: Most visual tokenizers for image generation are bifurcated into two families with complementary limitations: continuous VAEs offer high-fidelity reconstruction but suffer from dense, entangled latents that are poorly suited for semantic control, whereas discrete VQ-based models enable autoregressive generation yet struggle with gradient sparsity, unstable training, and codebook collapse. In this work, we introduce MergeTok, a unified tokenizer that jointly optimizes continuous (VAE) and discrete (VQ) tokenizers within a encoder-decoder architecture, leveraging token merging techniques as a semantic bridge. By clustering similar tokens during encoding, MergeTok establishes a structural prior that provides dual supervision signals: (i) it imposes merged-token semantic alignment in the VAE branch, regularizing its latent space toward disentangled, semantic-aware representations; (ii) it derives group-wise constraints, promoting intra-group diversity and inter-group exclusivity that stabilize VQ training. MergeTok shows competitive reconstruction and generation performance on ImageNet-256, with substantially lower rFID than strong VAE and VQ models under matched token budgets, while producing semantically-organized token representations compatible with both autoregressive and diffusion generators. This shows that a single architecture can endow visual tokenizers with robust semantic organization and generator-friendly discreteness.

</details>


### [87] [Lumos-Nexus: Efficient Frequency Bridging with Homogeneous Latent Space for Video Unified Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31603)
*Jiazheng Xing, Hangjie Yuan, Lingling Cai, Xinyu Liu, Yujie Wei, Fei Du, Hai Ci, Tao Feng, Jiasheng Tang, Weihua Chen, Fan Wang, Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Lumos-Nexus 提出一种训练高效的统一视频生成框架，采用两阶段设计：训练时仅对齐轻量生成器与理解模块，推理时通过渐进式频率桥接（UPFB）在共享潜空间中逐步过渡到高容量预训练生成器，实现粗到细的精细生成，在保持推理质量的同时显著提升视觉保真度。同时提出 VR-Bench 基准评估推理驱动的视频生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于连接器的视频统一模型在指令驱动的视频合成方面表现良好，但将大型高保真生成器集成到统一训练循环中计算成本过高，限制了可实现的视觉质量。因此需要一种训练高效的方案，既能保持推理驱动的语义控制能力，又能显著提升生成视频的视觉保真度。

Method: Lumos-Nexus 采用两阶段设计：(1) 训练阶段，仅将轻量生成器与理解模块对齐，学习接收推理驱动的语义控制；(2) 推理阶段，提出统一渐进式频率桥接（UPFB），在共享潜空间中逐步将生成任务过渡到高容量预训练生成器，实现粗到细的精细化生成。同时构建 VR-Bench 基准来评估推理驱动的视频生成能力。

Result: 在 VBench 上，Lumos-Nexus 在视觉真实感和时序连贯性方面取得了显著提升；在 VR-Bench 上展示了强大的基于推理的生成性能。

Conclusion: Lumos-Nexus 通过训练-推理解耦的两阶段设计和渐进式频率桥接策略，有效解决了统一视频模型中高保真生成与训练效率之间的矛盾，为构建兼具强推理能力和高视觉质量的统一视频生成模型提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Lumos-Nexus，一个连接理解模块与生成模块的统一视频生成框架，属于理解与生成一体化的统一多模态模型方向。multimodal_generation 和 multimodal_understanding_generation 两个方向下均有 unified_multimodal_model 这个现有 subtopic，且论文核心正是统一视频理解与生成，因此复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Proposes a training-efficient unified video generation framework with frequency bridging, directly addressing the user's priority on unified multimodal models and efficient inference. Signals: unified multimodal model, efficient inference, video generation, frequency bridging, VR-Bench benchmark

Abstract: Connector-based video unified models have demonstrated strong capability in instruction-grounded video synthesis, but integrating a large high-fidelity generator into the unified training loop is computationally prohibitive, limiting achievable visual quality. We therefore propose Lumos-Nexus, a training-efficient unified video generation framework that facilitates the development of strong reasoning-driven generation capabilities while significantly enhancing visual fidelity. Lumos-Nexus adopts a two-stage design: 1) During training, only a lightweight generator is aligned with the understanding block to learn to take in reasoning-driven semantic control. 2) During inference, we introduce Unified Progressive Frequency Bridging (UPFB) to progressively hand off generation to a high-capacity pretrained generator in the shared latent space, enabling coarse-to-fine refinement and producing high-fidelity videos without compromising reasoning quality. To fill the gap in reasoning-driven video generation benchmarks, we introduce VR-Bench, which assesses a model's capability to translate inferred intent into coherent and semantically aligned video content. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Lumos-Nexus achieves substantial gains in visual realism and temporal coherence on VBench, while exhibiting strong reasoning-based generative performance on VR-Bench. Code and models are available at .

</details>

### [88] [Physically Viable World Models: A Case for Query-Conditioned Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30542)
*Adam J. Thorpe, Stepan Tretiakov, Cheng-Hsi Hsiao, Su Ann Low, Xingjian Li, Hassan Iqbal, Neel P. Bhatt, Ufuk Topcu, Krishna Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (92.63) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文指出现有观测预测型世界模型在具身AI中会产生视觉合理但物理错误的推演，提出具身AI需要"物理可行的世界模型"——即能够识别回答干预查询所需的最简物理抽象，并模块化组合环境表征、状态估计、动作规范、干预动力学和查询级响应等组件，由自主编排器动态组装。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型仅预测未来观测，可能产生视觉合理但物理错误的推演，导致推荐不可行动作、误判交互结果或认证不安全行为。不同物理系统可能外观相同但在干预下表现迥异，因此需要物理可行的世界模型。

Method: 提出物理可行世界模型的设计原则：模型应识别回答干预查询所需的最简物理抽象，包含模块化组件（环境表征、潜在状态与参数估计、动作规范、干预动力学、查询级响应），由自主编排器根据查询动态组合兼容的学习组件和结构化组件。转移模型可以是解析、模拟、学习或混合形式，但必须保留决定干预结果的结构。

Result: 通过控制基准实验（固定可见场景、变化潜在物理参数）暴露了现有模型的失败案例，并展示了所提方法在现有系统无法正确回答的查询上的有效性。

Conclusion: 正确的抽象不是最详细的世界模型，而是保留查询相关区分的最简模型。该分解使模型可解释、组件可验证、输出可审计，并为新世界模型的设计和现有模型的可行性测试提供了设计原则。

Classification reason: 该论文讨论具身AI的世界模型，强调物理可行性、干预查询与动作结果建模，属于视频世界模型（video_world_model）这一规范子主题的范畴。

Importance reason: Directly addresses a critical failure of video world models and proposes a modular, query-conditional framework that aligns with your high-priority subtopics. Signals: world model, video world model, embodied AI, physical viability, intervention query

Abstract: World models for embodied AI must be physically viable: constructed to answer intervention queries by representing the physical structure governing action outcomes, rather than merely predicting future observations. Existing observation-predictive world models can produce visually plausible but physically wrong rollouts. This failure is structural; distinct physical systems can look identical yet diverge under intervention. We expose this problem with controlled benchmarks that fix the visible scene while varying latent physics. We show that such models may recommend infeasible actions, mispredict interaction outcomes, or certify unsafe behavior. We argue that embodied AI requires world models that identify the simplest physical abstraction sufficient to answer an intervention query. Such a model comprises modular components, including environment representation, latent state and parameter estimation, action specification, interventional dynamics, and query-level response. An autonomous orchestrator should identify the relevant abstraction and compose compatible learned and structured components per query. When closed-form physics is unavailable, uncertain, or costly, the transition model may be analytic, simulated, learned, or hybrid, but it must preserve the structure that determines interventional outcomes. This decomposition makes the model interpretable, its components verifiable, and its outputs auditable against the query. It also provides a design principle for new world models and a feasibility test for existing ones: the right abstraction is not the most detailed model of the world, but the simplest model that preserves the distinctions relevant to the query. We demonstrate this approach on queries that existing systems fail to answer correctly, and outline how an orchestrator can dynamically assemble and adapt physically viable models for planning, control, and verification.

</details>


### [89] [Robust Dreamer: Deviation-Aware Latent Gaussian Memory for Action-Controlled AR Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30855)
*Hanlin Chen, Jiaxin Wei, Xibin Song, Yifu Wang, Steve Wang, Hongdong Li, Pan Ji, Gim Hee Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (95.7) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Robust Dreamer 提出了一种记忆增强框架用于动作控制的 AR 视频生成，通过 Latent Gaussian Memory（在潜空间用高斯原语锚定扩散潜变量并通过高斯泼溅召回）和 Deviation Learning（通过动态偏差存档模拟推理时的记忆退化）来解决长程自回归生成中的视觉漂移和 3D 不一致问题，在 ScanNet、DL3DV 和 OmniWorldGame 上取得了 SOTA 长程生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帧级动作控制的图像到视频生成是实现交互式世界模拟的有前景范式，但现有 3D 感知方法在长程自回归生成中面临两大问题：(1) Latent-RGB Cycling 导致信息损失——生成的潜变量反复解码为 RGB 再重新编码用于后续条件化，VAE 转换累积退化；(2) 训练-推理 gap——训练时使用干净记忆，推理时记忆被预测误差污染，导致灾难性漂移。

Method: (1) Latent Gaussian Memory：将生成过程中继承的扩散潜变量锚定到高斯原语上，通过潜空间的高斯泼溅（Gaussian Splatting）召回，提供密集、几何感知、视角对齐的条件化信息，避免反复 VAE 转换的累积退化。(2) Deviation Learning with Dynamic Deviation Archive：通过一步近似合成自回归 rollout 导致的潜变量偏差，按自回归阶段和去噪时间戳存储，在训练时注入历史记忆，使生成器暴露于真实的退化记忆状态并学会内部修正。

Result: 在 ScanNet、DL3DV 和 OmniWorldGame 三个数据集上的实验表明，Robust Dreamer 在长程视频生成任务上取得了最先进的性能，有效缓解了视觉漂移和 3D 不一致问题。

Conclusion: Robust Dreamer 通过 Latent Gaussian Memory 和 Deviation Learning 两个核心设计，有效解决了动作控制 AR 视频生成中的长期漂移问题，为交互式世界模拟提供了更鲁棒的生成框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出动作控制的图像到视频生成框架用于交互式世界模拟，属于视频世界模型方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视频世界模型" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses autoregressive video generation with world model capabilities, a top priority subtopic, and introduces novel memory solutions for long-horizon consistency. Signals: autoregressive video generation, world model, action-controlled generation, 3D consistency, memory-augmented framework

Abstract: Frame-wise action-controlled image-to-video generation is a promising paradigm for interactive world simulation, where each control signal should elicit an immediate visual response. However, maintaining visual fidelity and 3D consistency over long autoregressive rollouts remains challenging. Existing 3D-aware methods often suffer from catastrophic drift due to two impediments: information loss from \textit{Latent--RGB Cycling}, where generated latents are repeatedly decoded to RGB and re-encoded for future conditioning, and the training--inference gap induced by the \textit{error-free hypothesis}, where clean training memory fails to match prediction-corrupted inference memory. To address these challenges, we present \textbf{Robust Dreamer}, a memory-augmented framework built around how to design 3D memory and how to use it robustly. First, we introduce \textbf{Latent Gaussian Memory}, which anchors diffusion latents inherited from the generation process to Gaussian primitives and recalls them via latent-space Gaussian splatting. This provides dense, geometry-aware, view-aligned conditioning while avoiding accumulated degradation from repeated VAE conversion. Second, we propose \textbf{Deviation Learning with Dynamic Deviation Archive}, which synthesizes rollout-induced latent deviations through a one-step approximation, stores them by autoregressive stage and denoising timestamp, and injects them into historical memory during training. This exposes the generator to realistic corrupted memory states and teaches internal correction before inference. Experiments on ScanNet, DL3DV, and OmniWorldGame demonstrate state-of-the-art long-horizon performance.

</details>


### [90] [Light Interaction: Training-Free Inference Acceleration for Interactive Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31158)
*Jiacheng Lu, Haoyi Zhu, Sipei Yi, Enze Xie, Yu Li, Cheng Zhuo*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (96.2) - Top 2 精读候选

TL;DR: Light Interaction 是一种无需训练的交互式视频世界模型推理加速框架，通过自适应上下文管理、去噪缓存加速和3D块稀疏注意力，实现高达2.59倍加速，同时保持视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式视频世界模型在实时游戏模拟、虚拟场景导航和具身AI训练中具有重要应用，但长交互轨迹的推理成本极高，主要瓶颈包括上下文记忆增长、二次注意力复杂度和重复去噪步骤。现有方法缺乏针对交互场景特点的加速方案。

Method: 提出 Light Interaction 框架，核心洞察是交互过程天然支持轨迹依赖的自适应计算：1) 自适应上下文管理：探索新区域时丢弃检索到的空间记忆，根据局部潜在动态调整时间上下文；2) 去噪缓存加速：当相机回到熟悉区域时复用早期步骤的模型输出；3) 硬件-软件协同设计的3D块稀疏注意力，使用融合Triton内核实现高效计算。

Result: 在 HY-WorldPlay 和 Matrix-Game-3.0 基准上，Light Interaction 实现高达2.59倍加速，无需模型重训练，同时保持有竞争力的视觉质量。

Conclusion: Light Interaction 证明了利用交互式世界模型的轨迹依赖特性进行自适应计算可以有效加速推理，为交互式视频生成的实际部署提供了可行的训练无关方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出交互式视频世界模型的推理加速框架，属于视频世界模型方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视频世界模型" (video_world_model) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses the user's high-priority topic of video world models with a practical, training-free acceleration method. Signals: video world model, training-free acceleration, interactive video generation, adaptive context management, denoising cache

Abstract: Interactive video world models generate video chunk by chunk in response to user-controlled camera movements, enabling applications such as real-time game simulation, virtual scene navigation, and embodied AI training. However, scaling to long interactive trajectories is prohibitively expensive due to growing context memory, quadratic attention complexity, and repeated denoising steps. We present Light Interaction, a training-free inference acceleration framework for interactive video world models. Our key insight is that interaction naturally enables trajectory-dependent adaptive computation: retrieved spatial memory can be discarded during novel exploration, temporal context can be adjusted according to local latent dynamics, and early-step model outputs can be reused when the camera revisits familiar regions. Based on this insight, Light Interaction combines adaptive context management, denoising cache acceleration, and hardware-software co-designed 3D block sparse attention with fused Triton kernels. Evaluated on HY-WorldPlay and Matrix-Game-3.0, Light Interaction achieves up to 2.59x speedup without model retraining while maintaining competitive visual quality.

</details>


### [91] [Probabilistic Precipitation Nowcasting with Rectified Flow Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31204)
*Johannes Schusterbauer, Jannik Wiese, Nick Stracke, Timy Phan, Björn Ommer*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (81.27) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 FREUD（Frame-wise Encoder and United Decoder），一种基于整流流变换器的概率降水临近预报模型。通过逐帧编码保持不确定性，统一视频解码器保证时序一致性，在 SEVIR 基准上取得 SOTA 性能，并展示了模型与测试时扩展的进一步收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的天气临近预报方法依赖确定性压缩来降低高维天气数据的复杂度，这限制了模型在解码过程中捕捉不确定性的能力，尤其对极端天气事件的高变异性解码表现不佳。

Method: 提出 FREUD 框架：1）逐帧编码器（Frame-wise Encoder）对每个雷达帧独立编码，保留逐帧不确定性；2）统一视频解码器（United Decoder）确保时序一致性；3）基于整流流（Rectified Flow）的潜空间变换器作为生成骨干，通过集成（ensembling）捕获偶然不确定性。

Result: 在 SEVIR 基准上取得降水临近预报的 SOTA 性能，并通过模型缩放和测试时计算扩展进一步提升了性能。

Conclusion: 不确定性保持的编码策略结合整流流变换器能有效提升天气临近预报的精度和可靠性，尤其对极端天气事件具有更好的建模能力。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于整流流变换器的降水临近预报（precipitation nowcasting），属于视频生成式世界模型/物理世界建模范畴。论文将天气雷达数据视为时空视频序列，使用视频扩散/流匹配模型进行高分辨率短临预报，与 canonical subtopic "视频世界模型"（video_world_model）中"视频生成式世界模型、物理世界建模"的描述高度吻合。

Importance reason: Directly advances video world models for weather nowcasting with a novel uncertainty-preserving rectified flow transformer, achieving SOTA on SEVIR. Signals: rectified flow transformer, video world model, probabilistic nowcasting, uncertainty-preserving encoding, model scaling benefits

Abstract: Accurate weather forecasts are essential across various domains and are safety-critical in extreme weather conditions. Compared to simulation-based forecasting, data-driven approaches show greater efficiency, enabling short-term, high-resolution nowcasting. In particular, diffusion models proved effective in weather nowcasting due to their strong probabilistic foundation. However, existing methods rely on deterministic compression to reduce the complexity of high-dimensional weather data, limiting their ability to capture uncertainty in the decoding process. In this work, we introduce $\textbf{FREUD}$, a $\textbf{Fr}$ame-wise $\textbf{E}$ncoder and $\textbf{U}$nited $\textbf{D}$ecoder model based on rectified flow transformers for efficient compression of spatio-temporal weather data. Frame-wise encoding enables continuous forecast updates, while the unified video decoder ensures temporal consistency. Our uncertainty-preserving first stage allows us to capture aleatoric uncertainty via ensembling, which is particularly beneficial for extreme weather events with high decoding variability. We achieve state-of-the-art performance in precipitation nowcasting with a compact latent-space rectified flow transformer on the SEVIR benchmark and show further performance gains by model and test-time scaling. Code available here:

</details>


### [92] [PatchWorld: Gradient-Free Optimization of Executable World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30880)
*Jiaxin Bai, Yue Guo, Yifei Dong, Jiaxuan Xiong, Tianshi Zheng, Yixia Li, Tianqing Fang, Yufei Li, Yisen Gao, Haoyu Huang, Zhongwei Xie, Hong Ting Tsang, Zihao Wang, Lihui Liu, Jeff Pan, Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (88.17) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PatchWorld 是一个无需梯度的框架，通过反例引导的代码修复，将离线轨迹转化为可执行的 Python 世界模型，实现部分可观测环境下的符号化信念状态预测与规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本智能体环境通常建模为 POMDP，但缺乏将可执行代码作为世界模型进行预测和规划的研究。黑盒模型难以解释和调试，因此需要一种可检查、可重放、可局部修补的符号化世界模型。

Method: PatchWorld 框架通过反例引导的代码修复（counterexample-guided code repair），将离线轨迹转化为可执行的 Python 世界模型。它诱导符号化的信念状态程序，其动作更新可以被检查、重放和局部修补，无需梯度优化，且世界模型预测模块内部不调用 LLM。

Result: 在七个 AgentGym 环境中，PatchWorld-Simple 在基于代码的规划评分中达到最高，单步前瞻的宏平均成功率为 76.4%。实验还发现人类指定的残差记忆偏差能改善表面观测保真度，但会削弱决策效用，揭示了可执行世界模型中观测保真度与动作判别动力学之间的权衡。

Conclusion: PatchWorld 证明了通过反例引导的代码修复可以从离线轨迹中诱导出可执行的世界模型，在无需 LLM 调用的情况下实现有效的规划。同时揭示了可执行世界模型中观测保真度与决策效用之间的内在权衡。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 PatchWorld，通过离线轨迹诱导可执行的 Python 世界模型，用于部分可观测环境下的预测与规划，属于世界模型（world model）范畴。现有 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视频世界模型"（video_world_model）涵盖生成式世界模型、可交互模拟器等，虽然本文聚焦于文本智能体环境而非视频，但世界模型的核心概念完全匹配，且该 canonical 主题描述中包含"可交互模拟器"等广义世界模型概念，因此复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Directly addresses executable world models for planning, a top priority subtopic with high weight. Signals: world model, executable code, gradient-free, counterexample-guided repair, planning under partial observability

Abstract: Text-agent environments are typically modeled as partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs), assuming that the simulator's latent state and transition dynamics are hidden from the agent. Yet little work has examined whether executable code can be induced to serve as a world model for prediction and planning under partial observability. We introduce PatchWorld, a gradient-free framework that turns offline trajectories into executable Python world models through counterexample-guided code repair. Instead of predicting the next observation with a black-box model, PatchWorld induces symbolic belief-state programs whose action updates can be inspected, replayed, and locally patched. Across seven AgentGym environments, PatchWorld-Simple achieves the highest code-based planning score among evaluated methods, reaching 76.4\% macro success in live one-step lookahead while invoking no LLM calls inside the world-model prediction module itself. We further find that a human-specified residual-memory bias improves surface observation fidelity but weakens decision utility. This exposes a tradeoff in executable world models, since improving observation fidelity can come at the expense of action-discriminative dynamics, and vice versa. Code is available at .

</details>


### [93] [DecMem: Towards Minute-Long Consistent World Generation with Decoupled Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31336)
*Zhenhao Yang, Xiaoshi Wu, Zhengyao Lv, Xiaoyu Shi, Xintao Wang, Pengfei Wan, Kun Gai, Kwan-Yee K. Wong*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (97.15) - Top 1 精读候选

TL;DR: 本文提出 DecMem，一种解耦记忆架构，通过稀疏全局记忆（Sparse Global Memory）和锚定局部记忆（Anchored Local Memory）解决长时程世界生成中的注意力分散和计算效率问题，实现分钟级一致可控长视频生成，显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型在长时程推理下难以保持细粒度时空一致性，显式3D记忆和粗粒度帧级隐式建模均存在局限。可学习的记忆架构在长时程外推中面临计算效率低下和注意力分散（attention dispersion）两大根本性问题。

Method: 提出 DecMem 解耦记忆架构：(1) Sparse Global Memory（稀疏全局记忆）—— 对全局历史进行高效细粒度访问；(2) Anchored Local Memory（锚定局部记忆）—— 提供稳定高质量的外推。通过系统分析注意力分散现象，设计解耦结构分别处理全局和局部信息。

Result: 大量实验表明 DecMem 显著优于当前最先进方法，实现了分钟级可控长视频生成，具有高保真度和一致性。

Conclusion: DecMem 通过精确高效的长时记忆和优越的外推能力，有效解决了长时程世界生成中的一致性问题，为分钟级可控长视频生成提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DecMem，一种用于长时程一致世界生成的解耦记忆架构，实现分钟级可控长视频生成。论文明确聚焦于可控世界模型（controllable world model）和长视频生成，属于多模态生成方向下的 [CANONICAL] 视频世界模型（video_world_model）子主题。

Importance reason: Directly addresses the user's top-priority video world model and long video generation with a novel memory architecture. Signals: video world model, autoregressive video generation, efficient inference, long video generation, controllable generation

Abstract: Recent advances in video generative models have promoted rapid progress in controllable world models. However, maintaining fine-grained spatio-temporal consistency under long-horizon reasoning remains a key challenge. In this work, we move beyond explicit 3D memory and coarse frame-level implicit modeling, and propose a fine-grained, learnable, and scalable memory for consistent world generation. We first identify two fundamental limitations of naïve learnable memory architectures in long-horizon extrapolation, namely computational inefficiency and attention dispersion. Through a systematic analysis of attention dispersion, we propose DecMem, a decoupled memory architecture that employs Sparse Global Memory for efficient fine-grained access to global history and Anchored Local Memory for stable and high-quality extrapolation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DecMem significantly outperforms current state-of-the-art methods. By ensuring precise and efficient long-term memory and achieving superior extrapolation capabilities, DecMem enables minute-level controllable long video generation with high fidelity and consistency.

</details>


### [94] [Subspace-Decomposed JEPAs: Disentangling Progression and Content in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31111)
*Lucas Thil, Jesse Read, Rim Kaddah, Guillaume Doquet*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (92.63) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出SD-JEPA，将JEPA世界模型的潜空间分解为正交的进度子空间（低维，由余弦间隔三元组损失塑造）和内容子空间（高维，由SIGReg正则化），两者抗坍缩力作用于不相交的坐标上。该方法在控制基准上优于LeWM基线，且1D角度进度坐标可作为场景感知的"罗盘"，在任务进度跟踪、回溯检测、扰动重定位等方面优于传统预测误差标量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA世界模型学习紧凑的潜空间表征，但没有任何单一坐标被指定编码任务进度。这导致模型难以区分"发生了什么变化"（进度）和"变化的是什么"（内容），限制了其在规划、异常检测和场景理解中的能力。

Method: 将JEPA潜空间分解为两个正交子空间：低维进度子空间（使用余弦间隔三元组损失塑造，使角度坐标随任务进度单调变化）和高维内容子空间（使用SIGReg目标正则化）。证明两种抗坍缩力作用于不相交坐标，因此加性组合而非竞争。在多个控制基准上评估，并设计了子空间消融验证器来确认分解的有效性。

Result: SD-JEPA在大多数控制基准上优于LeWM基线，在Push-T上优于最强的非LeWM JEPA基线。1D角度进度坐标在40个保留立方体片段上，|Δθ_t|在定位语义事件方面比标准潜预测误差惊喜度高出最多+0.18 pooled AUROC（±1步容忍度下97.5%每片段胜率）。跨4个环境的线性探针显示，仅占潜空间4.2%的8维进度子空间解释了72-95%的任务进度方差。

Conclusion: SD-JEPA通过潜空间分解有效解耦了任务进度与内容信息，提供了比传统预测误差更丰富的场景感知信号。该方法不仅提升了控制任务的规划性能，还提供了可解释的进度感知能力，为世界模型在具身智能和机器人领域的应用提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出SD-JEPA，一种基于联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）的潜空间世界模型，将潜空间分解为进度子空间和内容子空间，用于控制任务中的规划与状态表征。这属于视频世界模型（video_world_model）范畴，该[CANONICAL] subtopic涵盖世界模型、可交互模拟器、物理世界建模等方向，与本论文高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances video world models with a principled subspace decomposition that improves planning and yields a novel scene-aware progress compass. Signals: world model, video world model, JEPA, latent decomposition, task progression

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) learn compact latent world models by predicting future embeddings, but no single coordinate of the latent is designated to encode task progression. We carve the JEPA latent into two orthogonal subspaces with disjoint roles: a low-dimensional progression subspace shaped by a cosine-margin triplet loss, and a high-dimensional content subspace regularised by the existing SIGReg objective of LeWM. We prove that the two anti-collapse forces act on disjoint coordinates, so they compose additively rather than competing on the same dimensions. Our method, SD-JEPA improves over the LeWM baseline on the majority of its control benchmarks at matched compute, and outperforms the strongest non-LeWM JEPA baseline on Push-T; a subspace-ablation falsifier confirms the split is the load-bearing ingredient. Beyond planning, the resulting 1-D angular progression coordinate functions as a scene-aware compass on the latent. It advances with task progress, regresses when the agent backtracks, and under controlled perturbations both spikes and relocalises to a semantically appropriate new task-phase sector, separating the moment of surprise from its meaning in a way that prediction-error scalars cannot. Three quantitative tests back this up: $|\Delta\theta_t|$ outperforms the standard latent-prediction-error surprise at localising semantic events on 40 held-out cube episodes by up to +0.18 pooled AUROC (97.5% per-episode win rate at $\pm 1$-step tolerance); a within-episode linear probe across all four environments (40 episodes per env) shows the 8-dimensional progression subspace (4.2% of the latent) explains 72-95% of task-progress variance..

</details>

### [95] [Towards Streaming Synchronized Spatial Audio Generation via Autoregressive Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30940)
*Ke Lei, Yu Zhang, Changhao Pan, Xueyi Pu, Wenxiang Guo, Ruiqi Li, Zhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.SD, eess.AS, cs.MM

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 音频与多模态联合生成

Importance: 值得关注 (73.17) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SwanSphere 是一个统一的流式框架，采用因果自回归扩散 Transformer 架构，结合空间视频-音频对比学习（SVAC）和多目标在线直接偏好优化（ODPO），实现从全景视频和文本提示实时生成高质量同步空间音频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间音频合成技术在生成质量与推理延迟之间存在权衡，且难以从多模态输入中准确捕获空间信息，缺乏实时、高质量的空间音频生成方案。

Method: 1) 提出因果自回归扩散 Transformer 架构，支持流式高质量空间音频生成；2) 设计空间视频-音频对比学习（SVAC）策略对齐视频编码器与声学域，并采用多目标在线直接偏好优化（ODPO）增强空间感知能力；3) 开发自动化标注流水线生成详细空间描述以缓解数据稀缺问题。

Result: 在视频到空间音频和文本到空间音频两个任务上均取得了优于现有方法的性能，展示了高质量的同步空间音频生成效果。

Conclusion: SwanSphere 通过因果自回归扩散 Transformer 和对比学习-偏好优化联合训练，有效解决了空间音频生成的实时性与质量权衡问题，为沉浸式空间音频生成提供了统一的流式解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出全景视频和文本提示驱动的流式空间音频生成框架，属于音频与多模态联合生成方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "audio_av_generation（音频与多模态联合生成）" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with autoregressive video generation and diffusion transformer priorities, with high boost from autoregressive and video generation keywords. Signals: autoregressive diffusion transformer, spatial audio generation, multimodal generation, streaming generation, video-to-audio

Abstract: Real-time and accurate spatial audio generation is pivotal for delivering an immersive experience. However, existing spatial audio synthesis technologies are often encumbered by a tradeoff between generation quality and high inference latency, as well as difficulty in capturing precise spatial information from multimodal inputs. To address these challenges, we propose SwanSphere, a unified streaming framework for high-fidelity spatial audio generation from panoramic videos and text prompts. SwanSphere mainly makes the following contributions: 1) We introduce a causal autoregressive diffusion transformer architecture that enables streaming high-quality spatial audio generation. 2) We design a Spatial Video-Audio Contrastive (SVAC) learning strategy to align the video encoder with the acoustic domain, and further employ a multi-objective online direct preference optimization (ODPO) scheme, resulting in strong spatial perception and robust multimodal spatial audio synthesis. 3) To alleviate the current scarcity of spatial audio datasets, we also develop an automated annotation pipeline for generating detailed spatial captions. Experimental results demonstrate that SwanSphere achieves superior performance in both video-to-spatial and text-to-spatial audio generation tasks. Demos can be found at: .

</details>


### [96] [Chatterbox-Flash: Prior-Calibrated Block Diffusion for Streaming Zero-Shot TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30748)
*Deokjin Seo, Gangin Park, Kihyun Nam*

Main category: eess.AS, cs.SD, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 音频与多模态联合生成

Importance: 普通相关 (57.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Chatterbox-Flash 通过将预训练的自回归 TTS 解码器微调为块扩散解码器，实现零样本 TTS 的流式并行生成。针对离散语音 token 的长尾分布导致质量下降的问题，提出先验校准评分和早期解码调度两种推理时技术，在保持流式推理的同时达到与强基线相当的高保真合成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归 TTS 模型生成质量高但推理速度慢（逐 token 生成），非自回归模型速度快但难以支持流式推理。现有块扩散解码方法在离散语音 token 上直接迁移会导致质量下降，因为长尾 token 分布使并行位置选择偏向高频 token。本文旨在实现既支持流式推理又具有高生成速度的零样本 TTS 模型。

Method: 将预训练的自回归 TTS 解码器微调为块扩散解码器（block-diffusion decoder），在块内并行生成 token 的同时保持逐块流式输出。提出两种推理时技术：(1) 先验校准评分（prior-calibrated scoring），减去块级边际 token 分布以纠正长尾偏差；(2) 早期解码调度（early-decoding schedule），基于校准置信度自适应终止迭代，减少不必要的扩散步数。

Result: 在标准零样本 TTS 基准上，Chatterbox-Flash 达到了与强自回归和非自回归基线相当的高保真合成质量。流式推理的首包时间与流式自回归系统相当，实时因子显著更低。

Conclusion: 通过先验校准和早期解码调度，块扩散解码可以成功应用于离散语音 token 生成，在保持流式能力的同时实现并行加速，为零样本 TTS 提供了一种高效且高质量的方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出零样本文本到语音（TTS）模型，属于音频生成方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "音频与多模态联合生成"（audio_av_generation）。

Importance reason: Focuses on TTS streaming efficiency, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's video/image generation and world model interests. Signals: streaming zero-shot TTS, block diffusion decoder, prior-calibrated scoring, early-decoding schedule, discrete speech token long-tail issue

Abstract: We present Chatterbox-Flash, a zero-shot text-to-speech model obtained by fine-tuning a pretrained autoregressive TTS decoder into a block-diffusion decoder, enabling parallel token generation within each block while retaining block-by-block streaming. We find that naively transferring mainstream block-diffusion decoding to discrete speech tokens degrades quality, as a long-tail token distribution biases parallel position selection toward a few high-frequency tokens. To mitigate this without architectural modification, we introduce two inference-time techniques: prior-calibrated scoring, which subtracts the block-level marginal token distribution, and an early-decoding schedule, which adaptively terminates iteration based on calibrated confidence. On standard zero-shot TTS benchmarks, Chatterbox-Flash attains high-fidelity synthesis comparable to strong autoregressive and non-autoregressive baselines, while supporting streaming inference with time-to-first-packet on par with streaming AR systems and substantially lower real-time factor. Code and audio samples are available at .

</details>


### [97] [ImmersiveTTS: Environment-Aware Text-to-Speech with Multimodal Diffusion Transformer and Domain-Specific Representation Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30965)
*Jun-Hak Yun, Seung-Bin Kim, Seong-Whan Lee*

Main category: eess.AS, cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 音频与多模态联合生成

Importance: 普通相关 (54.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ImmersiveTTS 提出了一种环境感知的文本到语音（TTS）模型，利用多模态扩散 Transformer 和领域特定表征对齐，在生成语音的同时自然地融入环境音频上下文，实现了更高的自然度、可懂度和音频保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本引导音频生成在音效、语音、音乐等领域取得了进展，但联合生成语音与环境音频仍然具有挑战性，因为两者的声学模式和时序动态存在固有差异。本文旨在生成与环境上下文无缝融合的自然语音。

Method: 基于多模态扩散 Transformer，通过联合注意力机制融合文本对齐的语音潜变量与文本条件化的环境上下文；引入领域特定表征对齐目标，利用语音和音频编码器的互补自监督表征增强语义一致性。

Result: 在客观指标和人类听力测试中，ImmersiveTTS 在自然度、可懂度和音频保真度上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: ImmersiveTTS 通过显式建模跨模态交互和领域特定表征对齐，有效实现了环境感知的语音生成，为沉浸式 TTS 提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出环境感知的文本到语音生成模型（TTS），融合语音与环境音频的跨模态交互，属于音频生成与多模态联合生成范畴，与 canonical subtopic "audio_av_generation" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel multimodal diffusion transformer for environment-aware TTS, but outside core video/image generation and world model priorities. Signals: multimodal diffusion transformer, domain-specific representation alignment, environment-aware TTS, cross-modal speech-audio generation, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Recent advancements in text-guided audio generation have yielded promising results in diverse domains, including sound effects, speech, and music. However, jointly generating speech with environmental audio remains challenging due to the inherent disparities in their acoustic patterns and temporal dynamics. We propose ImmersiveTTS, an environment-aware text-to-speech (TTS) model that generates natural speech seamlessly integrated within environmental contexts by explicitly modeling cross-modal interactions. Our model builds on a multimodal diffusion transformer and fuses transcript-aligned speech latent with text-conditioned environmental context via joint attention. To enhance semantic consistency, we introduce a domain-specific representation alignment objective tailored to environment-aware TTS, leveraging complementary self-supervised representations from speech and audio encoders. Experimental results show that ImmersiveTTS achieves higher naturalness, intelligibility, and audio fidelity than existing approaches across objective metrics and human listening tests.

</details>


### [98] [Latent Space Disentanglement via Activation Steering for Interpretable Attribute Control in Symbolic Music Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31295)
*Ioannis Prokopiou, Pantelis Vikatos, Maximos Kaliakatsos-Papakostas, Theodoros Giannakopoulos, Themos Stafylakis*

Main category: cs.IR, cs.SD, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 音频与多模态联合生成

Importance: 普通相关 (49.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于激活引导（Activation Steering）的推理时干预框架，利用 Difference-in-Means 方法从 Multitrack Music Transformer 的残差流中解耦出音高（Pitch）和时长（Duration）的潜在方向，并通过 Gram-Schmidt 正交化实现多属性独立控制，无需重新训练即可实现可解释的符号音乐生成属性调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 在符号音乐生成中取得了显著进展，但缺乏细粒度、可解释的属性控制能力。现有方法通常需要重新训练或引入额外模块，无法在推理时灵活调节生成属性（如音高、时长），且多属性之间存在特征纠缠问题。

Method: 采用 Difference-in-Means (DiffMean) 方法从 MMT 残差流中提取音高和时长的线性方向，验证线性表征假设；提出 Dual Steering 框架，利用 Gram-Schmidt 正交化解耦多属性方向，实现独立控制。

Result: 实验表明，引导强度与属性偏移之间存在高相关性，验证了线性表征假设；Gram-Schmidt 正交化相比朴素向量加法显著减少了概念干扰和信号退化，实现了对音高和时长的独立确定性控制，即使在强自回归条件下依然有效。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在符号音乐生成模型中可以通过推理时激活引导实现可解释的属性控制，Gram-Schmidt 正交化有效解决了多属性特征纠缠问题，为音乐生成的可控性和可解释性提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 论文研究符号音乐生成中的可解释属性控制，属于音乐生成范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 audio_av_generation（音频与多模态联合生成）子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on symbolic music generation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative-keyword-filtered domains. Signals: activation steering, interpretable control, symbolic music, difference-in-means, gram-schmidt orthogonalization

Abstract: Transformer-based architectures have significantly advanced the generation of complex symbolic sequences, yet a significant gap remains in achieving fine-grained, interpretable control over discrete signal attributes. This paper investigates the mechanistic interpretability of the Multitrack Music Transformer (MMT) and proposes a framework for deterministic attribute modulation without retraining to bridge this gap via inference-time activation steering. Utilizing the Difference-in-Means (DiffMean) methodology, we isolate latent directions for signal attributes, specifically Pitch and Duration, within the residual stream. We validate the Linear Representation Hypothesis in this domain, achieving high correlation between steering magnitude and attribute shift. To address the inherent feature entanglement in multi-attribute steering, we introduce a Dual Steering framework utilizing Gram-Schmidt Orthogonalization. Experimental results demonstrate that this geometric decoupling reduces conceptual interference and signal degradation compared to naive vector addition, enabling independent deterministic control even against strong autoregressive conditioning.

</details>

### [99] [Hide-and-Seek in Trajectories: Discovering Failure Signals for VLA Runtime Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30834)
*Seongheon Park, Wendi Li, Changdae Oh, Samuel Yeh, Zsolt Kira, Michael Hagenow, Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Hide-and-Seek框架，将VLA模型的故障检测建模为粗粒度监督学习问题，通过轨迹间和轨迹内对比学习，仅利用轨迹级标签即可定位故障指示性动作，无需逐步骤标注。在LIBERO、VLABench和真实机器人平台上，使用OpenVLA、π₀和π₀.₅三种策略，实现了多任务故障检测的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在真实部署中容易发生执行故障，现有故障检测方法要么依赖昂贵的动作重采样或外部模型，要么将轨迹级标签均匀传播到每个时间步，掩盖了局部故障信号。需要一种无需逐步骤标注、能精确定位故障时刻的检测方法。

Method: 将VLA故障检测建模为粗粒度监督学习问题，设计轨迹间对比学习（inter-trajectory contrastive）和轨迹内对比学习（intra-trajectory contrastive）两个目标函数。轨迹间对比拉近同类轨迹、推开异类轨迹；轨迹内对比在故障轨迹中区分故障时刻与正常时刻。结合两者，从轨迹级标签中诱导出具有时间结构的故障信号，无需步骤级标注。最后结合保形预测（conformal prediction）实现实用化的准确率-时效性权衡。

Result: 在LIBERO、VLABench和真实机器人平台上，使用OpenVLA、π₀和π₀.₅三种VLA策略进行评测，Hide-and-Seek在多任务故障检测中达到SOTA性能，在保形预测框架下实现了良好的准确率-时效性权衡，并泛化到未见过的任务。

Conclusion: Hide-and-Seek通过粗粒度对比学习有效解决了VLA故障检测中缺乏步骤级标注的问题，为具身系统的可靠部署提供了一种实用且高效的运行时监控方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLA模型在执行过程中的故障检测（failure detection），属于多模态模型在具身系统中的安全性与可靠性问题。现有[多模态安全与攻防]（multimodal_safety_attack）子主题涵盖多模态安全、防御与可靠性评测，该论文的故障检测可视为运行时安全监控的一种形式，因此映射至此子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLA failure detection, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: VLA failure detection, trajectory-level supervision, contrastive learning, robotics safety, no step-level annotation

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable robots to follow natural language instructions and generalize across diverse tasks, but they remain vulnerable to execution failures that compromise reliability in real-world deployment. Detecting such failures during execution is therefore critical for the robust deployment of embodied systems. Existing failure detection methods either rely on expensive action resampling or external models, while alternatives propagate trajectory-level labels uniformly across every timestep, obscuring localized failure signals. In this paper, we propose \textbf{Hide-and-Seek}, a framework that formulates VLA failure detection as a coarsely supervised learning problem. By combining inter-trajectory and intra-trajectory contrastive objectives, Hide-and-Seek localizes failure-indicative actions and induces temporally structured failure signals from trajectory-level supervision alone, without any step-level annotation. We evaluate Hide-and-Seek on LIBERO, VLABench, and a real-world robotic platform across three representative VLA policies: OpenVLA, $\pi_0$, and $\pi_{0.5}$.Our method achieves state-of-the-art multi-task failure detection performance with a practical accuracy--timeliness trade-off under conformal prediction, and generalizes well to both seen and unseen tasks.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_hallucination'></div>
