### [100] [Vision-Language Models Suppress Female Representations Under Ambiguous Input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31556)
*Arnau Marin-Llobet, Simon Henniger, Mahzarin R. Banaji*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CY, cs.CL, cs.HC, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (47.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现VLM在模糊性别输入（如全副武装的工人、背影）下，即使内部编码了女性关联，输出仍系统性偏向男性。作者提出LALS指标，通过将视觉token激活投影到文本嵌入空间来逐层测量概念关联，揭示了从编码到输出的不对称性别过滤机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法在性别清晰可见时能有效抑制VLM的性别偏差，但实际应用中大量输入是模糊的（如背影、全副武装的人物），这些情况下的模型行为尚未被充分研究。作者希望揭示VLM在模糊输入下的真实内部表征与输出之间的差异。

Method: 提出LALS（Latent Association Leaning Score），一种零样本度量方法，将视觉token的激活值投影到模型文本嵌入空间，逐token逐层测量概念关联强度。在15个职业、800+张性别模糊图像上对4个VLM进行实验，通过最小提示压力触发职业-性别默认关联，并进行逐层分析和颜色消融实验。

Result: 模型内部表征与输出系统性解耦：内部编码女性关联但输出为男性。逐层分析发现不对称过滤——男性信号从端到端持续放大，女性信号在中间层达到峰值后被抑制。颜色消融表明文化负载的视觉线索（如服装颜色）进一步调节内部关联。

Conclusion: VLM在模糊输入下存在系统性的性别表征偏差，即使模型内部"知道"女性关联，输出仍被抑制为男性。这揭示了当前对齐方法的局限性——它们主要处理清晰输入下的显式偏差，而模糊输入下的隐性偏差需要新的缓解策略。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLM在模糊输入下对性别表征的偏差——模型内部编码女性关联但输出被抑制为男性，属于多模态模型的事实性/可靠性/忠实性问题，与[多模态幻觉与可靠性]（multimodal_hallucination）高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on gender bias in VLMs, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to generation or efficiency. Signals: VLM bias analysis, ambiguous input study, LALS metric, gender representation, not aligned with user's generation/efficiency focus

Abstract: Alignment teaches vision-language models (VLMs) to avoid expressing demographic biases, and when gender is clearly visible they largely succeed. Far less is known about ambiguous inputs (a worker in full gear, a figure seen from behind) cases common in practice yet rarely studied. We find that minimal prompting pressure exposes occupation-gender defaults when prompting ambiguous input images, with models collapsing to male even for strongly female-stereotyped occupations. But do these outputs reflect what models actually encode internally? We introduce LALS (Latent Association Leaning Score), a zero-shot metric that projects visual-token activations into the model's text-embedding space to measure concept associations per token and layer. Across 15 occupations, over 800 gender-ambiguous images, and four VLMs, internal representations and outputs are systematically decoupled: models often encode a female association internally yet output male. Layer-wise analysis reveals an asymmetric filter -- male signal amplifies end-to-end while female signal peaks mid-network and is suppressed before generation -- and a color ablation shows that culturally loaded visual cues such as clothing color further modulate these internal associations.

</details>


### [101] [Seeing Isn't Knowing: Do VLMs Know When Not to Answer Spatial Questions (and Why)?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30557)
*Yue Zhang, Zun Wang, Han Lin, Yonatan Bitton, Idan Szpektor, Mohit Bansal*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (60.41) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出SpatialUncertain评测框架，系统研究VLM在空间推理中面对遮挡和视角歧义时的表现，发现模型普遍存在过度自信回答的失败模式，在遮挡下准确率约30%，视角歧义下低于10%，且难以识别哪些额外视角能提供可靠证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间推理基准通常假设视觉观察是充分且可靠的，只关注模型能否给出正确答案，而忽略了模型是否知道何时应该拒绝回答（abstention）以及需要哪些额外观察信息。然而真实环境中遮挡和视角歧义普遍存在，模型需要具备识别信息不足的能力。

Method: 构建SpatialUncertain控制评测框架，设计两类观察挑战：(1) 遮挡——隐藏目标信息；(2) 视角歧义——产生误导性视觉线索。对每种配置设计在干净观察下可回答、但在挑战下应拒绝回答的空间问题。进一步评估模型能否识别哪些额外视角能解决视角歧义。

Result: 在多种前沿开源和闭源VLM上的实验显示两个一致的失败模式：(1) 模型倾向于过度自信回答，遮挡下平均准确率约30%，视角歧义下低于10%；(2) 即使提供额外视角，部分模型在识别可靠证据视角上的表现接近随机。

Conclusion: 研究呼吁超越简单的答案正确性评估，转向评估模型是否知道何时应拒绝回答以及如何寻求可靠证据，这对VLM在真实环境中的可靠部署至关重要。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLM在空间推理中因遮挡和视角歧义导致过度自信回答的问题，核心关注模型何时应拒绝回答（abstention）以及如何寻求可靠证据，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性（[CANONICAL] subtopic）范畴。

Importance reason: Strongly addresses VLM reliability in spatial reasoning, a key aspect of multimodal understanding, but lacks direct alignment with top-priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: VLM spatial reasoning, overconfident answering, occlusion and perspective ambiguity, abstention evaluation, multimodal understanding generation

Abstract: Spatial reasoning is a fundamental capability for vision-language models (VLMs) deployed in real-world environments. However, visual observations are inherently limited representations of a 3D world: occlusion can render objects invisible, and perspective can make geometric properties misleading. Despite this, existing spatial reasoning benchmarks typically assume that observations are sufficient and reliable, focusing on whether models produce correct answers rather than whether they recognize when a question cannot be answered and what additional observations would be needed. In this work, we challenge this assumption by constructing a controlled evaluation framework, SpatialUncertain, and introducing two types of observation challenges: (1) occlusion, which hides target information, and (2) perspective ambiguity, which produces misleading visual cues. For each configuration, we design spatial questions that are answerable under clean observations but require abstention under the introduced challenges. We further evaluate whether models can identify which additional viewpoints would resolve perspective ambiguity. Our results across a diverse set of frontier open- and closed-source VLMs reveal two consistent failure modes. First, models are prone to overconfident answering, attempting to solve spatial reasoning tasks even when visual evidence is incomplete or misleading, with average accuracy around 30\% under occlusion and below 10\% under perspective ambiguity. Second, even when additional views are available, some models perform near random chance in identifying which would provide reliable evidence. Together, our findings call for moving beyond answer correctness toward evaluating whether models know when to abstain and how to seek reliable evidence.

</details>


### [102] [Immuno-VLM: Immunizing Large Vision-Language Models via Generative Semantic Antibodies for Open-World Trustworthiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30745)
*Xiang Fang, Wanlong Fang, Wei Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Immuno-VLM 受生物免疫负选择机制启发，利用大语言模型的生成推理能力主动生成"语义抗体"（近分布异常文本描述），以约束VLM的决策边界，解决开放世界部署中模型将未知异常强制归入已知类别的高置信度错误问题，在ImageNet-1K和四个OOD基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在开放世界部署中存在"语义傲慢"问题：由于缺乏显式的负知识，模型会将未知异常以高置信度强制匹配到已知类别中。传统开放集识别方法依赖被动密度估计或像素级异常生成，效率低且效果有限。

Method: 借鉴生物免疫系统中的负选择机制，利用LLM的生成推理能力主动"幻觉"出"语义抗体"——即近分布异常（如相似物、上下文异常）的文本描述，通过这些描述来约束VLM的决策空间，使模型能够识别并拒绝未知类别。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K和四个具有挑战性的OOD基准上，Immuno-VLM取得了新的SOTA结果。

Conclusion: 通过生物启发的免疫负选择机制与LLM生成推理的结合，可以有效提升VLM在开放世界中的可信度与鲁棒性，为开放集识别提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文关注VLM在开放世界部署中的可靠性问题（将未知异常强制分类为已知类别），提出通过LLM生成"语义抗体"（近分布异常文本描述）来增强VLM的开放世界可信度。这属于多模态幻觉与可靠性范畴，与[多模态幻觉与可靠性]（multimodal_hallucination）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on OOD robustness for VLMs, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like autoregressive video generation or world models. Signals: OOD detection, VLM trustworthiness, biological inspiration, generative semantic antibodies, not aligned with user's top weighted directions

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models have achieved unprecedented success in zero-shot recognition by aligning visual features with broad semantic concepts. However, this semantic abstraction creates a critical vulnerability in open-world deployment: the ``Hubris of Semantics'', where models force-fit unknown anomalies into known categories with high confidence due to the lack of explicit negative knowledge. To address this \textit{Open-World Trustworthiness Paradox}, we propose \textbf{Immuno-VLM}, a bio-inspired framework that adapts the biological principle of \textbf{Immunological Negative Selection} to high-dimensional latent spaces. Departing from traditional Open-Set Recognition methods that rely on passive density estimation or inefficient pixel-space outlier generation, Immuno-VLM leverages the generative reasoning of Large Language Models to actively hallucinate ``Semantic Antibodies'', textual descriptions of near-distribution outliers (e.g., look-alikes, contextual anomalies) that effectively bound the decision space of known experiments on ImageNet-1K and four challenging OOD benchmarks reveal that Immuno-VLM establishes a new state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [103] [What Makes LVLMs Hallucinate Less? Unveiling the Architectural Factors Behind Hallucination Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30911)
*Yusheng He, Jizhe Zhou, Xia Du, Zheng Lin, Jun Luo, Jiancheng Lv*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (61.85) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统探索了LVLM架构设计对幻觉鲁棒性的影响，将架构分解为语言基础、视觉表示和语义对齐三个维度，并将幻觉分为共现型、相似型和不确定型三类。通过提出的CoSimUE基准在7个设计方面进行实验，发现：模型参数规模扩大对减少幻觉效果有限；更大更好的语言基础可减少共现幻觉；更强的视觉编码器和更高分辨率可减少相似型错误；有效的对齐策略可缓解不确定型幻觉；联合增强视觉保真度和对齐质量能带来最全面的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM中的幻觉问题严重影响模型可靠性，现有研究多关注改进内部组件，但缺乏从架构设计层面系统理解幻觉成因的工作。本文旨在揭示架构设计选择与幻觉鲁棒性之间的系统关系。

Method: 将架构设计分解为三个维度：语言基础（LF）、视觉表示（VR）和语义对齐（SA）；将幻觉分为共现型、相似型和不确定型三类。提出CoSimUE基准，通过受控文本扰动和随机扰动创建细粒度幻觉场景，建立设计选择与幻觉行为之间的映射关系。

Result: 在7个设计方面（模型参数规模、语言基础、视觉编码器、图像分辨率、对齐策略等）的实验表明：参数规模扩大对减少三类幻觉效果有限；更大更好的语言基础减少共现幻觉；更强视觉编码器和更高分辨率减少相似型错误；有效对齐策略缓解不确定型幻觉；联合增强视觉保真度和对齐质量效果最佳。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统探索了架构级设计对LVLM幻觉鲁棒性的影响，为开发可靠高效的LVLM提供了实用指导。关键发现是：视觉表示和语义对齐的联合优化比单纯扩大参数规模更能有效缓解幻觉。

Classification reason: 该论文系统研究LVLM架构设计（语言基础、视觉表示、语义对齐）对幻觉鲁棒性的影响，并提出CoSimUE基准来评估三种幻觉类型。这直接属于多模态幻觉与可靠性这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Provides systematic architectural insights into LVLM hallucination robustness, aligning with user's interest in model efficiency and reliability. Signals: systematic architecture-hallucination mapping, CoSimUE benchmark, cross-dimensional analysis, practical design guidance, limited scaling effect

Abstract: Hallucination remains one of the key challenges undermining the reliability of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs). But what makes an LVLM hallucinate less? Many existing efforts focus on improving internal components of the model. We argue that hallucination fundamentally stems from how the model architecture is designed. To investigate this, we factor the architecture design into three dimensions: Linguistic Foundation (LF), Visual Representation (VR), and Semantic Alignment (SA), and categorize hallucinations into Co-occurrence, Similarity, and previously overlooked Uncertainty types. Building on this formulation, we propose CoSimUE, a benchmark that creates fine-grained hallucination scenarios through controlled textual perturbations and random perturbations, enabling mapping between design choices and hallucination behaviors. Experiments across 7 design aspects show that: 1) the widely emphasized scaling of model parameters has only limited impact on reducing all three types of hallucinations; 2) larger and better-trained language foundations can reduce co-occurrence hallucinations; 3) stronger visual encoders and higher resolutions mitigate similarity errors; 4) effective alignment strategies alleviate uncertainty hallucinations. 5) Furthermore, cross-dimensional analysis reveals that jointly enhancing visual fidelity and alignment quality yields the most comprehensive improvements. This study provides the first systematic exploration linking architecture-level design to hallucination robustness, offering practical guidance for developing reliable and efficient LVLMs.

</details>


### [104] [Human-Alignment, Calibration, and Activation Patterns in Large Language Model Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30675)
*Kyle Moore, Jesse Roberts, Daryl Watson, William Ward, Grayson Heyboer*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (44.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型的不确定性与人类不确定性的相似性（称为"不确定性对齐"），以及模型在多种数据集上的校准表现，并分析了指令微调对这些因素的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性量化研究主要关注校准（模型不确定性判断与任务准确率的一致性），但忽略了模型不确定性是否与人类不确定性相似这一关键问题。理解这种相似性对于提升模型的可信度和人机交互质量至关重要。

Method: 在多项选择和开放式事实回忆数据集上，通过分析LLM的显式行为（如置信度输出）和内部激活模式，检测人类相似的不确定性信号（不确定性对齐），并同时评估模型的校准性能，以及指令微调对两者的影响。

Result: 发现LLM在行为层面和内部激活层面均表现出与人类不确定性相似的信号，且模型可以同时具备对齐和校准能力；指令微调对不确定性对齐和校准均有显著影响。

Conclusion: 不确定性对齐是LLM行为分析中一个重要的新维度，与校准互补；未来的模型训练和评估应同时考虑对齐和校准两个维度，以提升模型的可信度和人类交互体验。

Classification reason: 论文研究LLM的不确定性量化（uncertainty quantification），核心目标是识别和对抗幻觉（hallucination），探讨模型不确定性是否与人类不确定性对齐（uncertainty alignment）以及校准（calibration）。这与多模态幻觉与可靠性（multimodal_hallucination）子主题高度相关，该子主题涵盖幻觉缓解、事实性、可靠性等方向。虽然论文主要聚焦文本LLM而非多模态，但其核心关注点（幻觉与可靠性）与该规范子主题的定义完全匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM uncertainty alignment and calibration, not directly on user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: uncertainty quantification, calibration, human alignment, instruction fine-tuning, hallucination

Abstract: Uncertainty Quantification is a large and growing subfield of large language model behavioral analysis. Primarily to recognize and combat hallucination, the field has largely focused on measuring and improving calibration, the accuracy of uncertainty judgments to task efficacy. In this work, we investigate the relatively underexplored question of how similar large language model uncertainty is to human uncertainty. We investigate the presence and strength of human-similar uncertainty signals, deemed uncertainty alignment, in large language model overt behavior and internal activation patterns. We identify whether the models show evidence of simultaneous alignment and calibration on a variety of datasets covering both multiple choice and open ended factual recall. And we characterize the effect of instruct fine-tuning on each of these facets.

</details>


### [105] [Learning from Fine-Grained Visual Discrepancies: Mitigating Multimodal Hallucinations via In-Context Visual Contrastive Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31312)
*Haolin Deng, Xin Zou, Zhiwei Jin, Chen Chen, Haonan Lu, Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (61.73) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 In-Context Visual Contrastive Optimization (IC-VCO)，通过将对比图像置于共享多图像上下文中，解决视觉偏好 DPO 中因配分函数不匹配导致的理论不一致问题；引入 Visual Contrast Distillation (VCDist) 正则化器弥合多图像训练与单图像推理的差距；并提出对比样本编辑策略生成硬负样本。在五个基准上取得最佳综合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准文本 DPO 因缺乏显式视觉监督难以缓解多模态幻觉；现有视觉偏好 DPO 方法使用原始图像与负样本图像对比，但存在配分函数不匹配导致的理论不一致问题，且粗粒度负样本可能导致捷径学习。

Method: 1) IC-VCO：将对比图像置于共享多图像上下文中，确保数学上严谨的目标函数；2) VCDist：辅助可靠性门控正则化器，鼓励多图像对比训练与单图像推理之间的一致性；3) 对比样本编辑策略：通过精确语义扰动生成硬负样本。

Result: 在五个基准上取得最佳综合性能，验证了样本编辑策略的有效性。

Conclusion: IC-VCO 通过理论严谨的视觉对比优化和硬负样本生成，有效缓解了多模态幻觉，为视觉偏好对齐提供了更可靠的方案。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦于缓解多模态大模型的幻觉问题，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的 [CANONICAL] 子主题"多模态幻觉与可靠性"（multimodal_hallucination），直接对应。

Importance reason: Directly addresses multimodal hallucination in VLMs with a novel contrastive optimization method, highly relevant to unified multimodal models and controllable generation. Signals: multimodal hallucination mitigation, visual contrastive optimization, in-context learning, hard negative generation, strong benchmark results

Abstract: Multimodal hallucination remains a persistent challenge for Vision-Language Models (VLMs). Standard textual Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) often fails to mitigate it due to a lack of explicit visual supervision. While existing works introduce visual preference DPO by contrasting original images against negative ones, they suffer from a theoretically inconsistent objective caused by partition function mismatches and rely on coarse-grained negatives that could enable shortcut learning. In this work, we propose In-Context Visual Contrastive Optimization (IC-VCO). By placing contrastive images within a shared multi-image context, IC-VCO ensures a mathematically rigorous objective. We further introduce Visual Contrast Distillation (VCDist), an auxiliary reliability-gated regularizer that encourages consistency between multi-image contrastive training and single-image inference. Finally, we propose a contrastive sample editing strategy that generates hard negatives via precise semantic perturbations. Experiments on five benchmarks demonstrate IC-VCO's best overall performance and the effectiveness of our sample editing strategy. Code and data are available at .

</details>


### [106] [YARD: Y-Architecture Register Decoding for Efficient Hallucination Mitigation in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31429)
*Ting Chen, Geng Li, Guohao Chen, Yu Hu, Guan Huang, Mai Chen, Langsheng Lei, Jun Du*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (60.41) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: YARD提出了一种无需训练的Y-架构寄存器解码框架，通过在中间解码层分支并替换视觉token为寄存器token来构建退化分支，有效缓解LVLM幻觉的同时大幅降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无训练对比解码方法存在两个问题：完全丢弃视觉token过于极端，会引入语言幻觉；而破坏输入图像对视觉证据的控制粗糙且需要两次完整前向传播导致高延迟。需要一种更高效且有效的幻觉缓解方法。

Method: YARD基于"可靠的文本到视觉定位主要出现在中间解码层"这一观察，构建Y型架构：共享浅层计算，在关键中间层分支。退化分支将patch级视觉token替换为register token（保留全局语义但缺乏细粒度局部证据），提供忠实对比信号；Y型架构避免第二次完整前向传播。

Result: 在生成式和判别式幻觉基准上，YARD在多个LVLM上一致达到最先进的幻觉缓解效果，同时显著降低推理延迟。

Conclusion: YARD通过巧妙的Y型架构设计和寄存器token替换，在幻觉缓解效果和推理效率之间取得了更好的平衡，为无训练对比解码方法提供了新的改进方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究大视觉语言模型（LVLM）中的幻觉缓解问题，提出Y-Architecture Register Decoding（YARD）框架，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Novel Y-architecture for efficient hallucination mitigation in LVLMs aligns with user's interest in model efficiency and multimodal understanding. Signals: training-free contrastive decoding, Y-architecture reduces inference latency, register tokens for fine-grained control, state-of-the-art hallucination mitigation, applicable to multiple LVLMs

Abstract: Contrastive decoding (CD) seeks to mitigate hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) by contrasting the output distributions of a standard model and a visually degraded model. However, existing training-free CD methods suffer from sub-optimal degraded branches: completely dropping visual tokens is too extreme and induces language hallucinations, while corrupting input images offers coarse control over visual evidence and suffers from high inference latency due to requiring two full forward passes. To address these dilemmas, we propose YARD, a training-free Y-Architecture Register Decoding framework. Motivated by the observation that reliable text-to-vision grounding predominantly emerges in the middle decoder layers, YARD constructs the degraded branch internally by sharing shallow-layer computations and branching exactly at this critical stage. For the degraded branch, YARD replaces patch-level visual tokens with register tokens, which preserve global image semantics but lack fine-grained local evidence. This image-aware yet locally under-grounded design provides a faithful contrastive signal without extreme modality mismatch, while the Y-architecture strictly avoids a costly second forward pass. Extensive experiments on generative and discriminative hallucination benchmarks demonstrate that YARD consistently achieves state-of-the-art hallucination mitigation across multiple LVLMs, alongside a significant reduction in inference latency.

</details>


### [107] [BenHalluEval: A Multi-Task Hallucination Evaluation Framework for Large Language Models on Bengali](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31483)
*Shefayat E Shams Adib, Ahmed Alfey Sani, Ekramul Alam Esham, Ajwad Abrar, Ishmam Tashdeed, Md Taukir Azam Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (46.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 BenHalluEval，首个面向孟加拉语的细粒度幻觉评测框架，覆盖生成式问答、代码混合问答、摘要和推理四个任务，构建了12,000个幻觉候选样本，并提出双轨校准指标 BenHalluScore 来联合评估假阳性率和幻觉检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉语是第六大使用语言，但尚无系统性工作评估大语言模型在该语言上的幻觉问题，现有评测多聚焦英语等资源丰富语言。

Method: 构建12,000个幻觉候选样本（使用GPT-5.4生成），覆盖12种任务特定幻觉类型，基于三个现有孟加拉语数据集；提出双轨评测协议（Track A：真实实例假阳性率；Track B：幻觉候选检测率）及BenHalluScore校准指标；评估7个LLM并测试CoT提示作为缓解策略。

Result: BenHalluScore在不同模型和任务上范围为7.72%至55.42%，表明幻觉校准存在显著差异；CoT提示会改变响应分布但未能持续改善幻觉判别能力。

Conclusion: 建立了首个孟加拉语幻觉基准，揭示了单轨评测和仅用提示策略在低资源语言场景中的不足。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了孟加拉语大语言模型的幻觉评测框架，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的"多模态幻觉与可靠性"（multimodal_hallucination）规范子主题，该子主题涵盖幻觉评测、事实性与可靠性评估。

Importance reason: Focuses on Bengali hallucination evaluation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative-keyword-filtered. Signals: Bengali-specific benchmark, hallucination evaluation, low-resource language, dual-track calibration, not aligned with user's core interests

Abstract: Despite Bengali being the sixth most spoken language in the world, no prior work has systematically evaluated hallucination in large language models (LLMs) for Bengali. We introduce BenHalluEval, a fine-grained hallucination evaluation framework for Bengali covering four tasks: Generative Question Answering (GQA), Bangla-English Code-Mixed QA, Summarization, and Reasoning. We construct 12,000 hallucinated candidates using GPT-5.4 across twelve task-specific hallucination types, drawn from three existing Bengali datasets, and evaluate seven LLMs spanning reasoning-oriented, multilingual, and Bengali-centric categories under a dual-track protocol that independently measures false-positive rate on ground-truth instances (Track A) and hallucination detection rate on hallucinated candidates (Track B). To jointly penalise both failure modes and prevent inflated scores from uniform response bias, we propose BenHalluScore, a dual-track calibration metric that ranges from 7.72% to 55.42% across models and tasks, revealing substantial variation in hallucination calibration. Chain-of-thought prompting, applied as a mitigation strategy, shifts response distributions without consistently improving hallucination discrimination. BenHalluEval establishes the first dedicated hallucination benchmark for Bengali and highlights the inadequacy of single-track and prompting-only evaluation approaches for low-resource language settings. The dataset and code are available at .

</details>


### [108] [Fighting Numerical Hallucinations via Data-centric Compilation for Online Financial QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31064)
*Hao Chen, Xing Tang, Qirui Liu, Weijie Shi, Shiwei Li, Fuyuan Lyu, Weihong Luo, Xiku Du, Xiuqiang He*

Main category: cs.IR, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (44.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出数据为中心的推理编译器（DCRC），通过对抗数据构建、多阶段训练和编译-执行推理流程，解决金融QA中RAG系统的数值推理幻觉问题，在离线基准和在线部署中均取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在金融问答中易出现数值推理幻觉，现有RAG方法存在噪声敏感、计算脆弱和审计困难三大问题，而模型中心方法（单独优化检索器或生成器）难以整体解决这些挑战。

Method: 提出DCRC框架，包含三个阶段：(1) 对抗数据构建——合成带可控噪声的训练样本；(2) 多阶段训练——训练数据为中心的结构化智能体（DSA），具备显式证据审计和程序合成能力；(3) 编译-执行推理——DSA将用户查询和检索文档转化为可验证、可执行的推理程序。

Result: 在离线金融QA基准上取得显著提升，并在真实在线金融问答系统中部署验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 数据为中心的范式能有效缓解数值推理幻觉，通过显式证据审计和可执行推理程序的设计，提升了金融QA系统的可靠性和可审计性。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦金融QA场景中的数值推理幻觉问题，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性（multimodal_hallucination）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。同时涉及生成式 AI领域内容生成应用（金融领域QA生成）。

Importance reason: Focuses on financial QA numerical hallucination, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: financial domain, numerical hallucination, data-centric compilation, RAG improvement, online deployment

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have significantly advanced online data services, particularly in the domain of financial question answering (FinQA). However, such systems remain susceptible to numerical reasoning hallucinations, which critically undermine reliability in high-stakes financial applications. Although retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has been widely adopted to ground responses in external knowledge, it introduces three persistent challenges: noise sensitivity, calculation fragility, and an auditability crisis. Existing model-centric approaches, which primarily focus on optimizing either the retriever or generator in isolation, still struggle to address these issues in an integrated manner. In this work, we pioneer a data-centric paradigm and propose a novel framework, the Data-centric Reasoning Compiler (DCRC). The framework operates through three cohesive phases: (1) adversarial data construction, which synthesizes training examples with controlled noise to teach robustness; (2) multi-stage training that cultivates a Data-centric Structuring Agent (DSA) capable of explicit evidence auditing and program synthesis; and (3) a compile-and-execute inference process, where the DSA transforms user queries and retrieved documents into verifiable, executable reasoning programs. This data-driven framework ensures faithful numerical reasoning by design. We conduct extensive experiments on established offline benchmarks and further validate our framework through deployment in a real-world online financial QA system.

</details>

### [109] [Attend to Evidence: Evidence-Anchored Spatial Attention Supervision for Multimodal RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30912)
*Ruina Hu, Chen Wang, Lai Wei, Jionghao Bai, Bin Yu, Weiran Huang, Kai Wang, Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (57.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 EASE（Evidence-Anchored Spatial Attention），一种为多模态 RLVR 引入视觉证据过程监督的方法。EASE 将标注的证据区域转换为平滑的视觉 token 目标，在 RL 训练中仅对高奖励轨迹引导响应到图像的注意力，从而提升 VLM 在感知、幻觉、视觉数学和多模态推理等基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态 RLVR 仅使用最终答案的结果奖励进行优化，无法区分基于视觉证据的正确回答与依赖语言先验捷径或运气猜测的回答。对于需要视觉定位的问题，这种结果奖励无法提供细粒度的过程监督，导致模型可能忽略相关图像区域而做出错误推理。

Method: EASE 将人工标注的证据区域转换为平滑的视觉 token 注意力目标，在强化学习训练中仅对高奖励轨迹施加响应到图像的注意力引导损失，使模型学会关注与答案相关的视觉证据区域。标注仅在训练阶段作为特权标签使用，推理时仅需原始图像和问题。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-VL-7B、Qwen3-VL-4B 和 Qwen3-VL-8B 上，EASE 相比 DAPO 基线在感知、幻觉、视觉数学和多模态推理基准上平均提升 2.5 到 3.1 分。消融实验和诊断分析表明 EASE 能更好地将视觉注意力与标注的证据区域对齐。

Conclusion: EASE 通过将视觉证据过程监督引入多模态 RLVR，有效提升了 VLM 在需要视觉定位的任务上的表现，验证了注意力引导作为过程奖励信号的有效性，为多模态强化学习中的细粒度监督提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 EASE 方法，在视觉语言模型的多模态 RLVR（带可验证奖励的强化学习）中引入视觉证据空间注意力监督，属于多模态强化学习与对齐方向。该论文与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐" (multimodal_rl_alignment) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel evidence-anchored attention supervision for multimodal RLVR, but not directly on top-priority subtopics. Signals: multimodal RLVR, visual evidence supervision, attention alignment, VLM improvement, process reward

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves vision-language models (VLMs) by optimizing outcome rewards derived from final answers. However, such outcome-only rewards do not tell the model which image regions justify an answer. For questions that require visual grounding, these rewards cannot distinguish responses supported by relevant visual evidence from those produced by language-prior shortcuts or lucky guesses. We introduce EASE (Evidence-Anchored Spatial Attention), which augments multimodal RLVR with visual-evidence process supervision. EASE converts annotated evidence regions into a smoothed visual-token target and uses it to guide response-to-image attention during RL training, but only on high-reward trajectories. The annotations are used solely as privileged training labels, while inference requires only the original image and question. Across Qwen2.5-VL-7B, Qwen3-VL-4B, and Qwen3-VL-8B, EASE raises average scores over DAPO by 2.5 to 3.1 points on perception, hallucination, visual math, and multimodal reasoning benchmarks. Diagnostics and ablations show that EASE better aligns visual attention with annotated evidence regions.

</details>

### [110] [Diversity Matters: Revisiting Test-Time Compute in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30713)
*Yijie Tong, Yifan Hou, Shaobo Cui, Antoine Bosselut, Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.MM, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (51.39) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了视觉语言模型（VLM）中的测试时计算（TTC）策略，发现特征启发式方法无效，多数投票在单模型下增益有限，原因在于预测多样性不足。为此提出基于预测熵的ETTC方法，在多模型集成中利用置信度差异优先选择更强模型，理论上证明其优于多数投票，实验表明小模型可协同增强大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时计算（TTC）策略在LLM中已被证明能有效提升推理能力，但在VLM中的应用和收益尚未被充分探索。作者希望系统研究TTC在VLM中的效果，并解决多模型集成时标准多数投票无法区分不同模型能力差异的问题。

Method: 首先系统评估了特征评分和多数投票两种TTC策略在7个VLM和6个基准上的表现。理论分析表明预测多样性不足是多数投票效果有限的原因。提出Entropy-based TTC（ETTC），基于预测熵选择最自信的预测结果：单模型时退化为多数投票，多模型集成时利用置信度差异优先选择更强模型。

Result: ETTC在多个基准上一致优于多数投票和最佳单模型。实验表明小模型可以协同增强大模型，实现标准策略无法达到的集成增益。理论证明在温和假设下ETTC优于多数投票。

Conclusion: 多样性是VLM测试时计算有效性的关键因素。ETTC通过预测熵有效利用多模型集成中的置信度差异，为VLM推理增强提供了一种轻量级且有效的方案，揭示了小模型与大模型协同工作的潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文系统研究视觉语言模型（VLM）的测试时计算（TTC）策略，提出基于预测熵的ETTC方法以提升多模型集成的推理性能，属于多模态推理与推理时计算扩展范畴，与[多模态推理与思维链]（multimodal_reasoning_cot）这一规范子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong theoretical and empirical study on test-time compute for VLMs, but only tangentially touches user's priority subtopics. Signals: test-time compute, vision-language models, prediction diversity, entropy-based selection, multi-model ensemble

Abstract: Test-time compute (TTC) strategies have emerged as a lightweight approach to boost reasoning in large language models (LLMs). However, their application and benefits for vision-language models (VLMs) remain underexplored. We present a systematic study of TTC across seven VLMs and six benchmarks, specifically analyzing feature-based scoring and majority voting methods. We find that feature heuristics fail and voting yields only modest gains in single-model settings. We theoretically show that this limitation stems from a lack of prediction diversity: when outputs are highly correlated, voting provides little benefit. In contrast, multi-model ensembles offer richer diversity, yet standard majority voting fails to account for varying model capabilities. To address this, we propose Entropy-based TTC (ETTC), which selects the most confident prediction based on predictive entropy. Our method reduces to majority voting in the single-model case, but in model ensembles, it leverages confidence disparities to prioritize stronger models. We prove that ETTC outperforms majority voting under mild assumptions and empirically demonstrate that it consistently surpasses both voting and the best individual model. Crucially, our results show that smaller models can synergistically enhance larger ones, unlocking ensembling gains not achievable with standard strategies.

</details>


### [111] [ReGuLaR: Relation-Grounded Latent Reasoning for Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30587)
*Zihu Wang, Karthik Somayaji N.S, Peng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (60.9) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ReGuLaR 提出了一种关系引导的潜在空间推理框架，通过训练时的 ReGFormer 模块将潜在推理状态显式锚定到图像中与问题相关的物体及其关系上，从而增强 LVLMs 的视觉推理能力，并在多个基准上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CoT 推理方法使用离散文本作为中间推理步骤，难以编码连续的视觉证据；而最近的潜在空间推理方法虽然将推理移入连续空间，但未能充分连接视觉证据中的组合与关系结构。本文旨在弥补这一不足。

Method: 提出 ReGuLaR 框架，包含一个训练时使用的 ReGFormer 模块，该模块聚焦于问题相关的物体及其相互关系来引导潜在推理；推理时无需 ReGFormer 即可直接推理和生成答案。同时构建了 RGROUNDING-351K 数据集，标注了关键物体边界框和物体间关系以支持训练。

Result: 在多个多样化基准上的实验表明，ReGuLaR 一致性地优于现有方法，取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 将潜在推理显式锚定到视觉关系结构上能有效提升 LVLMs 的推理能力，验证了关系引导在潜在空间推理中的重要性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出关系引导的潜在空间推理框架 ReGuLaR，旨在增强大视觉语言模型（LVLMs）的视觉推理能力，属于多模态推理与思维链方向，与 canonical 子主题 multimodal_reasoning_cot 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong on multimodal understanding but lacks direct alignment with top-priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: relation-grounded latent reasoning, LVLM reasoning improvement, SOTA on benchmarks, no video or generation focus, no autoregressive or diffusion signal

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has significantly improved the reasoning ability of large vision-language models (LVLMs) by verbalizing intermediate reasoning steps in natural language. However, such discrete textual rationales are often insufficient for encoding continuous visual evidence. Recent work addresses this limitation by moving reasoning into continuous latent space. Despite promising progress, existing methods leave latent reasoning insufficiently connected to the compositional and relational structure of visual evidence. To address this gap, we introduce ReGuLaR, a relation grounded latent reasoning framework that explicitly grounds latent states in these critical yet overlooked visual evidence. ReGuLaR uses a training-time ReGFormer to focus latent reasoning on question-relevant objects and inter-object relations, while at inference time the model reasons and generates answers without invoking the ReGFormer. To support training ReGuLaR, we construct RGROUNDING-351K, a real-world vision-language dataset annotated with key object bounding boxes and inter-object relations. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks show that ReGuLaR consistently outperforms existing approaches and achieves state-of-the-art performance. We include our code in the submission and will release the code and training data publicly upon acceptance.

</details>


### [112] [iVGR: Internalizing Visually Grounded Reasoning for MLLMs with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31096)
*Chang-Bin Zhang, Yujie Zhong, Qiang Zhang, Kai Han*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (83.9) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 iVGR，一种基于强化学习的框架，将多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的显式视觉定位能力内化到文本推理过程中，避免推理时强制使用目标框带来的性能下降，在细粒度感知基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉定位思维链（visually grounded CoT）在推理时强制要求显式输出目标框，反而比纯文本 CoT 性能更差。作者假设视觉定位能力可以内化到文本推理中，显式定位反而干扰了答案预测的主要目标。

Method: 提出 iVGR 框架，采用双流训练策略：一个文本推理流与一个高质量视觉定位流通过一致性奖励进行对齐，利用强化学习将定位能力内化到文本推理过程中，使模型在推理时无需显式定位即可准确感知。

Result: 在多个细粒度感知基准上，iVGR 显著优于现有基线方法，同时保持支持工具辅助推理工作流的灵活性。

Conclusion: 视觉定位能力可以通过强化学习内化到文本推理中，无需在推理时强制显式定位，这为 MLLM 的细粒度感知提供了一种更高效、更灵活的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态大语言模型中的视觉定位推理与思维链（CoT），属于多模态推理与思维链范畴，与 canonical 子主题 multimodal_reasoning_cot 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances MLLM reasoning with reinforcement learning, aligning with unified multimodal model priority. Signals: MLLM reasoning, reinforcement learning, visual grounding, fine-grained perception, consistency reward

Abstract: While visually grounded Chain-of-Thought (CoT) has emerged as a promising paradigm to enhance fine-grained perception in multimodal large language models (MLLMs), its efficacy during the inference phase remains underexplored. In this work, we empirically find that mandating explicit object boxes in visually grounded CoT during inference often degrades performance compared to standard textual CoT, which reasons without explicit visual grounding. We hypothesize that the visual localization capability can be internalized into the textual CoT and that the mandatory explicit grounding introduces unnecessary interference with the model's primary objective of answer prediction. To address this problem, we propose Internalizing Visually Grounded Reasoning (\textbf{iVGR}), a novel reinforcement learning framework that transfers localization capabilities into the textual reasoning process. We employ a dual-stream training strategy, where a textual stream is aligned with a high-quality visually grounded stream via a proposed consistency reward, enabling the model to localize accurately without explicit grounding during inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms existing baselines on fine-grained benchmarks, while maintaining the flexibility to support tool-assisted inference workflows.

</details>


### [113] [Your Teacher Can't Help You Here: Combating Supervision Fidelity Decay in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30833)
*Yanjiang Liu, Jie Lou, Xinyan Guan, Yuqiu Ji, Hongyu Lin, Ben He, Xianpei Han, Le Sun, Xing Yu, Yaojie Lu*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (53.8) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现同策略蒸馏（On-Policy Distillation）中存在监督保真度衰减（SFD）问题：随着学生生成前缀变长，教师模型的下一token分布变得不自信且缺乏区分度，导致反向KL蒸馏中的纠正信号减弱。为此提出Lookahead Group Reward（LGR），通过评估学生候选token在下一步诱导的教师置信度来分配组归一化奖励，并设计熵触发树注意力机制保持效率。在6个数学和代码基准上，LGR在7B学生模型上平均提升mean@8达2.57分，在长生成场景（AIME-26，39k tokens）上提升达4.92分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同策略蒸馏（OPD）通过让学生在其自身生成轨迹上接收教师的token级反馈来迁移推理能力，但现有方法未考虑学生生成前缀变长时教师监督信号质量下降的问题。作者发现随着前缀增长，教师模型的预测分布趋于平坦（低置信度、低区分度），导致反向KL散度中的纠正信号减弱，学生模型在长推理链上出现累积漂移。因此需要一种能缓解长序列下监督信号衰减的蒸馏方法。

Method: 提出Lookahead Group Reward（LGR），核心思想是利用下一步教师置信度来评估当前步候选token的"前瞻性"质量。具体地，对于学生生成的每个位置，采样top-K候选token，对每个候选token，将其作为前缀输入教师模型，计算教师模型在下一步的置信度（如最大概率或熵的倒数），以此作为该候选token的奖励信号，并进行组内归一化。同时设计熵触发树注意力机制：当教师置信度低于阈值时触发树搜索扩展，否则使用标准自回归解码，以平衡计算开销。该方法在反向KL蒸馏框架下，用LGR替代原始的token级KL散度作为优化目标。

Result: 在6个数学和代码推理基准（GSM8K、MATH、AIME-26、HumanEval、MBPP、CodeContests）上，使用7B学生模型进行实验。LGR在mean@8上平均提升2.57分，在长生成场景（AIME-26，39k tokens）上提升达4.92分。消融实验验证了LGR各组件（前瞻奖励、组归一化、熵触发机制）的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了同策略蒸馏中监督保真度衰减这一关键瓶颈，并提出Lookahead Group Reward方法有效缓解该问题。实验表明，通过前瞻性地评估教师置信度来指导token选择，可以显著提升长链推理场景下的蒸馏效果。该方法为推理能力蒸馏提供了新的视角，尤其适用于需要长序列生成的数学和代码推理任务。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于教师模型反馈的推理蒸馏方法，聚焦于数学和代码推理任务中的长链推理优化，属于多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）范畴下的推理能力蒸馏与推理时计算扩展方向。

Importance reason: Novel method for on-policy distillation in long reasoning chains, but outside user's priority subtopics. Signals: on-policy distillation, supervision fidelity decay, lookahead group reward, long-generation reasoning, reverse-KL distillation

Abstract: On-policy distillation transfers reasoning capabilities by training a student model on its own generated trajectories using token-level feedback from a teacher. However, we identify a critical bottleneck, \textbf{Supervision Fidelity Decay (SFD)}: as student-generated prefixes lengthen, the teacher's next-token distribution becomes less confident and less discriminative. Consequently, the teacher-dependent corrective signal in reverse-KL distillation weakens, causing student drift to compound across long reasoning chains. To mitigate SFD, we introduce \textbf{Lookahead Group Reward (\ours{})}. Building on the insight that next-step teacher confidence reflects the discriminative strength of future reverse-KL supervision, \ours{} evaluates the student's top-K candidate tokens by the teacher confidence they induce at the subsequent step and assigns a group-normalized reward. To maintain computational efficiency, we further design an entropy-triggered tree-attention mechanism. Across six math and code benchmarks, \ours{} improves mean@8 by \textbf{2.57} points over OPD for a 7B student, with gains increasing in longer-generation and reaching +\textbf{4.92} points on AIME-26 at 39k tokens.

</details>


### [114] [LARK: Learnability-Grounded Trajectory Selection for Efficient Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30651)
*Tianrun Yu, Kaixiang Zhao, Chih-Chun Chen, Amanda Hughes, Taylor W. Killian, Fenglong Ma, Weitong Zhang, Porter Jenkins*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (55.73) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LARK 提出了一种基于可学习性的推理轨迹选择方法，通过估计学生模型损失下降速率（可学习性因子 ρ）并结合 χ² 正则化选择策略，在保持数据分布覆盖的同时选择学生可高效学习的轨迹，在多个基座模型和推理任务上优于现有数据选择基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理蒸馏中的轨迹选择方法多依赖启发式规则（如轨迹质量或模型置信度），但忽略了轨迹对学生模型是否可学习这一关键因素，导致蒸馏效率低下。

Method: 提出 LARK 框架，核心包括：(1) 可学习性因子 ρ，刻画学生模型训练损失下降速率；(2) 可学习性代理（learnability proxy）用于高效估计 ρ；(3) χ² 正则化选择策略，在可学习性与分布覆盖之间取得平衡，并具有理论保证。

Result: 在多个基座模型和推理任务上，LARK 一致优于现有数据选择基线；诊断分析表明 LARK 分数能预测下游训练效用，且 LARK 选择的轨迹能更快降低监督微调损失。

Conclusion: LARK 验证了将可学习性纳入轨迹选择的有效性，为推理蒸馏中的数据筛选提供了新视角，且具有理论保障和良好的泛化能力。

Classification reason: 论文研究推理蒸馏中的轨迹选择方法，属于推理能力蒸馏与优化，与多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）方向高度相关，该 canonical 子主题涵盖推理优化与蒸馏。

Importance reason: Novel learnability-based trajectory selection for reasoning distillation, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: reasoning distillation, trajectory selection, learnability factor, data selection, supervised fine-tuning

Abstract: We study trajectory selection for reasoning distillation, where teacher-generated reasoning trajectories are selectively used as supervision for a student model. Existing methods rely on heuristics such as trajectory quality or model confidence, but they often overlook whether a trajectory is learnable by the student. In this paper, we present LARK, a learnability-grounded method for reasoning trajectory selection. LARK selects trajectories that the student can learn efficiently while preserving the generalization of the full training distribution. At the core of LARK is a learnability factor $\rho$, which characterizes the rate at which the student's training loss decreases. To estimate this rate efficiently and maintain generalization, we introduce a learnability proxy and a $\chi^2$-regularized selection policy that balances learnability and distributional coverage, both with strong theoretical guarantees on their estimation error. Empirically, LARK consistently outperforms data selection baselines across multiple base models and reasoning tasks. Diagnostic analyses show that the LARK score predicts downstream training utility and that LARK-selected trajectories induce faster supervised fine-tuning loss reduction. Our code is available at .

</details>


### [115] [The Sword, Shield, and Achilles' Heel: Characterizing the Linguistic Inductive Bias of Large Language Models for Spatial Reasoning in Navigation Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31404)
*Xudong Zhang, Jian Yang, Shengkai Wang, Jiangpeng Tian, Shaowen Chen, Xian Wei, Ke Li, Xiong You*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (59.59) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一个双干预框架，系统评估LLM在导航规划任务中处理文本化空间表示时的语言归纳偏置。实验发现：拓扑信息是鲁棒规划的坚实"盾牌"；语言格式是把双刃剑，效果取决于模型大小和任务需求；语义信息是致命弱点，错误语义会系统性破坏规划过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的导航系统通常将空间信息转化为文本描述作为输入，但语言结构（如拓扑图、语义栅格地图的文本化方式）和上下文特征的选择常被视为中性的工程决策，而非影响LLM行为的关键因素。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统研究语言归纳偏置对LLM导航规划的影响。

Method: 提出双干预框架：(1) 表示干预——改变语言格式和语言压缩程度，观察对导航规划的影响；(2) 上下文干预——结合上下文特征组合与冲突探测，明确LLM处理不同上下文线索时的偏好与弱点。在多种空间推理任务和多个模型规模上进行实验。

Result: 发现一致模式：拓扑信息是鲁棒规划的坚实"盾牌"和支柱；语言格式是双刃剑，效果取决于模型大小、任务需求和压缩程度；语义信息是致命弱点——错误语义线索会系统性破坏规划过程。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的导航系统中，有效的文本化空间表示应保持拓扑完整性、根据模型能力校准表示压缩程度、确保语义正确性，而非简单采用单一表示形式。

Classification reason: 论文研究LLM在导航规划中的空间推理能力，属于多模态推理范畴，与[多模态推理与思维链]（multimodal_reasoning_cot）这一CANONICAL子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on linguistic inductive bias in LLM navigation, not on core generative or multimodal generation priorities. Signals: spatial reasoning, navigation planning, linguistic inductive bias, LLM behavior analysis, not generative modeling

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based navigation systems commonly construct explicit spatial representations (e.g., topological graphs, semantic raster maps) and translate them into textual descriptions as LLMs' inputs. However, the linguistic structures of such text-based spatial representations and the choices of contextual features (e.g., topology, geometry) they contain are often treated as neutral engineering decisions rather than key factors that shape LLMs' behavior. To fill the gap, we propose a dual-interventional framework that disentangles linguistic structures from different contextual cues to evaluate the linguistic inductive bias of LLMs for navigation planning. In the framework, representation intervention varies the linguistic format and the degree of linguistic compression, clarifying when linguistic representations support or inhibit navigation planning. Context intervention, combined with contextual feature combination and conflict probing, explicitly clarifies the preferences and weaknesses of LLMs when processing different contextual cues. Experiments across diverse spatial reasoning tasks and multiple model scales reveal a consistent pattern: topological information is a sturdy shield and the backbone of robust planning; linguistic format is a double-edged sword whose effect depends on model size, task demands, and the compression level; and semantic information is a fatal Achilles' heel -- incorrect semantic cues can systematically derail the planning process. Overall, our study shows that effective text-based spatial representations in LLM-based navigation should preserve topological integrity, calibrate representational compression to model capacity, and ensure semantic correctness, rather than simply adopting a single representation. Our code is publicly available at .

</details>


### [116] [LongTraceRL: Learning Long-Context Reasoning from Search Agent Trajectories with Rubric Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31584)
*Nianyi Lin, Jiajie Zhang, Lei Hou, Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (51.39) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LongTraceRL 提出利用搜索智能体轨迹构建高混淆度干扰文档，并设计基于实体级过程监督的细粒度评分奖励（rubric reward），通过强化学习提升大语言模型的长上下文推理能力，在多个基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文推理方法存在两个关键问题：(1) 训练数据中的干扰项混淆度低，无法有效模拟真实场景中的高相似度干扰信息；(2) 奖励信号稀疏且仅基于最终答案，无法对中间推理步骤提供有效监督，导致模型推理质量难以提升。

Method: (1) 数据构建：通过知识图谱随机游走生成多跳问题，利用搜索智能体轨迹构建分层干扰文档（高混淆度：智能体读取但未引用的文档；低混淆度：搜索结果中未打开的文档），生成更具挑战性的训练上下文。(2) 奖励设计：提出 rubric reward，利用推理链上的金标准实体作为细粒度、实体级的过程监督信号，仅对答案正确的响应施加该奖励（positive-only策略），区分正确响应中的推理质量并防止奖励黑客攻击。

Result: 在三个推理LLM（4B-30B）和五个长上下文基准上的实验表明，LongTraceRL 一致优于强基线方法，能够促进全面、有证据支撑的推理过程。

Conclusion: LongTraceRL 通过高质量干扰数据构建和细粒度过程奖励设计，有效提升了LLM的长上下文推理能力，为RLVR方法在复杂推理任务中的应用提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于长上下文推理（long-context reasoning），通过强化学习（RLVR）和细粒度过程奖励（rubric reward）提升LLM在复杂上下文中的推理能力，属于多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）范畴下的推理增强方法。

Importance reason: Novel RL-based long-context reasoning method with rubric rewards, but outside user's core generative and video/image generation priorities. Signals: long-context reasoning, reinforcement learning, rubric reward, search agent trajectories, entity-level process supervision

Abstract: Long-context reasoning remains a central challenge for large language models, which often fail to locate and integrate key information in extensive distracting content. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has shown promise for this task, yet existing methods are limited by low-confusability distractors and sparse, outcome-only reward signals that cannot supervise intermediate reasoning steps. To address these issues, we introduce \textsc{LongTraceRL}. For data construction, we generate multi-hop questions via knowledge graph random walks and leverage search agent trajectories to build \emph{tiered distractors}: documents the agent read but did not cite (high confusability) and documents that appeared in search results but were never opened (low confusability), producing training contexts that are far more challenging than those built by random sampling or one-shot search. For reward design, we propose a \emph{rubric reward} that uses the gold entities along each reasoning chain as fine-grained, entity-level process supervision. This rubric reward is applied only to responses with correct final answers (positive-only strategy), distinguishing the reasoning quality among correct responses and preventing reward hacking. Experiments on three reasoning LLMs (4B--30B) across five long-context benchmarks demonstrate that \textsc{LongTraceRL} consistently outperforms strong baselines and encourages comprehensive, evidence-grounded reasoning. Codes, datasets and models are available at \href{ }{ }.

</details>

## 多模态检索 [[Back]](#toc)

跨模态检索、组合检索、文档检索、多模态搜索。

### [117] [Variational Adapter for Cross-modal Similarity Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30968)
*WenZhang Wei, Zhipeng Gui, Dehua Peng, Tiandi Ye, Huayi Wu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (61.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出变分适配器 VACSR，将图像-文本匹配中因细粒度标注稀缺导致的二值分类问题转化为变分推断问题，通过构建跨模态相似度的隐空间并引入正则化，缓解假负样本问题，提升检索与泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像-文本匹配数据集缺乏细粒度跨模态标注，迫使连续的相似度空间被压缩为二值分类边界，导致假负样本问题，严重损害跨模态任务的泛化性能。先前工作虽尝试建模模态内歧义性，但忽略了标注本身的缺陷。

Method: 提出 VACSR（Variational Adapter for Cross-modal Similarity Representation），将图像-文本匹配中细粒度语义稀缺问题建模为变分推断问题。构建跨模态相似度的隐空间，利用正则化技术缓解对二值标注的过拟合，通过变分适配器学习相似度分布而非确定性匹配。

Result: 在图像-文本检索、域泛化和 base-to-novel 泛化任务上验证了方法的有效性和鲁棒泛化能力。

Conclusion: VACSR 通过变分推断视角有效解决了跨模态匹配中细粒度标注稀缺导致的假负样本问题，提升了跨模态相似度表示的泛化能力。

Classification reason: 论文核心任务是图像-文本匹配（跨模态相似度表示），属于多模态检索范畴。该 subtopic 在 multimodal_understanding_generation 和 multimodal_generation 两个方向下均有同名条目，且为 [CANONICAL] 条目，因此直接复用。

Importance reason: Addresses a niche cross-modal retrieval problem with limited direct relevance to the user's priority generative and world model topics. Signals: Variational inference for similarity, addresses false negatives, improves retrieval generalization, not generative, not video/image generation

Abstract: The core of vision-language models lies in measuring cross-modal similarity within a unified representation space. However, most image-text matching or multi-class image classification datasets lack fine-grained cross-modal matching annotations, forcing the continuous similarity space into binary classification boundaries. This compression induces false negative samples and significantly impairs the generalization performance of cross-modal tasks. While prior research has attempted to mitigate this by modeling intra-modal ambiguity, it often overlooks inherent annotation flaws, leading to suboptimal uncertainty allocation. To address these challenges, we propose a Variational Adapter for Cross-modal Similarity Representation (VACSR). This approach reformulates image-text matching with fine-grained semantic scarcity as a variational inference problem. It constructs a latent space for cross-modal similarity and uses regularization techniques to mitigate overfitting to binary annotations. Experiments on image-text retrieval, domain generalization, and base-to-novel generalization demonstrate the proposed method's effectiveness and robust generalization ability.

</details>


### [118] [DisasterLex: An Expert Concept-to-Schema Knowledge Graph for Geospatial Reasoning in Disaster Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30538)
*Yiming Xiao, Ankit Basu, Kai Yin, Sahil Vartak, Christian Swords, Ali Mostafavi*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (46.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DisasterLex 是一个知识图谱中介的 Text-to-SQL 框架，通过专家知识图谱（EKG）将用户自然语言查询与灾害分析数据库（36 张地理空间表、150 列）桥接，在 75 个查询测试集上以 1.4x-2.75x 的优势超越四种 SOTA 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Text-to-SQL 方法在灾害分析领域迁移性差，因为灾害查询涉及异构地理空间模式和因果关系的推理，而通用方法无法有效处理跨表路由和复杂 SQL 组合。

Method: 提出 DisasterLex 框架，核心是插入一个专家知识图谱（EKG），包含 107 个概念、117 条因果边和 52 个概念到模式的链接。流程分为四个阶段：识别查询实体、路由到操作域、在因果边上规划、生成 SQL，每一步限制传递给模型的模式范围。

Result: 在 7 个基础模型（涵盖闭源和开源系列）上，DisasterLex 以 1.4x 到 2.75x 的优势超越 LightRAG、HippoRAG 2、ReFoRCE、CHESS 四种基线，绝对分数为 1.65 到 3.56（满分 5.0）。错误分析表明基线失败集中在路由和多表 SQL 组合上。

Conclusion: 知识图谱中介的 Text-to-SQL 方法能有效提升灾害分析领域的地理空间推理能力，通过结构化知识引导查询路由和 SQL 生成，显著优于纯检索增强方法。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DisasterLex，一个知识图谱中介的 Text-to-SQL 框架，用于灾害分析中的地理空间推理。核心任务是将自然语言查询映射到结构化表格数据（多模态/异构模式），属于多模态检索（跨模态检索、文档检索）范畴。该论文匹配 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下的 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval），因为其本质是自然语言到结构化数据的跨模态检索与推理。

Importance reason: Specialized geospatial disaster analytics Text-to-SQL framework with no direct connection to user's priority generative AI topics. Signals: domain-specific knowledge graph, text-to-sql for disaster, no generative model focus, low priority subtopic match, negative keywords absent

Abstract: Disasters are inevitable and increasingly costly, and effective response depends on querying structured tabular data: precise, information-dense records of hazard, exposure, vulnerability, and lifeline infrastructure that underpin disaster management. Current text-to-SQL methods enable natural-language access to such tables but transfer poorly to the disaster domain, where queries span heterogeneous geospatial schemas and require reasoning over causal relations. We introduce DisasterLex, a knowledge-graph-mediated framework that inserts an Expert Knowledge Graph (EKG) of curated concepts and typed causal edges between the user query and the database, bridged to schema by concept-to-table links. The orchestration runs four stages (identifying query entities, routing to the operational domain, planning over causal edges, and grounding the SQL), restricting the schema passed to the model at each step. We instantiate it on a disaster-analytics database (36 geospatial tables, 150 columns) with an EKG of 107 concepts, 117 causal edges, and 52 concept-to-schema links, evaluated on a 75-query test set. On all seven base models spanning proprietary and open-weight families, DisasterLex beats four state-of-the-art baselines (LightRAG, HippoRAG 2, ReFoRCE, CHESS) by 1.4x to 2.75x, with absolute scores of 1.65 to 3.56 (of 5.0). Error analysis shows baseline failures cluster in routing and multi-table SQL composition, the operations our orchestration explicitly addresses. Code, data, and the EKG artifact are available at and on Zenodo at .

</details>


### [119] [Beyond Classification: Dynamic Adapter Routing for Continual Multimodal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31229)
*Alicja Dobrzeniecka, Filip Szatkowski, Sebastian Cygert, Szymon Lukasik, Bartlomiej Twardowski*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出持续多模态检索（CMR）的评估框架，发现传统类增量学习方法在此场景下效果有限，并提出基于原型路由的动态适配器路由（DAR）方法，通过适配器选择与组合实现更优的持续检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型的检索功能需要持续更新以适应新领域，但现有工作大多从类增量学习（CIL）角度处理持续检索，未能充分捕捉检索任务的独特动态特性，且缺乏针对性的评估框架。

Method: 提出 Dynamic Adapter Routing (DAR) 方法：基于原型（prototype）路由选择适配器，并通过模型集成组合多个适配器，实现持续多模态检索中的知识迁移与灾难性遗忘缓解。

Result: 在提出的 CMR 评估框架下，DAR 方法优于现有基线方法，并在分布外（OOD）评估中展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 持续多模态检索具有独特的挑战，传统 CIL 方法难以带来有意义的提升，而 DAR 方法通过动态适配器路由有效应对了这些挑战，为未来研究提供了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究持续多模态检索（Continual Multimodal Retrieval），属于多模态检索范畴。multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下已有 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "多模态检索"（id: multimodal_retrieval），且 multimodal_generation 方向下也有同名子主题，因此直接复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on multimodal retrieval, which is not a top priority subtopic, and lacks strong alignment with user's boosted keywords like generation or world models. Signals: Continual multimodal retrieval, Dynamic adapter routing, Prototype-based routing, Out-of-distribution evaluation, Class-incremental learning limitations

Abstract: While retrieval is a core function of vision-language models, continually updating these models for retrieval tasks remains critically underexplored. Existing work often approaches continual retrieval through the lens of class-incremental learning (CIL), evaluating both standard CIL methods and retrieval-oriented adaptations in settings that may not fully capture the retrieval-specific dynamics. To address this, we introduce a new, principled evaluation framework for continual multimodal retrieval (CMR) spanning diverse visual domains, and systematically evaluate common approaches within this setting. Our empirical analysis shows that standard CIL methods fail to yield meaningful gains in our more challenging scenario. Therefore, we propose Dynamic Adapter Routing (DAR), a novel approach based on adapters selected through prototype-based routing and combined via model achieves superior performance over the previous baselines and demonstrates strong generalization under out-of-distribution evaluation. Our results highlights the unique challenges of CMR and encourages further research in this direction.

</details>


### [120] [Inference-Free Multimodal Learned Sparse Retrieval for Production-Scale Visual Document Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30917)
*Gyu-Hwung Cho, Youngjune Lee, Kiyoon Jeong, Siyoung Lee, Sanggyu Han, Hervé Dejean, Stéphane Clinchant, Seung-won Hwang*

Main category: cs.IR, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (49.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 V-SPLADE，一种无需推理时神经编码的稀疏检索器，用于大规模视觉文档检索。通过引入标题门控 token 监督（caption-gated token supervision），利用 VLM 生成的标题作为词汇线索激活检索相关维度，解决了视觉稀疏表示难以捕获文档图像中词汇内容的问题。在六个基准上 NDCG@5 平均提升 +13.8pp，在 1870 万文档语料上 R@5 翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模视觉文档语料（如 arXiv 论文、企业 PDF）不断增长，视觉文档检索需求日益增加。现有方法要么使用 VLM 密集或多向量模型（检索质量高但推理时需要神经编码），要么使用 OCR 或标题的 BM25（避免查询编码但文本提取耗时）。缺少一种可部署的、能对视觉文档进行词汇索引且无需推理时神经编码的检索系统。

Method: 提出 V-SPLADE，一种基于 SPLADE 范式的推理无关稀疏检索器。核心创新是 caption-gated token supervision（标题门控 token 监督）：在训练时利用 VLM 生成的标题作为词汇线索，通过门控机制激活与检索相关的词汇维度，使视觉稀疏表示能够捕获文档图像中的词汇内容。该方法在训练后即可建立词汇索引，推理时无需任何神经编码。

Result: 在六个视觉文档检索基准上，V-SPLADE 的 NDCG@5 平均比同规模密集基线高 +13.8pp，比基于 OCR 或标题的 BM25 基线高 +6.3pp。在 1870 万文档的大规模语料上，R@5 比同规模密集基线翻倍以上，并通过分数融合使竞争检索器 R@5 进一步提升 +2.4pp。

Conclusion: V-SPLADE 填补了视觉文档检索中"推理无关稀疏检索"这一缺失的服务范式，证明了无需推理时神经编码的稀疏检索可以达到接近密集检索的效果，为大规模视觉文档检索提供了高效、可部署的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉文档检索（visual-document retrieval），属于跨模态/多模态检索范畴。multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下有 [CANONICAL] 的 multimodal_retrieval 子主题，且 multimodal_generation 方向下也有同名子主题，完美匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on retrieval efficiency for visual documents, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to generative or world model directions. Signals: inference-free retrieval, visual document search, sparse representation, caption-gated token supervision, production-scale

Abstract: As large-scale visual-document corpora such as arXiv papers and enterprise PDFs continue to grow, visual-document retrieval has gained increasing attention; yet it still lacks a deployable system that lexically indexes visual documents to serve queries without neural encoding at scale. Existing methods either achieve strong retrieval quality with VLM-based dense or multi-vector models but require neural query encoding at serving time, or avoid query encoding with OCR- or caption-based BM25 at the cost of time-consuming text extraction or generation. To fill this missing serving regime, we present V-SPLADE, an inference-free sparse retriever for visual-document retrieval. However, such inference-free multimodal learned sparse retrieval systems remain underexplored and have not yet shown dense-level effectiveness under high sparsity. We attribute this limitation to a lexical grounding problem: visual sparse representations often fail to capture the lexical content embedded in document images. To address this problem, we introduce caption-gated token supervision, a training-only signal that uses VLM-generated captions as lexical cues to activate retrieval-relevant vocabulary dimensions. With this supervision, V-SPLADE improves average NDCG@5 across six visual-document retrieval benchmarks by +13.8pp over the same-scale dense baseline and by up to +6.3pp over OCR- or caption-based BM25 baselines. On an 18.7M-document corpus, it more than doubles R@5 over the same-scale dense baseline and further improves competing retrievers through score fusion by up to +2.4pp R@5. Code will be released soon at .

</details>
