### [121] [FBHM: Functional Benchmarking and Steering of VLMs for Hateful Meme Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31349)
*Paramananda Bhaskar, Naquee Rizwan, Daksh Jogchand, Saurabh Kumar Pandey, Animesh Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL, cs.MM, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (51.14) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出FBHM，一个基于功能性的仇恨模因检测基准，沿修辞功能与目标社区两个正交轴构建5000个模因。评测发现现有VLM在该基准上性能骤降至接近随机水平，表明模型依赖数据集特定启发式而非鲁棒的多模态推理。作者提出LSV（可学习引导向量），仅需500个样本即可提升约30个Macro-F1点，优于上下文学习和PEFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仇恨模因检测基准是结构观察性的，混淆了修辞仇恨机制与目标社区特征，无法对模型漏洞进行因果评估。需要一种能够解耦功能性与社区特征的系统性基准，以揭示VLM的真实推理能力缺陷。

Method: 1) 构建FBHM基准：沿25种修辞功能×10个目标社区两个正交轴，系统生成5000个仇恨模因。2) 提出LSV（可学习引导向量）：在少量样本（500个，50个独特基础模因）上应用因果干预目标，学习引导向量来修正模型内部表征，从而提升检测性能。

Result: 现有SOTA VLM在FBHM上性能从标准数据集的~90%骤降至接近随机水平。LSV方法仅用500个样本即可提升约30个Macro-F1点，优于上下文学习和PEFT，且不降低源域性能。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在仇恨模因检测中依赖数据集特定启发式而非真正的多模态推理。FBHM基准为因果评估模型漏洞提供了有效工具，LSV方法为高效修复这些漏洞提供了轻量级方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了FBHM，一个针对仇恨模因检测的功能性基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。同时涉及生成式 AI方向的安全治理（safety_governance）相关内容，但核心贡献是评测基准。

Importance reason: Focuses on hateful meme detection, a niche multimodal understanding task, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: multimodal understanding, hateful meme detection, functional benchmark, VLM evaluation, low-data steering

Abstract: Hateful meme detection remains a formidable challenge for vision-language models, as existing benchmarks are structurally observational - confounding rhetorical hate mechanisms with target community features and preventing causal evaluation of model vulnerabilities. To address this, we introduce FBHM, a systematically curated benchmark of Functionality Based Hateful Memes constructed along two orthogonal axes: 25 distinct rhetorical functionalities and 10 target communities (5,000 memes total). Benchmarking state-of-the-art VLMs reveals a severe generalization gap: models highly accurate on standard datasets catastrophically drop to near-random performance on FBHM, proving they exploit dataset-specific heuristics rather than robust multimodal reasoning. To efficiently close this gap, we propose LSV (learnable steering vectors), an ultra-low data regime strategy that applies a causal intervention objective on as few as 500 steering samples (50 unique base memes), boosting FBHM performance by ~30 Macro-F1 points while outperforming in-context learning and PEFT without degrading source-domain performance.

</details>


### [122] [BilliardPhys-Bench: Benchmarking Physical Reasoning and Visual Dynamics of Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30900)
*Ben Wang, Xiaogang Li, Ruochen Gao, Peiyao Xiao, Chengliang Xu, Zeyu Wang, Zichao Chen, Bing Zhao, Hu Wei*

Main category: physics.app-ph, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (69.34) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: BilliardPhys-Bench 是一个用于评估多模态大模型在台球物理场景中直觉物理推理能力的基准测试，包含碰撞、反弹和最终位置预测等任务，揭示了模型存在"静态偏差"（stasis bias）等系统性失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态模型在静态图像识别上表现良好，但在直觉物理推理（如预测物体运动与交互）方面仍然薄弱，缺乏系统性的评测基准来揭示这些不足。

Method: 设计了一个程序化生成的合成台球环境基准，包含随机生成的场景（摩擦、弹性碰撞），测试三种能力：球间碰撞预测、墙壁反弹推理、运动停止后最终位置估计。评估了 GPT、Claude、Gemini、Qwen 等系列的最新 MLLM。

Result: 模型性能随模拟时间增加和场景几何复杂度上升而下降；发现了一个一致的失败模式——"静态偏差"（stasis bias）：当正确物理结果难以推断时，模型倾向于预测无交互发生。

Conclusion: 当前多模态大模型在视觉动力学推理上存在明显短板，需要更好的物理归纳偏置来改进多模态架构。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个用于评估多模态大模型物理推理能力的基准（BilliardPhys-Bench），属于多模态评测范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on physical reasoning benchmarking, not directly on generation or efficiency priorities. Signals: multimodal understanding benchmark, physical reasoning, stasis bias, synthetic billiards, not generation or efficiency

Abstract: Current multimodal models handle static image recognition well, but intuitive physical reasoning remains a weakness. Predicting how objects will move and interact from a single image is still difficult for these systems. We present BilliardPhys-Bench, a benchmark for physical reasoning in synthetic billiards environments. Its procedural engine generates randomized scenarios with friction and elastic collisions. The benchmark tests three abilities: (1) predicting ball-to-ball collisions, (2) reasoning about wall bounces, and (3) estimating final ball positions after motion stops. We evaluate recent MLLMs from the GPT, Claude, Gemini, and Qwen families. Performance drops as simulation time increases and scene geometry grows more complex. We also observe a consistent failure mode we call "stasis bias": when the correct physical outcome is harder to infer, models tend to predict no interaction. These findings show where current MLLMs break down on visual dynamics and point toward the need for better physical inductive biases in multimodal architectures.

</details>


### [123] [PInVerify: An Offline Embodied Benchmark for Active Instance Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30639)
*Yuhang Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (71.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Active Instance Verification (AIV) 任务，要求具身智能体主动选择视角验证候选物体是否匹配细粒度自然语言描述。作者构建了 PInVerify 离线基准（3000 个 episode，18 个类别），并基于开源 MLLM 构建了无需训练和 LoRA 微调的基线系统，实验表明最佳 MLLM 基线优于最佳嵌入基线 4.9 个百分点，LoRA 微调后达到 85.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身导航方法能引导智能体到达目标物体附近，但无法保证找到正确实例——细微属性差异（如"白色花卉" vs "白色条纹"）需要近距离多视角验证。现有基准缺乏对此类细粒度语义验证任务的系统评测。

Method: 形式化 AIV 为有限时域决策过程，构建 PInVerify 基准（3000 episode，18 类物体，6 扇区导航拓扑，包含陷阱视角和不可达扇区）。构建两类基线：1）无需训练 pipeline（属性分解 + 可见性加权多视角追踪 + 三种 next-best-view 策略）；2）LoRA 微调端到端智能体（SFT+GSPO）。评估多种 MLLM（Qwen3-VL 4B/8B、SenseNova-SI-1.2-InternVL3-8B）和视觉嵌入模型（CLIP、SigLIP2）。

Result: 最佳 MLLM 基线超过最佳嵌入基线 4.9 个百分点；GT-box 消融显示 +3.1 pp 检测差距；在测试的 NBV 策略中未观察到主动视角选择的可靠增益；LoRA 微调智能体（SFT+GSPO）达到 85.6%。

Conclusion: PInVerify 为具身 AI 中的主动细粒度语义验证提供了系统评测基准，当前基线表明 MLLM 在此任务上优于视觉嵌入模型，但主动视角选择策略尚未带来显著提升，有待进一步研究。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个具身智能体的主动实例验证（AIV）离线基准 PInVerify，属于多模态评测与基准范畴，与 canonical 子主题 multimodal_benchmark 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on embodied AI and multimodal benchmarks, which is less aligned with your priority on generative models and efficient inference. Signals: Embodied AI benchmark, Active Instance Verification, Multimodal understanding, LoRA fine-tuning, Next-best-view strategies

Abstract: Embodied agents have made strong progress in navigating to target objects, but reaching the goal vicinity does not guarantee that the agent has found the correct instance: subtle attribute differences (e.g., "white floral" vs. "white striped") often require close-range, multi-view inspection. We address this gap with Active Instance Verification (AIV), a task in which an agent actively selects viewpoints around a candidate object to decide whether it matches a fine-grained natural-language description. We formalize AIV as a finite-horizon decision process and introduce PInVerify, an offline embodied benchmark for AIV: 3,000 evaluation episodes across 18 object categories, delivered as multi-view captures with a 6-sector navigation topology that exposes trap views (navigable but uninformative) and unreachable sectors. As reference baselines we build a training-free pipeline and a LoRA-fine-tuned end-to-end agent around open-source multimodal large language models (MLLMs) at on-device scale ($\leq$8B parameters), with attribute decomposition, a visibility-weighted multi-view tracker, and three next-best-view (NBV) strategies. In our evaluation across Qwen3-VL (4B/8B), SenseNova-SI-1.2-InternVL3-8B, CLIP, and SigLIP2, the best MLLM-based baseline exceeds the best embedding baseline by 4.9 pp; GT-box ablations show a +3.1 pp detection gap; and we do not observe reliable gains from active viewpoint selection within the tested NBV strategies. A LoRA-fine-tuned agent (SFT+GSPO) reaches 85.6%. PInVerify aims to support further work on active, fine-grained semantic verification in embodied AI. Code: .

</details>


### [124] [FAM-Bench: A Multimodal Benchmark for Condition-Aware Food-as-Medicine Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31410)
*Mingyang Mao, Bhargav Rishi Medisetti, Utkarsh Grover, Tanvir Ibrahim, Wenyan Li, Tingting Zhang, Xiaomin Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (41.49) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 FAM-Bench，一个多模态食品即药物（Food-as-Medicine）推理基准，包含2500个营养专家验证的实例，覆盖13种饮食相关健康条件。基准包含两个互补任务：菜肴级别适宜性评估（判断一道菜是否适合特定健康状况）和比较性菜肴分析（对四道候选菜按条件适宜性排序）。该基准为评估语言和视觉语言模型在健康感知推理方面的能力提供了标准化测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的食品AI基准主要评估菜肴识别、食谱理解、营养估算或一般营养问答，缺乏对"特定食物选择是否适合特定健康状况"这一健康感知决策层的测试。作者旨在填补这一空白，构建一个标准化的多模态基准来评估模型在食品即药物场景下的条件感知推理能力。

Method: 构建了FAM-Bench基准，包含2500个营养专家验证的实例，覆盖13种饮食相关健康条件（如糖尿病、高血压、肾病等）。基准包含两个任务：(1) 菜肴级别适宜性评估——模型根据菜肴图像和配料列表判断是否适合特定健康状况；(2) 比较性菜肴分析——模型对四道候选菜按条件适宜性排序。两个任务都需要整合配料证据、视觉制备线索和临床营养约束。

Result: 论文构建了基准数据集并进行了专家验证，但摘要未报告具体模型评测结果。基准为后续评估语言和视觉语言模型在健康感知推理方面的能力提供了标准化测试平台。

Conclusion: FAM-Bench填补了食品AI领域在健康感知决策层评测的空白，为评估模型在食品即药物场景下的条件感知推理能力提供了标准化基准，有望推动多模态模型在健康营养领域的应用发展。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个多模态食品即药物（Food-as-Medicine）推理基准 FAM-Bench，包含2500个专家验证实例，评估模型在饮食相关健康条件下的推理能力。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题，因为论文核心贡献是构建了一个新的多模态评测基准。同时该基准涉及AI生成内容在健康领域的应用评估，也匹配生成式 AI方向下的领域内容生成应用（domain_application），但主要贡献是评测基准，因此主方向为多模态理解与生成。

Importance reason: Niche benchmark for food-as-medicine reasoning, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: Multimodal benchmark, Food-as-Medicine, health-aware reasoning, dish-level suitability, comparative dish analysis

Abstract: Food-as-Medicine requires models to reason beyond what a dish is or what nutrition it contains: they must decide whether a concrete food choice is appropriate for a specific health condition. Existing food AI benchmarks primarily evaluate dish recognition, recipe understanding, nutrient estimation, or general nutrition question answering, leaving this health-aware decision layer largely untested. We introduce FAM-Bench, a multi-modal Food-as-Medicine benchmark with 2500 nutrition-expert-verified instances across 13 diet-related health conditions. The benchmark contains two complementary tasks: dish-level suitability assessment, where models judge whether a dish is suitable for a condition from its image and ingredient list, and comparative dish analysis, where models rank four candidate dishes by condition-specific suitability. Both tasks require integrating ingredient evidence, visual preparation cues, and clinical nutrition constraints, providing a standardized testbed for grounded health-aware reasoning in language and vision-language models.

</details>


### [125] [Does Visual Information Play a Decisive Role in Vision-Language-Action Model Driving Behavior?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31041)
*Jingtao He, Hongliang Lu, Xiaoyun Qiu, Yixuan Wang, Xinhu Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (71.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多级视觉扰动框架，系统分析VLA（视觉-语言-动作）自动驾驶模型对视觉信息的依赖程度，发现依赖模式随评估方式（开环vs闭环）和视觉抽象层级而变化，揭示了现有VLA模型视觉基础的不均衡性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA驾驶模型评估仅关注聚合性能指标，缺乏对视觉信息如何影响驾驶行为的结构化诊断工具，难以理解模型是否真正"看到"并依赖视觉信息做出决策。

Method: 提出一个多级视觉扰动框架，沿三个维度组织受控视觉扰动：通道级退化（颜色/噪声）、信息级破坏（遮挡/模糊）、结构级修改（语义编辑）。应用于VLA驾驶系统，在开环轨迹预测和交互式闭环安全评估两种设置下评估行为响应。

Result: 实验发现视觉依赖模式与评估方式高度相关：开环下模型对某些扰动不敏感，闭环下则表现出不同的依赖模式；模型在不同视觉抽象层级上的视觉基础不均衡，某些层级过度依赖而另一些层级利用不足。

Conclusion: 现有VLA驾驶模型对视觉信息的利用存在结构性缺陷，需要更结构化的分析和更原则性的模型设计来理解视觉信息如何塑造驾驶行为，从而构建更安全、更鲁棒的自动驾驶系统。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种结构化多级视觉扰动框架，用于系统分析VLA驾驶模型对视觉信息的依赖程度，本质上是一种针对VLA模型的诊断性评测方法，属于多模态评测与基准范畴，因此复用[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Systematic analysis of VLA visual grounding is valuable but the autonomous driving domain is not a user priority. Signals: VLA model analysis, visual perturbation framework, autonomous driving evaluation, uneven visual grounding

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated promising capability in autonomous driving, highlighting the potential of unified multimodal architectures for jointly modeling perception and planning. However, how current VLA-based driving behavior is grounded in visual information remains poorly understood. Existing evaluation protocols mainly focus on aggregate performance metrics, lacking structured and practical diagnostics to quantify visual-behavior dependency. In this work, we introduce a structured multi-level visual perturbation framework to analyze visual-behavior dependency in VLA-based driving models systematically. The framework organizes controlled visual perturbations along three complementary dimensions: channellevel degradation, information-level disruption, and structurelevel modification. We apply it to VLA-based driving systems and evaluate behavioral responses under both open-loop trajectory prediction and interactive closed-loop safety evaluation. Experimental results reveal evaluation-dependent dependency patterns and uneven visual grounding across abstraction levels. These findings call for more structured analyses and principled design of VLA driving models to better understand how visual information shapes behavior and develop safer, more robust systems.

</details>


### [126] [TeachObs: A Human-Validated Benchmark for Multimodal Teaching Observation and Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30673)
*Yeil Jeong, Youngjin Yoo, Seobin Sohn, Hyejin Han, Jinseo Lee, Scott Howard, Unggi Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (46.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了 TeachObs，一个经人工验证的多模态课堂教学观察基准。包含来自8个国家的30节公开课视频，分割为5158个15秒片段，由7位研究者标注39个二值观察编码（20个视觉编码+19个非视觉编码），并构建了基于信度和流行度的金标签。同时有3位专家对30节课进行课程级评分。基于此基准评估了5个视觉语言模型在三个赛道上的表现，发现没有单一模型在所有赛道上一致最优，添加中间帧会同时增加真/假归因，且模型评分相对于专家评分高估了流程清晰的课程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 课堂教学视频中包含丰富的可观察教学实践，但其教学和视觉信号很少以适合模型评估的形式组织。现有基准缺乏对课堂教学视频中多模态教学行为的细粒度标注和系统评估，因此需要构建一个经人工验证的基准来评估AI系统在课堂视频分析中的能力。

Method: 收集30节来自8个国家的公开课视频，分割为5158个15秒场景片段。7位研究者对每个片段标注39个二值观察编码（20个视觉编码如手势、板书、指向、视觉材料等，19个非视觉编码如指导、监控、提问、反馈、反思等）。基于Krippendorff's alpha构建信度和流行度感知的金标签。3位专家对30节课进行课程级评分（教学设计、教学传递、学习者反应、学习材料、课程收尾）。使用5个视觉语言模型在三个赛道（纯文本片段编码、文本+帧片段编码、LLM-as-judge课程级评分）上进行评估。

Result: 没有单一模型在所有三个评估赛道上一致优于其他模型；添加中间帧会同时增加每个场景的真归因和假归因；模型评估相对于专家评分高估了流程清晰的课程。TeachObs支持细粒度标注基准测试和完整课程评估，揭示了AI系统在课堂视频分析中的能力边界。

Conclusion: TeachObs为多模态课堂教学观察提供了首个经人工验证的基准，支持细粒度标注基准测试和完整课程评估。实验表明当前视觉语言模型在课堂视频分析中仍有局限，特别是在需要专家判断的方面。该基准可用于评估AI系统在课堂视频分析中的辅助能力，并明确专家判断仍然必要的领域。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个针对课堂教学视频的多模态观察基准 TeachObs，包含人工标注的视觉/非视觉教学行为编码和课程级评分，并评估了多个视觉语言模型。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范主题，同时与 生成式 AI 评测（evaluation_benchmark）也有一定关联。

Importance reason: This paper focuses on a niche educational benchmark, not on the user's priority subtopics like autoregressive video generation or world models. Signals: multimodal benchmark, classroom video, education, not generative, not video generation

Abstract: Classroom videos contain observable teaching practices, but their pedagogical and visual signals are rarely organized in forms suitable for model evaluation. We present \textit{TeachObs}, a human-validated benchmark for multimodal teaching observation in classroom videos. \textit{TeachObs} includes 30 public lesson videos from eight countries divided into 5,158 fixed 15-second scenes. Seven researchers annotated each scene with 39 binary observation codes, covering 20 visual codes, such as gesture, board work, pointing, and visual materials, and 19 nonvisual codes, such as instruction, monitoring, questioning, feedback, and reflection. Gold segment labels are constructed using reliability- and prevalence-aware rules based on Krippendorff's alpha. In addition to segment-level labels, three expert raters produced lesson-level ratings and qualitative evaluations of instructional design, instructional delivery, learner response, learning materials, and lesson closure across the 30 lessons, with rater coverage detailed in the body. Using these two human reference layers, we evaluate five vision-capable frontier LLMs across three tracks - text-only segment coding, text + frame segment coding, and lesson-level coverage scored under an LLM-as-judge protocol - and find that no single model consistently outperforms others across all three tracks, that adding a mid-frame inflates both true and false attributions per scene, and that model evaluations over-rate procedurally clear lessons relative to expert raters. \textit{TeachObs} therefore supports both fine-grained annotation benchmarking and whole-lesson evaluation, showing where AI systems can assist classroom video analysis and where expert judgment remains necessary across varied subjects, classroom formats, and annotation difficulty levels.

</details>


### [127] [Probing Collision Grounding in Vision-Language Models for Safe Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31196)
*Jun Wang, Xiaohao Xu, Xiaonan Huang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL, cs.RO, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (49.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 TouchSafeBench，一个基于 Habitat 3.0 构建的物理仿真基准，用于评估视觉语言模型在安全人机协作中的碰撞接地能力。该基准包含 2,940 个室内共处场景，涵盖社交导航与社交重排任务，并提供多视角 RGB-D 观测、轨迹地图、相机元数据和接触标签。实验表明，当前前沿 VLM 的最佳平均 Macro-F1 低于 50%，显式深度信息未能自动转化为碰撞证据，机器人-场景接触比人-机器人接触更难判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全人机协作需要监控系统判断机器人是否与场景或人发生碰撞或即将碰撞，而现有 VLM 缺乏这种"碰撞接地"能力。作者希望构建一个物理仿真的基准来系统评估 VLM 在这方面的局限性。

Method: 在 Habitat 3.0 中构建 TouchSafeBench 基准，包含 2,940 个模拟室内共处场景（社交导航和社交重排），提供同步多视角 RGB-D 观测、俯视轨迹地图、校准相机元数据和仿真器导出的接触标签。定义了两个任务：当前安全状态分类和即将碰撞预警。评估了三个前沿/机器人导向 VLM 和九种视觉表征。

Result: 最佳平均 Macro-F1 低于 50%，显式深度信息未能自动转化为机器人-身体碰撞证据，机器人-场景接触比人-机器人接触更难判断。当前 VLM 在碰撞接地任务上远未达到可靠水平。

Conclusion: 视觉流畅性不等于物理可问责性。可靠的机器人安全监控需要能够显式绑定视角、机器人形态、度量几何和未来碰撞的表征。TouchSafeBench 揭示了具身 VLM 的核心局限性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 TouchSafeBench，一个用于评估视觉语言模型（VLM）碰撞接地能力的物理仿真基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM safety evaluation for robotics, not on core generative modeling or efficiency priorities. Signals: VLM benchmark, collision grounding, human-robot collaboration, safety evaluation, Habitat 3.0

Abstract: Safe human--robot collaboration requires more than visual description: a monitor must determine whether the robot body is safely separated, already colliding with the scene or a person, or about to collide. We call this capability collision grounding: binding visual observations to robot body geometry, camera viewpoint, scene layout, human proximity, and temporal motion in order to infer present and imminent contact. We introduce TouchSafeBench, a physics-grounded benchmark for evaluating collision grounding in vision-language models (VLMs). Built in Habitat~3.0, TouchSafeBench contains 2,940 simulated indoor co-presence episodes across social navigation and social rearrangement, with synchronized multi-view RGB-D observations, top-down trajectory maps, calibrated camera metadata, and simulator-derived contact labels. We study two deployment-facing tasks: classifying the current safety state and warning about imminent collision before contact. Across three frontier or robotics-oriented VLMs and nine visual representations, current models remain far from reliable: the best average Macro-F1 stays below 50\%, explicit depth is not automatically transformed into robot-body collision evidence, and robot--scene contact is consistently harder than human-contact risk. TouchSafeBench reveals a central limitation of embodied VLMs: visual fluency does not imply physical accountability. Reliable robot safety monitors will need representations that explicitly bind viewpoint, robot morphology, metric geometry, and future collision. We will release the benchmark upon acceptance.

</details>


### [128] [ERGeoBench:A Comprehensive Benchmark for Embodied Reasoning and Geo-localization in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31251)
*Kaiwen Xue, Tao Wei, Guoxin Zhang, Zhonghong Ou, Kaoyan Lu, Yu Feng, Yifan Zhu, Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (69.34) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ERGeoBench是一个面向具身地理定位的多模态大模型诊断基准，包含2207个全球街景全景图，在单视图、全景视图和具身视图三种设置下评估基础感知、空间意识、常识推理和地理定位推理四种能力。实验表明现有MLLM能推断高层地理语义，但在细粒度感知、度量定位和跨视图空间一致性上仍存在困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在具身智能方面展现出潜力，但具身地理定位（embodied geo-localization）由于缺乏细粒度评测而未被充分探索。作者希望构建一个诊断性基准来系统评估MLLM在视觉驱动的具身地理定位任务上的能力。

Method: 构建ERGeoBench基准，包含2207个全球分布的街景全景图，设计三种渐进式评测设置（单视图、全景视图、具身视图），其中具身视图允许智能体通过偏航、俯仰和缩放的顺序变化主动获取观测。基准测量四种互补能力：基础感知、空间意识、常识推理和地理定位推理。

Result: 对主流闭源和开源MLLM的评估显示：当前模型能推断高层地理语义，但在细粒度感知操作、度量定位和跨视图空间一致性上表现不佳。地理定位与其他能力维度高度相关，表明准确定位依赖于感知、空间推理和常识推断的整合。

Conclusion: ERGeoBench为诊断和推进类人具身地理定位提供了统一框架，揭示了当前MLLM在该任务上的关键短板，为未来研究指明了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了ERGeoBench，一个用于具身地理定位的多模态大模型诊断基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题，因此直接复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Benchmark for embodied geo-localization is niche and not aligned with user's priority subtopics on generation, efficiency, or world models. Signals: Embodied geo-localization benchmark, MLLM evaluation, fine-grained perception gap, not generation or efficiency focused, no autoregressive or diffusion video/image generation

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong potential as embodied agents, yet embodied geo-localization remains underexplored due to the lack of fine-grained evaluation. We introduce ERGeoBench, a diagnostic benchmark for vision-driven embodied geo-localization. ERGeoBench evaluates models under three progressive settings -- single-view, panorama-view, and embodied-view -- where agents may actively acquire observations through sequential changes in yaw, pitch, and zoom. The benchmark contains 2,207 globally distributed street-view panoramas and measures four complementary capabilities: foundational perception, spatial awareness, common sense reasoning, and geo-localization reasoning. Evaluations of leading proprietary and open-source MLLMs show that current models can infer high-level geographic semantics, but still struggle with fine-grained perceptual operations, metric localization, and spatial consistency across views. We further observe that geo-localization is strongly correlated with the other capability dimensions, suggesting that accurate localization depends on integrated perception, spatial reasoning, and commonsense inference rather than isolated visual recognition. Overall, ERGeoBench provides a unified framework for diagnosing and advancing human-like embodied geo-localization. Project Page:

</details>


### [129] [MineExplorer: Evaluating Open-World Exploration of MLLM Agents in Minecraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30931)
*Tianjie Ju, Yueqing Sun, Zheng Wu, Wei Zhang, Yaqi Huo, Xi Su, Qi Gu, Xunliang Cai, Gongshen Liu, Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (74.15) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MineExplorer，一个用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLM）智能体在 Minecraft 开放世界中探索能力的基准。通过过滤依赖特定游戏知识的原子任务、构建 ReAct 风格的多跳任务，并采用多智能体合成工作流生成可靠的评测实例，实验表明开放世界探索对现有 MLLM 智能体仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 智能体评测多局限于短时程任务或与特定游戏机制深度耦合，难以反映智能体在动态开放世界中的持续探索能力。缺乏一个专注于开放世界探索能力评估的标准化基准。

Method: 1) 过滤 Minecraft 中依赖特定知识的原子任务，保留更通用的开放世界推理任务；2) 基于 ReAct 框架组织能力公式，将原子任务组合为隐式多跳任务；3) 使用多智能体合成工作流联合设计任务图、沙盒场景和基于规则的里程碑评估器，生成可靠评测实例。

Result: 人类评估表明多智能体合成工作流生成的实例显著优于单智能体基线。实验显示开放世界探索仍具挑战性：强模型能处理许多单跳任务，但在需要协调隐藏前提条件的较长轨迹中性能急剧下降。任务难度与智能体完成度相关，更大模型或思维模式并不一致地带来更好性能。

Conclusion: MineExplorer 为评估 MLLM 智能体的开放世界探索能力提供了有效基准，揭示了当前模型在长时程多跳任务中的局限性，为未来研究指明了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 MineExplorer 基准，用于评估 MLLM 智能体在 Minecraft 开放世界中的探索能力，属于多模态智能体评测。这与 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准 (multimodal_benchmark) 高度匹配，因为论文核心贡献是一个新的评测基准。同时，论文使用了多智能体合成工作流来构建评测实例，也与 生成式 AI 方向下的 AI 智能体与生成工作流 (agent_workflow) 相关。

Importance reason: Evaluates MLLM agents in open-world exploration, but focuses on a specific game benchmark rather than core model advances. Signals: MLLM agent evaluation, open-world exploration, Minecraft benchmark, multi-agent synthesis, ReAct-style tasks

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong capabilities in perception, reasoning, and action generation. However, their ability to sustain exploration in dynamic open worlds remains unclear. Existing embodied and game-based benchmarks often compress interaction into short-horizon tasks or entangle success with domain-specific game mechanics. In this paper, we introduce MineExplorer benchmark for evaluating open-world exploration capabilities of MLLM agents in Minecraft. We first filter atomic tasks whose solutions rely heavily on Minecraft-specific knowledge to better reflect general open-world reasoning. Then we organize the benchmark around a ReAct-style capability formulation and compose atomic tasks into implicit multi-hop tasks. To further construct reliable instances, MineExplorer uses a multi-agent synthesis workflow that jointly designs task graphs, sandbox scenes, and rule-based milestone evaluators. Human evaluation shows that the multi-agent synthesis workflow produces significantly more reliable instances than a single-agent baseline. Experiments with advanced MLLM agents show that open-world exploration remains challenging, as strong models can handle many single-hop tasks but degrade sharply when hidden prerequisites must be coordinated over longer trajectories. Further analysis finds that task difficulty tracks agent completion, and larger models or thinking modes do not consistently translate into better performance. Code and dataset are available at .

</details>


### [130] [Beyond Static Dialogues: Benchmarking Realistic, Heterogeneous, and Evolving Long-Term Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31086)
*Han Zhang, Zihao Tang, Xin Yu, Xiao Liu, Yeyun Gong, Haizhen Huang, Yan Lu, Weiwei Deng, Feng Sun, Qi Zhang, Hanfang Yang*

Main category: cs.IR, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出RHELM基准，通过精心设计的用户画像和LOOP模块（规划-展开-演化-剪枝），构建具有长期语义一致性和动态时间演进的真实对话场景，并整合异构外部数据源（文档、邮件等），生成涵盖7种查询类型、27种记忆特征的问答对，系统评估了全上下文模型、RAG方法和代表性记忆框架在复杂真实场景中的长期记忆能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM记忆基准存在三个关键缺陷：对话缺乏长期语义一致性、用户画像扁平静态、交互数据流单一（仅限对话），无法反映真实世界中用户与助手交互的多样性和异构性（如文档、邮件等数据流），导致评测缺乏现实性和有效性。

Method: 提出RHELM基准，核心包含：(1) 精心设计的用户画像，包含丰富的个人背景和偏好；(2) LOOP模块（pLan-rOllout-evOlve-Prune），通过规划、展开、演化、剪枝四个步骤生成具有动态时间演进和长期一致性的真实对话；(3) 将对话与异构外部源（文档、邮件等）深度集成，并与用户时间事件轨迹同步；(4) 构建涵盖7种查询类型、27种关键记忆特征的问答对评测集。

Result: 对全上下文模型、RAG方法和代表性记忆框架的综合实验表明，现有方法在复杂真实场景中仍存在关键弱点，特别是在多源聚合和真实世界上下文推理方面表现不足。

Conclusion: 当前LLM记忆方法在真实、异构、演进的长期记忆场景中仍面临重大挑战，尤其是在多源信息聚合和上下文推理方面，RHELM基准为未来研究提供了更贴近实际需求的评测平台。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了RHELM基准，用于评测LLM在真实、异构、演进的长期记忆场景中的表现，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题。虽然论文主要聚焦于文本对话和记忆，但其评测涉及多源异构数据（文档、邮件等）和复杂推理，与多模态评测基准的范畴高度吻合。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for long-term memory in LLMs but focuses on dialogue and retrieval, not the user's priority generative video/image or efficient inference topics. Signals: long-term memory benchmark, heterogeneous data streams, LOOP module, 27 memory characteristics, cs.IR, cs.CL categories

Abstract: In existing memory benchmarks for Large Language Models (LLMs), the evaluated dialogue sessions often lack long-term semantic consistency, and the underlying personas tend to be flat and static. Furthermore, in real-world scenarios, interactions between users and assistants involve more diverse, heterogeneous data streams, such as documents and emails. These shortcomings significantly limit the realism and effectiveness of current evaluations. To address these limitations, we introduce RHELM (Realistic, Heterogeneous, and Evolving Long-term Memory). Driven by meticulously crafted user profiles and a novel LOOP (pLan-rOllout-evOlve-Prune) module, we construct realistic dialogues across diverse interaction scenarios that exhibit dynamic temporal evolution and long-term coherence. Crucially, these dialogues are deeply integrated with heterogeneous external sources synchronized with the user's temporal event trajectory. The resulting benchmark encompasses challenging question-answer pairs spanning seven inquiry types, with each question mapping to at least one of 27 critical memory characteristics that we identify as essential yet underexplored in current research. Comprehensive experiments across full-context models, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) methods, and representative memory frameworks reveal that contemporary approaches still expose critical weaknesses in complex, real-world settings, particularly in resolving multi-source aggregation and real-world contextual reasoning.

</details>


### [131] [SVI-Bench: A Dynamic Microworld for Strategic Video Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31529)
*Yulu Pan, Han Yi, Seongsu Ha, Md Mohaiminul Islam, Benjamin Zhang, Lorenzo Torresani, Gedas Bertasius*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (63.91) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SVI-Bench 是一个利用团队体育作为动态微世界的大规模基准，包含约 35K 小时广播视频、15M 标注动作等数据，构建了从感知到因果推理、战略模拟和智能体合成的四层九任务评测体系。实验发现当前最强多模态模型在感知任务上表现尚可（~73%），但在因果推理和战略规划任务上急剧下降，智能体任务准确率仅 5%，揭示了视频智能的"能力悬崖"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频基准要么使用真实世界视频但缺乏因果和战略问题的可验证真实标签，要么使用合成环境但牺牲了真实多智能体系统的复杂性。作者希望填补这一空白，构建一个兼具真实世界复杂性和可验证性的视频智能评测基准。

Method: 利用团队体育（篮球、足球、冰球）作为动态微世界，通过数据引擎将原始比赛数据转换为密集交叉引用的语料库，包含广播视频、动作标注、专家解说、比赛报告和统计数据。将评测组织为四个层级（动态场景理解、因果推理、战略模拟、智能体合成）共 9 个任务。

Result: 最强模型在细粒度动作问答上达到约 73% 准确率，但在因果推理任务上显著下降，战略模拟任务更难，智能体合成任务中最佳模型仅 5% 准确率。模型在需要自主收集和整合来自 180 万片段语料库的证据时表现极差。

Conclusion: 当前多模态和智能体模型在视频智能上存在显著的"能力悬崖"：感知能力尚可，但因果推理、战略模拟和自主智能体能力严重不足。SVI-Bench 为未来视频智能研究提供了一个具有挑战性的评测平台。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 SVI-Bench，一个大规模多模态评测基准，用于评估从感知到因果推理、模拟和战略规划的视频智能能力。这直接对应 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准 (multimodal_benchmark) 子主题。同时，论文涉及视频生成式世界模型/模拟（Strategic Simulation 任务），因此也匹配 multimodal_generation 方向下的 video_world_model 子主题。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for video intelligence hierarchy, directly relevant to multimodal understanding and evaluation. Signals: video intelligence benchmark, causal reasoning, strategic simulation, agentic synthesis, multimodal evaluation

Abstract: True video intelligence demands more than recognizing what is visible: it requires reasoning about why events unfold, predicting what would change under different conditions, and deciding what to do next. We refer to this progression, from perception through causal reasoning and simulation to strategic planning, as Strategic Video Intelligence (SVI). No existing benchmark evaluates this capability stack: in-the-wild videos lack verifiable ground truth for causal and strategic questions, while synthetic environments sacrifice the complexity of real multi-agent systems. To bridge this gap, we introduce SVI-Bench, a large-scale benchmark that leverages team sports as a dynamic microworld, combining the complexity of real-world multi-agent interaction (10-22 agents making coordinated decisions under adversarial pressure) with the verifiability of explicit rules and definitive outcomes. SVI-Bench comprises approximately 35K hours of broadcast video, 15M annotated actions, 15K hours of expert commentary, 23K game reports, and 103K structured statistical records across basketball, soccer, and hockey, all constructed via a data engine that transforms raw game data into a dense, cross-referenced corpus. We organize evaluation into 9 tasks spanning a progressive four-pillar hierarchy: Dynamic Scene Understanding, Causal Reasoning, Strategic Simulation, and Agentic Synthesis. Evaluating strong multimodal and agentic baselines, we find a capability cliff: models perform competently on perceptual tasks, achieving approximately 73% on fine-grained action QA, but degrade sharply at each successive cognitive level. Agentic tasks prove hardest: the strongest model achieves only 5% accuracy when required to autonomously gather and integrate evidence across a corpus of 1.8M clips.

</details>


### [132] [On the Robustness of Multilingual Text Embedding Rankings Across Learning Tasks, Languages, and Benchmark Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31142)
*Ana Gjorgjevikj, Barbara Koroušić Seljak, Tome Eftimov*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文对MTEB基准中多语言文本嵌入模型的排名鲁棒性进行了元研究，提出了数据集组成鲁棒性和排名方案鲁棒性两个指标，分析了五种主要语言和约230种其他语言上模型排名对评估设计变化的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MTEB等基准平台报告了250多种语言的结果，但关于模型优越性的结论往往依赖于数据集组成和性能聚合方法的隐式选择，模型在语言特定、多任务设置下的行为尚未被充分理解。

Method: 采用多准则决策排序方案，引入数据集组成鲁棒性和排名方案鲁棒性两个指标，对五种语言（英语、法语、德语、印地语、西班牙语）在九项任务上进行深入分析，并扩展至约230种其他语言。

Result: 大规模基于LLM的模型通常是鲁棒的顶级表现者，但在检索任务中并非一致；任务无关分析显示只有少数模型在跨任务、排序方案和数据子样本中保持稳定强表现。

Conclusion: 多语言文本嵌入模型的排名对评估设计选择敏感，基准结论的鲁棒性需要系统性分析，仅少数模型能在不同设置下保持一致的优越性能。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多语言文本嵌入模型在MTEB基准上的排名鲁棒性，属于多模态/多语言评测的元分析，与[多模态评测与基准]（multimodal_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on multilingual embedding ranking robustness, not aligned with user's priority on generative video/image models or efficient inference. Signals: Multilingual text embeddings, MTEB benchmark, ranking robustness, not generative, no video/image generation

Abstract: Large-scale multilingual text embedding models play crucial role in both research and industry, yet their behavior in language-specific, multi-task settings remains insufficiently understood. Although benchmarking platforms such as MTEB report results across more than 250 languages, conclusions about model superiority often depend on implicit choices of dataset compositions and performance aggregation methods. To address this gap, we present a meta-study of multilingual model performance robustness in MTEB, applying a diverse set of multi-criteria decision-making ranking schemes and introducing two robustness indicators: dataset-composition robustness (sensitivity of rankings to changing dataset compositions) and ranking-scheme robustness (sensitivity to aggregation method change). They enable systematic sensitivity analysis of whether benchmarking conclusions remain stable under different evaluation designs. We conduct an in-depth analysis on five languages (English, French, German, Hindi, and Spanish) across nine tasks (e.g., classification, clustering, retrieval) and release results for approximately 230 additional languages. The task-specific analyses show that large-scale LLM-based models are often robust top performers, though not uniformly (e.g., in retrieval task), while task-agnostic results reveal that only a small subset of models remains consistently strong across tasks, ranking schemes, and data subsamples.

</details>


### [133] [EGOSTREAM: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Streaming Episodic Memory in Egocentric Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31557)
*Rosario Forte, Giuseppe Lando, Antonino Furnari*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (69.34) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: EGOSTREAM是一个用于第一视角视频流式情景记忆评测的诊断基准，包含2250个覆盖7个认知维度的问答，并引入答案有效期窗口（AVW）概念，支持从即时到超长期的记忆评测。在统一Qwen3-VL框架下对多种记忆管理机制进行基准测试，发现不同机制在记忆特性上差异显著，且所有方法均远低于实时处理速度，最高准确率仅约45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体在动态真实环境中需要持续的 episodic memory（情景记忆）能力，但现有流式视频基准缺乏对模型"记住了什么、记住了多久"的诊断性评测工具。

Method: 构建EGOSTREAM基准：从Ego4D中筛选视频片段，设计2250个问题覆盖7个认知维度（细节、空间、时间、事件、社交、因果、前瞻记忆）；引入AVW（答案有效期窗口）概念，将问题扩展为8528个回忆条件评测，区分模型遗忘与自然世界状态变化。在统一Qwen3-VL框架下集成多种记忆管理机制（滑动窗口、attention sinks、KV缓存剪枝/合并/卸载）进行系统对比。

Result: 不同记忆管理机制在相同聚合准确率下表现出截然不同的记忆特性：token剪枝在保留细粒度细节和时间结构上显著优于token合并；量化卸载可改善超长期记忆；所有方法均远低于实时处理速度（>1秒/帧）；最优方法准确率仅约45%。

Conclusion: EGOSTREAM揭示了当前流式MLLM在持续情景记忆方面的严重不足，为未来架构改进提供了诊断性测试平台。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了EGOSTREAM，一个用于第一视角视频流式情景记忆评测的诊断基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Strong diagnostic benchmark for episodic memory in video, but misaligned with user's priority on generative models and world models. Signals: egocentric video benchmark, episodic memory, diagnostic evaluation, streaming MLLM, memory management mechanisms

Abstract: Continuous episodic memory is a core capability for autonomous agents operating in dynamic, real-world environments, yet current streaming video benchmarks provide limited tools for diagnosing what models remember and for how long. We introduce \egostream, a diagnostic benchmark for streaming episodic memory evaluation in egocentric vision. \egostream organizes 2,250 curated questions along seven cognitive dimensions: detail, spatial, temporal, event, social, causal, and prospective memory. We introduce the Answer Validity Window (AVW), which specifies the temporal span an answer remains valid as the observed scene evolves. This allows us to expand the questions into 8,528 recall-conditioned evaluations, enabling controlled testing from instant to ultra-long-term recall while separating genuine model forgetting from natural world-state changes. We rigorously establish baseline performance through a unified streaming MLLM framework that compares several state-of-the-art memory-management mechanisms, covering sliding windows, attention sinks, KV-cache pruning, merging, and offloading. Experiments within a unified Qwen3-VL backbone reveal that comparable aggregate accuracies mask starkly different memory profiles. For instance, token pruning preserves fine-grained details and temporal structure significantly better than token merging, while quantized offloading rescues ultra-long-term recall. Ultimately, all mechanisms operate well below real-time (>1s per frame), and top performing methods ceil at about 45\% accuracy, exposing critical gaps in current architectures. \egostream provides the diagnostic testbed needed to close these gaps.

</details>


### [134] [nuReasoning: A Reasoning-Centric Dataset and Benchmark for Long-Tail Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31572)
*Zhiyu Huang, Johnson Liu, Rui Song, Zewei Zhou, Ruining Yang, Yun Zhang, Tianhui Cai, Hanyin Zhang, Mingxuan Gao, Valeria Xu, Jiali Chen, Yishan Shen, Yiluan Guo, Tony, Jiaqi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.71) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: nuReasoning 是一个面向长尾自动驾驶场景的大规模真实世界推理数据集与基准，包含 20,000 个 20 秒长的视频片段，覆盖空间推理、决策推理和反事实推理三类标注。实验表明，在该数据集上微调 VLM 能显著提升驾驶场景问答能力，且将推理监督融入 VLA 训练可改善规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶数据集和基准主要关注感知、预测或规划任务，缺乏对长尾场景中推理能力的充分监督。自动驾驶系统需要在复杂长尾场景中运用常识、理解空间关系、推断智能体交互并做出安全决策，因此亟需一个以推理为中心的基准来评估和提升模型的推理能力。

Method: 基于 nuScenes 和 nuPlan 数据，构建了包含 20,000 个 20 秒长视频片段的数据集，覆盖多个城市，包含同步多相机图像、LiDAR 数据、高清地图、目标标注以及人工验证的推理标注（空间推理、决策推理、反事实推理）。支持推理评估和规划评估，可直接研究推理监督对驾驶性能的影响。

Result: 在 nuReasoning 上微调 VLM 显著提升了驾驶场景问答能力；将推理监督融入 VLA 训练后，即使在推理时禁用文本推理输出，规划性能也得到了改善。

Conclusion: nuReasoning 为评估和改进长尾场景中鲁棒、可解释、推理驱动的自动驾驶系统提供了基础性基准，证明了推理监督对驾驶性能的积极作用。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 nuReasoning，一个面向自动驾驶长尾场景推理的大规模真实世界数据集与基准，包含空间推理、决策推理和反事实推理标注。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）范畴，因为其核心贡献是构建了一个新的多模态推理评测基准。同时，该数据集也涉及自动驾驶领域的生成/规划任务，因此也匹配 multimodal_generation 方向。

Importance reason: Strong dataset contribution for autonomous driving reasoning, but low alignment with user's priority subtopics on generative models and efficient inference. Signals: Long-tail autonomous driving, reasoning dataset, VLM fine-tuning, planning improvement, multimodal understanding benchmark

Abstract: Reasoning is essential for autonomous driving (AD) in long-tail scenarios, where vehicles must apply commonsense knowledge, understand spatial relations, infer agent interactions, and make safe decisions. However, existing AD datasets and benchmarks mainly target perception, prediction, or planning, and provide limited supervision for reasoning over realistic long-tail driving scenes. We introduce nuReasoning, a large-scale real-world dataset and benchmark for reasoning-centric AD. Following the lineage of nuScenes and nuPlan, nuReasoning advances real-world AD datasets and benchmarks toward reasoning in long-tail driving scenarios. The dataset contains 20,000 clips, each 20 seconds long, collected across multiple cities, with synchronized multi-camera images, LiDAR data, HD maps, object annotations, and human-verified reasoning annotations spanning Spatial Reasoning, Decision Reasoning, and Counterfactual Reasoning. Unlike prior datasets that focus primarily on visual question answering, nuReasoning supports both reasoning evaluation and planning evaluation, enabling a direct study of how reasoning supervision affects driving performance. Experiments show that fine-tuning VLMs on nuReasoning substantially improves driving-specific question answering, while incorporating reasoning supervision into VLA training improves planning performance even when textual reasoning outputs are disabled at inference time. These results establish nuReasoning as a foundation for evaluating and improving robust, interpretable, reasoning-driven AD systems in realistic long-tail settings.

</details>


### [135] [Recognizing Co-Speech Gestures in-the-Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31589)
*Sindhu B Hegde, K R Prajwal, Andrew Zisserman*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (51.14) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了 GRW（Gesture Recognition in the Wild）数据集，这是首个大规模、帧级标注的共语手势识别基准，包含 156,688 个视频片段和 150 个语义词汇类别，用于训练和评估视频模型在语义手势分类、词汇识别和时序定位三个任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在说话时自然伴随手势，但只有少数手势具有视觉描绘性并与特定口语词汇存在语义关联。当前多模态模型难以捕捉这些语义共语手势，主要瓶颈在于缺乏精确标注的训练数据。

Method: 构建 GRW 数据集：包含 156,688 个人工标注的视频片段，覆盖 150 个词汇（物理动作、空间描述符、抽象概念），提供帧级精确的时序边界标注。基于该数据集训练视频模型完成三项任务：(a) 手势是否为语义手势的二分类，(b) 识别手势对应的词汇，(c) 时序定位手势起止位置。

Result: 建立了三个任务的基准评测结果，展示了当前视频模型在语义共语手势识别上的能力与局限，GRW 数据集为后续研究提供了标准化评估平台。

Conclusion: GRW 数据集填补了语义共语手势识别领域大规模精确标注数据的空白，为多模态模型理解人类非语言交流提供了重要基准，有望推动手势理解与多模态交互研究。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了首个大规模手势识别基准数据集 GRW，用于将自然手势与特定口语词汇进行帧级对齐映射，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）[CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on gesture recognition, not aligned with user's priority on generative models and efficient inference. Signals: New benchmark dataset, Co-speech gesture recognition, Frame-level annotation, Multimodal understanding, Not generative or efficiency-focused

Abstract: While humans naturally gesture during speech, only a sparse subset of these movements are visually depictive and semantically linked to specific spoken words. Current multimodal models struggle to capture these semantic co-speech gestures, heavily bottlenecked by a lack of precisely annotated training data. To address this, we introduce the Gesture Recognition in the Wild (GRW) dataset, the first large-scale benchmark designed to map unconstrained human gestures to specific words with frame-accurate temporal boundaries. Comprising 156,688 manually annotated video clips, GRW spans a highly diverse 150-word taxonomy of physical actions, spatial descriptors, and abstract concepts. We leverage GRW to train video models to (a) classify gestures as semantic or not, (b) recognize the word corresponding to a co-speech gesture, and (c) temporally localize the gesture. We also use GRW to establish benchmarks for these three tasks.

</details>


### [136] [SOCO: Benchmarking Semantic Object Correspondence in Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31597)
*Olaf Dünkel, Basavaraj Sunagad, Haoran Wang, David T. Hoffmann, Christian Theobalt, Adam Kortylewski*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (54.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SOCO是一个新的语义对象对应（Semantic Object Correspondence）基准，包含100个类别、超过100万对应点对，并引入对应类型分类法和关键点语言描述，系统评估视觉基础模型和LVLM的细粒度部件级理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉基础模型的结构化对象理解评估缺乏一致的评测协议和细粒度的部件级标注，难以系统衡量模型在跨实例、跨类别、大外观/视角/几何变化下的语义对应能力。

Method: 构建SOCO基准：定义对应类型分类法（实例内、实例间、类别间等），在100个类别上提供功能上有意义的关键点标注（超过100万对应点对），并为关键点附加语言描述以支持LVLM评估。在多种视觉基础模型和LVLM上进行系统实验。

Result: (i) 视觉基础骨干网络编码了强语义结构，但在跨类别对应迁移上表现差，且仅部分捕获对象-部件位置；(ii) LVLM在文本提示的部件定位上强于视觉参考的跨图像匹配，暴露了语言定位与细粒度视觉对应之间的差距；(iii) 对应性能比ImageNet分类更能预测分割、跟踪、3D姿态估计和3D检测等密集下游任务的表现。

Conclusion: SOCO可作为评估视觉和多模态基础模型结构化、部件级表示质量的基准，揭示了当前模型在细粒度语义对应方面的能力与局限。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了SOCO基准，用于评估视觉基础模型和视觉语言模型在语义对象对应（semantic correspondence）上的细粒度部件级理解能力，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for part-level understanding but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like generation or world models. Signals: New semantic correspondence benchmark, evaluates vision foundation models and LVLMs, includes keypoint language descriptions, predicts dense downstream task performance, no direct link to generation or world models

Abstract: Measuring structured object understanding in vision foundation models remains challenging due to inconsistent evaluation protocols and limited part-level supervision. Semantic correspondence (SC) evaluates this capability by testing whether object parts can be matched across instances and categories under large variations in appearance, viewpoint, and geometry. To enable a systematic SC evaluation, we introduce SOCO, a new benchmark for Semantic Object Correspondence that introduces a taxonomy of correspondence types and provides consistent, functionally meaningful keypoint annotations across 100 categories and over 1M correspondence pairs. In addition, SOCO includes keypoint language descriptions, enabling the evaluation of large vision-language models (LVLMs) and their fine-grained part-level understanding. Comprehensive experiments reveal that (i) vision foundation backbones encode strong semantic structure but transfer correspondences poorly across related categories and only partially capture object-part position, (ii) LVLMs are stronger at text-prompted part localization than at visual-reference cross-image matching, exposing a gap between language-grounded localization and fine-grained visual correspondence, and (iii) correspondence performance predicts performance on dense downstream tasks, including segmentation, tracking, 3D pose estimation, and 3D detection, more strongly than ImageNet classification. Together, these findings position SOCO as a benchmark for structured, part-level representation quality in vision and multimodal foundation models.

</details>


### [137] [A Visually Impaired Assistance Benchmark for VLM-as-a-Judge Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31351)
*Yi Zhao, Siqi Wang, Zhe Hu, Yushi Li, Jing Li*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (51.14) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 VIABLE，首个面向视障辅助（VIA）场景的 VLM-as-a-Judge 评测基准，包含超过 30 万条判断样本，引入 Effectiveness–Impartiality–Stability 评估框架和 12 种失败模式分类。系统评测 7 个 VLM 裁判模型后发现现有模型在 VIA 任务上不可靠，最强模型 GPT-5.4 仅 52.6% 诊断准确率且自偏好率达 94.2%。为此提出 VIA-Judge-Agent，通过视觉证据提取和分类引导工作流提升诊断准确率和下游响应质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 视障辅助（VIA）任务因人工评估成本高昂而发展受限，VLM-as-a-Judge 范式在通用领域虽有探索，但在 VIA 场景下的可靠性尚未被系统评估。现有裁判模型是否能在 VIA 任务中被信任是一个关键问题。

Method: 构建 VIABLE 基准，包含 300K+ 判断样本，覆盖三个场景；提出 Effectiveness–Impartiality–Stability 三维评估框架和 12 种失败模式分类；系统评测 7 个不同规模的 VLM 裁判模型；提出 VIA-Judge-Agent，一种模型无关的推理时增强方法，结合视觉证据提取和分类引导工作流。

Result: 现有 VLM 裁判在 VIA 任务上普遍不可靠：GPT-5.4 单失败诊断准确率仅 52.6%，自偏好率高达 94.2%；开源裁判存在严重偏差和对抗脆弱性。VIA-Judge-Agent 在诊断准确率和下游 VIA 响应质量上均取得正向提升。

Conclusion: VLM-as-a-Judge 在 VIA 场景中尚不可靠，需要专门的评测基准和增强方法。VIA-Judge-Agent 提供了一种有效的推理时增强方案，为未来 VIA 评估和模型改进奠定了基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 VIABLE，首个面向视障辅助（VIA）场景的 VLM-as-a-Judge 评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向。同时涉及 生成式 AI 评测（evaluation_benchmark）中的生成内容质量评估与人类偏好对齐评估。最佳匹配为 multimodal_understanding_generation 下的 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM-as-a-Judge for visually impaired assistance, which is a niche evaluation benchmark not aligned with user's priority subtopics in generation, efficiency, or world models. Signals: VLM-as-a-Judge benchmark, visually impaired assistance, failure taxonomy, GPT-5.4 low accuracy, self-preference bias

Abstract: AI-based Visually Impaired Assistance (VIA) remains challenging, largely due to the high cost of human evaluation. The VLM-as-a-Judge paradigm may offer a promising alternative, although it has mostly been studied in general domains. We therefore ask whether such judges can be trusted for VIA tasks. To investigate this question, we introduce VIABLE (Visually Impaired Assistance Benchmark for VLM-as-a-Judge Evaluation), the first benchmark for VLM-as-a-Judge evaluation in VIA. VIABLE contains over 300K judgment samples across three scenarios and introduces an Effectiveness--Impartiality--Stability framework with a 12-mode failure taxonomy. Based on VIABLE, our systematic study of seven judges across different model scales shows that existing models are largely unreliable across all evaluation axes. The strongest judge, GPT-5.4, achieves only 52.6% single-failure diagnostic accuracy, yet exhibits the highest self-preference rate at 94.2%; while open-source judges are strongly biased and adversarially fragile. To address these issues, we propose VIA-Judge-Agent, a model-agnostic inference-time harness that augments judges with visual evidence extraction and a taxonomy-guided workflow. It enables positive improvements in diagnostic accuracy and downstream VIA responses more preferred by BLV users. Data and code are available at:

</details>

### [138] [PEEK: Picking Essential frames via Efficient Knowledge distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31029)
*Killian Steunou, Anas Filali Razzouki, Khalil Guetari, Mounîm A. El-Yacoubi, Yannis Tevissen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 普通相关 (61.48) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PEEK 提出一种高效的动态帧采样方法，通过将教师模型的字幕条件帧相关性排序蒸馏到仅依赖视觉内容的轻量级时序模型中，在低帧预算下显著优于均匀采样和现有自适应方法，且仅增加 5.2% 的推理时间开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频语言模型只能处理有限数量的帧，帧选择成为高效视频字幕生成的关键瓶颈。现有方法要么依赖均匀采样（忽略视觉内容），要么采用计算昂贵的自适应采样方法。作者希望设计一种既高效又准确的自适应帧采样方法。

Method: PEEK 使用一个轻量级时序模型（仅基于视觉内容）来预测每帧的重要性分数，通过知识蒸馏从更强的教师模型（字幕条件帧排序）中学习帧相关性排名。训练时，教师模型根据视频帧与字幕的匹配度生成帧排序标签，学生模型仅以视觉特征为输入学习预测该排序，从而实现高效的推理时帧选择。

Result: 在 ActivityNet Captions 和 MSR-VTT 上，PEEK 在大多数帧预算配置下优于现有方法，尤其在仅选择 1-2 帧时表现最佳。在 ActivityNet Captions 上，PEEK 在 16 个配置中赢得 14 个。零样本迁移到 MSR-VTT 在低帧预算下表现最好。效率方面，PEEK 仅增加 5.2% 的字幕生成时间，远低于 CSTA（65.4%）和 MaxInfo（211.9%）。

Conclusion: PEEK 证明了通过知识蒸馏实现高效自适应帧采样的可行性，在低帧预算下兼具高准确性和高效率，为视频语言模型的帧选择提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一种高效的动态帧采样方法，通过知识蒸馏将教师模型的帧重要性排序迁移到轻量级时序模型，核心贡献在于提升视频语言模型处理效率。这与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化"（efficient_multimodal）高度匹配，涉及 token 压缩（帧选择本质是视觉 token 压缩）和蒸馏技术。

Importance reason: Efficient frame sampling directly boosts multimodal understanding and model efficiency, aligning with your priority on efficient inference and unified multimodal models. Signals: Efficient dynamic frame sampling, Knowledge distillation for frame selection, Low inference overhead (5.2%), Strong on low-frame budgets, Outperforms uniform and adaptive baselines

Abstract: Video-language models can process only a limited number of frames, making frame selection a key bottleneck for efficient video captioning. Most captioning pipelines still rely on uniform sampling, which is computationally cheap but agnostic to visual content. Adaptive frame sampling has recently emerged as a promising approach for selecting the most informative frames from a video; however, existing methods remain computationally expensive. We introduce PEEK, an efficient dynamic frame sampling method that distills caption-conditioned frame relevance rankings from a stronger teacher model into a lightweight temporal model that operates only on visual content. We find that, overall, on ActivityNet Captions and MSR-VTT, our method outperforms state-of-the-art methods across all evaluated downstream vision language models, especially when only one or two frames are selected for captioning, obtaining the best CIDEr for most frame budgets. On ActivityNet Captions, PEEK is particularly strong, winning 14 out of 16 configurations. Zero-shot evaluation on MSR-VTT shows that our model transfers best at low frame budgets, while results at four and eight frames are more mixed as temporal coverage and visual diversity become increasingly competitive. Compared with recent adaptive baselines, PEEK is both more accurate in the low-budget regime and more efficient: it adds only $5.2\%$ to the captioning time, compared with $65.4\%$ for CSTA and $211.9\%$ for MaxInfo. We release our code and pre-trained checkpoint at .

</details>


### [139] [VisionPulse: Dynamic Visual Sparsity for Efficient Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31457)
*Hengbo Xu, Shengjie Jin, Yanbiao Ma, Zhiwu Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 普通相关 (63.66) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VisionPulse 提出了一种推理时逐步视觉 token 剪枝框架，通过轻量级视觉注意力质量估计每步的保留预算，仅保留 5% 的关键视觉 token，同时将推理轨迹缩短 11.2%，且精度几乎不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型推理时开销大，现有方法通常在预填充阶段静态剪枝视觉 token，但忽略了视觉证据在推理过程中是逐步依赖的——不同解码步骤需要不同的关键视觉 token，且冗余视觉上下文会引导模型关注无关区域，延长推理轨迹。

Method: 提出 VisionPulse 框架：1) 计算轻量级视觉注意力质量（visual attention mass），利用其与 LMM 有效视觉 token 使用率的强正相关性来估计每步的保留预算；2) 在该预算下仅保留最关键的视觉 token；3) 通过推理时强制视觉稀疏性，过滤冗余视觉上下文同时保留相关证据，自然缩短推理轨迹。

Result: 实验表明，VisionPulse 每步仅保留 5% 的视觉 token，推理轨迹缩短 11.2%，同时保持精度几乎不变。

Conclusion: 视觉证据在推理过程中是逐步依赖的，动态剪枝视觉 token 可以有效降低推理开销并缩短推理轨迹，为高效多模态推理提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 VisionPulse，一种在推理过程中对视觉 token 进行逐步动态剪枝的方法，旨在降低多模态大模型（LMM）的推理开销。这属于多模态高效化与轻量化（efficient_multimodal）范畴，具体涉及 token 压缩与稀疏推理，与 canonical 子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient multimodal reasoning via dynamic visual sparsity, aligning with model efficiency and unified multimodal priorities. Signals: multimodal_understanding_generation, efficient inference, visual token pruning, step-wise sparsity, reasoning trace shortening

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of large multimodal models (LMMs), inference-time overhead has become a key bottleneck for real-world deployment. Existing methods typically prune visual tokens at prefill, assuming the required visual evidence remains static during reasoning. However, we empirically show that visual evidence is strongly step-dependent: only a sparse subset of visual tokens is critical at each decoding step, and the critical set evolves across reasoning. Furthermore, we identify a coupled bottleneck where redundant visual context can steer the model toward query-irrelevant regions, lengthening the reasoning trace. Guided by these insights, we propose VisionPulse, a step-wise visual token pruning framework during reasoning. VisionPulse computes a lightweight visual attention mass to estimate the step-wise retention budget by exploiting its strong positive correlation with LMMs' effective visual token usage and retain only the most critical tokens under this budget. By enforcing visual sparsity during reasoning, VisionPulse filters redundant visual context while preserving relevant visual evidence, shortening reasoning traces naturally. Extensive experiments show that VisionPulse only retains 5% of visual tokens per step with reasoning traces shortened by 11.2%, while keeping accuracy almost unchanged.

</details>


### [140] [Linear Scaling Video VLMs for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31598)
*Cristobal Eyzaguirre, Jiajun Wu, Juan Carlos Niebles*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 普通相关 (63.66) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 StateKV，一种无需微调或架构修改的推理时方法，通过固定容量的重要性驱动循环状态携带跨帧上下文，将预训练长视频 VLM 的视频预填充复杂度从二次降至线性，在多个长视频基准和模型上接近全自注意力性能，并优于滑动窗口等流式近似方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频 VLM 的视频编码器依赖时空自注意力，计算和延迟随帧数二次增长，难以扩展到长视频和流式场景。已有的效率方法（如帧/令牌丢弃、粗粒度注意力近似）虽提升了可扩展性，但往往以精度损失为代价。

Method: StateKV 在推理时引入一个固定容量的循环状态，基于重要性度量选择性地将跨帧上下文压缩到该状态中，实现线性时间的视频预填充；同时保留第二个完整的逐帧缓存用于解码阶段，以保持解码精度。该方法无需微调或改变模型架构，可直接适配预训练的长视频 VLM。

Result: 在三个长视频基准和七个模型（涵盖三个模型家族和多种规模）上，StateKV 的性能接近全自注意力，并一致优于主流的滑动窗口/基于近因的流式近似方法。同时，StateKV 降低了视频预填充的计算成本（FLOPs），使得在固定计算预算下可以运行更大的模型以获得更强的精度。

Conclusion: StateKV 提供了一种实用且有效的长视频理解可扩展方案，在不牺牲精度的情况下实现了线性时间推理，为长视频和流式场景下的 VLM 部署提供了可行路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 StateKV，一种将长视频 VLM 的预填充阶段从二次复杂度降至线性复杂度的高效推理方法，属于多模态模型效率优化，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化" (efficient_multimodal)。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for long video understanding, a high-priority subtopic, with a practical linear-time method that boosts model efficiency. Signals: linear scaling video VLM, efficient inference, long video understanding, StateKV, no fine-tuning

Abstract: Video vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used in long-horizon and streaming settings, yet most video encoders still rely on spatiotemporal self-attention, causing compute and latency to grow quadratically with the number of frames. Existing efficiency methods improve scalability but often lose accuracy relative to full self-attention, for example through aggressive frame/token dropping or coarse attention approximations. We introduce StateKV, an inference-time method that adapts pretrained long-video VLMs to linear-time video prefill by carrying cross-frame context in a fixed-capacity, importance-based recurrent state, paired with a second full per-frame cache used for decoding. Across three long-video benchmarks and seven models spanning three families and multiple scales, StateKV remains close to full self-attention and consistently outperforms dominant sliding-window / recency-based streaming approximations, without fine-tuning or architectural changes. StateKV also reduces video-prefill cost measured FLOPs, enabling stronger accuracy at a fixed compute budget by running larger models. These results suggest a practical step toward scalable long-video understanding.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-uncategorized'></div>
