### [141] [A Pilot Study on Curator-Guided Multilingual Art Description for Blind and Low-Vision Audiences with Small Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31080)
*Iosif Tsangko, Andreas Triantafyllopoulos, George Margetis, Ioana Crihana, Björn W. Schuller*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL, cs.HC, cs.CV, cs.MM

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (35.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本试点研究探索使用小型视觉语言模型（Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct）为盲人和低视力观众生成多语言（德语、罗马尼亚语、塞尔维亚语）艺术描述，比较了语言特定LoRA适配器与多语言适配器的效果，发现语言特定适配器在可控性和视觉描述质量上更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盲人和低视力（BLV）观众在博物馆等场景中缺乏多语言的艺术描述服务，而隐私和知识产权限制往往倾向于使用本地部署的小型视觉语言模型（VLM），而非大型云端模型。现有研究缺乏对小型VLM在多语言BLV艺术描述场景中的系统评估。

Method: 使用Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct作为基础模型，构建了平行BLV导向的描述语料库（包含艺术品图像和元数据）。比较了两种微调策略：语言特定LoRA适配器（每种语言单独训练）与单一多语言LoRA适配器（所有语言联合训练）。评估结合了自动词法和嵌入指标，以及经小型罗马尼亚BLV试点研究校准的LLM-as-Judge协议。

Result: 在试点设置下，语言特定适配器在罗马尼亚语和塞尔维亚语上表现出更稳定的可控性和视觉基础描述质量，而多语言适配器在德语上仍具有竞争力。整体而言，小型本地VLM在BLV艺术描述任务上展现出可行潜力。

Conclusion: 该研究为小型本地VLM在博物馆多语言无障碍艺术描述中的部署提供了初步证据，同时指出需要更大规模的BLV用户研究和更广泛的语言覆盖才能得出关于多语言无障碍访问的普适结论。

Classification reason: 该论文研究面向盲人和低视力（BLV）观众的多语言艺术描述生成，属于垂直领域（博物馆/艺术）的内容生成应用，与 domain_application（领域内容生成应用）高度匹配。同时涉及小规模视觉语言模型（VLM）的应用，也与多模态理解与生成方向相关。

Importance reason: Low priority due to niche accessibility application and lack of alignment with user's core generative model and efficiency interests. Signals: small VLM, multilingual art description, BLV accessibility, LoRA adapters, not autoregressive/diffusion/world model

Abstract: Blind and low-vision (BLV) audiences remain underserved by visual art descriptions, particularly across languages and in museum settings where privacy and intellectual-property constraints may favour small on-premise vision-language models (VLMs). This pilot study investigates curator-guided multilingual art description with Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct for German, Romanian, and Serbian. We construct a parallel BLV-oriented caption corpus from artwork images and metadata, and compare language-specific LoRA adapters with a single multilingual adapter under a fixed backbone and training budget. Evaluation combines automatic lexical and embedding-based metrics with an LLM-as-Judge protocol calibrated against a small Romanian BLV pilot study. Under our pilot setup, language-specific adapters show more stable controllability and visually grounded description quality for Romanian and Serbian, while multilingual adaptation remains competitive in German. We frame these findings as deployment-oriented evidence for small on-premise VLMs, and highlight the need for larger BLV user studies and broader language coverage before drawing general conclusions about multilingual accessibility.

</details>


### [142] [Rationalize: Shared Semantic Reasoning for Human-AI Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30632)
*Aritra Dasgupta, Naga Datha Saikiran Battula, Avina Nakarmi, Sohom Sen, Subhodeep Ghosh, Xun Song*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.HC, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (40.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Rationalize，一个角色对框架，用于人类与 AI 模型在数据驱动意义构建中的共享语义推理。通过定义 Explorer-Guide、Investigator-Informant、Teacher-Student、Judge-Advocate 四组互补角色对，在共享推理空间中使意图、问题、假设、证据、推理和含义显式化，实现人类与 AI 在推理层面的双向对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人机对齐主要关注输出层面的对齐（如 RLHF），缺乏对推理过程和意图层面的对齐。人类分析师与 AI 模型在数据驱动的意义构建中需要更深层次的语义对齐，而当前缺乏系统性的框架来建模这种双向对齐过程。

Method: 提出 Rationalize 角色对框架，将人机交互建模为四组互补角色对：(1) Explorer-Guide（探索者-引导者），(2) Investigator-Informant（调查者-信息提供者），(3) Teacher-Student（教师-学生），(4) Judge-Advocate（法官-辩护者）。这些角色对在共享推理空间中运作，使目的、问题、假设、证据、推理和含义等推理要素显式化。框架将双向对齐分为"AI 对齐人类"和"人类对齐 AI"两个方向，并针对不同角色对设计对齐评估方法。

Result: 论文提出了一个概念性框架，将人机对齐从输出层面扩展到推理层面，建立了角色对与双向对齐框架之间的映射关系，并勾勒了基于要素级和角色特定方法的对齐设计与评估研究议程。

Conclusion: Rationalize 框架为人类与 AI 在数据驱动意义构建中的共享语义推理提供了系统化的角色对建模方法，有助于实现更深层次的人机对齐。未来工作可围绕该框架进行对齐设计与评估的实证研究。

Classification reason: 论文提出 Rationalize 框架，将人机交互建模为互补角色对（Explorer-Guide, Investigator-Informant 等），在共享推理空间中实现人类与 AI（如 LLM）之间的语义对齐。这属于 AI 智能体与人类协作的工作流设计，与 [CANONICAL] agent_workflow（AI 智能体与生成工作流）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on human-AI alignment in sensemaking, not on core generative modeling or efficiency priorities. Signals: human-AI alignment, shared reasoning, role-pair framework, not generative model, not video/image generation

Abstract: We introduce Rationalize, a role-pair framework for shared semantic reasoning between humans and AI models in data-driven sensemaking. Building on ideas in human-machine teaming and critical thinking, we conceptualize human-AI interaction as a series of complementary role pairs (Explorer-Guide, Investigator-Informant, Teacher-Student, Judge-Advocate) operating in a shared reasoning space. In this space, human analysts and AI models (such as LLMs) make purposes, questions, assumptions, evidence, inferences, and implications explicit, facilitating alignment not only at the output level but at the level of rationalization of intent and action by each side. We relate these role pairs to the bidirectional human-AI alignment framework, illustrating how "aligning AI to humans" and "aligning humans to AI" differ by role, and sketch a collaborative research agenda for alignment design and assessment using element-level and role-specific approaches.

</details>


### [143] [EUDAIMONIA: Evaluating Undesirable Dynamics in AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30654)
*Jun Rui Huang, Wang Bill Zhu, Ziyi Liu, Nathanael Fast, Ravi Iyer, Robin Jia*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.HC, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (41.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Social AI Design Code框架和EUDAIMONIA基准（969条用户输入、3147项设计需求违规检查），用于评估LLM在社交互动中是否鼓励有害亲密、依赖或过度参与。评估22个最新LLM发现，即使最强模型（Claude-Opus-4.7和GPT-5.5）也分别违反30.7%和27.2%的检查项，且扩展推理无法降低违规率，表明这是持久的社会对齐问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用作社交伴侣、情感倾诉和人际建议的对话伙伴，但现有能力导向或传统安全评估无法捕捉这些互动中的社交动态危害（如有害亲密、依赖、过度参与）。需要专门的评估框架来检测这类社会对齐问题。

Method: 提出Social AI Design Code框架，包含设计原则和具体需求；基于WildChat通过弱到强过滤、多模型重标注和受控改写构建EUDAIMONIA基准（969条用户输入、3147项设计需求违规检查）；评估22个最新LLM的违规率。

Result: 最强模型Claude-Opus-4.7和GPT-5.5分别违反30.7%和27.2%的检查项；扩展推理（extended thinking）无法降低违规率；不同模型在社交动态危害方面表现差异显著。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在社交互动中存在持久的社会对齐问题，不是通过测试时推理就能解决的简单缺陷。需要专门的社会对齐评估框架和新的对齐方法来解决这些社交动态危害。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM作为社交伴侣时产生的有害社交动态（有害亲密、依赖、过度参与），属于生成式 AI安全与治理范畴，映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic safety_governance（生成内容安全与治理）。同时涉及多模态理解与生成方向中的安全对齐问题。

Importance reason: Focuses on social interaction safety, not on the user's priority generative modeling or efficiency topics. Signals: LLM social alignment, safety benchmark, not generative modeling, not efficiency, not video/image generation

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as conversational partners for companionship, emotional disclosure, and interpersonal advice, but the social dynamics of these interactions can create harms that are not captured by capability-oriented or traditional safety evaluations. We introduce the Social AI Design Code, a framework for evaluating whether LLMs align with user welfare in social interactions, including whether they encourage harmful intimacy, dependence, or prolonged engagement. To evaluate these risks in natural and diverse user-LLM interactions, we operationalize the code with EUDAIMONIA, a benchmark of 969 user inputs and 3,147 design-requirement violation checks built from WildChat through weak-to-strong filtration, multi-model relabeling, and controlled rewriting. Evaluating 22 recent LLMs, we find that even the strongest models, Claude-Opus-4.7 and GPT-5.5, violate 30.7% and 27.2% of checks, respectively. Extended thinking does not reduce violation rates, suggesting that these failures are persistent social-alignment problems rather than deficits solvable through test-time reasoning alone.

</details>


### [144] [Harness Updating Is Not Harness Benefit: Disentangling Evolution Capabilities in Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30621)
*Minhua Lin, Juncheng Wu, Zijun Wang, Zhan Shi, Yisi Sang, Bing He, Zewen Liu, Tianxin Wei, Zongyu Wu, Zhiwei Zhang, Dakuo Wang, Xiang Zhang, Benoit Dumoulin, Cihang Xie, Yuyin Zhou, Suhang Wang, Hanqing Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (46.59) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了LLM智能体在"自我进化"中的两种能力——生成有用外部组件更新（harness-updating）和从更新中受益（harness-benefit），发现前者在不同能力层级的模型中表现平坦，后者则呈非单调关系：中等模型受益最大，弱模型和强模型受益较少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体越来越多地部署在可编辑的外部组件（prompt、技能、记忆、工具）上，这些组件可以通过执行经验进行自我进化。然而，模型的基础任务解决能力与其自我进化能力之间的关系尚不明确：哪些模型能产生有用的组件更新？哪些模型能真正从中受益？本文旨在厘清这两个问题。

Method: 作者将智能体自我进化能力分解为两个维度：(1) harness-updating——从执行经验中产生有用的持久化组件更新的能力；(2) harness-benefit——在任务求解中从更新后的组件中受益的能力。通过设计对照实验，在不同能力层级的模型（弱、中、强）上进行系统评估，分析两种能力与模型基础能力之间的关系。

Result: 研究发现：(1) harness-updating能力在基础能力上呈现"平坦"特征——不同能力层级的模型产生的组件更新带来的性能提升惊人地相似，甚至Qwen3.5-9B的更新效果可与Claude Opus 4.6媲美；(2) harness-benefit能力与基础能力呈非单调关系——弱模型受益很少，中等模型受益最大，强模型受益反而低于中等模型。弱模型受益低的原因有两种失败模式：无法激活相关组件，或激活后无法忠实遵循。

Conclusion: 研究建议将能力预算投入到任务求解智能体本身而非进化器，并在智能体训练中重点关注组件调用和长程指令跟随能力。这些发现为LLM智能体的自我进化设计和资源分配提供了重要指导。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM智能体的自我进化能力（harness self-evolution），涉及prompt、skills、memories、tools等外部组件的更新与利用，属于LLM智能体与生成工作流范畴，因此映射到生成式 AI下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "AI 智能体与生成工作流"。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM agent self-evolution, not directly on user's priority subtopics like video/image generation or world models. Signals: LLM agent evolution, harness-updating vs. harness-benefit, non-monotonic capability relationship, weak-tier model failure modes

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly deployed as systems built around editable external harnesses, including prompts, skills, memories and tools, that shape task execution without changing model parameters. Harness self-evolution adapts such agents by updating these harnesses from execution evidence. Yet it remains unclear whether a model's base capability in task-solving predicts its capabilities in harness self-evolution: which models produce useful harness updates, and which actually benefit from them? We analyze two harness self-evolution capabilities: (i) harness-updating, the capability to produce useful persistent harness updates from execution evidence; (ii) harness-benefit, the capability to benefit from updated harnesses during task solving. Our analysis reveals two findings. First, harness-updating is flat in base capability: models from different capability tiers produce harness updates that lead to surprisingly similar gains; even Qwen3.5-9B's updates yield gains comparable to those of Claude Opus~4.6. Second, harness-benefit is non-monotonic in base capability: weak-tier models benefit little from updated harnesses, mid-tier models benefit most, and strong-tier models benefit less than mid-tier. We trace low gains at the weak tier to two failure modes: weak-tier models may fail to activate relevant harness artifacts, or activate them but fail to follow them faithfully. These findings suggest investing capability budget in the task-solving agent rather than the evolver, and targeting harness invocation and long-horizon instruction following in agent training. Our source code is publicly available at .

</details>


### [145] [EHRBench: An Automated and Reliable EHR-based Benchmark for Clinical Decision Making with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30637)
*Yuzhang Xie, Keqi Han, Yunpeng Xiao, Hejie Cui, Guanchen Wu, Ziyang Zhang, Kai Shu, Jiaying Lu, Xiao Hu, Carl Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (35.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: EHRBench是一个基于真实电子健康记录（EHR）的自动化、可靠的临床决策LLM评测基准，通过EHR-LLM-知识库交互流水线构建了近100万条QA数据，覆盖诊断、治疗和预后三类任务，对30余个LLM进行了系统评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学决策基准缺乏对真实EHR数据的充分利用，且构建过程难以兼顾规模与可靠性。LLM在临床决策中的可靠性尚未被充分理解，需要一个自动化且可靠的、基于真实EHR的评测基准来系统评估LLM的临床决策能力。

Method: 提出EHR-LLM-KB交互流水线：1）使用专用LLM将EHR轨迹自动转换为结构化模板；2）确定性实例化模板生成QA条目；3）基于知识库（KB）的系统性验证与丰富，过滤幻觉或模糊关系。最终构建了约96万条QA条目，覆盖诊断、治疗、预后三类临床决策任务。

Result: 对30余个代表性LLM进行了基准测试，提供了详细的性能和鲁棒性分析。结果显示不同设置下能力趋势一致，验证了EHRBench的可靠性，并揭示了LLM在临床可靠性方面的可改进差距。

Conclusion: EHRBench为评估LLM在真实临床决策中的能力提供了一个自动化、可靠的大规模基准，有助于推动LLM在临床场景中的可靠应用。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了基于真实EHR的临床决策LLM评测基准EHRBench，属于生成式 AI评测与基准（evaluation_benchmark）范畴，同时涉及多模态理解与生成方向中的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark），但核心更偏向生成式 AI领域的生成式模型评测。

Importance reason: Focuses on clinical LLM evaluation, which is outside your core interests in generative video/image models and efficient inference. Signals: clinical decision making, EHR benchmark, LLM evaluation, medical domain, low priority subtopic match

Abstract: Clinical decision-making (CDM) is central to real-world clinical workflows, where clinicians infer diagnoses, select treatments, or anticipate future health outcomes under incomplete evidence. LLMs are increasingly used to support these decisions due to strong language capabilities, broad biomedical knowledge, and efficiency, yet the reliability of LLMs on real-world clinical decision tasks remains insufficiently understood. To evaluate CDM models, especially LLM-based models, an ideal and practical medical decision benchmark should be constructed via an automated yet reliable pipeline to ensure both scale and quality. Moreover, the grounding of a CDM benchmark in real patient EHRs can better support evaluation on practical CDM tasks that require substantive biomedical knowledge and clinical inference. To fill the gaps, we introduce EHRBench, an automated and reliable EHR-grounded benchmark for evaluating LLM-based clinical decision-making at scale. To ensure scalability and reliability, EHRBench is constructed through an EHR-LLM-KB(knowledge-base) interaction pipeline. For efficiency, we use a specialized LLM to automatically convert encounter-level EHR trajectories into structured templates and deterministically instantiate the templates into QA items. In parallel, we apply systematic KB-based verification and enrichment to filter hallucinated or ambiguous relations and to improve reliability. Using this pipeline, we construct nearly 1M (960,067) QA items spanning three core inference-required clinical decision tasks: diagnosis, treatment, and prognosis. We benchmark more than 30 representative LLMs on EHRBench and provide detailed analyses of performance and robustness. The results show consistent capability trends across settings, further validating the reliability of EHRBench and highlighting actionable gaps toward clinically reliable LLM systems.

</details>


### [146] [Planner-Centric Reinforcement Learning for Deep Research with Structure-Aware Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30824)
*Mustafa Anis Hussain, Xinle Wu, Yao Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (50.58) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DecomposeR 提出以规划为中心的深度研究框架，将研究计划表示为结构化 DAG，通过两阶段强化学习（规划器 RL + 回答器 RL）分别优化图结构分解与分支执行合成，在长文本基准上超越同类开源模型 5.1-8.0 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究训练范式要么依赖短格式可验证 QA 作为代理任务，要么优化整体长轨迹，导致规划与执行难以解耦，对规划过程的信用分配薄弱。需要一种显式、结构化、可奖励的研究规划方法。

Method: 将研究计划表示为类型化有向无环图（DAG），分两阶段训练 Qwen3-8B：第一阶段规划器 RL 学习图结构与查询分解以改进研究规划；第二阶段回答器 RL 在已学规划条件下学习分支级执行与最终合成。奖励信号直接分配给显式的规划 token 和结构化组件而非扁平轨迹。

Result: DecomposeR-8B 在多个长文本基准上比强可比开源基线提升 5.1-8.0 分，验证了结构化规划奖励和两阶段 RL 的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将规划显式建模为 DAG 并分别优化规划与回答能力，DecomposeR 实现了更细粒度的规划优化，减少了端到端训练的歧义，为深度研究任务提供了有效的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DecomposeR，一个以规划为中心的深度研究框架，将研究计划表示为有向无环图（DAG），并通过两阶段强化学习优化规划与回答能力。这属于 LLM 智能体驱动的自动化研究工作流，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "AI 智能体与生成工作流" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel planner-centric RL with structured DAG rewards for deep research, but focuses on AI agent workflows rather than core multimodal generation. Signals: structured DAG planning, two-stage RL for planning and answering, improves long-form benchmarks, not directly related to video/image generation or world models

Abstract: Deep research tasks require LLMs to plan what to investigate, retrieve evidence, and synthesize long-form answers across multiple branches of inquiry. Existing training paradigms either rely on short-form verifiable QA as a proxy or optimize monolithic long trajectories, which makes planning and execution difficult to disentangle and yields weak credit assignment for the planning process. We propose DecomposeR, a planner-centric deep research framework that represents research plans as typed directed acyclic graphs (DAGs), allowing planning to be made explicit, structured, and rewardable. We train a Qwen3-8B model in two stages: planner reinforcement learning (RL) first learns graph structure and query decomposition to improve research planning, and answerer reinforcement learning (RL) then learns branch-level execution and final synthesis conditioned on the learned plan. By assigning rewards to explicit planner tokens and structured components rather than to a flat trajectory, DecomposeR enables finer-grained optimization of planning while reducing the ambiguity of end-to-end training. Experiments show that DecomposeR-8B improves over strong comparable open baselines by 5.1-8.0 points on popular long-form benchmarks due to improved planning and answering capabilities.

</details>


### [147] [COMPASS: Cognitive MCTS-Guided Process Alignment for Safe Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30838)
*Wenkai Shen, Pengyang Zhou, Jiahe Xu, Jiaming Qian, Haozhe He, Zhihao Huang, Chaochao Chen, Xiaolin Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (43.34) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: COMPASS 提出了一种基于认知 MCTS 引导的过程对齐框架，用于提升 LLM 搜索智能体的安全性。通过认知树探索（CTE）合成隐蔽攻击轨迹，并结合内省式逐步对齐（ISA）对中间步骤进行细粒度监督，在保持通用能力的同时实现安全对齐，且所需训练数据大幅减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 驱动的搜索智能体具备多步推理和工具使用能力，但这也引入了检索诱导的安全退化问题——有害意图可能分解为看似无害的子查询，最终导致不安全结果。现有对齐方法难以捕获稀疏的安全信号，也无法有效监督多步交互中的多样化违规行为。

Method: COMPASS 框架包含两个核心模块：(1) 认知树探索（CTE），利用 MCTS 高效合成隐蔽的攻击轨迹，模拟智能体在多步交互中可能产生的安全违规行为；(2) 内省式逐步对齐（ISA），对中间步骤进行细粒度的过程监督，识别并隔离有风险的中间动作，实现精细化的安全对齐。

Result: 实验结果表明，COMPASS 在安全性与通用能力之间取得了良好的平衡，同时所需的训练数据量大幅减少。

Conclusion: COMPASS 通过认知 MCTS 引导的过程对齐，有效解决了 LLM 搜索智能体的安全对齐难题，为多步交互场景下的安全治理提供了新的思路。

Classification reason: 论文关注 LLM 搜索智能体的安全对齐问题，属于 生成式 AI 方向下的生成内容安全与治理（safety_governance）范畴，同时涉及多步推理与工具使用，也与多模态理解与生成方向下的智能体工作流（agent_workflow）相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM agent safety alignment, which is not a prioritized subtopic and lacks direct connection to video/image generation or model efficiency. Signals: LLM agent safety, process alignment, MCTS-guided, stealthy attack trajectories, safety-utility trade-off

Abstract: LLM-powered search agents enable multi-step reasoning and tool use. However, these capabilities introduce retrieval-induced safety degradation, as harmful intents may decompose into seemingly innocuous sub-queries that lead to unsafe outcomes. Existing alignment methods struggle to capture sparse safety signals and fail to supervise diverse violations across multi-step interactions. We propose COMPASS, a Cognitive MCTS-Guided Process Alignment framework designed to achieve robust safety alignment throughout the agent workflow while preserving general utility. COMPASS integrates cognitive tree exploration (CTE) to efficiently synthesize stealthy attack trajectories, and introspective step-wise alignment (ISA) to isolate risky intermediate actions for fine-grained process supervision. Empirical results show that COMPASS achieves a favorable safety-utility trade-off while requiring substantially less training data.

</details>


### [148] [UniScale: Adaptive Unified Inference Scaling via Online Joint Optimization of Model Routing and Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30898)
*Kaiyu Huang, Xingyu Wang, Mingze Kong, Zhubo Shi, Yuqian Hou, Hong Xu, Zhongxiang Dai, Minchen Yu, Qingjiang Shi*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (55.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: UniScale 提出统一推理扩展（UIS）框架，将模型路由与测试时扩展联合建模为上下文多臂老虎机问题，通过 LinUCB 在线学习最优推理策略，在动态推理场景中实现细粒度的质量-成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将模型路由（在不同规模模型间切换）和测试时扩展（TTS，在固定模型内调整推理计算量）作为两个独立维度处理，导致模型路由只能提供粗粒度的离散性能变化，而单模型TTS存在容量上限且收益递减，两者分离限制了动态推理环境中的适应性。

Method: 提出统一推理扩展（UIS）框架，将模型路由和TTS统一到单一优化空间中。在此基础上设计UniScale在线框架，将自适应UIS建模为上下文多臂老虎机问题，使用LinUCB算法学习推理策略。框架包含效率感知学习和成本建模，以在高维动作空间中实现稳定可扩展的优化。

Result: 实验表明UniScale能够有效利用UIS空间中的协同效应，在多样化的动态推理场景中实现细粒度且一致更优的质量-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 将模型路由与测试时扩展统一优化能够突破各自独立设计的局限性，在线学习框架可以自适应地平衡推理质量与计算成本，为LLM部署提供更灵活的推理策略。

Classification reason: 该论文提出UniScale框架，将模型路由（在不同规模模型间切换）与测试时扩展（TTS）统一为在线联合优化问题，建模为上下文多臂老虎机并通过LinUCB学习推理策略。这属于LLM推理工作流的自动化优化，与agent_workflow（AI智能体与生成工作流）中的自动化创作流水线、工具使用、智能体优化高度相关。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel unified inference scaling framework that jointly optimizes model routing and test-time scaling, addressing a key efficiency challenge in LLM deployment. Signals: Unified inference scaling, online optimization, contextual bandit, quality-cost trade-off, LLM deployment efficiency

Abstract: In real-world deployments of large language models (LLMs), balancing inference quality and computational cost has become a central challenge. Existing approaches tackle this trade-off along two largely independent dimensions: model routing, which switches among models of different scales to match request complexity, and test-time scaling (TTS), which adjusts inference-time compute within a fixed model for fine-grained control. However, this decoupled design introduces inherent limitations. Model routing yields coarse-grained, discrete performance changes due to the sparse set of model scales, while single-model TTS often encounters capacity ceilings and exhibits diminishing returns as compute increases. Moreover, treating the two mechanisms separately restricts adaptability in dynamic inference environments. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Unified Inference Scaling (UIS), which unifies model routing and TTS in a single optimization space. Building on this formulation, we propose UniScale, an online framework that models adaptive UIS as a contextual multi-armed bandit problem and learns inference policies via LinUCB. The framework incorporates efficiency-aware learning and cost modeling to ensure stable and scalable optimization over high-dimensional action spaces. Evaluation shows that UniScale effectively exploits the synergy in the UIS space to deliver a fine-grained and consistently better quality-cost trade-off across diverse, dynamic inference scenarios.

</details>


### [149] [Simple Token-Efficient Vision-Language Model for Case-level Pathology Synoptic Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30716)
*Zhiyuan Yang, Jiahao Cheng, Vincent Quoc-Huy Trinh, Mahdi S. Hosseini*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (36.31) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种简单且 token 高效的视觉语言模型，用于从全切片图像（WSI）生成病例级病理学结构化报告。模型采用冻结的病理图像编码器、轻量两层 MLP 对齐器和 LLM 解码器的三组件架构，通过 WSI 标记 token 区分多切片病例。使用 5× 放大倍率的 512×512 图块将序列长度减少至传统 20× 方法的 1/64，仅需半张 H100 GPU 即可训练，在 ROUGE-L/METEOR/BLEU-4 指标上取得高分数，AI 评估中优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理学报告生成面临全切片图像分辨率极高（十亿像素级）、视觉 token 序列过长、病例级推理复杂（单个病例可能包含多个 WSI，组织异质且发现模糊）等挑战。现有方法在有限 GPU 内存下难以实用，亟需一种 token 高效、内存友好的解决方案。

Method: 采用三组件架构：冻结的病理图像编码器（UNI）、轻量两层 MLP 视觉-语言对齐器、大语言模型解码器（Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct）。引入显式 WSI 标记 token 区分同一病例中的不同切片。两阶段监督训练：(1) 对齐器微调阶段，使用异质 WSI-文本对进行 WSI 描述生成；(2) 病例级监督微调阶段，在病例-报告对上进行结构化报告生成。使用 5× 放大倍率的 512×512 图块，将平均序列长度减少至传统 20× 图块的 1/64。

Result: 在 ROUGE-L/METEOR/BLEU-4 指标上取得高分，AI 评估中模型输出一致优于强基线。内存和运行时间效率显著提升，仅需半张 H100 GPU 即可完成训练。消融实验揭示了性能-效率权衡，并识别出提升多 WSI 场景鲁棒性的简单策略。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个强有力且可复现的高效病理报告生成基线，降低了有限计算资源下多 WSI VLM 研究的门槛。简单设计（低倍率图块、轻量对齐器、两阶段训练）在实用性和性能之间取得了良好平衡。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦病理学全切片图像（WSI）的结构化报告生成，属于医疗垂直领域的生成应用，与现有 canonical 子主题"领域内容生成应用"（domain_application）高度匹配。同时涉及视觉语言模型（VLM）用于病理图像理解与报告生成，因此也匹配 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向。

Importance reason: Domain-specific pathology report generation is far from user's priority subtopics in video/image generation and world models. Signals: domain-specific pathology, token-efficient VLM, case-level report generation, low GPU requirement, not aligned with user's priority subtopics

Abstract: Generating clinically useful pathology reports for pathology cases from whole-slide images (WSIs) is challenging due to gigapixel resolution, long visual-token sequences, and the complexity of case-level reasoning, where a single case may contain multiple WSIs with heterogeneous tissues and ambiguous findings. We present a simple token-efficient vision--language model for case-level synoptic report generation that remains practical under constrained GPU memory. Our architecture follows a minimal three-component design: a frozen pathology patch encoder, a lightweight two-layer MLP vision-language aligner, and a large language model decoder, with an explicit WSI marker token to separate slides within a case. Training proceeds in two supervised stages: (1) aligner-only WSI captioning using heterogeneous WSI-text pairs, and (2) case-level supervised fine-tuning on case-report pairs for structured report generation. To reduce sequence length, we represent each slide using $512 \times 512$ patches at $5\times$ magnification, which reduces the average sequence length by up to $64\times$ times compared to the commonly used $20\times$ patches. Combined with efficient training techniques, we enable practical training with only half a NVIDIA H100 GPU. Across both training stages, our approach achieves high ROUGE-L/METEOR/BLEU-4 scores while being substantially more efficient in memory and runtime. In AI-based evaluations, our model is consistently preferred over strong baselines. Extensive ablations characterize performance-efficiency trade-offs and identify simple choices that improve robustness in multi-WSI settings. Overall, this work provides a strong, reproducible baseline for efficient pathology report generation, lowering the barrier to multi-WSI VLM research under limited compute.

</details>


### [150] [COLLEAGUE.SKILL: Automated AI Skill Generation via Expert Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31264)
*Tianyi Zhou, Dongrui Liu, Leitao Yuan, Jing Shao, Xia Hu*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (43.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 COLLEAGUE.SKILL，一个开源的自动化 trace-to-skill 蒸馏系统，能够从目标人物/角色的异构痕迹（如文档、对话、反馈）中提取可检查、可更新、可移植的 AI 技能包，包含能力轨道（实践、心智模型、决策启发式）和行为轨道（沟通风格、交互规则、修正历史），支持版本管理、回滚、跨宿主部署和受控分发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体虽然能完成孤立任务，但缺乏承载人类专业知识、判断力和交互风格的能力。现有的记忆系统和角色系统只能捕获碎片化证据，技能框架提供了可移植的封装格式，但缺乏从异构痕迹到可检查、可修正、可被智能体使用的技能的端到端蒸馏工作流。

Method: 提出一个自动化的 trace-to-skill 蒸馏系统，输入目标人物/角色的材料，输出版本化的技能包。技能包包含两个协调轨道：能力轨道（实践、心智模型、决策启发式）和行为轨道（沟通风格、交互规则、修正历史）。系统支持通过自然语言反馈进行检查、调用、更新、回滚，可跨智能体宿主安装，并可选受控分发。系统包含工件契约、生成工作流、修正生命周期、部署面和领域预设。

Result: 开源系统在 GitHub 上获得约 18.5k 星标；技能画廊列出 215 个技能（来自 165 位贡献者），技能卡片累计超过 100k 星标。展示了人格化技能可以作为可移植、可修正的包而非不透明的提示或隐藏记忆来表征。

Conclusion: 该系统证明了从异构痕迹到可检查、可修正、可移植的 AI 技能包的自动化蒸馏是可行的，为人格化 LLM 智能体的构建提供了新的范式，使技能不再是黑盒提示或隐藏记忆，而是可审计、可版本管理的标准化工件。

Classification reason: 该论文提出从专家知识痕迹自动蒸馏为可移植、可检查、可更新的 AI 技能包（skill package），用于构建人格化 LLM 智能体。核心贡献是端到端的 trace-to-skill 自动化工作流，属于 AI 智能体与生成工作流范畴，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 agent_workflow。

Importance reason: Focuses on AI agent skill generation, not directly aligned with user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: AI agent skill generation, expert knowledge distillation, trace-to-skill pipeline, person-grounded agents, GitHub stars 18.5k

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly expected not only to complete isolated tasks, but also to carry bounded representations of human expertise, judgment, and interaction style. Building such person-grounded agents remains difficult because actionable knowledge associated with a person or role is usually embedded in heterogeneous traces rather than written as clean instructions. Existing memory and persona systems capture fragments of this evidence, while skill frameworks provide portable packaging formats; however, there is no end-to-end workflow for distilling these traces into inspectable, correctable, and agent-usable skills. We present an automated trace-to-skill distillation system for generating person-grounded AI skills via expert knowledge distillation. Given materials from a target person or role, produces a versioned skill package with two coordinated tracks: a capability track for practices, mental models, and decision heuristics, and a bounded behavior track for communication style, interaction rules, and correction history. The package can be inspected, invoked, updated through natural-language feedback, rolled back, installed across agent hosts, and optionally prepared for controlled distribution. We describe the artifact contract, generation workflow, correction lifecycle, deployment surface, and domain presets implemented in the open-source system. At the time of writing, the public repository has approximately 18.5k GitHub stars; the gallery lists 215 skills from 165 contributors and more than 100k cumulative stars across listed skill cards. The system illustrates how person-grounded skills can be represented as portable, correctable packages rather than opaque prompts or hidden memories.

</details>


### [151] [Industrializing Prediction-Powered Inference: The GLIDE Library for Reliable GenAI and Agentic Systems Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31278)
*Grégoire Martinon, Ibrahim Merad, Mohammed Raki*

Main category: stat.ME, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (43.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GLIDE 是一个开源的 Python 库，统一了预测驱动推断（PPI）的各种方法，用于对生成式 AI 和智能体系统进行无偏估计和有效置信区间评估，显著减少人工标注成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统的可靠评估需要在昂贵的人工标注和有偏的 LLM-as-judge 代理之间取得平衡。现有 PPI 方法分散在各论文中且实现不完整，缺乏统一易用的工具库。

Method: GLIDE 库统一了多种 PPI 估计器（PPI++、分层 PPI、Predict-Then-Debias 及其分层变体、主动统计推断）和采样器（均匀、分层、主动、成本最优），采用 scipy 风格 API，并附带可复现的蒙特卡洛验证套件和基于经验的方法选择决策树。

Result: 在智能体评估案例研究中，GLIDE 在同等精度下实现了显著的人工标注节省，验证了 PPI 方法在减少标注成本方面的有效性。

Conclusion: GLIDE 为生成式 AI 和智能体系统的可靠评估提供了统一、易用的工具，有望推动 PPI 方法在 生成式 AI 评估中的广泛采用。

Classification reason: 该论文关注智能体系统的可靠评估方法，提出基于预测驱动推断（PPI）的统一评估库 GLIDE，属于 生成式 AI 评测与基准方向。论文核心是解决 LLM-as-judge 评估偏差问题，与 [CANONICAL] evaluation_benchmark（生成式 AI 评测与基准）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on evaluation methodology for GenAI/agents, not directly on core generative model development or efficiency. Signals: Evaluation tool, PPI library, reduces annotation cost, not core generation method, no direct video/image generation focus

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of agentic systems requires unbiased estimates with valid uncertainty, but standard practice navigates between costly human annotation and biased LLM-as-judge proxies. Prediction-powered inference (PPI) combines both into debiased estimates with valid confidence intervals, yet its various methods remain scattered across papers under partial implementations. We introduce GLIDE, an open-source Python library that unifies state-of-the-art PPI estimators (PPI++, Stratified PPI, Predict-Then-Debias and its stratified variants, Active Statistical Inference) and samplers (uniform, stratified, active, cost-optimal) under a scipy-style API specialized to mean estimation. GLIDE ships with a reproducible Monte Carlo validation suite, an empirically grounded decision tree for method selection, and an agentic evaluation case study showing substantial annotation savings at equivalent precision. The GLIDE package is available at this URL:

</details>


### [152] [TraceGraph: Shared Decision Landscapes for Diagnosing and Improving Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31308)
*Junjie Nian, Kang Chen, Ge Zhang, Yixin Cao, Yugang Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (48.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 TraceGraph，一种基于图的框架，将多模型智能体轨迹转化为共享决策景观图。通过构建动作-观测状态图、标注生产性核心与陷阱区域，并用三种事件（访问、陷阱暴露、修复）总结每条轨迹，揭示聚合分数隐藏的导航差异。在 SWE-bench 上，基于陷阱区域的恢复流水线将解决率从 40.4% 提升至 43.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评测通常将丰富的交互轨迹简化为通过率或奖励分数，丢失了过程信息，无法揭示模型在任务中的具体行为差异和失败模式。

Method: TraceGraph 为每个任务构建一个图，节点为可观测的动作-观测状态，边为状态转移，图来自多个模型的轨迹池（去标识化）。然后标注生产性核心（高成功率区域）和陷阱区域（低成功率区域），并用三种事件（访问、陷阱暴露、修复）总结每条轨迹。进一步提出陷阱感知恢复流水线：运行时检测器在状态匹配历史陷阱区域时触发，从同一前缀评估轻量级延续策略。

Result: 在五个基准分片上的分析显示，TraceGraph 能揭示聚合分数隐藏的导航差异，并区分不同分片是奖励避障还是恢复能力。在 SWE-bench 上，最佳单因素恢复策略将解决率从 40.4% 提升至 43.5%（按提供者分片）和从 41.0% 提升至 44.8%（公共实例）。

Conclusion: TraceGraph 提供了一种过程词汇表，用于理解智能体基准测试的内容、模型在共享景观上的差异，以及如何利用失败区域指导下游改进。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 TraceGraph 框架，将多模型智能体轨迹转化为共享决策景观图，用于诊断和改善智能体行为，核心贡献在于智能体评测方法创新（揭示聚合分数隐藏的导航差异、识别陷阱区域等），属于 生成式 AI 评测与基准方向，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 evaluation_benchmark。

Importance reason: Focuses on agent trajectory analysis, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: Agent evaluation, Graph-based trajectory analysis, SWE-bench improvement, Not aligned with priority subtopics, No boost keywords matched

Abstract: Agent benchmarks increasingly record rich interaction trajectories, yet evaluation often reduces each rollout to a pass rate or reward score. We introduce TraceGraph, a graph-based framework that turns released multi-model agent trajectories into shared decision landscapes. For each task, TraceGraph builds a graph over observable action-observation states from pooled rollouts before model identity is introduced. It then overlays outcome-informed productive cores and trap regions, and summarizes each rollout with three events: Access, Trap exposure, and Repair. Across trajectories spanning five benchmark splits, TraceGraph profiles reveal navigation differences hidden by aggregate scores and show that splits differ in whether they reward avoiding traps or recovering from them. The same TraceGraph landscape also motivates a trap-aware recovery pipeline for SWE-bench: aruntime detector fires on states matching historical trap regions, then lightweight continuation policies are evaluated from the same prefix. On fired states, the best pooled single-factor policy raises official resolved rate from 40.4% to 43.5% on the per-provider fired subset and from 41.0% to 44.8% on common-fired instances, with provider-specific active components. Overall, TraceGraph provides a process vocabulary for asking what agent benchmarks test, where models diverge on a shared landscape, and how failure regions can guide downstream improvement.

</details>


### [153] [Learning to Adapt: Self-Improving Web Agent via Cognitive-Aware Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31365)
*Weile Chen, Bingchen Miao, Qifan Yu, Wendong Bu, Guoming Wang, Wenqiao Zhang, Shengyu Zhang, Juncheng Li, Siliang Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

TL;DR: 本文提出SCALE框架，通过选择器、预测器和评判器三个对抗角色以及SCALE-Hop图探索策略，使Web智能体能够自主发现自身局限并通过环境探索扩展认知边界，无需人工专家轨迹即可实现自适应学习。在19个真实网站收集的SCALE-20k数据集上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Web智能体依赖手工设计的执行流程或昂贵的专家轨迹，难以适应复杂动态环境。需要一种无需人工标注、能自主探索并自我提升的Web智能体框架。

Method: 提出SCALE框架，包含三个对抗角色：Selector（选择器）识别智能体的认知边界，Predictor（预测器）预测动作结果，Judger（评判器）评估探索质量。同时提出SCALE-Hop图探索策略，通过全局规划避免局部探索陷阱。基于探索轨迹构建SCALE-20k数据集用于后续训练。

Result: 在多个真实Web环境上的实验表明，SCALE显著提升了多种MLLM基座模型的性能和泛化能力，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SCALE提供了一种可扩展、可泛化的Web智能体自主学习和自适应方案，无需人工专家轨迹即可实现持续自我提升，为构建真正自主的Web智能体奠定了基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于MLLM的Web智能体SCALE，通过自认知探索与环境交互实现自主学习和自适应，属于LLM智能体与自动化工作流范畴。映射到multimodal_understanding_generation方向下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "agent_workflow"（AI智能体与生成工作流），同时该方向也匹配生成式 AI方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on web agent self-adaptation, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: Web agent, self-improving, cognitive-aware exploration, MLLM, SCALE-20k dataset

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have led to promising progress in web agents. However, existing web agents often rely on handcrafted execution pipelines or expensive expert trajectories, limiting their adaptability to complex, dynamic environments. To address these challenges, we propose SCALE (Self-Cognitive-Aware Learning and Exploration), which leverages three adversarial roles, Selector, Predictor, and Judger to autonomously discover the agent's limitations and expand its cognitive boundaries through environmental exploration. Moreover, we propose SCALE-Hop, a graph exploration strategy that facilitates global planning and helps agents avoid local exploration traps. To further support learning, we construct SCALE-20k, a large-scale dataset collected from 19 real-world websites, containing diverse task types and structured demonstrations generated from SCALE's exploration traces. Experimental results show that our approach significantly improves the performance and generalization of multiple MLLMs in various web environments. Our framework offers a scalable and generalizable solution for building truly autonomous and adaptive web agents.

</details>


### [154] [AutoSci: A Memory-Centric Agentic System for the Full Scientific Research Lifecycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31468)
*Weitong Qian, Beicheng Xu, Zhongao Xie, Bowen Fan, Guozheng Tang, Jiale Chen, Xinzhe Wu, Mingtian Yang, Chenyang Di, Jiajun Li, Lingching Tung, Peichao Lai, Yifei Xia, Ziyi Guo, Yanwei Xu, Yanzhao Qin, Shaoduo Gan, Xupeng Miao, Bin Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (48.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: AutoSci 是一个以记忆为中心的智能体系统，通过 SciMem（结构化科研记忆）、SciFlow（五阶段科研工作流）、SciDAG（DAG 多智能体算子）和 SciEvolve（反馈驱动的自我进化）四个模块，实现从文献理解到审稿回复的完整科研生命周期自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统科学研究依赖研究人员手动协调文献、想法、实验、手稿和审稿回复，过程耗时且难以系统化管理。现有 LLM 科研智能体系统要么只覆盖部分环节，要么缺乏持久记忆和自我进化能力，无法支持完整的科研生命周期。

Method: AutoSci 包含四个核心模块：(1) SciMem——基于模式（schema）的科研记忆系统，将长期知识记忆（可复用的科学知识）与活跃研究记忆（项目级产物如想法、实验、手稿、审稿）分离管理；(2) SciFlow——通过 harness 控制状态、上下文、验证、反馈和编排，执行从文献理解到 rebuttal 的五阶段生命周期；(3) SciDAG——用 DAG 形状的多智能体算子和可复用的阶段特定模板增强复杂技能；(4) SciEvolve——将来自用户、实验、审稿和外部环境的反馈信号转化为对 SciMem 组织、SciFlow 技能和 SciDAG 模板的版本化更新。

Result: 论文展示了 AutoSci 作为一个持久化科研环境，能够跨项目执行、记忆和进化，但未提供具体的定量实验结果或与基线系统的对比评测。

Conclusion: AutoSci 通过记忆中心化的智能体系统设计，填补了现有科研自动化系统在完整生命周期支持、持久记忆和自我进化方面的空白，为构建统一的自动化科学研究系统提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 AutoSci，一个基于 LLM 的科研智能体系统，覆盖文献理解、实验、写作、审稿回复等完整科研工作流，属于 AI 智能体与自动化工作流方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] agent_workflow。

Importance reason: Focuses on AI agent workflow for scientific research, not on core generative model advances. Signals: AI agent system, scientific research automation, memory-centric design, no direct generative model contribution, low match to priority subtopics

Abstract: Scientific research has traditionally been human-intensive, requiring researchers to coordinate literature, ideas, experiments, manuscripts, and review responses across long project cycles. The rise of LLM-based scientific agents creates an opportunity to automate this process. Such a system must support the full research lifecycle, maintain structured persistent memory across projects, and improve its own research procedures over time. However, existing systems either partially satisfy or fail to satisfy these requirements, leaving a gap for a unified automated scientific research system. As a result, we present AutoSci, a memory-centric agentic system for the full scientific research lifecycle. AutoSci is organized around four modules. SciMem provides schema-governed research memory, separating Long-Term Knowledge Memory for reusable scientific knowledge from Active Research Memory for project-level artifacts such as ideas, experiments, manuscripts, and reviews. SciFlow executes a five-stage lifecycle from literature understanding to rebuttal through a harness that controls state, context, verification, feedback, and orchestration. SciDAG augments difficult skills with DAG-shaped multi-agent operators and reusable stage-specific templates. SciEvolve converts feedback signals from users, experiments, reviews, and external environments into versioned updates to SciMem organization, SciFlow skills, and SciDAG templates. Together, these modules make AutoSci a persistent research environment that can execute, remember, and evolve across research projects. The code repository is available at .

</details>


### [155] [GUI-C$^2$: Coarse-to-Fine GUI Grounding via Difficulty-Aware Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30884)
*Junlong Li, Chao Hao, Lap-Pui Chau, Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (58.98) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 GUI-C²，一种基于难度感知强化学习的粗到细 GUI 元素定位方法。在数据层面，提出 GUI-D 数据挖掘与难度评分流水线，筛选高训练价值样本并分配难度权重；在策略层面，提出区域门控的粗到细精炼机制，通过模型内部不确定性信号逐步缩小视野，并配合改进感知的阶段奖励确保每次精炼有效提升定位精度。实验表明该方法达到 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的 GUI 智能体定位方法存在两个层面的局限：数据层面，所有训练样本被同等对待，忽略了不同难度样本对基线模型的训练价值差异，导致训练效率低下甚至崩溃；策略层面，现有方法难以平衡裁剪大区域以获取充分上下文与裁剪小区域以减少冗余之间的权衡，且复杂决策过程对小参数模型不友好且增加推理时间。

Method: 数据层面提出 GUI-D 流水线，通过适当测试识别高训练价值样本并分配难度分数以指导训练权重。策略层面提出 GUI-C²，采用区域门控的粗到细精炼机制，通过模型内部不确定性信号逐步缩小视野，自适应地为大目标保留上下文、为小目标放大精度，并配合改进感知的阶段奖励确保每次精炼真正推进定位。同时简化决策过程以减少额外推理时间。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法在 GUI 元素定位任务上达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 本文通过难度感知的数据筛选和粗到细的精炼策略，有效解决了 GUI 智能体定位中训练效率低和上下文-精度权衡的问题，为 GUI 智能体提供了更高效、更精确的定位方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 GUI 智能体通过强化学习进行粗到细的 GUI 元素定位，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的 AI 智能体与生成工作流（agent_workflow）范畴，涉及智能体决策、工具增强和自动化交互流程。

Importance reason: Strong GUI grounding method with novel difficulty-aware RL and coarse-to-fine refinement, but not directly aligned with top priority subtopics. Signals: GUI grounding, reinforcement learning, coarse-to-fine, difficulty-aware, agentic systems

Abstract: Existing agentic reinforcement learning methods for GUI grounding have limitations at two levels. At the data level, current approaches typically treat all training samples equally, although their training value to the baseline model varies with difficulty. Overlooking this can greatly reduce training efficiency or even cause collapse. At the strategy level, existing frameworks struggle to balance the trade-off between cropping larger regions for sufficient context and smaller ones for reduced redundancy, a tension inherent to tool-augmented grounding agents. In addition, overly complex decision-making is difficult for small-parameter models and significantly increases inference time. To address these issues, at the data level, we propose GUI-D, a data mining and difficulty scoring pipeline that identifies the training-worthy samples by proper testing and assigns difficulty scores to guide subsequent training weights. At the strategy level, we propose GUI-C$^2$, which employs an area-gated coarse-to-fine refinement mechanism that progressively narrows the visual field via model-internal uncertainty signals, adaptively reserving context for large targets while amplifying precision for small ones, reinforced by improvement-aware stage rewards that ensure each refinement genuinely advances grounding. Meanwhile, we simplify the decision-making process to greatly reduce additional inference time. Finally, extensive experiments show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance. The code and data will be publicly available.

</details>


### [156] [Configurable Reward Model for Balanced Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30487)
*Zhengping Jiang, Mehran Khodabandeh, Akash Bharadwaj, Manik Bhandari, Mayur Srungarapu, Anqi Liu, Benjamin Van Durme, Li Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类
