TL;DR: 本文提出可配置安全奖励模型（CSRM），通过配置目标数据增强和联合优化校准安全合规与奖励建模，在可配置安全基准上达到SOTA，并显著改善LLM的有用性-安全性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令微调LLM和独立安全分类器难以泛化到新的安全配置，缺乏能够根据不断变化的安全规范进行显式配置的奖励模型。

Method: 提出CSRM，联合优化校准安全合规与奖励建模；采用配置目标数据增强，强制指令遵循同时保留相对严重性结构；使RM对细粒度安全配置和对话细微差别敏感。

Result: 在CoSApien上达到94.6% F1，DynaBench上达到75.8% F1，无需额外人工标注；下游安全对齐中显著改善有用性-安全性权衡。

Conclusion: CSRM在可配置安全基准上达到SOTA，能有效泛化到未见过的安全配置，为LLM安全对齐提供了一种灵活且高效的解决方案。

Classification reason: 论文提出可配置的安全奖励模型（CSRM）用于LLM安全对齐，属于生成内容安全与治理范畴，与生成式 AI方向下的safety_governance（生成内容安全与治理）高度匹配。同时涉及多模态理解与生成方向下的generation_alignment（生成模型对齐与奖励），但核心贡献在安全治理。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM safety alignment, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in generation and efficiency. Signals: Safety alignment, reward model, helpfulness-safety tradeoff, configurable safety, LLM alignment

Abstract: Aligning large language models (LLMs) to heterogeneous and rapidly evolving safety requirements remains a critical challenge. Existing instruction-tuned LLMs and standalone safety classifiers often fail to generalize to new safety configurations, motivating the need for Reward Models (RMs) that are explicitly configurable to changing specifications. We introduce the Configurable Safety Reward Model (CSRM), which is jointly optimized for calibrated safety compliance and reward modeling. Our approach is supported by configuration-targeted data augmentation that enforces instruction adherence while preserving relative severity structure. The resulting RM is sensitive to fine-grained safety configurations and conversational nuances, substantially improving generalization to previously unseen safety configurations. CSRM achieves state-of-the-art performance on recent configurable safety benchmarks, including CoSApien (94.6% F1) and DynaBench (75.8% F1), without requiring additional human annotation. When used for downstream safety alignment, CSRM yields LLMs with a significantly improved helpfulness-safety tradeoff compared to existing baselines.

</details>


### [157] [Bounded Behavioral Indistinguishability for Black-Box LLM Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30448)
*Munawar Hasan*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (40.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出有界行为不可区分性（bounded behavioral indistinguishability）概念，用于评估黑盒LLM蒸馏中学生模型是否在行为上与教师模型不可区分，而不仅仅是输出语义相似。在Qwen和Llama系列模型上的实验表明，LoRA蒸馏虽能提升语义相似度，但对抗性评估仍能检测到行为差异，说明语义保真度不足以衡量蒸馏质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒LLM蒸馏评估仅关注输出语义相似性或任务一致性，但输出相似并不等同于行为不可区分。缺乏一个形式化的框架来评估学生模型是否在行为上与教师模型真正不可区分，尤其是在对抗性设置下。

Method: 形式化定义了(ε,q,t,A)-行为不可区分性，其中ε为区分优势界，q为查询次数界，t为计算资源界，A为敌手类别。在Qwen和Llama教师-学生对上，使用5000条提示的行为探测套件，通过语义相似度、对抗判别器、成对教师识别、A/B交换一致性过滤等方法评估蒸馏前后的行为不可区分性。

Result: LoRA将Qwen的语义相似度从0.788提升至0.862，Llama从0.814提升至0.874。但对抗评估显示行为差异仍然存在：学习判别器保持非零优势，风格/格式、鲁棒性和领域技术提示中存在伪影。跨族Llama评判器下，Qwen区分优势从0.158降至0.081。查询预算实验表明，分歧引导采样并不一致优于分层随机采样。

Conclusion: 语义保真度有用但不充分：黑盒LLM蒸馏需要基于有界、对抗性和类别感知的评估框架。行为不可区分性应作为蒸馏质量的核心评估维度。

Classification reason: 该论文研究黑盒LLM蒸馏的行为不可区分性评估，提出有界行为不可区分性形式化框架，属于生成式 AI评测范畴（评估蒸馏模型与教师模型的行为相似度而非仅输出相似度），因此映射到[生成式 AI]方向下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成式 AI 评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM distillation evaluation, not aligned with user's priority subtopics in multimodal generation or world models. Signals: LLM distillation evaluation, behavioral indistinguishability, adversarial evaluation, not multimodal generation, not world model

Abstract: Black-box LLM distillation is usually evaluated as an output-matching problem: a student is considered successful when its responses are semantically similar to, or task-consistent with, those of a teacher. However, output similarity does not imply that the student is behaviorally indistinguishable from the model it imitates. We introduce bounded behavioral indistinguishability, formalized as $(\epsilon,q,t,\mathbb{A})$-behavioral indistinguishability over an explicit prompt distribution, where $\epsilon$ bounds distinguishing advantage, $q$ bounds oracle queries, $t$ bounds computation, and $\mathbb{A}$ denotes the adversary class. We instantiate this notion on Qwen and Llama teacher-student pairs using a controlled $5,000$-prompt behavioral probe suite. For each family, we compare the teacher with both the base student and the LoRA-distilled student, measuring whether distillation reduces distinguishability rather than merely improving similarity. LoRA raises semantic similarity from $0.788$ to $0.862$ for Qwen and from $0.814$ to $0.874$ for Llama. Yet adversarial evaluation reveals remaining behavioral differences: learned discriminators retain nonzero advantage, and pairwise category analysis shows artifacts concentrated in style/format, robustness, and domain-technical prompts. A pairwise teacher-identification adversary confirms this trend. With a different-family Llama judge and A/B-swap consistency filtering, Qwen distinguishing advantage drops from $0.158$ for the base student to $0.081$ after LoRA distillation. Query-budget experiments show that disagreement-guided acquisition does not consistently outperform stratified random sampling, indicating that coverage and diversity remain strong baselines. Our results show that semantic fidelity is useful but insufficient: black-box LLM distillation requires bounded, adversarial, and category-aware evaluation.

</details>


### [158] [Generating Reports or Repeating Templates? Measuring and Mitigating Template Collapse in 3D CT Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30984)
*Tom Maye-Lasserre, Yitong Li, Bailiang Jian, Morteza Ghahremani, Benedikt Wiestler, Christian Wachinger*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (39.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现3D CT报告生成中的"模板坍缩"问题——模型生成流畅但病理检测率低、输出多样性差。提出CLarGen框架，将"检测什么"与"如何表述"解耦，通过潜在查询Transformer进行多标签病理检测、病理引导检索和医学语言模型合成报告，显著提升临床准确性（macro-F1从0.189提升至0.487）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D医学视觉语言模型虽然能生成流畅的放射学报告，但存在严重的模板坍缩问题：模型倾向于生成通用模板文本，忽略罕见但关键的病理发现。这一问题源于3D医学影像的独特约束（数据有限、标签严重不平衡、体素编码器信号弱），导致文本生成目标鼓励捷径学习和弱语义对齐的报告。

Method: 提出CLarGen解耦框架，将"检测什么"（临床检测）与"如何表述"（语言合成）分离。包含三个组件：(i) 潜在查询Transformer（Latent Query Transformer）进行多标签病理检测；(ii) 病理引导检索（pathology-guided retrieval）获取临床匹配的示例；(iii) 医学语言模型基于检测结果和检索上下文合成最终报告。

Result: 在多个SOTA 3D CT报告生成基线模型上，CLarGen有效缓解了模板坍缩，显著提升临床准确性：macro-F1从0.189提升至0.487，CRG从0.368提升至0.472，同时保持报告流畅性。

Conclusion: 显式、可测量的临床语义锚定对于构建抗模板坍缩的3D CT报告生成系统至关重要。解耦检测与生成的设计范式能有效提升医学报告生成的临床实用性和输出多样性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究3D CT医学报告生成，属于医疗垂直领域的生成应用，与现有 canonical subtopic "领域内容生成应用"（domain_application）高度匹配。同时涉及视觉语言模型（VLM）在医学影像中的应用，也匹配 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D CT report generation, a medical domain application with negative keywords, not aligned with priority subtopics. Signals: Template collapse in medical report generation, Decoupled detection and language synthesis, Low pathology detection in 3D CT VLMs, Clinical accuracy improvement, Not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Modern 3D medical vision-language models (VLMs) can generate fluent radiology-style text while exhibit critically low pathology detection and output diversity, collapsing to generic templates that under-report rare yet critical findings. We identify this failure mode as Template Collapse. This failure stems from the unique constraints of 3D medical imaging, e.g., limited data, severe label imbalance, and weak signals from volumetric encoders. Under these constraints, text-generation objectives encourage shortcut learning and fluent but weakly grounded reports. We systematically diagnose the Template Collapse through clinical fidelity, output diversity, normal-template bias, and rare-finding survival. To mitigate it, we propose CLarGen, a decoupled framework that separates what to say (clinical detection) from how to say it (language synthesis). CLarGen uses (i) a Latent Query Transformer for multi-label pathology detection, (ii) pathology-guided retrieval for clinically matched exemplars, and (iii) a medical language model to synthesize the final report from detected findings and retrieved context. Across state-of-the-art 3D CT report generation baselines, CLarGen mitigates Template Collapse and substantially improves clinical accuracy (macro-F1 0.487 vs. 0.189; CRG 0.472 vs. 0.368) while maintaining fluent reporting. Our results suggest that explicit, measurable clinical grounding is essential for template-collapse-resistant 3D CT report generation. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [159] [Task-Focused Memorization for Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31075)
*Tao Zou, Yichen He, Tian Qiu, Yuan Lin, Hang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 值得关注 (76.43) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 TaskMem，一种基于强化学习的任务聚焦记忆策略学习框架，使多模态智能体能够从无限的多模态观测流中动态选择与未来任务相关的信息进行记忆。采用两阶段训练范式：第一阶段学习如何记忆（优化记忆质量），第二阶段在部署后学习记忆什么（通过适配器微调使记忆策略聚焦于任务相关内容）。在 VideoMME、EgoLife、EgoTempo 的流式评测基准上，基于 Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B 的 TaskMem 将 VQA 准确率分别提升 6.3%、7.0% 和 5.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能体（如具身智能体）在环境中持续感知、推理和行动，接收无限的多模态观测流。现有长期记忆研究主要关注记忆模块设计和基本准确性/保真度，但关键挑战在于确定"记忆什么"——智能体需要从信息组合爆炸中选择性保留与角色相关且对未来任务有价值的内容。目前缺乏一种可学习的记忆策略来动态调整记忆焦点。

Method: TaskMem 将记忆生成建模为可学习的记忆策略，采用两阶段训练范式。第一阶段：在基本保真度要求下优化记忆质量，学习"如何记忆"；第二阶段：部署后，智能体通过适配器微调基础 MLLM（Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B），利用近期环境任务定义奖励模型，引导记忆策略聚焦于任务相关内容，学习"记忆什么"。同时将 VideoMME、EgoLife、EgoTempo 重构为流式评测基准，模拟智能体处理流式观测和在线任务的真实场景，且问题仅能通过记忆回答（无法访问原始视频）。

Result: 基于 Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B，TaskMem 在三个流式基准上分别将 VQA 准确率提升 6.3%（VideoMME）、7.0%（EgoLife）和 5.3%（EgoTempo）。

Conclusion: TaskMem 通过强化学习框架将记忆生成建模为可学习的策略，有效解决了多模态智能体在无限观测流中"记忆什么"的关键问题。两阶段训练范式使智能体既能保证记忆质量，又能动态适应任务需求。该方法在多个流式评测基准上取得了显著提升，为多模态智能体的长期记忆构建提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态智能体的长期记忆生成策略，属于多模态智能体（multimodal agent）的核心能力构建，与 agent_workflow（AI 智能体与生成工作流）高度匹配。同时涉及多模态理解与生成方向中的 MLLM 基础模型（Qwen3-VL）应用。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with unified multimodal and efficient inference priorities, but lacks direct focus on top-weighted subtopics like autoregressive or diffusion video generation. Signals: multimodal agent memory, reinforcement learning memorization, streaming VQA benchmarks, Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B, task-focused policy

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for multimodal agents to build coherent experience, accumulate world knowledge, and achieve continual learning. However, constructing effective memory goes beyond memory module design and basic requirements such as accuracy and fidelity; the key challenge lies in determining what to memorize. Multimodal agents, such as embodied agents, continuously perceive, reason, and act in real or virtual environments, receiving an unbounded stream of multimodal observations. From this combinatorial explosion of information, an agent must selectively retain content that is relevant to its role in the environment and valuable for future tasks. To bridge this gap, we frame memory generation as a learnable memorization policy and introduce TaskMem (Task-focused Memorization Policy Learning), a reinforcement-learning-based framework that enables the policy to dynamically adjust its focus to the demands of real tasks encountered in the environment. TaskMem adopts a two-phase training paradigm: Phase One learns how to memorize by optimizing memory quality under fundamental fidelity requirements; Phase Two occurs after deployment, where the agent learns what to memorize by tuning an adapter on its base MLLM, using recent environment tasks to define a reward model that guides the memorization policy toward task-relevant content. To evaluate our approach, we reformulate VideoMME, EgoLife, and EgoTempo into streaming benchmarks that simulate a realistic setting in which an agent processes streaming observations and handles tasks arriving online. To isolate memory assessment, the questions must be answered using only the agent's memory, without access to raw video. Built on Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B, TaskMem improves VQA accuracy by 6.3%, 7.0%, and 5.3% on these benchmarks, respectively.

</details>


### [160] [The Architecture of Errors: From Universal Impossibility to Patch-Local LLM Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30628)
*Mikhail L. Arbuzov, Lee Mosbacker, Sisong Bei, Ziwei Dong, Dmitri Kalaev, Alexey Shvets*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (46.59) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明通用 LLM 可靠性在无限域上是不可能的（任何有限干预字典都无法覆盖无限多的故障模式），但在操作有界补丁（如法律审查、医疗 RAG、代码修复等）内，故障是稀疏且重复的，可靠性可转化为局部目录发现与干预覆盖问题，所需干预预算随序列长度呈多对数增长，并在目录饱和后趋于常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 可靠性方法依赖有限干预字典，但理论上无法保证在所有可能任务、工具、知识源和评估者期望下都能覆盖所有故障模式。本文旨在形式化这一局限性，并论证在部署场景（操作有界补丁）中可靠性是可实现的。

Method: 提出两个命题和一个推论的形式化框架：命题1（最坏情况负结果）证明无限域上有限干预字典无法覆盖所有可区分的故障模式；推论1（逆发现蕴含）指出故障模式发现的对数上界无法在不指数增加观测硬失败事件的情况下容纳线性更多的尾部分布模式；命题2（补丁局部正结果）证明在活跃模式对数暴露和头部重覆盖条件下，每个硬决策的干预预算随序列长度呈多对数增长，目录饱和后趋于常数。

Result: 形式化证明了通用 LLM 可靠性的不可能性定理，同时给出了操作有界补丁内可靠性可实现的条件与预算上界。框架将长上下文困难重新定位为硬决策数量本身随任务长度增长的问题，而非干预字典的指数增长问题。

Conclusion: LLM 可靠性不应追求通用无限覆盖，而应聚焦于操作有界补丁内的局部目录发现与干预覆盖。该框架为构建实际可部署的可靠 LLM 系统提供了理论基础，并明确了长上下文场景下可靠性仍然困难的边界条件。

Classification reason: 该论文从理论层面论证了通用 LLM 可靠性在无限域上的不可能性，并提出在操作有界补丁（如法律审查、医疗 RAG、代码修复等）内通过局部故障目录发现与干预覆盖实现可靠性的框架。这属于 生成式 AI 安全与治理范畴，与现有 canonical 子主题「生成内容安全与治理」高度相关。

Importance reason: Theoretical LLM reliability analysis lacks direct application to user's priority generative tasks. Signals: theoretical impossibility result, patch-local reliability, no empirical validation, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Universal LLM reliability is not a finite-library problem: across all possible tasks, tools, schemas, knowledge sources, and evaluator expectations, new intervention-distinguishable failure modes can appear without bound, so no finite intervention dictionary can guarantee bounded residual error for every such mode. But deployed systems do not operate over the whole universe. They operate inside operationally bounded patches (legal review, medical RAG, code repair, customer-support agents, contract extraction) with recurring tasks, schemas, tools, and evaluator expectations. Within such patches, empirical evidence suggests failures are sparse, repetitive, and concentrated in a small recurring catalogue, so reliability becomes a local catalogue-discovery and intervention-coverage problem rather than an exponential token-length problem. We formalize this transition with two propositions and one corollary. Proposition 1 is the worst-case-mode-wise negative result: no finite intervention dictionary covers every distinguishable failure mode of an unbounded domain. Corollary 1 is the inverse-discovery implication: the logarithmic upper bound on mode discovery cannot accommodate linearly more distinct tail modes without exponentially more observed hard-failure events. Proposition 2 is the positive patch-local result: under log active-mode exposure and head-heavy coverage, a sufficient per-hard-decision intervention budget grows polylogarithmically in sequence length and becomes domain-constant once the patch catalogue saturates. The framework relocates rather than dissolves long-context difficulty: where the number of hard decisions itself grows with task length, reliability remains hard; the contribution is to identify the on-axis intervention rather than to make those regimes easy.

</details>


### [161] [Counterfactual Graph for Multi-Agent LLM Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30653)
*Jiatan Huang, Mingchen Li, Ziming Li, Sunjae Kwon, Hong Yu, Chuxu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (40.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出CAGE-CAL框架，通过反事实图对比（有通信 vs 无通信）来校准多智能体LLM系统的置信度，解决通信导致的虚假共识问题，提升可靠性判别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统常将智能体间的一致同意视为可靠性的证据，但通信可能导致相关性失败和虚假共识，使得相同投票份额在不同拓扑结构下反映不同的可靠性。现有方法仅简单统计同意数量，无法区分真实共识与虚假共识。

Method: 提出CAGE-CAL框架，对每个查询，比较观测到的有通信智能体图与匹配的反事实无通信图，捕获成对失败相关性和组级依赖关系，估计观测依赖与无通信依赖之间的反事实偏移，并据此校准置信度。

Result: 在五个基准上，CAGE-CAL提升了可靠性判别能力，ECE指标具有竞争力，且校准后的置信度进一步改进了拓扑选择，优于最佳固定拓扑策略。

Conclusion: 多智能体LLM系统中的通信会引入虚假共识，CAGE-CAL通过反事实图校准有效缓解了这一问题，提升了系统可靠性，并为拓扑选择提供了更好的置信度信号。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多智能体LLM系统的校准问题，属于LLM智能体工作流中的可靠性提升方向，与[生成式 AI]方向下的[AI智能体与生成工作流]（agent_workflow）高度匹配。同时涉及多智能体系统的置信度校准，也与多模态理解与生成方向下的智能体工作流相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on multi-agent LLM calibration, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to generation or efficiency. Signals: Multi-agent calibration, Counterfactual graph, LLM confidence, Not in priority subtopics, No boost keywords matched

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems often treat agreement as evidence: when many agents in a panel give the same answer, that answer is assumed to be more reliable. We show that this assumption can fail after agents communicate. Communication can induce correlated failures and false consensus, so the same vote share may reflect reliable agreement in one topology but over-confidence in another. We propose CAGE-CAL, a counterfactual agent-graph calibration framework for multi-agent LLMs. For each query, CAGE-CAL compares an observed post-communication agent graph with a matched counterfactual no-communication graph, capturing both pairwise failure correlations and group-level dependencies. Rather than simply counting how many agents agree, CAGE-CAL estimates the counterfactual shift between observed and no-communication dependence, and calibrates confidence accordingly. Across five benchmarks, CAGE-CAL improves reliability discrimination with competitive ECE, and its calibrated confidence further improves topology selection over the best fixed-topology strategy.

</details>


### [162] [ExpGraph: Model-Agnostic Experience Learning with Graph-Structured Memory for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30712)
*Tao Feng, Chongrui Ye, Tianyang Luo, Jingjun Xu, Xueqiang Xu, Haozhen Zhang, Zhigang Hua, Yan Xie, Shuang Yang, Ge Liu, Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (43.34) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ExpGraph 提出了一种模型无关的 LLM 智能体经验学习框架，通过自演化的经验图结构存储历史轨迹中的成功技能和失败教训，利用图扩散和效用感知排序检索有用经验，并训练轻量级检索副驾驶（retrieval copilot）来优化经验检索，使冻结的 LLM 执行器无需参数更新即可复用经验，在问答、数学推理、代码生成和智能体环境（ALFWorld、AppWorld）中显著提升性能并减少交互步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体通常从零开始解决任务，无法有效复用历史经验中的成功策略或失败教训。微调方法虽然可以复用经验，但缺乏灵活性——当出现更强或更合适的执行器时，需要重新微调。因此，需要一种模型无关的经验学习框架，使冻结的 LLM 执行器能够通过外部经验复用来持续改进。

Method: ExpGraph 包含三个核心组件：(1) 经验图（Experience Graph），将历史轨迹总结为可复用的技能节点和失败教训节点，图结构自演化更新；(2) 图扩散与效用感知排序（Graph Diffusion & Utility-Aware Ranking），通过图扩散检索相关经验，并基于效用评分排序；(3) 检索副驾驶（Retrieval Copilot），使用强化学习训练，通过比较有无检索经验时执行器的表现差异来优化检索策略。整个框架无需更新 LLM 执行器的参数。

Result: 在 ExpSuite 基准上（涵盖问答、数学推理、代码生成、ALFWorld 和 AppWorld 智能体环境），ExpGraph 在静态任务上分别比最强基线提升 12.2%（小执行器）和 4.7%（大执行器），在智能体环境中提升 21.4% 和 12.7%，同时平均交互步数减少 12.7% 和 21.6%。消融实验验证了图结构经验、效用感知排序和自适应检索三者联合的有效性。

Conclusion: ExpGraph 证明了模型无关的外部经验复用可以显著提升 LLM 智能体的任务性能和执行效率，且不依赖执行器参数更新，具有良好的灵活性和可扩展性。图结构化的经验组织方式和自适应检索机制是实现有效经验复用的关键。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ExpGraph，一种模型无关的经验学习框架，使 LLM 智能体能够通过外部经验图（experience graph）复用历史成功策略和失败教训，无需参数更新。核心贡献在于 LLM 智能体的经验复用与工作流优化，属于 AI 智能体与生成工作流（agent_workflow）这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM agent experience learning, not on the user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: LLM agent experience learning, graph-structured memory, model-agnostic, retrieval copilot, ALFWorld

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have shown strong capabilities in reasoning, tool use, and multi-step interaction, but they often solve tasks from scratch and fail to reuse successful strategies or failure lessons from prior experience. Fine-tuning on collected experience can improve reuse, but it is inflexible when stronger or more suitable executors emerge. We propose ExpGraph, a model-agnostic experience learning framework that enables frozen and replaceable LLM executors to improve through external experience reuse without parameter updates. ExpGraph summarizes historical trajectories into reusable skills and failure lessons, organizes them as nodes in a self-evolving experience graph, and retrieves useful experiences through graph diffusion and utility-aware ranking. A lightweight retrieval copilot is trained with reinforcement learning using feedback that compares executor performance with and without retrieved experiences, while the graph is updated online from downstream task outcomes. We evaluate ExpGraph on ExpSuite, covering question answering, mathematical reasoning, code generation, and multi-step agentic environments including ALFWorld and AppWorld. ExpGraph improves over the strongest baseline by 12.2% and 4.7% on static tasks with smaller and larger executors, and by 21.4% and 12.7% in agentic environments, while reducing average interaction steps by 12.7% and 21.6%. Ablations show that graph-structured experience, utility-aware ranking, and adaptive retrieval jointly enable effective experience reuse across diverse tasks and executor models.

</details>


### [163] [Counterfactual Evaluation Reveals Hidden Capability Profiles in Clinical LLMs and Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30590)
*Matt Turk*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (39.12) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出因果敏感性分数（CSS），一种预注册的干预式评测指标，通过沿五个临床维度突变肿瘤病例来评估临床LLM是否根据新信号正确更新推荐。实验表明CSS与现有覆盖式指标（CMS）排名几乎相反，并揭示了所有前沿模型在手术状态干预上的安全盲点。该指标还可迁移至工具使用智能体场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有覆盖式评测指标（如CMS）无法区分两个得分相近的临床AI系统在输入变化时的行为差异——一个能根据新临床信号更新推荐，另一个则输出不变。需要一种能衡量模型因果响应能力的评测方法。

Method: 提出Causal Sensitivity Score (CSS)，沿五个临床维度（生物标志物翻转、既往治疗失败、生物标志物移除、手术状态变化、分期扰动）对肿瘤病例进行反事实干预，使用{0, 0.5, 1.0}三级评分判断模型是否按正确方向更新推荐。在224个病例上对6个前沿模型进行单次推理评测，并在ReAct风格智能体实验中验证指标迁移性。

Result: CSS与CMS排名几乎相反：6个模型全部改变排名，CMS最差模型成为CSS最佳模型。所有模型在手术状态干预上表现极差（CSS最高仅17.2%），CMS无法暴露此安全盲点。工具使用使5/6模型CSS提升2.5-20.3个百分点，但最低CSS模型即使检索相同图表仍无法更新推荐。

Conclusion: 干预式预注册指标CSS能捕捉覆盖式指标遗漏的模型响应能力，揭示了临床LLM在反事实条件下的隐藏能力剖面和安全盲点，可作为未来智能体强化学习的密集奖励信号。

Classification reason: 该论文提出因果敏感性分数（CSS）作为临床LLM和智能体的干预式评测指标，属于生成式 AI评测与基准范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic evaluation_benchmark高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on clinical LLM evaluation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and has negative keywords. Signals: clinical LLM evaluation, counterfactual metric, safety blind spot, tool-using agents, low personal relevance

Abstract: Two clinical AI systems can score nearly identically on coverage-based rubrics yet behave radically differently when their patient inputs change: one updates its recommendations to match the new clinical signal, while the other produces the same output regardless. We introduce the Causal Sensitivity Score (CSS), a pre-registered interventional metric that mutates oncology tumor-board cases along five clinically meaningful dimensions - biomarker flips, prior-treatment failures, biomarker removals, surgery-status changes, and stage perturbations - and scores whether each model updates its recommendations in the pre-registered correct direction using a {0, 0.5, 1.0} scale. Benchmarked against the Consensus Match Score (CMS), a coverage-based weighted recall metric, six frontier models from three labs evaluated in single-shot inference across 224 cases rank in nearly opposite orders: all six models change rank, the CMS-worst model becomes CSS-best, and one upper-mid CMS model ranks last on CSS. We further surface a universal safety blind spot: every frontier model fails on surgery-status interventions (at most 17.2% CSS on Family D), a finding CMS does not expose. The metric also transfers to tool-using agents: in a ReAct-style experiment, tool use improves CSS for five of six models (+2.5 to +20.3 percentage points), yet the lowest-CSS model retrieves the same chart sections and still fails to update its recommendations - revealing a structural responsiveness deficit visible only under counterfactual evaluation. Cross-judge replication and three-rater medical-professional validation confirm the aggregate findings. Interventional pre-registered metrics like CSS complement coverage-based evaluation for clinical AI agents: they capture responsiveness that coverage metrics miss and offer a candidate dense reward signal for future agentic RL systems.

</details>


### [164] [Detect in Any Scene: An Agentic Framework for Object Detection with Experience-Aware Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31174)
*Wenlun Zhang, Jun Yin, Kentaro Yoshioka*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 值得关注 (68.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 DetAS/DetAS-X，一个基于 MLLM 智能体的目标检测框架，将检测建模为动态决策过程，通过自适应图像恢复和多专家检测器组合，并引入经验积累机制实现经验感知推理，在六个挑战性基准上平均 F1 提升 28.36%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景中的目标检测面临图像退化多样、目标分布异质等挑战，现有检测器泛化能力不足。传统方法依赖预定义条件和静态流水线，缺乏对动态环境的适应性。

Method: 提出 DetAS 框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 自适应性图像恢复模块，动态决定是否以及如何增强图像；2) 多专家检测模块，集成多个领域专用检测器并通过实例级推理解决预测冲突。进一步提出 DetAS-X，引入自进化经验收集机制，从少量标注数据中积累节点级决策经验，在推理时实现经验感知推理，逐步优化决策策略。

Result: 在六个挑战性基准上，DetAS-X 显著优于现有基于 MLLM 的检测器，平均 F1 提升 28.36%，在 DarkFace 上最高提升 37.01%。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明智能体式检测框架在复杂动态环境中具有巨大潜力，为实际应用奠定了坚实基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DetAS，一个基于 MLLM 作为中央智能体的目标检测框架，通过动态组合恢复模块和专用检测器来构建检测工作流，属于 LLM 智能体与自动化工作流范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 agent_workflow（AI 智能体与生成工作流）。同时涉及 MLLM 作为核心组件，也匹配 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向。

Importance reason: Novel agentic detection framework with experience-aware reasoning, but outside core priority subtopics and uses negative keywords. Signals: MLLM agent, object detection, experience-aware reasoning, adaptive image restoration, multi-expertise detection

Abstract: Object detection in real-world scenarios remains challenging due to diverse image degradations and heterogeneous object distributions, which significantly hinder the generalization of existing detectors. Conventional approaches, including scene-specific representation learning and end-to-end pipeline design, are inherently limited by their reliance on predefined conditions and lack adaptability to dynamic environments. In this paper, we propose DetAS, an agentic detection framework that formulates object detection as a dynamic decision process. Instead of relying on static pipelines, DetAS leverages a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) as a central agent to adaptively compose detection workflows by selecting from a toolbox of restoration modules and specialized detectors. Specifically, DetAS consists of two key components: Self-Adaptive Image Restoration, which dynamically determines whether and how to enhance images for downstream detection, and Multi-Expertise Detection, which integrates multiple domain-specialized detectors and resolves their predictions through instance-level reasoning. To further improve decision quality under fine-grained conditions, we introduce Self-Evolving Experience Harvesting and extend the framework to DetAS-X, which accumulates node-level decision experience from a small set of annotated data and enables experience-aware reasoning during inference. This mechanism allows the system to progressively refine its decision policy and adapt to diverse real-world scenarios. Extensive experiments on six challenging benchmarks demonstrate that DetAS-X significantly outperforms existing MLLM-based detectors, achieving an average improvement of 28.36% in F1 score, with up to 37.01% gain on DarkFace. These results demonstrate the promise of agentic detection and establish a solid foundation for its application in complex and dynamic environments.

</details>


### [165] [Eywa: Provenance-Grounded Long-Term Memory for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30771)
*Resham Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (48.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Eywa 是一种基于溯源（provenance）的 AI 智能体长期记忆架构，通过将不可变源证据、规范事实、验证记忆与确定性检索路径分离，实现了可审计、可诊断、可更新的记忆系统。在 LoCoMo、LongMemEval-S 和 BEAM 基准上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 智能体记忆系统将源证据、提取事实、检索上下文和回答策略混入一个不透明的提示路径中，导致错误难以诊断——错误答案可能来自缺失证据、不支持的提取、过时状态、检索损失或回答模型行为。需要一种可审计、可追溯、可诊断的记忆架构。

Method: Eywa 架构的核心原则是"先有证据，后有信念"（evidence before belief）。它首先存储不可变的源证据，然后从中推导规范事实；通过类型化信号和源支持验证提取的记忆；通过确定性的多路由读取路径检索有边界的记忆上下文，检索过程中不调用任何 LLM。检索到的上下文与回答指令分开返回，允许同一记忆基座在不同规模的回答模型上评估。

Result: 在冻结的、工件记录的检索配置下，Eywa 在 LoCoMo C1-C4 分割上使用 Claude Sonnet 4.6 的写入和 QA 角色达到 90.19% 的评判准确率；在 LongMemEval-S 上达到 88.2% 的检索充分性准确率；在包含 700 个问题的技术记忆压力基准 BEAM 上达到 81.45% 的平均 nugget 分数和 85.29% 的 pass@score >= 0.5。

Conclusion: Eywa 证明了基于溯源的记忆架构能够显著提升 AI 智能体的记忆可靠性、可审计性和可诊断性。通过将证据、事实、检索和回答策略解耦，系统能够更准确地定位错误来源，为构建可信赖的持久化智能体提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Eywa，一种基于溯源（provenance）的长期记忆架构，用于 AI 智能体的持久化记忆存储、检索、审计与更新。核心贡献在于智能体记忆系统的设计，属于 AI 智能体与工作流范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] agent_workflow（AI 智能体与生成工作流）。同时涉及多模态理解与生成方向中的智能体应用。

Importance reason: Focuses on AI agent memory architecture, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: AI agent memory, provenance-grounded, long-term memory, retrieval accuracy, benchmark results

Abstract: AI agents that persist across sessions need memory they can retrieve, audit, update, and erase. Existing memory systems often collapse source evidence, extracted facts, retrieved context, and answer policy into one opaque prompt path, making failures difficult to diagnose: a wrong answer may come from missing evidence, unsupported extraction, stale state, retrieval loss, or answer-model behavior. We present Eywa, a provenance-grounded memory architecture built around evidence before belief. Eywa stores immutable source evidence before deriving canonical facts, validates extracted memories against typed signals and source support, and retrieves bounded memory context through a deterministic multi-route read path with zero LLM calls inside retrieval. Retrieved context is returned separately from answer instructions, allowing the same memory substrate to be evaluated across frontier, budget, and local answer models. Under a frozen, artifact-recorded retrieval configuration, Eywa reaches 90.19% judge accuracy on the LoCoMo C1-C4 split with Claude Sonnet 4.6 write and QA roles. On LongMemEval-S, it reaches 88.2% retrieval-sufficiency accuracy. On BEAM, a 700-question technical-memory stress benchmark, it reaches 81.45% mean nugget score and 85.29% pass@score >= 0.5. Full per-question artifacts, including questions, gold answers, model answers, retrieved context, and labels, are published at .

</details>


### [166] [AMNESIA: A Large Scale Medical Unlearning Benchmark Suite with Disease-Informed Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30599)
*Saeedeh Davoudi, Reihaneh Iranmanesh, Ophir Frieder, Nazli Goharian*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (35.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: AMNESIA 是首个大规模医学遗忘学习基准，包含 70,560 个问答对，覆盖 11 种疾病类别，用于评估 LLM 在医学场景下的遗忘效果。实验发现随机遗忘单个患者会侵蚀同疾病其他患者的知识，揭示了患者级遗忘与共享临床知识分离的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学知识不断更新，需要在不完全重训练的情况下选择性遗忘已训练模型中的特定信息。现有遗忘学习基准依赖合成数据或小规模通用数据，缺乏针对临床场景的大规模评估。

Method: 构建 AMNESIA 基准：从 8,820 份患者病历中生成 70,560 个问答对，涵盖事实性（直接回忆）和推理性（临床推断）两类问题，覆盖 11 种疾病类别。在随机患者级和疾病级两种遗忘设置下，评估了四种主流遗忘方法（如梯度上升、KL 散度等），并引入新的医学术语泄露检测指标。

Result: 实验表明，随机遗忘单个患者会显著侵蚀同疾病类别下其他患者的知识，现有遗忘方法难以区分患者个体信息与共享的临床知识。新提出的术语泄露指标有效检测了遗忘后模型仍保留的医学知识。

Conclusion: AMNESIA 为医学遗忘学习提供了首个大规模基准，揭示了患者级遗忘与共享临床知识分离的核心挑战，呼吁开发能更好区分个体患者与通用医学知识的新遗忘方法。

Classification reason: 该论文研究医学大语言模型的机器遗忘（machine unlearning），属于生成模型概念擦除与遗忘学习范畴。现有 [CANONICAL] subtopic "unlearning_erasure" 完全匹配，且该论文也涉及多模态理解与生成方向（医学LLM）。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical unlearning, which is a negative keyword topic, and lacks direct connection to the user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: Medical unlearning benchmark, disease-informed analysis, patient-level forgetting, clinical knowledge separation, low relevance to user's generative AI focus

Abstract: Medical knowledge is continuously evolving. This creates a need to update or selectively forget information encoded in already-trained medical LLMs. Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of specific training data from a model without full retraining. Yet, existing unlearning benchmarks rely on synthetic or small-scale general data, leaving clinical unlearning understudied. We introduce AMNESIA, the first large-scale, open source benchmark for medical unlearning, with 70,560 question-answer pairs from 8,820 patient notes across 11 disease categories. AMNESIA includes both factual questions testing direct recall and reasoning questions testing clinical inference. We use it to evaluate four widely used unlearning methods at both random patient and disease-level, and introduce a new metric for detecting leakage of medical terminology. We show that unlearning individual patients erodes knowledge of others with the same condition, calling for methods that can better separate patients from shared clinical knowledge.

</details>


### [167] [The Regularizing Power of Language-Training Deepfake Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31192)
*Benedikt Hopf, Zongwei Wu, Radu Timofte*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 值得关注 (68.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出利用语言训练的正则化能力来提升深度伪造检测器的泛化性和可解释性。采用双编码器架构（冻结的专家检测器 + LoRA微调的MLLM编码器）和两阶段训练：先进行二值对齐缓解过拟合，再通过强化学习鼓励模型在分类前生成描述性推理，从而优先利用高层级可描述特征。实验在多个基准数据集上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度伪造检测面临两大挑战：泛化性（过拟合于低层级领域特定伪影）和可解释性。现有方法通常分别处理这两个问题，本文提出可以联合解决，因为可描述的伪影通常泛化性更好，因此可以利用语言作为正则化机制。

Method: 采用双编码器架构：一个冻结的专家检测器（提供低层级特征）和一个LoRA微调的MLLM编码器（提供高层级可描述特征）。两阶段训练：第一阶段进行二值对齐训练，使MLLM学会组合特征以缓解过拟合；第二阶段使用强化学习（奖励"先解释后分类"行为），鼓励模型在分类前生成描述性推理，从而优先利用高层级鲁棒特征。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的跨数据集评估中，该方法以较大幅度超越了现有最先进方法。即使推理时省略推理链，模型仍能保持性能提升，验证了语言正则化带来的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 语言训练可以有效作为深度伪造检测的正则化机制，同时提升泛化性和可解释性。双编码器架构和两阶段训练策略能够引导模型优先利用高层级可描述特征，在跨数据集泛化上取得显著提升。

Classification reason: 论文研究深度伪造检测（deepfake detection），属于 生成式 AI 检测与溯源这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题的核心范畴。

Importance reason: Novel use of language regularization for deepfake detection, but outside user's priority subtopics. Signals: deepfake detection, language regularization, MLLM, generalization, interpretability

Abstract: Recently, thanks to the advent of Multimodal-LLMs, deepfake detectors are striving not only to be generalizable but also interpretable. We propose that these two challenges can effectively be tackled jointly, since describable artifacts typically generalize better, opening the possibility to use language as a regularization mechanism. Since deepfake detection generally suffers from overfitting to low-level domain-specific artifacts, our intuition is that an LLM that has been pretrained on language would prefer high-level artifacts that can be described better. This way, we can use high-level features where possible, while training the model to use low-level features where necessary. We utilize a dual-encoder architecture, pairing a frozen specialist detector with a LoRA-tuned MLLM encoder, and a two-stage training curriculum: first, a binary alignment phase demonstrates that the intrinsic capability of MLLMs can effectively combine features to mitigate overfitting to dataset-specific artifacts. To further bolster generalization and achieve interpretability, we employ a reinforcement learning stage that encourages the model to generate descriptive reasoning before classifying, using only binary labels. By rewarding this "explain-then-classify" behavior, we explicitly incentivize the model to prioritize high-level, robust features. Crucially, this process yields both interpretable descriptions and a further boost in cross-dataset performance, even when reasoning chains are omitted at inference. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets validate our approach, outperforming state-of-the-art methods by a large margin.

</details>


### [168] [Extending AI for Research to the Humanities: A Multi-Agent Framework for Evidence-Grounded Scholarship](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30947)
*Yating Pan, Jiajun Zhang, Jun Wang, Qi Su*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (43.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 SPIRE，一个基于学术原语理论的多智能体框架，专门为人文学科设计，通过源发现、证据注释、比较、溯源检查、采样、引用绑定和论证综合等智能体角色，在古典中文和希腊罗马拉丁文献基准上实现了比 Naive LLM、Text RAG 和 GraphRAG 更可靠的引证证据恢复和更高质量的论证生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 研究智能体主要面向科学和工程领域，优化于可执行实验、代码和定量信号，但人文学科需要基于原始文献的阐释性、证据支撑的论证，要求忠实引用、可验证溯源和细读。现有方法无法满足这一需求。

Method: SPIRE 基于 Scholarly Primitives 理论，将人文学科常见操作建模为多个协作智能体角色（源发现、证据注释、比较、溯源检查、采样、引用绑定、论证综合），并在多尺度细读基底（段落、上下文内图社区、跨上下文语义聚类）上运行，实现证据支撑的学术论证生成。

Result: 在古典中文和希腊罗马拉丁文献的同行评审论文基准上，SPIRE 在引证证据恢复可靠性上优于 Naive LLM、Text RAG 和 GraphRAG，并在盲评中在答案准确性、深度、覆盖度和证据质量上获得更高分数。消融实验验证了学术操作智能体和细读检索的贡献。

Conclusion: SPIRE 证明了将人文学科学术原语建模为多智能体协作框架的有效性，为人文学科 AI 研究助手提供了新的范式，弥合了现有研究智能体与人文学科证据论证需求之间的差距。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一个面向人文学科的多智能体研究框架 SPIRE，属于 LLM 智能体在学术研究场景中的应用，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "AI 智能体与生成工作流" 高度匹配。同时涉及多模态理解与生成方向中的智能体工作流。

Importance reason: Focuses on humanities scholarship, not aligned with user's priority subtopics in multimodal generation, video, or efficient inference. Signals: humanities, multi-agent framework, evidence-grounded, classical Chinese, Greco-Roman Latin

Abstract: LLM-based research agents have advanced rapidly in science and engineering, where research is organized around executable experiments, code, and quantitative signals. Humanities scholarship, however, requires a different mode of reasoning: interpretive, evidence-grounded argument over primary sources, where scholarly value depends on faithful quotation, verifiable provenance, and close reading. Existing research agents remain largely optimized for execution and retrieval, not evidence-grounded interpretive reasoning. To address this gap, we introduce SPIRE (Scholarly-Primitives-Inspired Research Engine), a multi-agent framework for evidence-grounded humanities scholarship. Drawing on Scholarly Primitives theory, SPIRE casts recurring humanities operations as cooperating agent roles (source discovery, evidence annotation, comparison, provenance checking, sampling, citation binding, and argumentative synthesis) over a multi-scale close-reading substrate of passages, intra-context graph communities, and cross-context semantic clusters. On a peer-reviewed-paper benchmark over classical Chinese and Greco-Roman Latin scholarship, SPIRE recovers cited primary-source evidence more reliably than Naive LLM, Text RAG, and GraphRAG, and receives higher blind-judge scores on answer accuracy, depth, coverage, and evidence quality. Ablations show that both the scholarly-operation agents and close-reading retrieval contribute to evidence-grounded essays. Code, data catalogues, and reproduction scripts are released at .

</details>


### [169] [Learning Whom to Trust: Market-Feedback Adaptive Retrieval for Frozen LLMs in Event-Driven Financial RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31201)
*Zijie Zhao, Roy E. Welsch*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (29.23) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种市场反馈自适应的金融RAG系统，通过外部贝叶斯源记忆模块动态调整检索来源（新闻 vs SEC文件），在冻结LLM的情况下提升事件驱动型金融预测性能，在FNSPID数据集上将宏F1从0.438提升至0.471，夏普比率从0.52提升至0.84。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融RAG系统通常按文本相关性排序证据，但在金融市场中，有用的证据来源取决于事件类型、预测时间跨度和市场环境。现有方法忽略了检索来源的动态适应性，需要一种能根据市场反馈自动调整检索策略的方法。

Method: 提出Frozen Reader with Source Memory架构：保持LLM阅读器冻结，通过外部贝叶斯源记忆模块（Bayesian Source Memory）根据已到期的残差收益反馈更新检索层。系统为每个公司-新闻锚点检索相关金融新闻和SEC文件段落，附加决策前市场上下文卡片，预测多时间跨度的残差收益信号。

Result: 在89只纳斯达克股票的固定池上，Frozen Reader with Source Memory相比无记忆的冻结阅读器，宏F1从0.438提升至0.471，下游投资组合夏普比率从0.52提升至0.84。有监督LoRA阅读器对静态RAG有适度提升，但未超过冻结源记忆阅读器。

Conclusion: 对于金融RAG，学习从何处检索与学习如何阅读同等重要。所提出的市场反馈自适应检索方法提供了一种简单、模块化的途径，无需微调LLM即可显著提升金融事件预测性能。

Classification reason: 该论文研究金融领域的检索增强生成（RAG）系统，属于垂直领域（金融）的生成应用，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 domain_application（领域内容生成应用）。同时论文涉及多模态理解与生成方向中的检索增强生成，因此也匹配 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on financial RAG with negative keyword overlap, not aligned with priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: Financial RAG, market-feedback adaptation, frozen LLM, negative keyword: financial, low priority subtopic match

Abstract: Financial retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems typically rank evidence by textual relevance, but in financial markets the useful evidence source depends on event type, forecast horizon, and market context. We study news-triggered event-impact prediction as a point-in-time financial RAG problem. For each company-news anchor, the system retrieves related financial news and SEC filing passages, appends a pre-decision market-context card, and predicts multi-horizon residual-return signals. Our method keeps the large language model (LLM) reader frozen and adapts the retrieval layer through an external Bayesian source memory updated from matured residual-return feedback. On a fixed 89-stock Nasdaq-oriented universe derived from the FinRL-DeepSeek/FNSPID task, using original FNSPID news and point-in-time EDGAR filing passages, Frozen Reader with Source Memory improves held-out macro-F1 from 0.438 to 0.471 and downstream portfolio Sharpe from 0.52 to 0.84 relative to Frozen Reader with No Memory. A supervised LoRA reader improves static RAG modestly, but does not improve over the frozen source-memory reader. These results suggest that, for financial RAG, learning where to retrieve from can be as important as learning how to read, offering a simple, modular route to market-feedback adaptation.

</details>


### [170] [De-attribute to Forget for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30919)
*Xinyang Lu, Jiabao Pan, Rachael Hwee Ling Sim, See-Kiong Ng, Anthony Kum Hoe Tung, Bryan Kian Hsiang Low*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (45.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 DareU，首个基于数据归因奖励的 LLM 遗忘学习框架。它将遗忘目标重新定义为归零数据归因分数，通过强化学习减少模型生成响应与遗忘数据之间的归因得分，从而在保持模型效用的同时实现有效遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 遗忘方法多依赖优化预测损失（如最大化遗忘集损失），容易导致过度遗忘和模型效用下降。本文旨在提出一种新的遗忘范式，通过归零数据归因而非直接操作损失来平衡遗忘质量与模型效用。

Method: 提出 DareU 框架，将 LLM 遗忘重新定义为数据归因归零问题。使用强化学习更新 LLM，通过减少模型生成响应与遗忘数据所有者之间的归因分数（即去归因）来实现遗忘。采用 LLM 分类器作为归因的高效近似计算方法。

Result: 实验评估表明 DareU 在遗忘质量和模型效用之间取得了良好平衡，优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于数据归因奖励的遗忘框架 DareU 为 LLM 遗忘提供了一种新范式，有效解决了传统方法中过度遗忘和效用下降的问题，在遗忘效果和模型保持能力上均表现出色。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 LLM 遗忘学习（unlearning），属于生成模型安全与治理范畴。生成式 AI 方向下已有 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "概念擦除与遗忘学习"（unlearning_erasure），该论文提出的基于数据归因奖励的遗忘框架完全匹配该子主题定义。同时 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下也有同名子主题，但论文主要聚焦 LLM 而非多模态模型，故以 生成式 AI 为主方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM unlearning, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in generation and efficiency. Signals: LLM unlearning, data attribution, reinforcement learning, not in priority subtopics, no boost keywords matched

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) has raised concerns on the use of inappropriate data for training, which has led to a growing interest in LLM unlearning. Many existing LLM unlearning approaches rely on optimizing prediction loss(es), such as maximizing the loss on the forget set, but often face critical issues like over-forgetting and poor model utility. To address them, this paper novelly frames the optimization objective for LLM unlearning as one of zeroing out data attribution instead. In particular, we propose the first LLM unlearning framework based on data attribution rewards called DareU that performs reinforcement learning to update the LLM by reducing the attribution score of its generated responses (i.e., de-attributing) to the forget data owners. Empirical evaluation using an LLM classifier as an efficient approximation of attribution shows that DareU outperforms existing baselines by achieving effective unlearning while balancing forget quality and model utility well.

</details>


### [171] [BlueFin: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Financial Spreadsheets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30907)
*Srivatsa Kundurthy, Clara Na, Colton Moraine, Anoushka Mohta, Case Winter, George Fang, John Ling, Emma Strubell, Zach Kirshner*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.SE, cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (35.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: BlueFin 是一个面向金融电子表格场景的 LLM 智能体评测基准，包含 131 个复杂任务和 3,225 条细粒度评分标准，由专家人工验证，前沿 LLM 平均得分不足 50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子表格软件拥有数亿用户，远超专业开发者数量，但 LLM 在电子表格领域的能力探索不足，尤其缺乏反映真实金融职业任务的评测基准。

Method: 构建了包含 131 个金融电子表格任务的基准数据集，涵盖合成、操作和理解三类任务；设计了 3,225 条细粒度评分标准，由专家人工验证；使用 LM judge 自动评估，并与专家共识进行一致性验证。

Result: LM judge 与专家共识达到高度一致（α=0.826, macro-F1=0.839）；前沿 LLM 平均得分低于 50%，在动态正确性方面表现尤其薄弱。

Conclusion: BlueFin 揭示了当前 LLM 在金融电子表格任务上的显著不足，为未来研究提供了高质量评测基准和开源评估框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向金融电子表格的 LLM 智能体评测基准 BlueFin，属于 生成式 AI 评测与基准（evaluation_benchmark）范畴，同时也涉及多模态理解与生成方向中的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark），但核心更偏向 生成式 AI 领域的评测基准。

Importance reason: Focuses on financial spreadsheets, which is a negative keyword domain and far from your priority subtopics. Signals: financial domain, spreadsheet benchmark, LLM agents, low relevance to video/image generation or efficiency

Abstract: We present BlueFin, a benchmark that tasks large language model (LLM) agents with synthesis, manipulation, and comprehension tasks over spreadsheet workbooks in the professional finance domain. Though estimates of the global population of paying users of spreadsheet software range in the hundreds of millions -- an order of magnitude more than the estimated global population of professional developers -- comparatively fewer resources have been devoted to exploring and expanding LLM capabilities in the spreadsheet domain, with fewer still dedicated to mirroring real occupational tasks encountered by those in professional finance roles. In response, we curate a set of 131 challenging, complex tasks with real-world relevance in the domain, containing 3,225 granular rubric criteria; notably, our rubric criteria and LM judge evaluations are validated by a team of expert human annotators, resulting in high-quality, granular evaluations of complex tasks that are difficult to verify programmatically but can be reliably evaluated by an LM judge agent. Our judge achieves parity with expert consensus ($\alpha=0.826$) with a macro-F1 score of 0.839. Frontier LLMs demonstrate poor performance on the challenging benchmark, with the strongest LLMs achieving less than 50\% average scores across tasks -- models exhibit particular weaknesses in dynamic correctness. Our contributions include a dataset of examples across three categories of spreadsheet tasks, an open source harness and agentic evaluation framework, and a characterization of existing frontier models' performance on our benchmark.

</details>


### [172] [AdvScene: Rethinking Adversarial Patch Evaluation Through Scene Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30578)
*Xiaoyong, Yuan, Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (40.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 AdvScene，一个场景驱动的对抗性补丁评估框架，通过将补丁嵌入到重建的真实3D场景中，系统性地评估其在视角、距离和场景条件变化下的鲁棒性（称为"场景鲁棒性"），并引入 APSE（对抗性补丁到场景嵌入）方法解决跨视角一致性难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗性补丁评估方法存在不足：真实图像基准虽真实但固定不变，模拟器虽可控但缺乏真实场景锚定。对抗性补丁的真实风险取决于部署后在多变视角、距离和场景条件下是否仍然有效，而现有评估无法衡量这种"场景鲁棒性"。

Method: 提出 AdvScene 框架，将评估重新定义为操作测量：给定固定部署的补丁，刻画其"操作包络"（operational envelope）——攻击在何处、何时成功——作为视角、距离和场景上下文的函数。核心挑战是攻击通常仅在单个锚定视角定义，而评估需要跨视角保持一致的表示。作者将其形式化为约束提升问题，提出 APSE（Adversarial Patch-to-Scene Embedding），解决跨视角歧义，同时保留攻击关键外观并强制执行局部性、目标表面附着和跨视角一致性。

Result: 使用真实世界物理数据验证了 AdvScene，并对现有对抗性补丁进行了全面评估。结果表明，攻击有效性存在显著的场景依赖性变化，这是现有以图像为中心或基于模拟器的评估无法捕捉的。

Conclusion: AdvScene 揭示了对抗性补丁在真实场景中的鲁棒性评估需要场景驱动的框架，现有评估方法严重低估了场景条件对攻击效果的影响，为对抗性攻击的物理世界风险评估提供了新视角。

Classification reason: 该论文研究对抗性补丁（adversarial patches）在真实场景下的鲁棒性评估，属于AI安全与对抗攻击防御范畴，与生成式 AI方向下的"生成内容安全与治理"（safety_governance）主题高度相关，该主题涵盖有害内容检测与安全评测。

Importance reason: Focuses on adversarial patch evaluation in 3D scenes, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in generative models and efficient inference. Signals: adversarial patch evaluation, scene robustness, 3D scene reconstruction, cross-view consistency, not generative model

Abstract: Adversarial patches are physical patterns attached to real objects to mislead AI vision systems. Their real-world risk is not determined by a single successful prediction, but by whether they remain effective after deployment under changing viewpoints, distances, and scene conditions. We refer to this property as scene robustness, the effectiveness of a deployed patch across conditions in a real environment. Yet existing evaluations do not measure scene robustness well: real image benchmarks are realistic but fixed, while simulators are controllable but not grounded in a specific real scene. We present AdvScene, a scene-grounded framework for measuring the scene robustness of adversarial patches in reconstructed real environments. AdvScene reframes evaluation as operational measurement: given a fixed deployed patch, it characterizes the patch's operational envelope - where and when the attack succeeds - as a function of viewpoint, distance, and scene context. A key challenge is that the attack is typically defined only in a single anchor view, while evaluation requires a representation that remains faithful under viewpoint changes. We formalize this as a constrained lifting problem and introduce Adversarial Patch-to-Scene Embedding (APSE), which resolves cross-view ambiguity while preserving attack-critical appearance and enforcing locality, target-surface attachment, and cross-view consistency. We validate AdvScene using real-world physical data and conduct a comprehensive evaluation of existing adversarial patches. Our results show that AdvScene reveals substantial scene-dependent variation in attack effectiveness that is not captured by existing image-centric or simulator-based evaluations.

</details>


### [173] [Preference-Aware Rubric Learning for Personalized Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31545)
*Yilun Qiu, Xiaoyan Zhao, Yang Zhang, Yuxin Chen, Cilin Yan, Jiayin Cai, Xiaolong Jiang, Yao Hu, Yoko Yamakata, Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (45.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 PARL 框架，将个性化评估形式化为一个学习问题，通过从用户历史交互中归纳偏好感知评估准则（rubric），并结合判别式强化学习目标，实现对个性化文本生成的高保真评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法（自动指标、LLM-as-a-judge）无法捕捉长期交互历史中嵌入的主观、用户特定的个性化偏好，缺乏可靠的个性化对齐评估手段。

Method: 提出 Personalized Evaluation as Learning 范式，将个性化评估视为学习问题。PARL 框架包含两个核心模块：(1) 从原始用户历史中归纳偏好感知评估准则（rubric induction）；(2) 自验证机制，通过对比用户撰写的回复与竞争性个性化模型输出，结合判别式强化学习目标，确保准则与用户偏好一致。

Result: 在真实个性化文本生成任务上，PARL 能够一致地归纳出高保真评估准则，可靠地识别与用户对齐的回复，并在不同用户和任务间具有良好的泛化能力，同时能捕捉稳定的风格偏好和细粒度评估模式。

Conclusion: PARL 通过将个性化评估建模为学习问题，有效解决了现有方法无法捕捉用户特定偏好的局限，为个性化对齐评估提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究个性化对齐评估方法，提出从用户历史交互中学习评估准则（rubric）的框架 PARL，属于生成内容质量评测与人类偏好对齐评估范畴，与 生成式 AI 方向下的 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "生成式 AI 评测与基准" 高度匹配。同时涉及个性化对齐，也与多模态理解与生成方向相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on personalized evaluation of LLMs, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like video/image generation or world models. Signals: personalized evaluation, rubric learning, LLM-as-a-judge, user alignment, text generation evaluation

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) evolve from general-purpose assistants to user-centric agents, personalization has become central to aligning model behavior with individual preferences, making the evaluation of personalized alignment a critical bottleneck. Existing evaluation methods-ranging from automatic metrics to LLM-as-a-judge approaches-fail to capture subjective, user-specific preferences embedded in long-term interaction histories. We identify three essential principles for reliable and effective personalized evaluation: Representativeness, User-Consistency, and Discriminativeness. To address these principles, we introduce Personalized Evaluation as Learning, a paradigm that formulates personalized evaluation as a learning problem rather than a static judgment. Under this paradigm, we propose PARL (Preference-Aware Rubric Learning for Personalized Evaluation), a framework that learns to induce preference-aware evaluation rubrics directly from raw user histories and performs a self-validation mechanism to ensure consistency with the user's preferences. PARL integrates rubric induction with a discriminative reinforcement learning objective that contrasts user-authored responses against competitive personalized model outputs, enabling the learned rubrics to capture precise, user-specific decision boundaries. Experiments on real-world personalized text generation tasks show that PARL consistently induces high-fidelity rubrics that reliably identify user-aligned responses and generalize across users and tasks, while capturing stable stylistic preferences and fine-grained evaluative patterns. To ensure reproducibility, our code is available at .

</details>


### [174] [EvoDefense: Co-Evolving Black-Box Defense with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31140)
*Yu Li, Yuenan Hou, Yingmei Wei, Yanming Guo, Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.CR, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (45.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: EvoDefense 提出一种经验引导的共进化黑盒防御范式，利用守卫LLM检测恶意查询，并通过攻击-防御持续进化循环，使防御策略能泛化到未见过的攻击类型和目标模型，无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM黑盒防御方法依赖预定义的过滤启发式规则，难以泛化到未见过的攻击类型和目标模型架构，亟需一种能自适应进化的通用防御方案。

Method: EvoDefense 包含三个核心组件：(1) 守卫LLM，用于检测恶意查询；(2) 经验记忆模块，积累历史交互中的防御知识；(3) 攻击-防御共进化循环，攻击生成器与守卫模型通过经验引导的优化迭代改进攻击策略和防御策略，实现跨攻击类型和跨模型的泛化。

Result: 在HarmBench、AdvBench和AlpacaEval上，EvoDefense在7个流行模型和5种代表性LLM攻击下均取得一致的强防御性能。在HarmBench上，将AutoDAN-turbo对Gemini-3-flash和LLaMA-3-8B-Instruct的攻击成功率分别从29.4%和43.4%降至8.4%和6.2%。

Conclusion: EvoDefense通过共进化范式有效解决了黑盒防御的泛化难题，在保持模型通用能力的同时显著降低攻击成功率，为LLM安全防御提供了新的自适应框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出针对LLM黑盒攻击的防御方法，属于生成内容安全与治理范畴，与生成式 AI方向下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成内容安全与治理"（safety_governance）高度匹配。同时涉及多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态安全与攻防（multimodal_safety_attack），但核心贡献更偏向生成式 AI安全防御。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM security defense, not aligned with user's priority on generative models and efficiency. Signals: Black-box defense, LLM security, co-evolution, guard LLM, no retraining needed

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) remain highly vulnerable to diverse attacks, particularly in black-box settings where the internals of target models are inaccessible. Existing black-box defenses typically rely on pre-defined filtering heuristics, which often fail to generalize to unseen attack types and target model architectures. We introduce EvoDefense, an experience-guided co-evolving black-box defense paradigm. EvoDefense employs a guard LLM to detect malicious queries and an experience memory module to accumulate defense knowledge from previous interactions. At the core of EvoDefense is a continuous attack-defense evolution loop, where an attack generator and the guard model iteratively refine their attack strategies and defense policies through experience-guided optimization. This design enables EvoDefense to generalize across unseen attacks and target models without retraining. Experiments on HarmBench, AdvBench, and AlpacaEval show that EvoDefense achieves consistently strong defense performance across seven popular models and five representative LLM attacks, while preserving competitive general capabilities. On HarmBench, EvoDefense reduces the attack success rate (ASR) of AutoDAN-turbo on Gemini-3-flash and LLaMA-3-8B-Instruct from 29.4% and 43.4% to 8.4% and 6.2%, respectively.

</details>


### [175] [DeMaVLA: A Vision-Language-Action Foundation Model for Generalizable Deformable Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31286)
*Taiyi Su, Jian Zhu, Tianjian Wang, Youzhang He, Zitai Huang, Jianjun Zhang, Chong Ma, Hanyang Wang, Tianjiao Zhang, Munan Yin, Weihao Ding, Yi Xu*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DeMaVLA 是一个面向可变形物体操作的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）基础模型，采用 VLM 骨干网络与流匹配动作生成，通过剪枝 Transformer 层提高效率，在约5000小时真实双臂演示上预训练，并通过 DAgger 管道进行后训练，实现了跨类别、跨场景的衣物折叠操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 系统通常为不同物体类别训练独立的策略，而简单混合多任务训练会导致任务干扰和性能下降。需要一种能够跨不同类别、几何形状、材料和场景泛化的通用可变形物体操作策略。

Method: DeMaVLA 采用 VLM 骨干网络（如 SigLIP+LLaMA）结合动作专家模块，使用流匹配（flow matching）生成连续动作。动作专家通过每隔一层剪枝 Transformer 层构建，同时保持与 VLM 骨干的逐层对齐，降低训练和推理成本。先在约5000小时真实双臂演示数据上预训练获得通用操作先验，再通过人类参与的 DAgger 数据聚合管道，在混合折叠数据上进行后训练。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 基准上取得有竞争力的性能，在自建的家庭折叠基准上展示了强大的真实世界结果，验证了可扩展真实数据、高效动作生成和纠正学习对通用 VLA 策略的价值。

Conclusion: DeMaVLA 证明了通过大规模真实世界数据预训练、高效动作生成架构和人类参与的纠正学习，可以构建跨类别、跨场景泛化的可变形物体操作 VLA 基础模型，为通用家庭机器人操作提供了有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究机器人操作领域的视觉-语言-动作基础模型，属于垂直领域（机器人操作）的应用，与现有 subtopic "领域内容生成应用"（domain_application）匹配。该 subtopic 在 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下已有记录。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotic manipulation, not aligned with user's priority subtopics in generative AI and video/image generation. Signals: VLA foundation model, deformable manipulation, flow matching action generation, DAgger pipeline, not generative AI core

Abstract: Real-world household robots require Vision-Language-Action (VLA) foundation models that can acquire reusable manipulation skills across diverse objects, task conditions, and household environments. Deformable-object folding is a representative challenge, requiring robots to handle clothing items from random initial states across varying categories, geometries, materials, and scenes. However, existing VLA systems commonly train separate policies for different object categories, while naively mixed multi-task training often suffers from task interference and degraded performance. To move beyond category-specific folding policies, we introduce DeMaVLA, a VLA foundation model for generalizable Deformable Manipulation. DeMaVLA adopts a VLM backbone with an action expert and formulates continuous action generation using flow matching. To improve efficiency, the action expert is constructed by pruning every other transformer layer while preserving layer-wise alignment with the VLM backbone, reducing training and inference cost. DeMaVLA is first pre-trained on approximately 5,000 hours of selected real-world dual-arm demonstrations to acquire general manipulation priors. It is then post-trained on mixed folding data that aggregates self-collected demonstrations and corrective trajectories from real-robot failures across multiple folding tasks through a human-in-the-loop Data Aggregation~(DAgger) pipeline. Experiments show that DeMaVLA achieves competitive performance on RoboTwin and strong real-world results on our household folding benchmark. These results highlight the value of scalable real-world data, efficient action generation, and corrective learning for general-purpose VLA policies in deformable-object manipulation.

</details>


### [176] [Send a SCOUT First: Pre-hoc Reasoning for Adaptive Detector Allocation in Prompt-Injection Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30837)
*Shuhao Zhang, Jiarui Li, Qi Cao, Ruiyi Zhang, Pengtao Xie*

Main category: cs.CR, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (46.59) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出SCOUT框架，将提示注入防御重新定义为检测器分配问题：根据每个样本预测不同检测器的可靠性和延迟，动态决定使用哪些检测器以及是否升级到LLM裁判，从而在安全性和实用性之间取得更好的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的提示注入检测器各有优劣，没有一个检测器在所有攻击类型上都可靠，但现有系统仍采用固定单检测器流水线，导致每个请求都会遇到检测器的盲区。作者希望将防御问题重新定义为检测器分配问题，实现动态、自适应的防御策略。

Method: 提出SCOUT框架，核心思想是：给定一个异构检测器池，通过预测每个检测器在给定样本上的可靠性和延迟（基于该样本与历史输入的相似性），动态决定每个请求使用哪些检测器以及是否升级到LLM裁判。框架暴露一个安全-效用阈值供操作员调节。同时构建了SCOUT-450基准数据集，包含结构复杂的面向智能体的提示注入攻击样本。

Result: 在SCOUT-450上，安全导向的操作点相比始终使用GPT-4o裁判，攻击成功率降低46%，总耗时降低40%，良性效用仅下降5.1个百分点。在BIPIA、IPI和IHEval三个外部基准上也实现了安全-效用前沿的改进。

Conclusion: 将提示注入防御重新定义为检测器分配问题是有效的，SCOUT框架通过预测驱动的动态分配策略，能够在提升安全性的同时显著降低延迟，为实际部署提供了灵活的安全-效用权衡机制。

Classification reason: 该论文研究提示注入攻击的防御方法，属于生成式 AI安全治理范畴。论文提出的SCOUT框架通过动态分配检测器来防御提示注入攻击，与safety_governance（生成内容安全与治理）中的内容审核、有害内容检测高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on prompt-injection defense, which is not a priority subtopic and has negative keyword overlap. Signals: Prompt-injection defense, Detector allocation, Safety-utility trade-off, LLM judge escalation, Benchmark SCOUT-450

Abstract: Prompt-injection detectors are heterogeneous: each is strong on a different slice of attacks, and none is always reliable. Yet existing systems still treat detection as a fixed single-detector pipeline, committing every request to one detector's blind spots. We reframe defense as detector allocation: given a heterogeneous pool, decide per request which detectors to run and whether to escalate to an LLM judge. Our framework SCOUT (Scalable and Controllable Outcome-prediction for Uncertainty-aware Triage) makes this decision dynamic by predicting each detector's per-sample reliability and latency from how it behaved on similar past inputs, and exposes a single safety-utility threshold to the operator (where utility bundles benign-pass rate and wall-clock). To evaluate this setting, we build SCOUT-450, a benchmark that captures the structurally complex, agent-facing injections that older prompt-injection sets under-represent. On SCOUT-450, a safety-oriented operating point reduces attack-success rate by 46% and total wall-clock by 40% relative to an always-on GPT-4o judge, at a 5.1-point benign-utility drop. SCOUT also transfers to three external benchmarks (BIPIA, IPI, and IHEval), improving the safety-utility frontier.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-generation_alignment'></div>

## 生成模型对齐与奖励 [[Back]](#toc)

生成模型对齐、RLHF、奖励建模、偏好优化、guidance 与可控性。

### [177] [Federated Variational Preference Alignment with Gumbel-Softmax Prior for Personalized User Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30873)
*Jabin Koo, Hoyoung Kim, Minwoo Jang, Jungseul Ok*

Main category: cs.DC, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 普通相关 (41.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 FedVPA-GP，一种联邦变分偏好对齐框架，通过 Gumbel-Softmax 先验和正交损失在保护隐私的前提下解耦个性化用户偏好，解决数据稀疏导致的 posterior collapse 问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦对齐框架使用单一奖励模型会平均化用户间冲突的偏好（如 helpfulness vs. harmlessness），而变分偏好学习在去中心化场景下因本地数据稀疏和异质性导致后验坍塌，亟需一种既能保护隐私又能解耦多样化偏好的方法。

Method: 提出 FedVPA-GP 框架：1) 引入联邦混合先验（Federated Mixture Prior），让客户端利用聚合的全局分布作为动态先验以稳定变分推断；2) 设计正交损失（Orthogonal Loss）在隐空间中显式分离偏好原型；3) 使用 Gumbel-Softmax 先验实现离散偏好建模。

Result: 在 HH-RLHF 数据集上的实验表明，FedVPA-GP 显著优于单一奖励模型的基线方法，成功解耦了冲突的用户意图，并支持动态偏好切换。

Conclusion: FedVPA-GP 为联邦场景下的个性化偏好对齐提供了有效方案，在保护隐私的同时实现了多样化偏好的解耦与动态调整。

Classification reason: 该论文研究联邦学习环境下的大语言模型偏好对齐与个性化，核心是偏好优化与奖励建模，与现有 canonical subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）高度匹配。同时涉及多模态理解与生成方向中的对齐问题。

Importance reason: Focuses on federated preference alignment for LLMs, which is outside the user's core interests in generative models and video/image generation. Signals: Federated learning, preference alignment, posterior collapse, Gumbel-Softmax, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving pathway for aligning Large Language Models (LLMs); however, existing frameworks typically enforce a monolithic reward model, inevitably averaging out inherently conflicting user preferences (e.g., helpfulness vs. harmlessness). While Variational Preference Learning (VPL) offers a pathway to personalization, adapting it to decentralized settings presents a fundamental challenge: posterior collapse driven by severe local data scarcity and heterogeneity. In this paper, we propose Federated Variational Preference Alignment with Gumbel-Softmax Prior (FedVPA-GP), a framework designed to disentangle diverse preferences without compromising privacy. To stabilize variational inference, we introduce a Federated Mixture Prior that enables clients to leverage the aggregate population distribution as a dynamic prior. Furthermore, we incorporate an Orthogonal Loss that explicitly enforces the separation of preference prototypes in the latent space. Experiments on the HH-RLHF dataset demonstrate that FedVPA-GP significantly outperforms monolithic baselines, successfully disentangling conflicting user intents and enabling dynamic preference switching.

</details>

### [178] [VLM3: Vision Language Models Are Native 3D Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30561)
*Zhipeng Cai, Zhuang Liu, Yunyang Xiong, Zechun Liu, Vikas Chandra, Yangyang Shi*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (60.41) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出VLM3，通过焦距统一、文本像素引用和数据混合缩放三种简单策略，证明标准VLM无需架构修改、复杂损失或大规模模型即可成为原生3D学习者，在深度估计、像素对应、相机位姿估计和物体级3D理解等任务上达到专家模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前3D理解任务仍依赖专门的专家视觉模型和复杂的任务特定设计，而VLM虽然在语义理解上表现出色，但在3D任务上能力有限。作者希望证明VLM本身就是原生的3D学习者，只需简单设计即可掌握多种3D任务，无需复杂的架构修改或回归损失。

Method: 提出VLM3方法，核心包含三个关键设计：(1) 焦距统一（Focal Length Unification）——将不同焦距的图像统一到标准焦距，消除透视畸变差异；(2) 文本像素引用（Text-based Pixel Reference）——使用文本坐标（如"<x1><y1>"）替代特殊视觉token来引用像素位置；(3) 数据混合与缩放（Data Mixture and Scaling）——混合多种3D任务数据进行训练并扩大数据规模。无需改变VLM架构、无需大模型、无需复杂数据增强或回归损失。

Result: VLM3在深度估计任务上将准确率从0.84提升至0.9（大幅提升）；在像素对应、相机位姿估计和物体级3D理解等任务上达到与专家视觉模型相当的精度，同时保持标准VLM架构和基于文本的训练范式。

Conclusion: 本文证明VLM是原生的3D学习者，通过焦距统一、文本像素引用和数据混合缩放三种简单策略即可实现强大的3D理解能力，为简单可扩展的3D学习开辟了新范式，挑战了"3D任务需要复杂专家模型"的传统认知。

Classification reason: 该论文研究如何让标准VLM模型掌握深度估计、像素对应、相机位姿估计、物体级3D理解等多种3D任务，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的"空间与3D多模态推理"（spatial_3d_reasoning）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。同时，论文也涉及3D生成相关能力，因此也匹配多模态生成方向。

Importance reason: Shows standard VLMs can natively handle 3D tasks without complex modifications, aligning with unified multimodal and efficient inference priorities. Signals: Unified multimodal 3D learning, Simple scaling strategy, Matches expert models, No architecture change, Focal length unification

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) enable a unified model to solve various vision tasks through prompting. They have shown promising performance in semantic understanding. However, 3D understanding still largely relies on expert vision models with complex task-specific designs. The key argument this work wants to make is that VLMs are native 3D learners. Our in-depth large scale study shows that 1) focal length unification, 2) text-based pixel reference and 3) data mixture and scaling, are all you need for effective 3D learning. Model architecture changes, large models, heavy data augmentations, and complex losses including the regression formulation, many of which form the foundation of expert vision models, are actually not necessary conditions. As a result, we propose VLM3, a scalable method with the simplest design that enables standard VLMs to master diverse 3D tasks. VLM3 not only advances the VLM depth estimation accuracy by a large margin (0.84 -> 0.9), but also enables diverse 3D tasks such as pixel correspondence, camera pose estimation and object-level 3D understanding, matching expert vision model accuracy while maintaining standard architectures and text-based training. We believe VLM3 opens up a new paradigm for simple and scalable 3D learning.

</details>


### [179] [SpatialAct: Probing Spatial Reasoning-to-Action Capabilities of VLM Agents in 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31148)
*Tianhui Liu, Jie Feng, Zhiheng Zheng, Shengyuan Wang, Yiming Guo, Yanxin Xi, Hangyu Fan, Yong Li, Pan Hui*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 SpatialAct，一个基于模拟器的基准测试，用于探测 VLM 智能体在 3D 场景中的动作条件空间推理能力。实验发现当前 VLM 在孤立空间推理任务上表现良好，但在多轮交互反馈中难以维持连贯的空间信念并产生可靠动作，存在明显的推理到动作的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够轻松感知空间布局、形成认知表征、推理空间关系并将推理转化为行动。虽然现有 VLM 在观察条件空间感知和推理任务上表现不错，但它们能否建立连贯的空间理解、据此行动并通过多轮反馈优化动作仍不清楚。本文旨在系统性地探测这一问题。

Method: 提出 SpatialAct 基准，包含最困难的多轮交互精炼任务、单步错误检测与修复任务，以及五个基础空间能力任务（用于诊断模型失败的根本原因）。通过模拟器环境对 VLM 智能体进行系统评测。

Result: 当前 VLM 在孤立空间推理任务上表现良好，但在多轮反馈中难以维持连贯的空间信念并产生可靠动作，显著低于人类表现。存在明显的推理到动作鸿沟。

Conclusion: 当前 VLM 智能体在动作引起的环境变化下仍缺乏鲁棒的空间状态追踪能力，即使抽象掉了底层控制也是如此。这表明需要进一步研究如何弥合空间推理与动作执行之间的差距。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 VLM 智能体在 3D 场景中的空间推理到动作的能力，属于空间推理与 3D 多模态推理范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "空间与 3D 多模态推理" (spatial_3d_reasoning) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Probes VLM spatial reasoning-to-action gap but is not a core generative model paper. Signals: VLM spatial reasoning, 3D scene understanding, reasoning-to-action gap, benchmark, not generative model

Abstract: Humans can effortlessly perceive spatial layouts, form cognitive representations, reason about spatial relations, and translate such reasoning into actions in everyday 3D environments. Although recent vision-language models (VLMs) have shown promising performance on observation-conditioned spatial perception and reasoning tasks, it remains unclear whether they can build coherent spatial understanding, act upon it, and refine their actions through multi-turn feedback. To study this problem, we introduce \textbf{SpatialAct}, a simulator-grounded benchmark for probing \textit{action-conditioned spatial reasoning} in 3D scenes. Starting from the most challenging setting, Multi-turn Interactive Refinement, we further design its decomposed counterpart, Single-step Error Detection and Fix, together with five fundamental spatial ability tasks to diagnose the underlying causes of model failures. Experiments reveal a clear reasoning-to-action gap: current VLMs can perform well on isolated spatial reasoning tasks, but struggle to maintain coherent spatial beliefs and produce reliable actions during multi-turn feedback, substantially underperforming humans. These results suggest that current VLM agents still lack robust spatial state tracking under action-induced environment changes, even when low-level control is abstracted away.

</details>


### [180] [Multi-Turn Multi-Agent Dialogue for Collaborative Reconstruction Improves VLM Performance on Spatial Reasoning, But Only Barely](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31387)
*Chalamalasetti Kranti, Sherzod Hakimov, David Schlangen*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.RO

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (48.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉语言模型（VLM）在需要空间推理的协作对话任务中的表现。通过一个协作结构重建任务，作者构建了多轮多智能体对话框架，评估多种VLM在不同输入模态和图像表示下的空间推理能力。结果表明，VLM在视觉空间推理上仍存在显著困难，详细的文本描述比视觉输入更有效，分解的图像表示能带来有限提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在多样化环境中依赖视觉输入理解物体和空间布局，并在人机协作任务中通过语言沟通。现有VLM在视觉问答和指令跟随方面表现良好，但在需要空间推理的协作对话任务中的能力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 设计了一个协作结构重建任务，VLM通过多轮对话从视觉和文本输入中重建目标结构。评估了多种开源和闭源VLM，比较了不同交互设置、输入模态（纯文本、纯视觉、文本+视觉）和图像表示（整体图像 vs. 分解图像）对重建成功率的影响。

Result: VLM在视觉空间推理上表现困难；详细的文本描述比视觉输入带来更高的重建成功率；分解的图像表示相比整体图像能提升性能，但提升幅度有限。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在视觉空间定位和基于语言引导的指令生成方面存在明显局限，尚不足以胜任需要精细空间推理的协作对话任务。

Classification reason: 论文研究VLM在协作对话任务中的空间推理能力，通过结构重建任务评估视觉空间理解与语言引导交互，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的"空间与3D多模态推理"（spatial_3d_reasoning）规范子主题。

Importance reason: Spatial reasoning in VLMs is relevant but the paper's focus on dialogue and marginal gains is not a top priority. Signals: spatial reasoning, VLM evaluation, collaborative dialogue, limited improvement, not autoregressive or diffusion

Abstract: Robots operating in diverse environments rely on visual input to interpret objects and spatial layouts. In human-collaborative tasks, they are expected to communicate this understanding through language. Vision-language models (VLMs) support robotic tasks involving visual interpretation, question answering, and instruction following, but their capabilities in collaborative dialogue tasks requiring spatial reasoning remain underexplored. We study this gap through a collaborative structure-building task that combines visual interpretation, grounding, language-guided interaction, and action generation. We develop a framework in which VLMs use dialogue to reconstruct a target structure from visual and textual inputs. We evaluate open-weight and closed VLMs across interaction settings, input modalities, and image representations. Results show that spatial reasoning over visual representations remains difficult for the evaluated VLMs. Detailed text representations of the target yield higher reconstruction success across modality conditions, while decomposed image representations improve performance. These findings reveal limits in visual spatial grounding and grounded instruction generation for collaborative VLM agents.

</details>


### [181] [VLM-GLoc: Vision-Language Model Enhanced Monte Carlo Localization for Robust Semantic Global Localization in Cluttered Quasi-Static Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30506)
*Shivendra Agrawal, Bradley Hayes*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (51.14) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VLM-GLoc 提出一种结合视觉语言模型（VLM）的层次化语义蒙特卡洛定位方法，利用 VLM 提取判别性文本特征、隐式质量过滤和永久性推理，在杂乱的准静态环境（如杂货店和实验室）中实现鲁棒的全局定位，显著优于传统几何方法和领域专用视觉基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在几何混淆（如平行货架）且语义模糊的准静态环境（杂货店、办公室、医院等）中，移动机器人的全局定位极具挑战。传统方法依赖几何特征或领域专用视觉流水线，难以处理长尾语义分布和瞬态视觉杂乱，亟需一种更鲁棒、开放词汇的语义定位方法。

Method: 提出 VLM-GLoc，一种层次化语义蒙特卡洛定位（MCL）方法。核心创新包括：(1) 利用开放词汇 VLM 作为统一语义观测前端，提取高判别性文本特征；(2) 隐式质量过滤，自动排除模糊或动态物体；(3) 永久性推理，用于目标数据增强；(4) 引入逆语义提议机制，通过文本到地图检索播种粒子。

Result: 在两个真实环境（3,500 平方英尺杂货店 + 手机平台；3,700 平方英尺实验室 + 四足机器人）上评估，VLM-GLoc 分别达到 70% 和 74% 的全局定位成功率，大幅超越传统纯几何方法和领域专用基线。

Conclusion: VLM-GLoc 证明了 VLM 在机器人全局定位中的有效性，通过语义特征提取、质量过滤和永久性推理三大优势，在杂乱准静态环境中实现了鲁棒的语义全局定位，为移动机器人在复杂室内环境中的自主导航提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文利用视觉语言模型（VLM）增强移动机器人的全局定位，属于具身推理与空间定位范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "空间与 3D 多模态推理"（spatial_3d_reasoning）高度匹配。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on robotics localization, which is not a priority subtopic, and lacks strong alignment with the user's configured direction weights and boost keywords. Signals: VLM for semantic localization, hierarchical MCL, quasi-static environments, outperforms geometry baselines, not aligned with user's priority subtopics

Abstract: Global localization in geometrically aliased, quasi-static environments such as grocery stores, offices, schools, and hospitals poses a significant challenge for mobile robots. Grocery stores with parallel aisles and a long tailed distribution of products, as well as offices and labs with repetitive furniture such as chairs, desks, monitors, and doors, exemplify common indoor environments that present geometric and even semantic ambiguity. Traditional approaches rely either on distinct geometric features or on domain-specific vision pipelines that struggle with long-tail semantic distributions and transient visual clutter. We present VLM-GLoc, a method for hierarchical semantic Monte Carlo Localization (MCL) that leverages open-vocabulary Vision-Language Models (VLMs) as a unified semantic observation front-end. We hypothesize a three-fold benefit from VLMs: (1) extracting highly discriminative rich text features, (2) implicit quality filtering of blurry or dynamic objects, and (3) permanence reasoning for targeted data augmentation. We introduce an inverse semantic proposal mechanism that seeds particles via text-to-map retrieval. Evaluated across two real-world environments with different characteristics and two different platforms: a 3,500 sq. ft. grocery store with a cellphone and a 3,700 sq. ft. lab space with a quadruped, VLM-GLoc achieves 70% and 74% global localization success respectively, substantially outperforming traditional geometry-only and domain-specific baselines.

</details>

## 统一多模态模型 [[Back]](#toc)
