### [182] [Representation Forcing for Bottleneck-Free Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31604)
*Yuqing Wang, Zhijie Lin, Ceyuan Yang, Yang Zhao, Fei Xiao, Hao He, Qi Zhao, Zihan Ding, Fuyun Wang, Shuai Wang, Youliang Zhang, Haoqi Fan, Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (95.71) - Top 3 精读候选

TL;DR: 本文提出 Representation Forcing (RF) 技术，使统一多模态模型能够在像素空间中直接进行图像生成，无需依赖外部预训练 VAE 作为生成瓶颈。RF 强制解码器自回归预测视觉表示作为中间 token，这些 token 保留在上下文中引导后续像素扩散，从而在单一骨干网络中同时实现理解与生成，且性能达到甚至超越基于 VAE 的现有统一模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型（UMM）虽然旨在用单一模型处理感知与生成，但普遍依赖一个冻结的、单独预训练的 VAE 作为图像生成的潜在空间，这引入了结构瓶颈。简单移除 VAE 会导致生成质量下降，因为模型需要同时从原始像素中学习高层结构和低层细节。本文旨在消除这一瓶颈，实现真正的端到端无瓶颈统一多模态模型。

Method: 提出 Representation Forcing (RF) 技术：在解码阶段，强制模型自回归地预测视觉表示（如 DINOv2 特征）作为中间 token，这些 token 保留在上下文中，然后通过同一骨干网络中的扩散过程生成像素。RF 将表示从感知输出转化为生成目标，使模型无需外部生成潜在空间即可在像素空间中完成高质量生成。

Result: 在图像生成任务上，基于像素空间的 RF 模型达到了与最先进的基于 VAE 的统一模型相当的性能。在图像理解任务上，基于像素空间的 RF 模型普遍优于其基于 VAE 的变体。实验表明 RF 同时提升了理解与生成能力。

Conclusion: Representation Forcing 为构建无瓶颈的端到端统一多模态模型提供了一条有效路径，通过将表示预测作为模型原生能力，消除了对外部 VAE 的依赖，并在理解和生成两方面均取得优异表现。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Representation Forcing (RF) 技术，旨在构建无瓶颈的统一多模态模型（UMM），同时处理感知与生成任务。这与 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下的 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "统一多模态模型"（unified_multimodal_model）高度匹配，该子主题专门涵盖理解与生成一体化模型。同时该论文也涉及图像生成，因此也匹配 multimodal_generation 方向。

Importance reason: Directly advances unified multimodal models by removing VAE bottleneck, aligning with top priority subtopic. Signals: unified multimodal model, bottleneck-free, representation forcing, pixel-space generation, autoregressive prediction

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) aim to handle perception and generation in a single model. Yet existing UMMs still rely on a frozen, separately pretrained VAE for image generation, imposing a structural bottleneck. Naively removing it introduces a quality gap, as the model must learn both high-level structure and low-level details from raw pixels. In this paper, we propose Representation Forcing (RF), a technique that closes this gap by making representation prediction a native capability of the model. Concretely, RF forces the decoder to autoregressively predict visual representations as intermediate tokens before pixels; these tokens then stay in context to guide pixel diffusion within the same backbone. By turning representations from perception outputs into generation targets, RF eliminates the need for any external generative latent space. We find that RF benefits both understanding and generation. On image generation, our pixel-space model with RF matches state-of-the-art VAE-based unified models. On image understanding, pixel-space RF generally outperforms its VAE-based variant. Together, these results offer an effective step toward end-to-end, bottleneck-free UMMs.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-vlm_mllm_general'></div>

## 视觉语言模型与 MLLM [[Back]](#toc)

通用视觉语言模型、MLLM 架构、多模态指令微调与对话。

### [183] [FOCUS: Forcing In-Context Object Localization through Visual Support Constraints and Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31145)
*Mohammed Asad Karim, Vinay Kumar Verma*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (75.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出FOCUS框架，通过两阶段训练（上下文注意力优化 + GRPO强化学习）实现无需类别监督的上下文目标定位，使7B模型超越72B模型，证明了上下文感知定位目标可以超越单纯规模扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在上下文定位（ICL）任务中依赖显式类别监督，导致类别偏差（模型依赖语义先验而非视觉证据），且无法处理未命名或实例级物体。亟需一种无需类别监督、基于视觉对应关系的上下文定位方法。

Method: 提出两阶段训练框架：第一阶段通过上下文注意力优化（forcing in-context attention between support boxes and query images）建立视觉对应关系；第二阶段使用GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）强化学习直接最小化定位误差，强制模型依赖视觉证据而非语义先验。

Result: 7B参数模型在多个ICL基准上超越72B参数模型；消融实验验证了各组件贡献；模型在未见过的类别和实例级定位上表现鲁棒。

Conclusion: 上下文感知的定位目标可以超越模型规模扩展，通过显式优化视觉对应关系而非语义先验，能够实现更鲁棒的类别无关实例级定位。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种无需类别监督的上下文定位（ICL）训练框架，通过强化学习优化VLM的视觉定位能力，属于视觉语言模型（VLM）的架构与训练方法改进，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "vlm_mllm_general"（视觉语言模型与MLLM）。

Importance reason: Strong MLLM in-context localization work with RL optimization, but not directly in top priority subtopics. Signals: MLLM, in-context localization, GRPO reinforcement learning, category-agnostic, 7B outperforms 72B

Abstract: In-context localization (ICL) seeks to localize a target object specified by a small set of support examples in a query image, operating on the fly without training or parameter updates. Despite rapid advances in vision-language models (VLMs), achieving category-agnostic and visually grounded ICL remains an open problem, even though it is essential for applications such as image editing, personalized visual search, and retrieval. Existing methods are fragile and rely on explicit category supervision, which not only limits applicability in realistic settings with unnamed or instance-specific objects but also introduces category bias that steers predictions toward semantic priors rather than visual evidence. We introduce a two-stage training framework that explicitly optimizes in-context attention between support bounding boxes and query images without category supervision. We further refine localization via reinforcement learning using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to directly minimize localization error. This formulation enforces visual correspondence over semantic priors, yielding robust instance-level localization. Empirically, a 7B-parameter model trained with our objectives outperforms models up to 72B parameters, demonstrating that context-aware localization objectives can surpass scaling alone. Comprehensive ablations validate the contribution of each component.

</details>


### [184] [DriveMA: Driving Vision-Language-Action Models with verifiable Meta-Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31271)
*Weicheng Zheng, Yixin Huang, Qiao Sun, Derun Li, Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (83.9) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DriveMA 提出了一种基于可验证元动作（meta-actions）的驾驶视觉-语言-动作模型框架，通过将未来自车运动总结为紧凑的语言域意图，并利用轨迹标注管道和基于规则的投影实现可验证性，结合动作中心监督训练和回合级信用分配强化学习，在 Waymo Open Dataset 上取得了 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶 VLA 模型旨在利用语言改进端到端规划，但语言与动作之间存在语义鸿沟（language-action gap），导致高层语言决策难以有效指导底层轨迹规划。现有方法缺乏对语言意图的可验证性，无法确保语言输出与实际行动的一致性。

Method: 提出元动作（meta-actions）概念，将未来自车运动总结为紧凑的语言域意图（如"左转"、"加速"等），并通过轨迹标注管道从专家轨迹中自动构建。元动作具有可验证性：通过基于规则的投影可将生成的轨迹反向映射为元动作，从而验证语言与动作的一致性。框架包含两个训练阶段：(1) 动作中心监督训练，让模型学习从观测到元动作和轨迹的映射；(2) 回合级信用分配强化学习，利用元动作的可验证性设计密集奖励，实现高层决策与底层轨迹规划的显式对齐。

Result: 在 Waymo Open Dataset 基于视觉的端到端驾驶任务上，2B 模型达到 8.060 Rater Feedback Score，4B 模型进一步提升至 8.079，均达到 SOTA。在 NAVSIM 上获得有竞争力的闭环规划性能。

Conclusion: 即使简单的元动作接口，只要具备可验证性并针对语言-动作对齐进行优化，就能实现 SOTA 的驾驶规划性能。这表明可验证的元动作是弥合驾驶 VLA 中语言-动作鸿沟的有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DriveMA，一种驾驶视觉-语言-动作模型（Driving VLA），属于视觉语言模型在自动驾驶领域的应用，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 高度相关。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with unified multimodal and MLLM priorities, with verifiable meta-actions bridging language and action for driving. Signals: multimodal_understanding_generation, unified multimodal, mllm, controllable generation, efficient inference

Abstract: Driving Vision-Language-Action Models (Driving VLAs) aim to use language to improve end-to-end planning, but the language-action gap limits this promise. We propose DriveMA, a Driving VLA framework built on verifiable meta-actions, which summarize future ego motion into compact language-domain intentions and can be constructed from expert trajectories with a trajectory-grounded annotation pipeline and can be verified against generated trajectories through rule-based projection. DriveMA exploits this verifiability with action-centric supervised training and a data-efficient turn-level credit assignment reinforcement learning framework, explicitly aligning high-level decisions with low-level trajectory planning through dense rewards and precise credit assignment. DriveMA sets a new state of the art on the Waymo Open Dataset Vision-based E2E Driving, achieving a Rater Feedback Score of 8.060 with a 2B model and further improving it to 8.079 with a 4B model; it also obtains competitive closed-loop planning performance on NAVSIM. These results show that even a simple meta-action interface can achieve state-of-the-art planning when made verifiable and optimized for language-action alignment. Code, data, and models will be released to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [185] [How can embedding models bind concepts?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31503)
*Arnas Uselis, Darina Koishigarina, Seong Joon Oh*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (75.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究 CLIP 等视觉-语言嵌入模型中的概念绑定（binding）问题——即模型能识别单个概念（如颜色和形状），但无法正确绑定哪些颜色属于哪个物体。作者发现 CLIP 的场景嵌入可加性分解为物体表示，但绑定函数复杂度高，导致图像和文本编码器无法学习共享的绑定机制。通过可控 Transformer 实验证明，在足够数据覆盖下，模型可以学习低复杂度的乘法交互绑定函数，实现系统性泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类可以轻松判断多物体场景中哪个颜色属于哪个形状（概念绑定），但 CLIP 等视觉-语言嵌入模型在此任务上表现不佳。理解 CLIP 为何无法实现绑定泛化，以及这种限制是否是根本性的，对于改进多模态表示学习具有重要意义。

Method: 首先通过理论分析证明 CLIP 的场景嵌入可加性分解为物体表示，解释了为何单模态探针能恢复物体信息。然后通过分析绑定函数的复杂度，指出 CLIP 的高复杂度绑定函数阻碍了跨模态共享绑定机制的学习。最后在可控 Transformer 模型上从零训练，验证了在足够数据覆盖下，模型可以学习低复杂度的乘法交互绑定函数，实现系统性泛化。

Result: CLIP 的场景嵌入可加性分解为物体表示；CLIP 的绑定函数复杂度高，导致无法泛化到未见过的概念组合；在可控 Transformer 中，足够的数据覆盖可以学习低复杂度的乘法交互绑定函数，实现系统性泛化。

Conclusion: CLIP 的概念绑定限制并非根本性缺陷，通过足够的数据覆盖和合适的架构设计，模型可以学习具有系统性泛化能力的绑定机制。这为改进多模态嵌入模型的组合泛化能力提供了方向。

Classification reason: 论文研究 CLIP 等视觉-语言嵌入模型中的概念绑定（binding）机制，分析其为何无法泛化到未见过的概念组合，属于视觉语言模型（VLM）的基础能力分析，因此归入 [CANONICAL] 的 vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）。

Importance reason: Provides mechanistic insight into CLIP's binding failure and shows transformers can learn multiplicative binding, relevant to unified multimodal models. Signals: concept binding, CLIP analysis, systematic generalization, multimodal understanding, transformer binding function

Abstract: Humans easily determine which color belongs to which shape in multi-object scenes, an ability known as concept binding. Vision-language embedding models such as CLIP struggle with binding: they recognize individual concepts but fail to represent which concepts form which objects. Although CLIP behaves like a bag-of-concepts model in cross-modal retrieval, object information is recoverable from its image and text embeddings separately. We study this tension through the binding function, which maps concepts to scene embeddings. We find that scene embeddings decompose additively into object representations, explaining why uni-modal probes can recover object information. However, CLIP's binding function is high-complexity, which likely prevents the image and text encoders from learning a shared binding mechanism that generalizes to unseen concept combinations. We then ask whether this limitation is fundamental. We show that it is not. In controlled transformer models trained from scratch, binding generalization emerges with sufficient data coverage. These models learn low-complexity binding functions characterized by multiplicative interactions between concepts, enabling systematic generalization. Code is publicly available at .

</details>


### [186] [Personalize Your Large Vision-language Models With In-context Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31513)
*Yanshu Li, Jiaqian Li, Kuai Yu, Xi Xiao, Dongfang Liu, Tianyang Wang, Ruixiang Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (83.9) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 In-Context Prompt Tuning (ICPT)，一种轻量级投影模块，通过从多张参考图像中提取细粒度视觉语义并转化为连续提示，结合几何正则化来解耦身份与环境、分离不同概念，实现 LVLM 的高效个性化，无需推理时训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LVLM 个性化方法依赖推理时训练，效率低下，且在复杂多图像、多概念场景下准确性不足，限制了 LVLM 系统的广泛部署。

Method: 提出 ICPT（In-Context Prompt Tuning），包含：(1) 轻量级投影模块，从多张参考图像中提取细粒度视觉语义，将特征与身份标签映射转换为连续提示；(2) 自适应提示长度机制，根据每个概念的视觉复杂度动态确定提示长度；(3) 两种几何正则化约束，解耦关键身份与瞬态环境状态，分离不同概念以避免语义混淆。

Result: 在多种任务和 LVLM 骨干网络上，ICPT 达到了最先进的个性化准确率，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: ICPT 通过上下文提示调优实现了高效、准确的 LVLM 个性化，在复杂多概念场景下表现优异，为 LVLM 的个性化部署提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ICPT 方法用于大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）的个性化，属于通用视觉语言模型与 MLLM 架构的范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "vlm_mllm_general"（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）。

Importance reason: Directly addresses MLLM personalization with efficient in-context tuning, aligning with unified multimodal model priority. Signals: MLLM personalization, in-context prompt tuning, lightweight projection module, geometric regularization, efficient inference

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have demonstrated strong general multimodal capability and are increasingly deployed in downstream systems. This trend has driven growing interest in LVLM personalization, which aims to enable models to quickly and effectively learn out-of-distribution multimodal concepts to meet user-specific needs. However, many existing methods rely on inference-time training, which reduces efficiency. They also struggle to maintain accuracy in complex multi-image, multi-concept settings. These limitations restrict the broader deployment of LVLM-based systems. Therefore, this paper proposes in-context prompt tuning (ICPT). Specifically, ICPT employs a lightweight projection module capable of operating in complex scenarios to extract fine-grained visual semantics from multiple reference images, seamlessly transforming these features alongside identity-label mappings into continuous prompts. To maximize computational efficiency, this module adaptively determines the prompt length based on the intrinsic visual complexity of each concept. Crucially, to overcome the environmental biases and cross-concept interference prevalent in real-world applications, we introduce two novel geometric regularizations. These constraints refine prompt representations by decoupling key identities from transient environmental states and separating concepts to avoid semantic confusion. Extensive experiments show that ICPT achieves state-of-the-art personalization accuracy across diverse tasks and LVLM backbones.

</details>


### [187] ["Intelegi Româneşte?'' A Recipe for Romanian Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31401)
*Mihai Masala, Marius Leordeanu, Mihai Dascalu, Traian Rebedea*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (66.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了为罗马尼亚语构建视觉语言模型（VLM）的完整流程，包括翻译英文VLM训练和评测语料、消融视觉骨干和语言骨干的贡献、以及构建文化原生评测集HoraVQA。实验表明，罗马尼亚语适配的VLM在各项基准上一致优于同尺寸模型，甚至超越更大尺寸模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM主要面向英语等资源丰富语言，在低资源语言上性能急剧下降，缺乏大规模图像-文本语料和文化相关的评测基准。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统研究如何为罗马尼亚语构建高性能VLM。

Method: (1) 将英文VLM训练和评测语料通过机器翻译转换为罗马尼亚语，包括文本标注和图像内文字；(2) 消融实验：对比不同规模和预训练的视觉骨干、多语言vs罗马尼亚语适配的语言骨干、以及OCR风格图像-文本数据的影响；(3) 构建HoraVQA——基于罗马尼亚日常场景的文化原生评测集。

Result: 罗马尼亚语适配的VLM在所有评测基准上一致优于同尺寸的基线模型，甚至超越更大尺寸的模型类别。

Conclusion: 通过系统性的数据翻译、架构消融和文化原生评测，可以有效构建低资源语言的VLM，且语言适配带来的收益显著，为其他低资源语言的VLM构建提供了可复现的配方。

Classification reason: 该论文系统研究了为低资源语言（罗马尼亚语）构建视觉语言模型（VLM）的完整流程，涵盖数据构建、架构消融和评测，属于通用视觉语言模型构建与适配工作，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）。

Importance reason: Focuses on low-resource language VLM adaptation, not on core generative or efficient modeling priorities. Signals: Low-resource VLM, Language-specific adaptation, Cultural evaluation, Not autoregressive/diffusion, No efficiency focus

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) largely follow the text-only LLM trajectory, excelling on English benchmarks but sharply degrading on low-resource languages, where neither large-scale image-text corpora nor culturally grounded evaluations exist. We present a systematic study of building a language-specific VLM for Romanian, covering the full pipeline from data construction to architectural choices. We translate established English VLM training and evaluation corpora into Romanian, applying machine translation to textual annotations and to in-image text, preserving visual grounding while adapting the textual content. Using this data, we train and ablate a series of VLMs to isolate the contribution of (i) vision backbones of varying scale and pretraining, (ii) language backbones from multilingual to Romanian-adapted LLMs, and (iii) OCR-style image-text data. We further curate HoraVQA, a culturally native evaluation set grounded in Romanian everyday scenes. Romanian-adapted VLMs consistently outperform their same-sized counterparts and, across all evaluated benchmarks, even surpass models from the next larger size category.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_vqa_doc'></div>

## 视觉问答与文档理解 [[Back]](#toc)

VQA、文档/图表/表格理解、OCR-free 文档智能。

### [188] [Seeing Before Agreeing: Aligning Multi-Agent Consensus with Visual Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30698)
*Yuhan Wang, Shuochen Chang, Yalin Feng, Dongsheng Ma, Yuanzi Li, Zhengren Wang, Yinglong Yang, Yufei Chen, Yikang Wang, Shaoxu Sun, Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (47.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出EAGLE，一种无需训练的证据对齐多智能体框架，通过显式暴露各VLM智能体的视觉定位区域作为证据，实现证据层面的相互验证与一致性引导，从而提升多智能体VQA的可靠性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体VQA方法主要借鉴文本领域的讨论协议，聚焦于文本层面的答案协商，忽略了视觉信息对齐的重要性。作者发现仅答案层面的一致不足以保证可靠的多智能体VQA，对齐的视觉证据（智能体所依赖的图像区域）才是可信共识的关键。

Method: 提出EAGLE框架，包含三个步骤：(1) 显式暴露每个VLM智能体的定位区域作为视觉证据；(2) 智能体之间对彼此的证据进行相互验证；(3) 利用证据一致性引导最终决策。整个框架无需额外训练，轻量且可解释。

Result: 在六个VQA基准上取得最佳平均性能，同时保持轻量、可解释和易于部署的特点。

Conclusion: 对齐视觉证据而非仅对齐文本答案，是多智能体VQA实现可靠共识的关键。EAGLE框架验证了这一思路的有效性，为多智能体多模态推理提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 论文研究多智能体协作视觉问答（VQA），属于多模态理解与生成方向下的[视觉问答与文档理解]规范子主题。论文聚焦于通过多VLM智能体协作提升VQA可靠性，核心任务为VQA。

Importance reason: Novel evidence-alignment framework for multi-agent VQA, but outside user's priority subtopics and boost keywords. Signals: training-free, multi-agent VQA, visual evidence alignment, interpretability, no autoregressive or video generation focus

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance on visual question answering (VQA). To mitigate individual hallucinations and blind spots, aggregating diverse perspectives via multi-agent collaboration has emerged as a promising paradigm. While this approach has shown great success in textual QA, its potential in the multimodal domain remains under-explored. Existing multi-agent VQA methods predominantly adapt text-centric protocols, focusing on textual discussions while ignoring the alignment of visual information. In this work, we reveal a key insight: answer-level agreement is insufficient for reliable multi-agent VQA; \textit{aligned visual evidence} -- shared support from the image regions agents rely on -- is essential for trustworthy consensus. To leverage this insight, we propose EAGLE (\textbf{E}vidence-\textbf{A}ligned \textbf{G}rounded mu\textbf{L}ti-agent r\textbf{E}asoning), a training-free evidence-centered framework for coordinating multiple VLM agents. EAGLE explicitly exposes each agent's grounding regions as visual evidence, enables mutual verification over the evidence, and uses evidence consistency to guide final decision-making. Experiments on six VQA benchmarks show that EAGLE achieves best average performance across domains while remaining lightweight, interpretable, and practical for deployment.

</details>


### [189] [MechVQA: Benchmarking and Enhancing Multimodal LLMs on Comprehensive Mechanical Drawing Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30794)
*Qian Kou, Xiaofeng Shi, Yulin Li, Xiaosong Qiu, Xinyang Wang, Hua Zhou, Cao Dongxing*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 值得关注 (71.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了 MechVQA，首个面向机械工程图纸理解的多模态评测数据集，包含 3.3k 高密度图纸和 21K QA 对，覆盖识别、推理、判断三个能力层次共 10 种细粒度任务。基于该数据集，作者开发了 MechVL 模型，通过多阶段训练范式构建领域专用基线，在总得分上超越最强闭源基线 7.57 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在通用 VQA 任务上表现优异，但在机械工程图纸理解上存在明显不足——图纸具有高标注密度、弱领域知识、严格的投影规则和几何约束，导致模型容易遗漏关键线索并给出错误答案。目前缺乏专门针对机械图纸理解的数据集和评测基准。

Method: 1) 构建 MechVQA 数据集：通过半自动构建和质量控制流水线，收集 3.3k 高密度机械图纸，生成 21K QA 对，涵盖识别（如尺寸标注、符号识别）、推理（如投影关系、几何约束）和判断（如合规性检查）三个能力层次共 10 种任务。2) 开发 MechVL 模型：采用多阶段训练范式，在通用 MLLM 基础上进行领域知识注入和任务微调，构建领域专用基线。

Result: MechVL 在 MechVQA 总得分上超越最强闭源基线 7.57 个百分点，显著提升了机械图纸理解能力。

Conclusion: MechVQA 填补了机械工程图纸理解领域的数据集空白，MechVL 验证了多阶段训练范式在专业领域 VQA 任务上的有效性，为 MLLM 在机械设计与检测场景中的部署提供了可复用的基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了机械工程图纸理解数据集 MechVQA 和模型 MechVL，属于视觉问答与文档理解（特别是专业领域文档理解）范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "multimodal_vqa_doc（视觉问答与文档理解）" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Specialized benchmark for mechanical drawing understanding, but outside user's priority subtopics and contains negative keywords. Signals: multimodal understanding benchmark, domain-specific VQA, mechanical engineering, MLLM evaluation, multi-stage training

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated significant achievements in general visual question answering (VQA) tasks. However, they remain brittle on mechanical engineering drawings, where high annotation density and weak domain knowledge, compounded by unreliable spatial relation reasoning under strict projection rules and geometric constraints, make decisive cues easy to miss and frequently lead to wrong answers. To bridge this gap, we introduce the first comprehensive mechanical drawing understanding dataset, MechVQA, created through a semi-automated construction and quality-control pipeline. MechVQA contains 3.3k high-density pictures with 21K question-answer pairs, spanning 10 different fine-grained tasks across three capability levels: Recognition, Reasoning, and Judging, providing a testbed to evaluate and improve MLLM understanding on real-world mechanical drawings. On top of MechVQA, we then develop the MechVL model through a multi-stage training paradigm, building a strong domain-specialized baseline. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that MechVL outperforms the strongest closed-source baseline by 7.57 percentage points on the MechVQA total score, significantly enhancing mechanical drawing understanding ability and providing a reusable foundation for deploying MLLMs in mechanical design and inspection scenarios.

</details>

### [190] [Annotations Are Not All You Need: A Cross-modal Knowledge Transfer Network for Unsupervised Temporal Sentence Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30742)
*Xiang Fang, Daizong Liu, Wanlong Fang, Pan Zhou, Yu Cheng, Keke Tang, Kai Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出跨模态知识迁移网络（CKTNet），利用图像-名词和视频-动词任务中易获取的跨模态对齐知识，实现无监督时序句子定位（TSG），无需任何视频-查询配对标注或片段边界标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序句子定位方法严重依赖大量昂贵的视频-查询配对标注，实际应用中难以获取。本文旨在探索无需任何标注的无监督TSG设置，通过迁移其他跨模态任务中易获取的弱监督知识来解决该问题。

Method: 提出CKTNet框架，包含三个模块：1）从图像-名词配对任务中提取实体感知的外观知识，适配到每个视频帧；2）从视频-动词配对任务中提取事件感知的动作表示，并通过复制-粘贴方法增强到更复杂的真实场景；3）将外观和动作知识调制迁移到无监督TSG任务，直接利用通用知识关联视频和查询，无需训练即可检索相关片段。

Result: 在ActivityNet Captions和Charades-STA两个数据集上，该方法显著优于现有无监督方法，甚至与有监督方法相比也具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 跨模态知识迁移可以有效解决无监督时序句子定位问题，利用其他任务中易获取的弱监督知识替代昂贵的视频-查询配对标注，为实际应用提供了可行的无监督方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究无监督时序句子定位（Temporal Sentence Grounding），属于视频理解中的时序定位任务，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "视频理解与时序推理"（video_understanding_temporal）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Unsupervised temporal grounding is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's focus on generation and efficiency. Signals: unsupervised temporal sentence grounding, cross-modal knowledge transfer, no video-query annotations, outperforms unsupervised methods, negative keyword: none

Abstract: This paper addresses the task of temporal sentence grounding (TSG). Although many respectable works have made decent achievements in this important topic, they severely rely on massive expensive video-query paired annotations, which require a tremendous amount of human effort to collect in real-world applications. To this end, in this paper, we target a more practical but challenging TSG setting: unsupervised temporal sentence grounding, where both paired video-query and segment boundary annotations are unavailable during the network training. Considering that some other cross-modal tasks provide many easily available yet cheap labels, we tend to collect and transfer their simple cross-modal alignment knowledge into our complex scenarios: 1) We first explore the entity-aware object-guided appearance knowledge from the paired Image-Noun task, and adapt them into each independent video frame; 2) Then, we extract the event-aware action representation from the paired Video-Verb task, and further refine the action representation into more practical but complicated real-world cases by a newly proposed copy-paste approach; 3) By modulating and transferring both appearance and action knowledge into our challenging unsupervised task, our model can directly utilize this general knowledge to correlate videos and queries, and accurately retrieve the relevant segment without training. Extensive experiments on two challenging datasets (ActivityNet Captions and Charades-STA) show our effectiveness, outperforming existing unsupervised methods and even competitively beating supervised works.

</details>


### [191] [SLAP: The Semantic Least Action Principle for Variational Video-Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30750)
*Xiang Fang, Wanlong Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 普通相关 (56.4) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出语义最小作用量原理（SLAP），将视频语义轨迹建模为黎曼流形上的路径，通过离散欧拉-拉格朗日方程求解边值问题，实现无需像素级渲染的语义一致视频插值，解决了LVLMs中稀疏帧采样导致的时序间隙和物体消失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大视频语言模型（LVLMs）因计算限制只能采样稀疏帧，导致严重的"时序间隙"，模型无法感知关键的因果转换。现有方法（如潜扩散生成幻觉、自回归外推）在长时序上难以保持语义一致性，存在物体消失和能量不稳定性问题。

Method: 将经典力学与语义动力学建立严格的同构关系，把潜在视频轨迹建模为黎曼流形上的路径，由语义拉格朗日量支配。将插值任务形式化为边值问题（BVP），通过离散欧拉-拉格朗日方程求解，自然保持物体持久性而无需像素级渲染。

Result: 大量实验表明SLAP在语义一致性保持和长时序视频插值上优于现有方法，有效缓解了物体消失和能量不稳定问题。

Conclusion: 从概率生成转向变分力学范式，SLAP为视频语言模型中的时序间隙问题提供了新的理论框架和实用解决方案，验证了语义最小作用量原理在视频语义建模中的有效性。

Classification reason: 论文提出SLAP框架，通过变分力学方法解决大视频语言模型（LVLMs）中稀疏帧采样导致的时序间隙问题，核心是视频语义轨迹的插值与建模，属于视频理解与时序推理范畴。同时涉及视频生成式插值，也与多模态生成方向相关。

Importance reason: Novel variational mechanics approach for video semantic interpolation, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like autoregressive generation or world models. Signals: Semantic Least Action Principle, variational video-language modeling, temporal gap in LVLMs, object persistence without pixel rendering, discrete Euler-Lagrange equations

Abstract: In the era of Large Video-Language Models (LVLMs), the computational necessity of sparse frame sampling creates a fundamental ``temporal gap'', rendering models blind to critical causal transitions. Existing solutions relying on generative hallucination (e.g., latent diffusion) or autoregressive extrapolation often fail to maintain semantic consistency over long horizons, suffering from object vanishing and energetic instability. We propose a paradigm shift from probabilistic generation to variational mechanics with the \textbf{Semantic Least Action Principle (SLAP)}. Drawing a rigorous isomorphism between classical mechanics and semantic dynamics, we model the latent video trajectory as a path on a Riemannian manifold governed by a Semantic Lagrangian. By formulating the interpolation task as a Boundary Value Problem (BVP) solved via the discrete Euler-Lagrange equations, SLAP naturally enforces object persistence without pixel-level rendering. Extensive experiments show the effectiveness of our proposed SLAP.

</details>


### [192] [Towards Effective Long-Video Event Prediction via Multi-Level Event Semantics Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31069)
*Bo Peng, YuanJie Lyu, PengGang Qin, Tong Xu*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 普通相关 (49.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 VISTA 框架，通过多层级事件语义挖掘（细节级语义、事件级叙事、未来级预测）实现长视频事件预测，解决了现有长视频语言模型在事件预测任务上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注文本或短视频场景的事件预测，长视频事件预测因涉及大量多模态上下文和复杂叙事而未被充分探索。长视频语言模型在问答和摘要任务上表现良好，但难以泛化到事件预测，因为它们既无法精确提取事件相关细节，也无法进行细粒度的事件发展分析。

Method: VISTA 框架包含三个层级：(1) 细节级语义——采用以角色为中心的视觉提示精确提取事件相关视觉细节；(2) 事件级叙事——采用知识增强的迭代检索策略，引导 LLM 逐步构建逻辑连贯的事件链；(3) 未来级预测——采用类人的"先提出后检索"策略生成多样化的未来提案并整合多层级线索，产生鲁棒且准确的预测。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的大量实验验证了 VISTA 在长视频事件预测任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: VISTA 通过多层级事件语义挖掘有效解决了长视频事件预测问题，为长视频理解与未来事件预测提供了新的框架思路。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦长视频事件预测，属于长视频理解与时序推理范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "视频理解与时序推理"（video_understanding_temporal）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on long-video event prediction, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's key boost keywords. Signals: long-video event prediction, multi-level semantics, character-centric visual prompt, knowledge-enhanced retrieval, propose-then-retrieve

Abstract: Accurately predicting future events is fundamental to content understanding and decision-making across various domains. While prior research has primarily focused on text or short-video scenarios, long-video event prediction, characterized by vast multimodal context and more complex narratives, remains underexplored. Meanwhile, although recent Long-Video Language Models (LVLMs), built on Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs), have shown promise in long-video question answering and summarization, they struggle to generalize to event prediction, as they can neither precisely extract event-related details nor perform fine-grained analysis of event development. To address this gap, we propose VISTA, a multi-level event semantics mining framework for long-video event prediction. Initially, VISTA applies a character-centric visual prompt to precisely extract event-related visual details, enhancing detail-level semantics; subsequently, it employs a knowledge-enhanced iterative retrieval strategy, guiding the LLM to progressively construct logically coherent event chains, thereby improving event-level narratives; ultimately, VISTA adopts a human-like propose-then-retrieve strategy to generate diverse future-oriented proposals and integrate multi-level clues, producing robust and accurate predictions. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets validate the effectiveness of VISTA for long-video event prediction.

</details>


### [193] [HiERO-StepG @ Ego4D Step Grounding Challenge: hierarchical activity understanding enables zero-shot step grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31227)
*Andrea Zenotto, Simone Alberto Peirone, Francesca Pistilli, Giuseppe Averta*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 普通相关 (44.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 HiERO-StepG，一种零样本步骤定位方法，利用弱监督层次化表示学习从无标注视频中自动发现程序性活动的层次结构，无需任务特定微调即可实现步骤定位，在 Ego4D Step Grounding 挑战赛中取得第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的步骤定位方法需要大量标注数据且扩展性差。程序性活动天然具有层次结构（步骤和子步骤），但现有方法未能有效利用这种结构。作者希望利用无标注视频中动作共现的规律模式，让层次结构自然涌现，从而实现零样本步骤定位。

Method: 基于 HiERO 框架，采用弱监督表示学习将功能相关的动作在特征空间中映射到相近位置，仅利用细粒度的动作级叙述。在该特征空间中，通过简单聚类即可检测步骤，无需额外微调。针对 Ego4D 挑战赛，进一步引入粗细粒度步骤分配一致性约束、严格的时间单调性约束，以及后处理步骤以减少噪声预测的影响。

Result: 在 Ego4D Step Grounding 挑战赛全球排行榜上以 R@1 (IoU=0.3) 指标达到 56.27%，排名第二，且完全零样本，无需程序特定的标注。

Conclusion: 层次化活动理解可以有效实现零样本步骤定位，弱监督方法能够从无标注视频中自动发现程序性活动的结构，为减少对大量标注的依赖提供了可行路径。

Classification reason: 论文关注第一视角视频中的步骤定位（step grounding），属于视频理解与时序推理范畴，与 canonical subtopic "视频理解与时序推理" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on zero-shot step grounding for procedural video understanding, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's key interests in generation and efficiency. Signals: zero-shot step grounding, hierarchical activity understanding, Ego4D challenge, weak supervision, procedural video

Abstract: Procedural activities follow well-defined structures: whether we consider a cooking recipe or a mechanic repairing a car, these activities naturally decompose in a hierarchy of steps and sub-steps. Traditional approaches for step grounding require extensive annotations and scale poorly. Instead, we argue that such hierarchical structure can emerge naturally from uncurated videos of human activities through recurring patterns of co-occurring actions and activities. Our approach builds on HiERO, a weakly-supervised representation learning approach that maps close in the feature space actions that are functionally related to each other, leveraging only fine-grained action-level narrations. In this feature space, procedure steps can be detected by a simple clustering, with no additional task-specific fine-tuning. For the Ego4D Step Grounding challenge, we augment this approach by ensuring fine and coarse level agreement in step assignments, enforcing strict temporal monotonicity of the grounded steps and post-processing the detected steps to reduce the impact of noisy predictions. We call this approach HiERO-StepG and it achieves 56.27 % on the R@1 (IoU = 0.3) metric on the global leaderboard at submission time, ranking second while being completely zero-shot and not requiring procedure-specific annotations. Project page: .

</details>

### [194] [Sound effects in media:A comparative analysis of recorded and synthetic samples in live-action and animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31082)
*Nelly Garcia, Joshua Reiss*

Main category: cs.SD, cs.MM

Direction: 未分类

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (40.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文比较了程序化生成的合成音效与真实录音在实拍和动画媒体中的可信度。研究发现，合成音效在戏剧和科幻场景（如激光、打击、空气、火箭声）中效果良好且可信，但在卡通动画中表现日常动作时不够可信。研究还识别了需要优化的特定声音模型和音频特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拟音制作需要重复录制、分层处理或使用音效库，耗时且重复。程序化音频（数字拟音）可以快速生成样本，但其合成音效相比真实录音的可信度尚不明确，需要系统评估。

Method: 使用在线程序化音频引擎生成合成音效样本，将其分别与动画和实拍视频结合，通过主观听感实验比较合成音效与真实录音在不同类型场景（戏剧、科幻、卡通）中的可信度，并收集音频专业人士的反馈。

Result: 合成音效在戏剧和科幻场景中对激光、打击、空气、火箭等声音模型效果良好且可信；但在卡通动画中表现日常动作时可信度不足。识别了需要优化的特定声音模型和需要改进的音频特征。

Conclusion: 程序化音频在特定场景（戏剧、科幻）和特定声音类型（激光、打击等）中具有较高的可信度，可作为传统拟音的有效补充；但在卡通动画等风格化场景中仍需改进，尤其是日常动作音效的合成质量。

Classification reason: 该论文研究程序化音频（数字拟音）在媒体制作中的应用，比较合成音效与真实录音在动画和实拍场景中的可信度，属于生成式 AI方向下的视频音频生成应用（video_audio_creation）范畴，该子主题涵盖音视频编辑与内容创作产品级生成应用。

Importance reason: Focuses on sound effects believability in media, not aligned with user's priority on video/image generation or model efficiency. Signals: procedural audio, sound design, believability study, live-action vs animation, not autoregressive/diffusion video generation

Abstract: Creating sound for storytelling is crucial to establishing the environment in productions such as films, TV series and video games. This process often involves repeating, layering and recording real objects or using sound libraries, which can be time-consuming and repetitive. To address these challenges, procedural audio, also known as digital foley, offers a solution by allowing sound designers to quickly generate samples. Despite its efficiency, questions remain about the believability of synthetic samples compared to real ones. In our study, we compared synthetic samples generated by an online procedural engine and integrated them with both animated and live-action visuals. Our results indicate that procedural audio is highly effective and perceived as believable in drama and sci-fi scenes, particularly for sound models such as lasers, hits, air and rockets, whereas synthetic sounds weren't as believable in cartoon productions when representing everyday actions. Finally, we identified specific models that needed optimisation and highlighted audio features that needed improvement with feedback from audio professionals.

</details>


### [195] [UXR PoV for Neuroinclusive Emotion Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31131)
*Melike Akca, Mona Giff, Deniz Cetinkaya, Huseyin Dogan, Stephen Giff*

Main category: cs.HC, cs.AI

Direction: 未分类

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (35.9) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种生成式AI增强的用户体验研究方法论（UXR PoV Playbook），用于设计面向ADHD成人的神经包容性情绪调节数字干预系统。该方法整合了DBT、SDT和COM-B等心理学框架，利用生成式AI作为协同分析工具，通过四阶段UXR流程产出10张理论驱动的设计指导卡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字情绪调节干预系统存在理论整合薄弱、对神经多样性群体（如ADHD患者）的适应性不足、缺乏结构化用户体验研究方法论等问题，亟需一种将心理学洞察与设计实践有效桥接的方法框架。

Method: 提出生成式AI增强的UXR PoV方法论，包含四个阶段：(1) AI支持的假设生成，(2) 基础规划，(3) 通过Building Blocks进行洞察生成，(4) 构建利益相关者特定的PoV叙事。该方法整合DBT、SDT和COM-B心理学框架，利用生成式AI作为协同分析工具辅助综合、假设形成和设计表达，最终产出10张理论驱动的UXR Play Cards。

Result: 产出了一套包含10张理论驱动的UXR Play Cards的设计指导工具，将心理学机制和实证发现转化为可操作的设计指南，为神经包容性数字心理健康设计提供了可复现的、具有偏误意识的框架。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是一个可复现的、具有偏误意识的生成式AI增强UXR框架，推动了以人为本和神经包容性的数字心理健康设计方法发展，为将生成式AI整合到用户体验研究实践中提供了结构化路径。

Classification reason: 该论文将生成式AI应用于ADHD情绪调节的数字心理健康设计，属于医疗健康垂直领域的生成应用，与现有 canonical subtopic "领域内容生成应用"（domain_application）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Low relevance to user's priority subtopics on generative models for video/image generation and efficient inference. Signals: generative AI as co-analytic tool, neuroinclusive design, ADHD emotion regulation, UXR methodology, psychological framework integration

Abstract: Attention-deficit/hyperactivity disorder (ADHD) is a psychiatric disorder which presents itself in individuals through patterns of developmentally inappropriate levels of inattentiveness, hyperactivity, and impulsivity, with difficulties in decision making and emotional regulation (ER). Although digital and AI-based interventions have expanded access to ER support, many existing systems remain limited by weak theoretical integration, insufficient accommodation of neurodiversity, and a lack of structured user experience research (UXR) methodologies, that bridge psychological insight with design practice. This paper introduces a Generative AI-augmented UXR methodology, grounded in the UXR Point of View (PoV) Playbook, to support the design of emotionally intelligent and Neuroinclusive digital ER interventions for adults with ADHD. The approach integrates empirical evidence with established psychological frameworks Dialectical Behaviour Therapy (DBT), Self-Determination Theory (SDT), and the COM-B behavioural model and leverages Generative AI as a co-analytic tool to support synthesis, hypothesis formation, and design articulation. The methodology is operationalized through a four-stage UXR process encompassing AI-supported hypothesis generation, foundational planning, insight generation via Building Blocks, and the construction of stakeholder-specific PoV narratives. This process results in a set of ten theory informed UXR Play Cards that translate psychological mechanisms and empirical findings into actionable design guidance. The primary contribution of this work is a replicable, bias-aware framework for integrating Generative AI into UXR practice, advancing human-centred and Neuroinclusive approaches to digital mental health design.

</details>


### [196] [Extending the UXR Point of View Pyramid: A Generative AI-Augmented Methodology for Human-Centred AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31143)

TL;DR: 本文针对英国金融科技债务管理场景，将UXR PoV金字塔扩展为生成式AI增强的方法论框架，包含AI增强PoV金字塔、结构化提示架构和AI驱动的Playbook卡片系统，将GenAI定位为认知支持机制而非分析权威，以提升高利害金融AI系统中用户研究的方法论严谨性与伦理可追溯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UXR PoV框架未针对AI驱动的金融系统设计，而这类系统面临可解释性、公平性和问责制等核心挑战。同时，家庭债务和生活成本压力加剧，AI金融技术日益影响信贷评估和债务支持等关键决策，亟需一种将生成式AI融入用户研究工作流、同时保持可追溯性和伦理监督的方法论框架。

Method: 本文形式化了三个核心组件：(1) AI增强的UXR PoV金字塔，扩展传统金字塔以适配AI系统；(2) 结构化提示架构，用于综合研究证据和生成假设；(3) AI驱动的Playbook卡片系统，将GenAI嵌入UXR工作流，同时保留人类验证和伦理监督机制。框架以英国债务管理技术（可负担性评估、还款规划、财务压力预测）为应用场景进行具体化。

Result: 提出了一个完整的AI增强UXR方法论框架，将生成式AI定位为认知支持机制（epistemic support mechanism）而非分析权威，确保人类验证和监管意识贯穿始终。该框架为高利害金融AI环境中的用户研究提供了结构化方法论指导。

Conclusion: 本文通过将生成式AI融入UXR PoV方法论，为负责任、AI驱动的用户研究实践提供了理论框架，推动了CHI社区中高利害金融AI系统的以人为中心的设计方法论发展。GenAI作为辅助工具而非替代人类判断的角色定位，确保了方法论的伦理可追溯性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种生成式AI增强的UXR PoV金字塔方法论，将GenAI嵌入用户研究工作流（包括结构化提示架构和AI驱动的Playbook卡片系统），属于AI辅助的生成工作流与自动化创作流水线，因此映射到生成式 AI下的[CANONICAL]子主题"AI智能体与生成工作流"。

Importance reason: Focuses on UXR methodology for financial AI, not on core generative model advances or video/image generation. Signals: human-centred AI, UXR methodology, financial debt management, generative AI augmentation, not core model research

Abstract: Rising household debt and cost-of-living pressures in the United Kingdom have intensified the role of AI-driven financial technologies in mediating credit assessment, repayment structuring, and debt support services. These systems increasingly shape consequential financial decisions, yet they operate within complex socio-technical environments characterised by regulatory constraint, algorithmic opacity, and heightened vulnerability risk. User Experience Research (UXR) Points of View (PoVs) are critical in translating heterogeneous research evidence into strategic direction for product and governance decisions. However, the existing UXR PoV framework was not designed for AI-mediated financial systems where interpretability, fairness, and accountability are central. This paper extends the UXR PoV pyramid into an AI-augmented methodological framework for Human-Centred AI debt management technologies in the UK financial services context. We formalise (1) an AI-Augmented PoV Pyramid, (2) a structured prompt architecture for synthesis and hypothesis generation, and (3) an AI-enabled Playbook Card system that embeds Generative AI into UXR workflows while preserving traceability and ethical oversight. Generative AI is positioned not as an analytic authority, but as an epistemic support mechanism subject to human validation and regulatory awareness. By grounding the framework in debt management technologies, including affordability assessment, repayment planning, and financial stress prediction systems, this work advances UXR methodology for high-stakes financial AI environments and contributes to the evolution of responsible, AI-powered UXR practice within the CHI community.

</details>


### [197] [Personalized to Persuade: The Effects of Contextualization and Warmth on Trust and Reliance in Conversational AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31275)
*Mert Yazan, Suzan Verberne, Frederik Bungaran Ishak Situmeang*

Main category: cs.HC, cs.AI

Direction: 未分类

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (38.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文通过2×2被试间实验（N=380）研究对话式AI的个性化（contextualization）与温暖感（warmth）如何影响用户对AI助手的信任和依赖。结果表明：个性化降低了AI的说服力，但与温暖感结合后通过交叉交互恢复说服力；用户对AI的依赖普遍存在且不受对话设计影响；AI素养高的用户对AI信任更低但反而更易被说服和依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化（contextualization）已被证明是政治和营销中的说服策略，但在日常任务中用户缺乏先验知识时，个性化对AI说服力的影响尚不明确。本文旨在探究对话式AI中个性化与温暖感如何共同影响用户的信任和依赖行为。

Method: 采用2×2被试间实验设计（N=380），操纵两个因素：个性化（有/无）和温暖感（有/无）。AI助手被设计为反驳专家建议的论证型助手，测量用户的信任、说服力感知和依赖行为，并分析AI素养的调节作用。

Result: 个性化降低了AI的说服力，但与温暖感结合后通过交叉交互恢复说服力；用户对AI的依赖在所有条件下普遍存在且不受对话设计影响；信任是说服和依赖的强预测因子，但个性化和温暖感并非通过信任起作用；AI素养高的用户对AI信任更低，但反而更易被说服和依赖。

Conclusion: 用户倾向于依赖AI建议而非人类专家判断，但界面级别的对话设计选择（个性化和温暖感）对用户行为的影响有限。AI素养会解耦信任与行为之间的关系，高素养用户表现出信任-行为分离现象。

Classification reason: 该论文研究对话式AI中个性化（contextualization）与温暖感（warmth）对用户信任和依赖的影响，属于生成式 AI方向下的个性化与对话生成（personalization_persona）范畴，该子主题涵盖个性化助手、对话生成与角色扮演等主题，与本论文高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on human-AI trust and persuasion in conversational AI, which is peripheral to the user's core interest in generative model architectures and efficiency. Signals: Human-computer interaction, Persuasion and trust study, Not generative model architecture, No technical novelty in generation, Low alignment with priority subtopics

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) agents personalize their responses by tailoring explanations to users' backgrounds, interests, and prior interactions, referred to as contextualization. Personalization has been identified as a persuasive strategy in politics or in marketing. However, the persuasive effect of contextualization in everyday tasks, where users often lack prior knowledge, remains unclear. We conducted a $2\times2$ between-subjects experiment ($N = 380$) examining how contextualization, combined with conversational warmth, shapes reliance and persuasiveness of an AI assistant arguing against expert recommendations. Our findings reveal that contextualization reduces the persuasive power of AI, but its combination with warmth restores persuasiveness through a crossover interaction. Reliance on AI is present across conditions and is invariant to the conversational design. Trust strongly predicts both persuasion and reliance, yet neither contextualization nor warmth operates through trust. AI literacy decouples trust from behavior: more literate users report lower trust in the assistant, yet are more persuaded and more reliant on its advice. These results suggest that users are prone to deferring to AI agents over human expert judgment; however, interface-level conversational design choices have a limited role in shaping the behavior.

</details>


### [198] [LLM-FACETS: A Privacy-Preserving Framework for Evaluating LLM Transparency and Accountability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31167)
*Tom Lucas, Alessio Buscemi, Alfredo Capozucca, German Castignani, Barbara Delacroix*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 未分类

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (43.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LLM-FACETS 是一个面向非技术从业者的开源 LLM 评测框架，提供浏览器界面和插件架构，支持确定性指标本地运行和 LLM-as-judge 指标可控调用，通过 token 级 log-probability 可视化、多裁判共识和 RAG Triad 指标实现透明度与可问责性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 审计工具需要编程技能和复杂环境配置，且云平台会将评估数据发送到外部服务，导致领域专家和合规官员难以参与 AI 监督。论文旨在为不同角色（技术专家、领域专家、合规官员）提供可访问、隐私保护且可复现的 LLM 评测框架。

Method: 设计了三类用户画像（技术专家、领域专家、合规官员）对应的框架架构；确定性指标（BLEU、ROUGE、BERTScore）在自托管服务器本地运行，无数据外传；LLM-judge 指标通过外部 API 调用但用户完全控制凭证；通过 token 级 log-probability 可视化、多裁判共识机制和 RAG Triad 指标（Faithfulness、Answer Relevance、Context Relevance）实现透明度；插件架构支持新指标和数据集的无缝集成。

Result: 通过 18 个指标实现与规范参考库的交叉验证，验证了框架的正确性。框架支持跨多个指标对同一属性进行交叉检查，确保可复现性，并将 AI 可问责性与构建被评估系统的团队解耦。

Conclusion: LLM-FACETS 为 LLM 透明度与可问责性评估提供了一个隐私保护、可访问且可扩展的开源框架，使非技术从业者也能参与 AI 审计，符合 EU AI Act 和 NIST AI RMF 的监管要求。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 LLM-FACETS，一个用于评估 LLM 输出事实性、校准性和可复现性的开源框架，属于 生成式 AI 方向下的生成内容质量评测范畴，与 canonical 子主题 evaluation_benchmark（生成式 AI 评测与基准）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM evaluation transparency and privacy, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to generative modeling or efficiency. Signals: LLM evaluation framework, privacy-preserving, non-technical practitioners, EU AI Act alignment, plugin architecture

Abstract: Assessing whether Large Language Models outputs are factually grounded, epistemically calibrated, and methodologically reproducible is a prerequisite for responsible AI deployment. Yet auditing LLMs remains inaccessible to non-technical practitioners: existing tools require programming expertise and non-trivial environment setup, and cloud-hosted platforms transmit evaluation data to external services, creating barriers for domain experts and compliance officers legally responsible for AI oversight. We introduce LLM-FACETS (LLM FActuality Cross-EvaluaTion System): an open-source framework with a browser-accessible interface and a plugin architecture, structured around three practitioner profiles (technical experts, domain experts, compliance officers) that mirror the stakeholder categories identified in the EU AI Act and the NIST AI Risk Management Framework. The architecture makes data flows explicit: deterministic metrics (BLEU, ROUGE, BERTScore) run entirely within the self-hosted server with no outbound transmission; LLM-judge metrics contact external APIs explicitly, with users retaining full credential control. The framework operationalizes transparency through three mechanisms: token-level log-probability visualization for epistemic uncertainty, multi-judge consensus to mitigate judge bias, and RAG Triad metrics (Faithfulness, Answer Relevance, Context Relevance) to detect and localize hallucinations. A plugin architecture allows any new metric or dataset to be integrated without modifying the evaluation pipeline. The open-source implementation enables cross-checking across multiple metrics targeting the same property, ensuring reproducibility and decoupling AI accountability from the teams building the systems assessed. We verify the framework through cross-validation of 18 metric implementations against canonical reference libraries.

</details>


### [199] [Protocol for evaluating ChatGPT in biomedical association generation and verification using a RAG-enabled, cross-model majority voting workflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30400)

TL;DR: 本文提出一个评估ChatGPT生成疾病中心生物医学关联能力的协议，利用生物医学本体验证实体、文献验证关联，并通过RAG增强的开源LLM工作流进行语义验证，以暴露幻觉并评估生成可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ChatGPT等大语言模型在生物医学领域生成关联时存在幻觉和可靠性问题，缺乏系统性的评估协议来验证其生成内容的准确性和一致性。

Result: 该协议能够系统性地评估ChatGPT生成生物医学关联的可靠性，通过RAG和跨模型多数投票策略有效暴露幻觉，并提供了本体精确匹配与语义验证相结合的评估框架。

Conclusion: 提出的协议为评估LLM在生物医学领域生成内容的可靠性提供了系统化方法，RAG增强的跨模型验证工作流能够有效检测幻觉，为生物医学知识发现中的AI生成内容可信度评估奠定了基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出评估ChatGPT生成生物医学关联能力的协议，涉及生成内容可靠性评测与幻觉检测，属于生成式 AI评测范畴，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成式 AI 评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Focuses on biomedical evaluation protocol, far from user's priority subtopics in video/image generation and efficient models. Signals: biomedical evaluation, ChatGPT hallucination, RAG workflow, ontology validation, low priority direction

Abstract: We present a protocol to evaluate ChatGPT's ability to generate disease-centric biomedical associations. It outlines how we generate the associations, validate the biological entities using biomedical ontologies, and verify associations using literature. The protocol includes a self-consistency strategy to assess generative reliability across ChatGPT models. To address ontology exact-match limitations, we provide a use case performing semantic verification through a workflow enabled by Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) powered by open-source large language models (LLMs). This enables LLMs to establish truth over content generated by other LLMs and expose hallucination.

</details>


### [200] [LongDS-Bench: On the Failure of Long-Horizon Agentic Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30434)
*Kewei Xu, Xiaoben Lu, Shuofei Qiao, Zihan Ding, Haoming Xu, Lei Liang, Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.MA, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 未分类

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (55.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LongDS-Bench 是一个面向长程、多轮交互式数据分析的基准，包含 68 个从真实 Kaggle 笔记本构建的任务（共 2225 轮），覆盖六个领域。评估发现最佳模型仅达 48.45% 准确率，早期到后期性能下降近 47 个百分点，长程错误占失败的 52%-69%，表明维持正确分析状态是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据分析基准大多评估孤立或短交互任务，无法测试智能体在长程多轮分析中追踪、维护和组合演化分析状态的能力，因此需要构建一个长程、多轮的数据分析基准来揭示当前模型的局限性。

Method: 从真实 Kaggle 笔记本构建 68 个长程任务（2225 轮），设计状态演化模式（反事实扰动、回滚、多状态组合），平均依赖跨度 11.3 轮，覆盖地球科学、商业、教育等六个领域，评估五个 SOTA 模型。

Result: 最佳模型仅达 48.45% 平均准确率；从早期到后期轮次性能下降近 47 个百分点；长程错误占失败的 52%-69%；增加智能体步骤数不一定提升性能。

Conclusion: 长程数据分析中维持正确的分析状态是主要瓶颈，而非增加交互预算；LongDS-Bench 为研究可靠的长程智能体数据分析提供了重要评测平台。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向长程、多轮数据分析的基准 LongDS-Bench，属于 生成式 AI 评测与基准（evaluation_benchmark）范畴，评测智能体在长程数据分析中的状态追踪与演化能力。

Importance reason: High-quality benchmark exposing a critical bottleneck in long-horizon agentic data analysis, directly relevant to 生成式 AI evaluation and model efficiency. Signals: Long-horizon agent benchmark, state-evolution patterns, 52%-69% long-horizon errors, performance drop 47 points, best model 48.45% accuracy

Abstract: Real-world data analysis is inherently iterative, yet existing benchmarks mostly evaluate isolated or short interactive tasks, leaving agents' ability to track evolving analytical context over long horizons untested. We introduce LongDS, a benchmark for long-horizon, multi-turn data analysis where agents must maintain, update, restore, and compose evolving analytical states. LongDS comprises 68 tasks constructed from real-world Kaggle notebooks, spanning 2,225 turns across six domains including Geoscience, Business, and Education. Tasks are designed around state-evolution patterns (e.g., counterfactual perturbation, rollback, multi-state composition), with an average dependency span of 11.3 turns. Evaluating five state-of-the-art models, we find that the best model reaches only 48.45% average accuracy, performance drops nearly 47 points from early to late turns, and long-horizon errors account for 52%--69% of failures. Further analysis shows that additional agent steps do not necessarily improve performance, suggesting that the key bottleneck is maintaining a correct analytical state rather than increasing interaction budget. We release LongDS to support research on reliable long-horizon agentic data analysis. Code and data will be released at .

</details>


### [201] [MAAT: Multi-phase Adapter-Aware Targeted Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30514)
*Suryash Yagnik, Shubham Gaur, Saksham Thakur, Vinija Jain, Aman Chadha, Amitava Das*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.LG

Direction: 未分类

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (41.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文指出现有机器遗忘评测中"Why"类因果知识占比极低（<0.06%~1.3%），导致方法在因果知识上的失败被掩盖。作者构建了平衡的5W评测基准5WBENCH，并提出MAAT（多阶段适配器感知定向遗忘）框架，通过梯度投影上升、SVD秩剪枝、任务向量否定和混合KL-隐状态保留修复，首次在Why型因果知识上同时实现高遗忘与高保留，达到遗忘-保留帕累托前沿的新操作点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘评测存在结构性偏差：Why型（因果/关系知识）问题占比极低，导致在聚合指标下表现良好的方法可能在因果知识遗忘上完全失败而无法被检测。需要平衡的评测基准和能同时处理因果知识遗忘与保留的方法。

Method: 提出5WBENCH平衡基准（5类各1000样本），以及MAAT三阶段框架：1）梯度投影上升（GPA）进行遗忘；2）SVD秩剪枝去除冗余遗忘维度；3）任务向量否定与混合KL-隐状态保留修复（KL-HS retain repair），在LoRA适配器权重上操作。

Result: MAAT在Why型因果知识上首次同时实现高遗忘率和高保留率，在遗忘-保留帕累托前沿上达到新的操作点。5WBENCH揭示了现有基线方法在因果知识上的系统性失败。

Conclusion: 机器遗忘评测需要平衡的5W覆盖以避免评估偏差；因果知识的遗忘难度源于多跳推理链和长答案跨度导致的梯度稀释；MAAT通过多阶段适配器操作有效解决了这一挑战。

Classification reason: 该论文研究机器遗忘（machine unlearning），提出面向因果知识的遗忘方法 MAAT 和评测基准 5WBENCH，属于 生成式 AI 方向下的「概念擦除与遗忘学习」规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on machine unlearning evaluation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and boosted keywords. Signals: machine unlearning, causal knowledge evaluation, 5W benchmark, LoRA adapter, Pareto frontier

Abstract: Machine unlearning evaluation is structurally skewed: Why-type questions, which probe causal and relational knowledge, comprise less than 0.06% of CounterFact, 0.6% of ZSRE, and less than 1.3% of TOFU, MUSE, and WMDP-Cyber. This near-zero representation means that methods that fail on causal knowledge can score highly in aggregate, and this failure is undetectable without balanced evaluation. We present 5WBENCH, a balanced 5,000-sample benchmark with 1,000 examples per 5W category (Who, What, When, Where, Why), making causal unlearning failures quantifiable for the first time. Using 5WBENCH, we show that no existing baseline simultaneously achieves high forgetting and high retention on Why-type questions: aggressive forgetting degrades retained knowledge, while conservative methods fail to forget causal facts. Why-type difficulty stems from multi-hop reasoning chains (44% of Why entries vs. less than or equal to 2% for others) and gradient dilution over 40.1-token answer spans. We present MAAT (Multi-phase Adapter-Aware Targeted Unlearning), a three-phase framework operating on LoRA adapter weights, combining gradient-projected ascent, SVD rank-dimension pruning, task vector negation, and hybrid KL-hidden-state retain repair. MAAT is the first method to simultaneously achieve high forgetting and high retention on Why-type causal knowledge, reaching a new operating point on the forget-retain Pareto frontier. We make our code publicly available.

</details>


### [202] [Same Patient, Different Words, Different Diagnosis? Evaluating Semantic Stability in Clinical LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30646)
*Mahdi Alkaeed, Adnan Qayyum, Nabeel Abo Kashreef, Muhammad Bilal, Junaid Qadir*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 未分类

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (31.76) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出基于自然语言推理（NLI）的语义验证框架，用于筛选临床LLM中保持语义不变的提示改写，并引入MVS、ΔC、WCI三个指标量化模型对语义等价改写的敏感性。在16个通用和医学LLM上的实验表明，领域专业化并不一致地提升或降低模型对语义保持改写的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在临床应用中行为对细微语言变化高度敏感，而现有嵌入相似度指标无法有效区分涉及否定、时态、严重程度等关键语义差异的改写，亟需可靠的语义验证方法和敏感性评估指标。

Method: 提出基于NLI的语义验证框架，通过NLI过滤保持语义的提示改写，结合LLM-as-a-judge和临床专家审核进一步精炼；引入三个指标：MVS（语义保持改写敏感性）、ΔC（置信度变化）、WCI（最差情况不稳定性）。在DiagnosisQA和MedQA数据集上评估16个开源通用和医学LLM。

Result: 领域专用模型与通用模型之间的鲁棒性差异高度依赖具体模型，领域专业化并不一致地提升或降低对语义保持改写的鲁棒性；部分领域专用模型属于最鲁棒的模型，但强通用基线模型也保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 临床LLM对语义等价改写存在显著敏感性，领域专业化不能保证鲁棒性提升，需要更系统的语义稳定性评估框架来保障临床安全。

Classification reason: 该论文研究临床LLM在语义等价改写下的稳定性与鲁棒性，属于医疗垂直领域的LLM应用评估，与现有规范子主题"领域内容生成应用"（domain_application）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on clinical LLM robustness, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and contains negative keywords. Signals: clinical LLM, semantic stability, NLI verification, domain specialization, medical diagnosis

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in clinical applications. However, their behavior remains highly sensitive to subtle linguistic variations, such as rephrasing or syntactic variation. This sensitivity poses risks in safety-critical healthcare settings, where semantically equivalent inputs should produce consistent predictions. However, a key challenge is to ensure that prompt variations truly preserve clinical meaning, as embedding-based similarity metrics often fail to capture distinctions involving negation, temporality, or severity. To address this limitation, we propose a semantic verification framework based on Natural Language Inference (NLI) to filter meaning-preserving prompt variations, which are further refined using an LLM-as-a-judge and audited by a clinical expert. In addition, we introduce three metrics to quantify model sensitivity: MeaningPreserving Variation Sensitivity (MVS), confidence variation (\Delta C), and Worst-Case Instability (WCI). We evaluate 16 open-source general-purpose (GP) and medical LLMs within the same model families and parameter scales, using reformulated prompts derived from the DiagnosisQA and MedQA datasets. Our results demonstrate that robustness differences between domain-specific (DS) models are mixed and highly model-dependent, i.e., domain specialization does not consistently improve or reduce robustness to meaning-preserving prompt reformulations. Several DS models rank among the most robust (when compared with GP counterparts), and strong GP baselines remain competitive as well.

</details>


### [203] [ElasticMem: Latent Memory as a Learnable Resource for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30690)
*Tao Feng, Chongrui Ye, Tianyang Luo, Jingjun Xu, Xueqiang Xu, Haozhen Zhang, Ge Liu, Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 未分类

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (49.25) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ElasticMem提出了一种将记忆作为可学习弹性潜变量资源的LLM智能体框架，通过离线潜记忆库、自适应检索、可变潜预算分配和GRPO优化，在记忆密集型QA和具身智能体控制任务上显著提升性能并降低token开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强方法将记忆视为固定资源：文本空间方法将检索到的记忆拼接到上下文中导致大量token开销且对噪声敏感；潜空间方法虽减少文本开销但依赖刚性检索或固定容量记忆接口。这造成了查询相关的记忆效用与固定记忆分配之间的不匹配。

Method: ElasticMem构建离线潜记忆库（含检索键和内容缓存），从推理器的隐藏状态自适应检索记忆，通过可学习策略为每条检索到的记忆分配可变潜预算，并将选中的潜状态作为软记忆token注入生成过程。整个记忆使用过程通过GRPO（Group-Relative Policy Optimization）以下游任务奖励进行端到端优化。

Result: 在MemorySuite（记忆密集型QA和ALFWorld具身控制）上，基于Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct和Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct，ElasticMem在加权平均QA准确率上分别提升26.2%和24.6%，在ALFWorld成功率上分别提升66.3%和27.2%，同时实现了最低的ALFWorld token开销。

Conclusion: ElasticMem通过将记忆建模为可学习的弹性潜变量资源，有效解决了固定记忆分配与查询相关效用之间的不匹配问题。自适应检索和弹性预算分配帮助模型优先利用有用证据和可迁移计划，超越了刚性余弦相似度的检索方式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM智能体的长期记忆机制，属于LLM智能体核心能力增强，与生成式 AI方向下的[AI智能体与生成工作流]规范子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel latent memory framework for LLM agents, but outside core priority subtopics and lacks direct connection to generation or efficiency. Signals: LLM agent memory, latent variable budget, GRPO optimization, token cost reduction, embodied agent control

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for LLM agents to reason coherently across extended interactions, personalize responses, and reuse past experience. However, existing memory-augmented methods typically treat memory as a fixed resource: text-space approaches concatenate retrieved memories into the context window, causing substantial token overhead and sensitivity to noisy evidence, while latent-space approaches reduce textual cost but still rely on rigid retrieval or fixed-capacity memory interfaces. This creates a mismatch between query-dependent memory utility and fixed memory allocation. We propose ElasticMem, a memory-augmented LLM framework that learns to use memory as an elastic latent resource. ElasticMem builds an offline latent memory bank with retrieval keys and content caches, retrieves memories adaptively from the reasoner's hidden state, assigns each retrieved memory a variable latent budget through a learned policy, and injects selected latent states as soft memory tokens for generation. The full memory-use process is optimized with downstream task rewards through group-relative policy optimization. We evaluate ElasticMem on MemorySuite, covering memory-intensive QA and embodied agent control. Across Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct backbones, ElasticMem improves weighted average QA accuracy by 26.2% and 24.6%, and improves ALFWorld success rate by 66.3% and 27.2%, respectively, over the strongest baselines, while achieving the lowest ALFWorld token cost. Ablations and qualitative analyses further show that adaptive retrieval and elastic budget allocation help ElasticMem prioritize useful evidence and transferable plans beyond rigid cosine similarity. Our code for ElasticMem will be released at .

</details>


### [204] [An Organization-Scoped LLM Agent Runtime Architecture for Regulated Cybersecurity Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30604)
*George Fatouros, Georgios Makridis, George Kousiouris, John Soldatos, Dimosthenis Kyriazis*

Main category: cs.CR, cs.IR, cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 未分类

Subtopic: 未分类

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向金融网络安全受监管场景的组织级 LLM 智能体运行时架构，通过类型化的安全上下文、共享运行时核心、分层人机交互门控和仅追加审计日志，实现检索、工具调用、记忆、报告和审计的全链路组织范围管控，并保持模型无关和本地可部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体系统在孤立网络安全任务上表现良好，但缺乏一个能在受监管的安全运营中心（SOC）和合规工作流中强制执行组织级范围（跨检索、工具调用、记忆、报告和审计）的运行时平台，且需要与现有 SIEM/XDR 堆栈集成而非作为独立分析层运行。

Method: 提出组织级 LLM 智能体运行时架构，核心组件包括：(1) 类型化安全上下文（Security Context），在每个入口点创建并在所有组件边界强制执行；(2) 共享运行时核心（Runtime Core）与逻辑专业子智能体；(3) 受治理的工具适配层（Tool Adapter Layer），在统一策略和审计下暴露 SIEM/XDR 查询、增强和响应原语；(4) 结构化发现与证据引用；(5) 分层人机交互（HITL）门控；(6) 仅追加审计日志。MCP、扩展遥测、数字孪生、图检索和联邦知识共享作为可选扩展路径。

Result: 论文描述了可实现的架构切片作为可测试性表面，并提出了一个可证伪的评估计划，包含架构就绪度、安全策略执行、证据可追溯性、输出质量和运营可观测性等维度的指标级通过标准。

Conclusion: 该架构为受监管网络安全场景下的 LLM 智能体部署提供了组织级管控和审计保障的运行时方案，未来可通过 MCP、数字孪生等扩展路径进一步增强能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种面向受监管网络安全运营的组织级 LLM 智能体运行时架构，涉及智能体工作流、工具调用、人机协作与审计追踪，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "AI 智能体与生成工作流" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on regulated cybersecurity operations, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and has negative keywords. Signals: cybersecurity, regulated operations, LLM agent runtime, SOC workflows, model-agnostic

Abstract: Regulated cybersecurity workflows lack a runtime substrate that enforces organization-level scope across retrieval, tool calls, memory, findings, reports, and audit while remaining model-agnostic and locally deployable. Recent large language model (LLM) agent systems report strong results on isolated cybersecurity tasks, yet they do not by themselves define an auditable platform architecture for regulated security operations centre (SOC) and compliance workflows, where a single analyst may trigger actions that bind the organization, and where the runtime must integrate with existing SIEM/XDR stacks as a primary source of context and alert-driven triggers rather than operate as a standalone analytical layer. This paper proposes an organization-scoped LLM agent runtime architecture for financial cybersecurity. The contribution is a typed Security Context that is created at every entry point, including SIEM/XDR notifications ingested as first-class triggers, and enforced at every component boundary, combined with a shared Runtime Core, logical specialist subagents, a governed Tool Adapter Layer exposing SIEM/XDR query, enrichment, and response primitives under uniform policy and audit, structured findings with evidence references, tiered human-in-the-loop (HITL) gates, and append-only audit. Model Context Protocol (MCP), extended telemetry, digital twins for pentesting, graph retrieval, and federated knowledge sharing are treated as optional extension paths rather than mandatory runtime assumptions. We describe an implementable slice as the architecture's testability surface, and we propose a falsifiable evaluation plan with metric-level pass criteria for architecture readiness, security-policy enforcement, evidence traceability, output quality, and operational observability.

</details>


### [205] [BIAS-ID: A Framework for Analyzing Transformation Biases in AI-Generated Image Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31153)
*Jonas Ricker, Asja Fischer, Erwin Quiring*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 未分类

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (46.59) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 BIAS-ID 框架，用于分析和量化 AI 生成图像检测器中的变换偏差（transformation biases），揭示现有检测方法在真实场景下因训练数据偏差而依赖虚假相关性，而非真正的取证痕迹。通过对六个检测器在两个数据集上的评估，发现多个 SOTA 方法受到严重偏差影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 生成图像检测器在受控条件下表现良好，但在真实数据上性能骤降。潜在原因是训练数据中存在细微偏差，导致检测器依赖虚假相关性而非真正的取证痕迹。目前缺乏评估检测器偏差程度的标准化协议。

Method: 提出 BIAS-ID 框架，明确定义检测器偏差与鲁棒性不足的区别，通过系统分析变换偏差（如 JPEG 压缩、缩放、滤波等图像变换）对检测器决策的影响，量化偏差程度。在六个检测器和两个数据集上进行实验验证。

Result: 多个 SOTA AI 生成图像检测方法受到严重的变换偏差影响，检测器对特定图像变换的敏感性远高于对真实伪造痕迹的捕捉能力。

Conclusion: 偏差感知评估对于开发可靠的 AI 生成图像检测器至关重要，BIAS-ID 为标准化偏差分析提供了透明框架。

Classification reason: 论文研究 AI 生成图像检测器中的变换偏差分析与量化框架，属于 生成式 AI 检测方向，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "生成式 AI 检测与溯源" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on bias analysis in AI-generated image detection, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in generation models. Signals: Bias analysis framework, AI-generated image detection, transformation biases, spurious correlations, not a priority subtopic

Abstract: Given the surge of harmful AI-generated imagery online, reliably distinguishing authentic images from generated ones has become an urgent research topic. While many proposed detection methods perform well under controlled settings, they often collapse when tested on real-world data. A potential root cause are subtle biases in the detectors' training data. As a result, detectors may rely on spurious correlations instead of learning true forensic artifacts. While a recent line of work has identified the problem, there is not yet an established protocol to evaluate how biased a detector actually is. In this work, we therefore take a step back: First, we discuss what it means for a detector to be biased, and how this differs from a lack of robustness. Second, we propose BIAS-ID, a transparent framework for analyzing and quantifying the presence of transformation biases in AI-generated image detectors. We validate our framework by performing an evaluation of six detectors across two datasets, revealing that several state-of-the-art detection methods are strongly affected by biases. Our results highlight the importance of bias-aware evaluation for developing reliable AI-generated image detectors.

</details>


### [206] [Skill is Not One-Size-Fits-All: Model-Aware Skill Alignment for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30723)
*Jianxiang Yu, Jiapeng Zhu, Bochen Lin, Qier Cui, Zichen Ding, Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 未分类

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (45.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出MASA（Model-Aware Skill Alignment）框架，发现技能库对LLM智能体的效果具有模型依赖性——同一技能对不同骨干模型效果差异显著。MASA通过分层技能进化流水线和轻量级模型条件技能重写器，为每个目标模型自适应调整技能，无需修改模型权重，在多个交互环境和骨干模型上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能库通常被视为模型无关的，同一套技能在不同规模的骨干模型上复用。但实验发现技能有效性高度依赖模型——对某个模型有益的技能可能对另一个模型有害。因此需要为不同模型自适应调整技能。

Method: MASA包含两阶段：(1) 分层技能进化流水线，利用爬山算法和UCB驱动的树搜索，结合环境反馈和模型能力画像，迭代重写通用和任务特定技能；(2) 轻量级模型条件技能重写器，在进化轨迹上训练，可在单次前向传播中复现技能适配过程。

Result: 在三个交互环境和四个骨干模型上，MASA一致取得最佳整体性能，相比最强基线提升高达25.8分。训练后的重写器能泛化到未见任务和环境，无需额外搜索，推理成本远低于更大的教师LLM。

Conclusion: 技能并非"一刀切"，模型感知的技能对齐对LLM智能体至关重要。MASA框架有效解决了技能与模型不匹配的问题，且轻量级重写器具有良好的泛化能力和效率优势。

Classification reason: 论文研究LLM智能体的技能对齐与自适应工作流，属于生成式 AI方向下的AI智能体与生成工作流（agent_workflow）这一规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM agent skill alignment, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like video generation or world models. Signals: LLM agent skill alignment, model-aware adaptation, hierarchical skill evolution, lightweight skill rewriter, interactive environments

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly retrieve externally curated skills-procedural instructions retrieved at decision time-to improve performance on long-horizon interactive tasks. Existing skill libraries are typically treated as model-agnostic, reusing the same skill formulations across backbones with substantially different capacities and behaviors. However, our controlled experiments across multiple model scales show that skill effectiveness is strongly model-dependent: a skill that benefits one backbone can harm another. Motivated by this observation, we propose MASA Model-Aware Skill Alignment, a framework that adapts skills to each target backbone without modifying agent weights. MASA operates in two stages: (1) a hierarchical skill evolution pipeline that iteratively rewrites general and task-specific skills using hill climbing and UCB-driven tree search, guided by environment feedback and model capability profiles; and (2) a lightweight model-conditioned skill rewriter trained on evolution trajectories to reproduce the adaptation in a single forward pass. Experiments across three interactive environments and four backbones show that MASA consistently achieves the best overall performance, with gains of up to 25.8 points over the strongest baseline. The learned rewriter further generalizes to unseen tasks and environments without additional search, consistently outperforming a much larger teacher LLM at a fraction of the inference cost.

</details>


### [207] [MosaicLeaks:Privacy Risks in Querying-in-the-Open for Deep Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30727)
*Alexander Gurung, Spandana Gella, Alexandre Drouin, Issam H. Laradji, Perouz Taslakian, Rafael Pardinas*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 未分类

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (46.59) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出MosaicLeaks基准，系统评估深度研究智能体在结合私有文档与外部工具查询时的隐私泄露风险，发现模型普遍存在意图级、答案级和声明级的信息泄露，并提出Privacy-Aware Deep Research (PA-DR)强化学习框架，通过任务奖励与隐私分类器联合优化，将答案泄露率从34.0%降至9.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究智能体越来越多地结合私有本地文档与外部工具（如网络检索），但外部查询可能泄露本地上下文中的敏感信息。这种风险因马赛克效应（单个查询看似无害，聚合后却暴露敏感信息）而被放大。现有研究缺乏系统评估这种隐私泄露的基准和方法。

Method: 构建MosaicLeaks基准（1001个多跳深度研究任务），将私有企业文档与公开网络语料链接，迫使智能体生成依赖本地信息的外部查询。使用对抗LLM从外部查询中推断三个级别的隐私信息（研究意图、私有问题答案、可验证声明）。提出PA-DR框架，结合任务成功的情境奖励与学习型隐私分类器，对逐查询和马赛克级泄露提供密集信用分配。

Result: 多个模型系列和规模在三个泄露级别上均频繁泄露信息；零样本隐私提示可减少但无法消除泄露；仅优化任务性能的强化学习会加剧泄露。使用PA-DR训练Qwen3-4B-Instruct，准确率从48.7%提升至58.7%，答案和全信息泄露从34.0%降至9.9%。

Conclusion: 深度研究智能体的外部查询存在严重的隐私泄露风险，现有防护措施不足。PA-DR框架通过联合优化任务性能与隐私保护，能有效降低泄露，为构建隐私感知的AI智能体提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究深度研究智能体在查询外部工具时泄露私有信息的隐私风险，属于生成式 AI安全与治理范畴。论文提出的MosaicLeaks基准评估了智能体查询中的信息泄露，并提出了隐私感知的RL训练框架PA-DR来缓解泄露，与safety_governance（生成内容安全与治理）中的安全、合规、监管主题高度吻合。

Importance reason: Privacy risks in deep research agents are a niche security topic, not aligned with your priority subtopics on generation efficiency or world models. Signals: privacy leakage, deep research agents, RL safety, mosaic effect, not generation-focused

Abstract: Deep research agents increasingly combine private local documents with external tools like web retrieval, creating a privacy risk: an agent's external queries may leak sensitive information from its local context. This risk is amplified by the mosaic effect, where individual queries may appear harmless but become revealing in aggregate. We introduce MosaicLeaks, a benchmark of 1,001 multi-hop deep research tasks that chain private enterprise documents and a public web corpus, forcing agents to make external queries that depend on local information. We evaluate leakage with an adversary LLM that observes only the agent's external queries and attempts to infer private information at three levels: the agent's research intent, answers to specific private questions and verifiable claims about the enterprise documents. We find that models across families and sizes frequently leak at all three levels, that zero-shot privacy prompting reduces but does not eliminate leakage and that reinforcement learning for task performance alone worsens leakage. To address this, we propose Privacy-Aware Deep Research (PA-DR), an RL framework that combines situational rewards for task success with a learned privacy classifier to provide dense credit assignment over both per-query and mosaic-level leakage. Training Qwen3-4B-Instruct with PA-DR improves accuracy from 48.7% to 58.7% and reduces answer and full-information leakage from 34.0% to 9.9%.

</details>


### [208] [Authentication of Copy Detection Patterns via Cross-Camera Dual-Synthetic Referencing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31292)
*Ivan Oleksiyuk, Roman Chaban, Slava Voloshynovskiy*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 未分类

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (43.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于注册的跨相机双合成参考框架用于 CDP 认证，通过深度学习翻译器联合利用数字模板和注册相机捕获图像生成高质量参考图像，在异构移动相机上提升了认证性能和抗伪造鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CDP 认证方法仅考虑相机失真而忽略打印随机性，导致认证鲁棒性不足，易受伪造攻击。

Method: 提出 enrollment-based cross-camera dual-synthetic referencing 框架：先用受控注册相机捕获每个打印 CDP，再通过深度学习翻译器联合利用数字模板和注册图像，为验证相机生成高质量参考图像，并给出信息论解释。

Result: 在异构移动相机上实验表明，该方法提升了认证性能，对基于机器学习的复制攻击具有鲁棒性，且能在小区域 CDP 和低端设备上实现可靠验证。

Conclusion: 双合成参考比仅基于模板的参考包含更多信息，能有效提升 CDP 认证的鲁棒性和实用性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 Copy Detection Patterns (CDP) 的认证方法，属于物理对象上的防伪检测与溯源技术，与 生成式 AI 检测与溯源（detection_attribution）方向高度相关，因此复用该规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on niche 生成式 AI detection for physical copy patterns, not aligned with priority generative model topics. Signals: 生成式 AI detection, cross-camera authentication, copy detection patterns, not autoregressive/diffusion generation, no world model or efficient inference

Abstract: Copy Detection Patterns (CDPs) are structures printed on physical objects to enable cost-effective authentication. Verification is achieved by comparing a captured image with the digital template from which the CDP was printed. In practice, printer stochasticity and camera distortions hinder this comparison, limiting robustness against counterfeiting. Prior work addressed camera effects by synthesising reference images in the verification camera domain, but it ignored printing variability. We introduce an enrolment-based cross-camera dual-synthetic referencing framework. Each printed CDP is first captured by a controlled enrolment camera, and a deep-learning-based translator jointly exploits the digital template and the enrolled capture to generate a high-quality reference for the verification image. We provide an information-theoretic justification showing that the dual reference is more informative than template-based references. Experiments on heterogeneous mobile cameras demonstrate improved authentication performance, robustness to machine-learning-based copy attacks, and reliable verification from small CDP regions and on low-end devices.

</details>


### [209] [Beyond Accuracy: Evaluating Efficiency, Robustness and Explainability in Deep Learning for Malaria Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30734)
*Olivier Kanamugire, Kerol Djoumessi*

Subtopic: 未分类

TL;DR: 本文在 NLM-Malaria 数据集上系统评估了四种深度学习模型在疟疾诊断中的预测性能、鲁棒性和可解释性。结果表明轻量级模型与重型模型性能无显著差异，CAM 类 XAI 方法能定位诊断相关区域，但所有 XAI 方法在图像损坏下可靠性下降，为资源受限环境下的部署提供了指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疟疾在撒哈拉以南非洲是主要致死原因，诊断基础设施匮乏。深度学习有望实现自动化筛查，但临床部署受限于计算成本和决策不透明性，亟需对模型效率、鲁棒性和可解释性进行联合评估。

Method: 在 NLM-Malaria 数据集上对四种深度学习模型（涵盖轻量级到重型架构）进行基准测试，联合评估预测性能、鲁棒性（三种图像损坏类型）和事后可解释性（CAM 类与细粒度归因方法），并使用 Friedman 检验进行统计显著性分析。

Result: 轻量级模型与重型模型在预测性能上无统计显著差异；CAM 类 XAI 方法能一致定位诊断相关区域，细粒度归因方法在重型骨干网络上效果较差；模型置信度在损坏下比准确率下降更快；所有 XAI 方法在临床可行的噪声水平下可靠性均下降。

Conclusion: 支持在资源受限环境中部署轻量级架构用于疟疾诊断，同时指出事后可解释性在图像损坏下的脆弱性是负责任临床部署的重要考量因素。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦深度学习模型在疟疾诊断（医疗领域）中的应用评估，属于垂直领域内容生成应用范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 domain_application（领域内容生成应用）。

Importance reason: Focuses on malaria diagnosis, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and has negative keyword overlap. Signals: Medical diagnosis, negative keyword, no autoregressive/diffusion/world model, low priority subtopic match

Abstract: Malaria remains a leading cause of mortality in sub-Saharan Africa, where scarce diagnostic infrastructure makes timely, accurate diagnosis particularly challenging. While deep learning offers a compelling path toward automated malaria screening, clinical adoption is hindered by computational cost and opacity in decision-making. This work benchmarks four deep learning models spanning a wide range of designed design architectures and model capacities on the NLM-Malaria dataset, jointly evaluating predictive performance, robustness, and post-hoc explainability. We find that lightweight, efficient-by-design models match their heavier counterparts in predictive performance, and the Friedman test confirms no statistically significant performance differences. CAM-based XAI methods consistently localize diagnostically relevant regions, while fine-grained attribution methods produce less targeted explanations, particularly with heavier backbones. Robustness evaluation under three types of image corruption further reveals that model confidence degrades faster than accuracy, providing a practical signal for human review. However, no XAI method is robust to corruption, with explanation reliability degrading at noise levels plausible in clinical practice, even when predictions remain accurate. These findings support the deployment of lightweight architectures for malaria diagnosis in resource-constrained settings, while highlighting the vulnerability of post-hoc explanations as an important consideration for responsible clinical deployment.

</details>

*Ruiliang Liu, Tina Dongxu Li, Joshua Migdal, Ken Meszaros, Trevor Dardik*
