<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仓库设施中部署计算机视觉模型通常需要在新环境中重复进行相机安装、图像采集、标注、训练和部署，过程耗时耗力。本文旨在探索一种方法，使得仅在实验室环境中进行一次完整流程，就能让模型泛化到多样化的仓库环境，从而大幅节省资源。

Method: 在实验室环境中进行标准的数据采集、标注和训练流程，通过优化相机放置位置、策略性图像触发、精心选择模型以及模型集成等组合策略，使模型能够从实验室条件泛化到不同仓库设施环境。具体针对垂直物料搬运系统的叉臂异常检测任务进行实验验证。

Result: 实验表明，结合最优相机放置、策略性图像触发、谨慎的模型选择和模型集成，能够实现从实验室到多样化仓库环境的有效泛化，将部署简化为仅需相机安装、图像采集和模型部署三个步骤，省去了图像标注和模型重训练环节。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法有望改变仓库自动化部署流程，通过实验室训练+泛化策略显著降低在新环境中部署视觉模型的成本和时间。该研究为实验性研究，尚未投入生产部署。

Classification reason: 该论文研究仓库设施中垂直物料搬运系统的异常检测视觉模型泛化问题，属于工业/仓储领域的计算机视觉应用，与"领域内容生成应用"（domain_application）方向匹配，该子主题涵盖垂直领域应用。

Importance reason: This is a narrow industrial application paper with no technical novelty in generative models or core computer vision methods. Signals: warehouse anomaly detection, domain generalization, no generative model, no autoregressive or diffusion method, applied engineering

Abstract: Deploying computer vision models in Warehouse Facilities traditionally requires extensive resources for camera mounting, image collection, annotation, training, and deployment - a process often needing repetition in each new environment due to camera mounting constraints and environmental variability. This paper explores an innovative approach to streamline this process by conducting the standard procedure solely in a laboratory setting, focusing on vertical material handling systems and anomaly detection in forks of the systems. Through extensive experimentation, we have found that combining optimal camera placement, strategic image triggering, careful model selection and model ensemble enables effective generalization from laboratory conditions to diverse warehouse facilities environments, potentially transforming warehouse automation implementation by simplifying warehouse facilities deployment to just camera mounting, image collection, and model deployment, thereby saving significant resources and time typically spent on image annotation and model retraining. This is an experimental research study and not a production deployment.

</details>


### [211] [Reliable Multilingual Orthopedic Decision Support from Clinical Narratives: Language-Aware Adaptation and Verification-Guided Deferral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31512)
*Danish Ali, Li Xiaojian, Sundas Iqbal, Farrukh Zaidi*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 未分类

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (28.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向低资源医疗场景的多语言骨科临床决策支持框架，通过语言感知的领域自适应编码器 IndicBERT-HPA 和选择性验证层，在英语、印地语和旁遮普语的骨科临床文本分类中实现了高可靠性和可控的 deferral 机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言骨科临床决策支持在低资源医疗场景中面临专业术语、混合脚本、不完整证据、标签不平衡和语言依赖的文档模式等挑战，现有方法缺乏针对这些问题的可靠性框架。

Method: 提出 IndicBERT-HPA，在 IndicBERT 基础上增加语言感知的骨科适配器头以支持多语言表示学习；并设计了一个确定性选择性验证层，结合置信度门控、证据一致性检查和语言风险筛查，实现可控制的 deferral 机制。

Result: 在自然临床分布下，IndicBERT-HPA 达到平均 Macro-F1 0.8792、Macro-AUROC 0.894、AUPRC 0.902；选择性验证层在 72.3% 覆盖率下达到 84.4% 的准确率和 0.76 的 Macro-F1，显著优于全接受基线。

Conclusion: 任务适配的编码器在封闭集分类中显著优于零样本 LLM，结合选择性验证的 deferral 机制能有效提升临床决策支持的可靠性，为低资源多语言医疗场景提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于多语言骨科临床决策支持，属于医疗垂直领域的文本分类与决策辅助应用，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题"领域内容生成应用"（domain_application）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on low-resource medical NLP, not aligned with user's priority generative AI and video/image generation topics. Signals: medical NLP, low-resource languages, clinical decision support, not generative AI, not video/image generation

Abstract: Multilingual orthopedic decision support remains challenging in low-resource healthcare settings, where clinical narratives contain specialized terminology, mixed scripts, incomplete evidence, label imbalance and language-dependent documentation patterns. This article presents a reliability-oriented framework for classifying free-text orthopedic notes in English, Hindi and Punjabi. We compare task-aligned multilingual transformer encoders, a task-fine-tuned DistilBERT baseline, zero-shot instruction-tuned large language models (LLMs) and a domain-adaptive encoder, IndicBERT-HPA. IndicBERT-HPA augments IndicBERT with language-aware orthopedic adapter heads to support clinically relevant multilingual representation learning. Evaluation extends beyond aggregate accuracy to per-class performance, ROC-AUC, AUPRC, expected calibration error, cross-language stability and robustness under controlled balanced and natural-prevalence distributions. The evaluated zero-shot LLMs remain substantially less effective than task-adapted encoders for closed-set classification, with language-dependent instability. Under natural clinical prevalence, IndicBERT-HPA achieves the strongest overall performance, reaching an averaged Macro-F1 of 0.8792, Macro-AUROC of 0.894 and AUPRC of 0.902. We further implement a deterministic selective-verification layer combining confidence gating, evidence-consistency checking and language-risk screening. On a randomly selected held-out 5,000-record subset, it achieves 84.4% selective accuracy and 0.76 selective Macro-F1 at 72.3% coverage, compared with 71.5% accuracy and 0.65 Macro-F1 for accept-all prediction. These results support reliability-oriented multilingual clinical decision support with explicit deferral.

</details>


### [212] [Forgetting Has Neighbors: Localized Collateral Forgetting in Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31317)
*Polina Dolgova, Sebastian U. Stich*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 未分类

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (38.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现机器遗忘方法（如梯度上升、随机标签）在示例级别上存在高度非均匀的遗忘效果：与遗忘集几何邻近的样本会遭受更大的预测偏差，称为"局部连带遗忘"。作者分析了该现象的机制（替代目标与重训练局部预测结构不一致），并提出局部教师蒸馏（Local Teacher Distillation）作为缓解策略，在 CIFAR-100 上有效缩小了与重训练模型的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘评估通常使用聚合指标（如准确率、遗忘分数），这些指标可能掩盖局部失败模式。作者希望揭示遗忘方法在示例级别上的非均匀行为，并理解其背后的机制。

Method: 1) 在示例级别上比较遗忘模型与重训练模型的预测差异，发现该差异随样本与遗忘集的几何邻近度增大而增大。2) 分析机制：遗忘过程中使用的替代目标（如随机标签）与重训练产生的局部预测结构不一致，这种不一致通过共享表征传播到邻近样本。3) 提出局部教师蒸馏（Local Teacher Distillation）：仅在遗忘集的邻近保留样本上训练一个小型教师模型，用其软标签替代随机目标进行遗忘。

Result: 在 CIFAR-100 部分类别删除任务上，局部教师蒸馏显著缩小了遗忘模型与重训练模型之间的预测差距，尤其是在遗忘集附近区域，同时保持了有竞争力的聚合遗忘指标。

Conclusion: 机器遗忘存在局部连带遗忘现象，即遗忘效果在几何邻近区域非均匀退化。通过局部教师蒸馏提供与局部预测结构一致的软标签，可以有效缓解该问题，使遗忘模型更接近理想的重训练模型。

Classification reason: 该论文研究机器遗忘（machine unlearning）中的局部连带遗忘现象，属于模型 unlearning 范畴。生成式 AI 方向下已有 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "概念擦除与遗忘学习"（unlearning_erasure），与该论文主题高度匹配，因此直接复用。

Importance reason: Focuses on machine unlearning, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and has no overlap with boost keywords. Signals: Machine unlearning, localized collateral forgetting, gradient ascent, random labeling, CIFAR-100

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of selected training examples without full retraining. Standard evaluations often summarize unlearning quality with aggregate metrics, such as accuracy- and forgetting-based scores, which can hide localized failures. We study this failure mode at the example level by comparing the predictions of an unlearned model to those of the model retrained after deletion. We show that this pointwise discrepancy can be highly non-uniform: for gradient-ascent and random-labeling methods, with and without retain-set fine-tuning, it grows with geometric proximity to the forget set. We call this phenomenon localized collateral forgetting. Our analysis identifies a mechanism behind the effect: surrogate targets used during unlearning can be inconsistent with the local prediction structure induced by retraining, and this inconsistency propagates through shared representations to nearby examples. Motivated by this mechanism, we propose Local Teacher Distillation, a simple mitigation strategy that replaces random targets with soft labels from a small teacher trained only on retained neighbors of the forget set. On CIFAR-100 partial-class deletion, this local teacher brings the unlearned model substantially closer to retraining, especially near the forget set, while maintaining competitive aggregate unlearning metrics.

</details>


### [213] [Knowledge Boundary Probing and Demand-Guided Intervention for LLM-Based Power System Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31478)
*Hui Wu, Xiaoyang Wang, Zhong Fan*

Main category: cs.SE, cs.CL, eess.SY

Direction: 未分类

Subtopic: 未分类

Importance: 普通相关 (43.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 PowerCodeBench 基准和知识边界探测方法，通过文档注入与路由纠错干预，显著提升开源 LLM 在电力系统代码生成任务上的准确率，无需微调或云端推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力系统分析中，许多机构因保密、合规、可复现性和成本原因需要本地部署 LLM，但开源模型在电力系统代码生成中可靠性不足，且失败原因不仅是推理错误，更多是 API 知识边界错误（幻觉函数名、参数误用、结果表处理错误）。

Method: 提出 PowerCodeBench 基准生成器（自然语言查询→pandapower 代码+数值真值）；L0-L3 文档驱动探测流程（测量模型 API 知识轮廓）；边界感知干预（查询侧 API 需求估计 + 定向主动文档注入 + 路由被动纠错）。

Result: 在 2000 任务集上评估 10 个开源 LLM（1.5B-480B）和 4 个商业 API，干预使 7B 以上开源模型和所有商业 API 提升 32-56 个准确率点；70B-120B 开源模型达到商业中端水平；Llama-3.1-405B 和 Qwen3-Coder-480B 表现最佳；目标提示仅用 41% 的 token 成本即可保持全上下文准确率上限。

Conclusion: 本文证明通过知识边界探测与需求引导的文档注入干预，可以在不微调、不依赖云端推理的情况下，显著提升开源 LLM 在电力系统代码生成中的可靠性，为本地部署提供实用路径。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦电力系统代码生成，属于垂直领域（代码/科学工程）的生成应用，与 [CANONICAL] 的 domain_application（领域内容生成应用）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on domain-specific code generation for power systems, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative-keyword-filtered. Signals: domain-specific code generation, power system, knowledge boundary probing, documentation injection, no fine-tuning needed

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to automate power-system analysis, but many utilities and energy-research labs require on-premise serving for confidentiality, regulatory, reproducibility, and cost reasons. This makes the reliability of open-weight models a deployment issue. We show that first-pass failures in power-system code generation are dominated not by reasoning alone, but by structured API-knowledge boundary errors: hallucinated function names, misused parameters, and mishandled result tables in versioned simulation libraries. We introduce PowerCodeBench, an execution-validated benchmark generator that pairs natural-language operator queries with pandapower code and numerical ground truth; an L0-L3 documentation-driven probing procedure that measures per-model API knowledge profiles; and a boundary-aware intervention that combines query-side API demand estimation with targeted proactive documentation injection and routed reactive correction. On a 2,000-task frozen release, we evaluate ten open-weight LLMs (1.5B-480B parameters) and four commercial mid-tier APIs. The intervention improves every evaluated open-weight model of at least 7B parameters and every commercial API by 32 to 56 accuracy points. Open-weight models in the 70B-120B range match the commercial mid-tier accuracy range, while Llama-3.1-405B and Qwen3-Coder-480B lead the panel. The targeted prompts preserve the full-context accuracy ceiling while using 41% of the prompt-token cost. The result is an accuracy-side, deployment-time path toward reliable on-premise LLM assistance for grid-analysis workflows without fine-tuning or cloud inference.
